                            .with_label_values(&[&bot_name])
                            .inc();
                        tracing::warn!(
                            "[{}] entries BLOCKED (KILL/daily-stop): {}",
                            bot_name, block_reason
                        );
                        continue;
                    }
                    let _ = trade_journal.record_signal(&sig).await;

                    match execution_agent.execute(sig.clone()).await {
                        Ok(execution::ExecOutcome::Entry { price, qty, .. }) => {
                            metrics::ORDERS_PLACED
                                .with_label_values(&[&bot_name])
                                .inc();
                            let _ = trade_journal
                                .record_execution(&sig, "PLACED")
                                .await;
                            strategy.notify_position_open();
                            tracing::info!(
                                "[{}] вњ… Р’РҐРћР”: {} {:.5} @ {:.8}",
                                bot_name, sig.side, qty, price
                            );
                        }
                        Ok(execution::ExecOutcome::Missed(reason)) => {
                            let _ = trade_journal
                                .record_execution(&sig, "MISSED")
                                .await;
                            tracing::info!("[{}] вЏ­ РІС…РѕРґ РЅРµ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏР»СЃСЏ: {}", bot_name, reason);
                            // рџ”§ РЎРђРњРћРЎРћР—РќРђРќРР•: РІРµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊСЃСЏ РІ FLAT СЃ РїСЂРёС‡РёРЅРѕР№
                            strategy.on_entry_failed(&reason);
                        }
                        Ok(other) => {
                            tracing::warn!("[{}] РІС…РѕРґ: РЅРµРѕР¶РёРґР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ РёСЃС…РѕРґ {:?}", bot_name, other);
                        }
                        Err(e) => {
                            tracing::error!("[{}] вќЊ РѕС€РёР±РєР° РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРёСЏ РІС…РѕРґР°: {:?}", bot_name, e);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // ============================================================
            // POSITION CHECK (СЃ РїР°СЂС‚РёР°Р»-РІРµС‚РєРѕР№)
            // ============================================================
            _ = position_check_interval.tick() => {
                let positions = state_manager.get_positions();
                for pos in &positions {
                    if let Some(exit_signal) = state_manager
                        .check_and_update_position(&pos.symbol, pos.current_price)
                    {
                        match execution_agent.execute(exit_signal.clone()).await {
                            Ok(execution::ExecOutcome::Exit(pnl)) => {
                                if exit_signal.strategy_name == "Exit_Partial" {
                                    risk_agent.record_trade(&exit_signal.symbol, pnl);
                                    let _ = trade_journal
                                        .record_close(&exit_signal, pnl)
                                        .await;
                                    global_risk.add_closed_pnl(pnl); // PATCH P15
                                    metrics::PNL_TOTAL
                                        .with_label_values(&[&bot_name])
                                        .add(pnl);
                                    tracing::info!(
                                        "рџ’µ [{}] PARTIAL Р—РђР¤РРљРЎРР РћР’РђРќ (С‚Р°Р№РјРµСЂ) | PnL={:+.4} USDT",
                                        bot_name, pnl
                                    );
                                    continue;
                                }

                                strategy.notify_position_closed(pnl);
                                risk_agent.record_trade(&exit_signal.symbol, pnl);

                                metrics::SUSPENDED.with_label_values(&[&bot_name]).set(
                                    if risk_agent.is_suspended() { 1 } else { 0 }
                                );

                                let result = if pnl > 0.0 { "win" }
                                             else if pnl < 0.0 { "loss" }
                                             else { "flat" };
                                metrics::TRADES_CLOSED
                                    .with_label_values(&[&bot_name, result])
                                    .inc();
                                metrics::PNL_TOTAL
                                    .with_label_values(&[&bot_name])
                                    .add(pnl);
                                metrics::WIN_RATE
                                    .with_label_values(&[&bot_name])
                                    .set(risk_agent.win_rate());
                                metrics::PROFIT_FACTOR
                                    .with_label_values(&[&bot_name])
                                    .set(risk_agent.profit_factor());

                                let _ = trade_journal
                                    .record_close(&exit_signal, pnl)
                                    .await;
                                global_risk.add_closed_pnl(pnl); // PATCH P15

                                tracing::info!(
                                    "рџ”” [{}] РЎР”Р•Р›РљРђ Р—РђРљР Р«РўРђ (РїРѕ С‚Р°Р№РјРµСЂСѓ) | СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚={} | PnL={:+.4}",
                                    bot_name, result, pnl
                                );
                            }
                            Ok(execution::ExecOutcome::ExitUnconfirmed) => {
                                tracing::warn!("[{}] Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ (С‚Р°Р№РјРµСЂ) РЅРµ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРѕ вЂ” РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂ", bot_name);
                                // рџ”§ РЎРђРњРћРЎРћР—РќРђРќРР•: СЃСѓРґСЊР±Р° РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё РЅРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅР°
                                strategy.on_position_unknown();
                            }
                            Ok(other) => {
                                if exit_signal.strategy_name == "Exit_UpdateSL" {
                                    // qty=0 вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ СЃРёРЅС…СЂРѕРЅРёР·Р°С†РёСЏ Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІРѕРіРѕ СЃС‚РѕРїР°, РЅРµ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ
                                    tracing::info!("[{}] рџ›Ў Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІРѕР№ СЃС‚РѕРї СЃРёРЅС…СЂРѕРЅРёР·РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ ({:?})", bot_name, other);
                                } else {
                                    tracing::error!("[{}] Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ (С‚Р°Р№РјРµСЂ): РЅРµРѕР¶РёРґР°РµРјС‹Р№ РёСЃС…РѕРґ {:?}", bot_name, other);
                                }
                            }
                            Err(e) => {
                                if exit_signal.strategy_name == "Exit_UpdateSL" {
                                    tracing::warn!("[{}] рџ›Ў СЃРёРЅС…СЂРѕРЅРёР·Р°С†РёСЏ СЃС‚РѕРїР° РЅРµ СѓРґР°Р»Р°СЃСЊ (РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂ РЅР° СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РµРј С‚РёРєРµ): {:?}", bot_name, e);
                                } else {
                                    tracing::error!("[{}] Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ (С‚Р°Р№РјРµСЂ): {:?}", bot_name, e);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // ============================================================
            // LOG STATE
            // ============================================================
            _ = log_interval.tick() => {
                state_manager.log_state();
                state_manager.persist_positions().await;
                                // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ C: GROWTH/SURVIVAL/HALT в†’ РјРЅРѕР¶РёС‚РµР»СЊ в†’ СЃР°Р№Р·РёРЅРі СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёРё
                strategy.set_risk_multiplier(risk_agent.risk_multiplier());
                risk_agent.flush_stats().await;
                if let Some(diag) = strategy.diagnostics() {
                    tracing::info!("[{}] {}", bot_name, diag);
                }

                // вњ… v3.5: С‚РѕРї-Р°Рї РёР· РєСЌС€Р° С…Р°Р±Р° вЂ” РґРѕР±РѕСЂ СЃРІРµС‡РµР№, РЅРµ РґРѕС€РµРґС€РёС…
                // РїРѕ broadcast (РЅРѕСЂРјР°: 0 РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ С‚РёРє). Р“Р°СЂР°РЅС‚РёСЂСѓРµС‚ Р°РєС‚СѓР°Р»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ
                // РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂРѕРІ РґР°Р¶Рµ РїСЂРё РґРµРіСЂР°РґР°С†РёРё broadcast-РїСѓС‚Рё.
                let mut topup_fed: u64 = 0;
                for k in hub.recent_klines() {
                    if !k.is_closed || k.timestamp <= last_fed_candle_ts {
                        continue;
                    }
                    if k.is_backfill && !strategy.wants_backfill() {
                        continue;
                    }
                    last_fed_candle_ts = k.timestamp;
                    let msg = websocket::WebSocketMessage::Kline(k.clone());
                    match strategy_agent.process(&msg, strategy.as_mut()).await {
                        Ok(Some(_)) => {} // СЃРёРіРЅР°Р» РёР· РґРѕР±РѕСЂР° РЅРµ РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ
                        Ok(_) => {}
                        Err(e) => {
                            tracing::warn!("[{}] С‚РѕРї-Р°Рї: СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ РѕС‚РєР»РѕРЅРёР»Р° СЃРІРµС‡Сѓ: {}", bot_name, e);
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    topup_fed += 1;
                }
                if topup_fed > 0 {
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "[{}] рџ”Ѓ С‚РѕРї-Р°Рї РёР· РєСЌС€Р° С…Р°Р±Р°: {} СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ (РјРёРјРѕ broadcast вЂ” РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂС‹ Р°РєС‚СѓР°Р»СЊРЅС‹)",
                        bot_name, topup_fed
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }

    metrics::BOTS_ACTIVE.with_label_values(&[&bot_name]).set(0);
    Ok(())
}

use crate::exchange::ExchangeClient;
use crate::state::StateManager;
use crate::strategies::Signal;
use crate::websocket::WebSocketMessage;
use anyhow::Result;
use std::sync::Arc;

pub struct StateAgent {
    manager: Arc<StateManager>,
    _exchange_client: Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>,
}

impl StateAgent {
    pub fn new(manager: Arc<StateManager>, exchange_client: Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>) -> Self {
        Self {
            manager,
            _exchange_client: exchange_client,
        }
    }

    pub async fn update(&self, msg: &WebSocketMessage) -> Result<Option<Signal>> {
        match msg {
            WebSocketMessage::Trade(trade) => {
                if let Some(exit_signal) =
                    self.manager.check_and_update_position(&trade.symbol, trade.price)
                {
                    return Ok(Some(exit_signal));
                }
            }
            // вњ… 3Рі: С†РµРЅР° РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё РїРѕ РјРёРґСѓ bookTicker
            WebSocketMessage::BookTicker(bt) => {
                if bt.best_bid > 0.0 && bt.best_ask > 0.0 {
                    let mid = (bt.best_bid + bt.best_ask) / 2.0;
                    if let Some(exit_signal) =
                        self.manager.check_and_update_position(&bt.symbol, mid)
                    {
                        return Ok(Some(exit_signal));
                    }
                }
            }
            // вњ… 6 (Рљ-6 РґРѕРІРѕРґ): С†РµРЅР° РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё РїРѕ СЃРІРµС‡Рµ вЂ” futures-Р±РѕС‚С‹ РѕСЃС‚Р°СЋС‚СЃСЏ
            // Р¶РёРІС‹РјРё РїСЂРё РѕР±СЂС‹РІРµ WS: kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ РїРѕ REST.
            // вњ… Рџ3: backfill-СЃРІРµС‡Рё (РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ РїСЂРё СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚Рµ) РџР РћРџРЈРЎРљРђР•Рњ вЂ” СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Рµ
            // С†РµРЅС‹ РЅРµ РґРѕР»Р¶РЅС‹ СЃСЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°С‚СЊ SL/TP РІРѕСЃСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРЅС‹С… РїРѕР·РёС†РёР№.
            WebSocketMessage::Kline(k) => {
                if k.close > 0.0 && !k.is_backfill {
                    if let Some(exit_signal) =
                        self.manager.check_and_update_position(&k.symbol, k.close)
                    {
                        return Ok(Some(exit_signal));
                    }
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        Ok(None)
    }

    pub async fn sync_if_needed(&self) -> Result<()> {
        Ok(())
    }
}

=== C:\Users\BASE_PC\scalping_agentsGLM\src\bin\analyze_slippage.rs ===
//! analyze_slippage.rs вЂ” РєР°Р»РёР±СЂРѕРІРєР° РјРѕРґРµР»Рё РїСЂРѕСЃРєР°Р»СЊР·С‹РІР°РЅРёСЏ РЅР° bookTicker-РґР°С‚Р°СЃРµС‚Рµ.
//!
//! Р—Р°РїСѓСЃРє:
//!   cargo run --release --bin analyze_slippage -- data/bookticker_live
//!   cargo run --release --bin analyze_slippage -- data/bookticker_live --symbol 1000PEPEUSDT
//!   cargo run --release --bin analyze_slippage -- data/bookticker_live --sizes 100,500,1000,5000
//!
//! Р§С‚Рѕ РґРµР»Р°РµС‚:
//!   1. РЎРєР°РЅРёСЂСѓРµС‚ РґРёСЂРµРєС‚РѕСЂРёСЋ РЅР° CSV-С„Р°Р№Р»С‹ bookTicker (С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚: SYMBOL-YYYY-MM-DD.csv)
//!   2. РџР°СЂСЃРёС‚ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё: update_id,bid,bid_qty,ask,ask_qty,txn_time,event_time
//!   3. Р”Р»СЏ РєР°Р¶РґРѕРіРѕ С‚РёРєР° СЃС‡РёС‚Р°РµС‚ spread, L1 depth, slippage РґР»СЏ СЂР°Р·РЅС‹С… СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂРѕРІ РѕСЂРґРµСЂРѕРІ
//!   4. РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚ reservoir sampling вЂ” РїР°РјСЏС‚СЊ РѕРіСЂР°РЅРёС‡РµРЅР° ~10K РѕР±СЂР°Р·С†РѕРІ РЅР° СЃС‚Р°С‚РёСЃС‚РёРєСѓ
//!   5. Р’С‹РІРѕРґРёС‚ mean/median/p95 РґР»СЏ spread, depth, slippage
//!
//! РџР°РјСЏС‚СЊ: ~640 KB РЅР° СЃРёРјРІРѕР» (reservoir РїРѕ 10K РѕР±СЂР°Р·С†РѕРІ Г— 8 stats).
//! Р’СЂРµРјСЏ: ~1-3 РјРёРЅСѓС‚С‹ РЅР° 18M С‚РёРєРѕРІ.

use std::env;
use std::fs;
use std::fs::File;
use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader, Write};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::time::Instant;

const RESERVOIR_CAPACITY: usize = 10_000;

/// Streaming-СЃС‚Р°С‚РёСЃС‚РёРєР° СЃ reservoir sampling РґР»СЏ РѕС†РµРЅРєРё percentile.
/// РџР°РјСЏС‚СЊ: O(capacity) вЂ” С„РёРєСЃРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅР°СЏ.
struct StreamingStats {
    n: u64,
    sum: f64,
    sum_sq: f64,
    min: f64,
    max: f64,
    reservoir: Vec<f64>,
    capacity: usize,
    lcg_state: u64,
}

impl StreamingStats {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            n: 0,
            sum: 0.0,
            sum_sq: 0.0,
            min: f64::INFINITY,
            max: f64::NEG_INFINITY,
            reservoir: Vec::with_capacity(RESERVOIR_CAPACITY),
            capacity: RESERVOIR_CAPACITY,
            lcg_state: 0x6d5f9c3a8b2e1f04, // seed
        }
    }

    /// Linear Congruential Generator вЂ” pseudo-random Р±РµР· Р·Р°РІРёСЃРёРјРѕСЃС‚Рё РѕС‚ rand crate.
    fn next_rand(&mut self) -> u64 {
        // РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј Numerical Recipes LCG
        self.lcg_state = self
            .lcg_state
            .wrapping_mul(6364136223846793005)
            .wrapping_add(1442695040888963407);
        self.lcg_state
    }

    fn add(&mut self, value: f64) {
        self.n += 1;
        self.sum += value;
        self.sum_sq += value * value;
        if value < self.min {
            self.min = value;
        }
        if value > self.max {
            self.max = value;
        }

        // Reservoir sampling (Algorithm R):
        //   Р•СЃР»Рё Р±СѓС„РµСЂ РЅРµ Р·Р°РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅ вЂ” РґРѕР±Р°РІР»СЏРµРј
        //   РРЅР°С‡Рµ вЂ” Р·Р°РјРµРЅСЏРµРј СЃР»СѓС‡Р°Р№РЅС‹Р№ СЌР»РµРјРµРЅС‚ СЃ РІРµСЂРѕСЏС‚РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊСЋ capacity/n
        if self.reservoir.len() < self.capacity {
            self.reservoir.push(value);
        } else {
            let j = self.next_rand() % self.n;
            if (j as usize) < self.capacity {
                self.reservoir[j as usize] = value;
            }
        }
    }

    fn mean(&self) -> f64 {
        if self.n == 0 {
            0.0
        } else {
            self.sum / self.n as f64
        }
    }

    fn percentile(&self, p: f64) -> f64 {
        if self.reservoir.is_empty() {
            return 0.0;
        }
        let mut sorted = self.reservoir.clone();
        sorted.sort_by(|a, b| a.partial_cmp(b).unwrap_or(std::cmp::Ordering::Equal));
        let idx = ((sorted.len() as f64 - 1.0) * p / 100.0).round() as usize;
        sorted[idx.min(sorted.len() - 1)]
    }
}

struct SymbolStats {
    n: u64,
    spreads: StreamingStats,
    bid_depths: StreamingStats,
    ask_depths: StreamingStats,
    mids: StreamingStats,
    buy_slippage: HashMap<u32, StreamingStats>,
    sell_slippage: HashMap<u32, StreamingStats>,
}

impl SymbolStats {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            n: 0,
            spreads: StreamingStats::new(),
            bid_depths: StreamingStats::new(),
            ask_depths: StreamingStats::new(),
            mids: StreamingStats::new(),
            buy_slippage: HashMap::new(),
            sell_slippage: HashMap::new(),
        }
    }
}

fn parse_line(line: &str) -> Option<(f64, f64, f64, f64, i64)> {
    let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split(',').collect();
    if parts.len() < 7 {
        return None;
    }
    let bid: f64 = parts[1].trim().parse().ok()?;
    let bid_qty: f64 = parts[2].trim().parse().ok()?;
    let ask: f64 = parts[3].trim().parse().ok()?;
    let ask_qty: f64 = parts[4].trim().parse().ok()?;
    let ts: i64 = parts[6].trim().parse().ok().unwrap_or(0);
    Some((bid, bid_qty, ask, ask_qty, ts))
}

/// РћС†РµРЅРєР° slippage РґР»СЏ СЂС‹РЅРѕС‡РЅРѕРіРѕ РѕСЂРґРµСЂР° СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂРѕРј size_qty С‡РµСЂРµР· L1 СЃС‚Р°РєР°РЅ.
/// Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ slippage РІ РїСЂРѕС†РµРЅС‚Р°С… РѕС‚ mid.
fn estimate_slippage(size_qty: f64, l1_qty: f64, half_spread_pct: f64) -> f64 {
    if l1_qty <= 0.0 {
        return half_spread_pct * 2.0;
    }
    if size_qty <= l1_qty {
        return half_spread_pct;
    }
    let excess_ratio = (size_qty - l1_qty) / l1_qty;
    half_spread_pct * (1.0 + excess_ratio)
}

fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let args: Vec<String> = env::args().collect();
    if args.len() < 2 {
        eprintln!("Usage: analyze_slippage <dir> [--symbol SYMBOL] [--sizes 100,500,1000,5000] [--out report.csv]");
        std::process::exit(1);
    }
    let dir = &args[1];
    let mut symbol_filter: Option<String> = None;
    let mut sizes: Vec<u32> = vec![100, 500, 1000, 5000];
    let mut out_path: Option<String> = None;

    let mut i = 2;
    while i < args.len() {
        match args[i].as_str() {
            "--symbol" => {
                symbol_filter = Some(args[i + 1].to_uppercase());
                i += 2;
            }
            "--sizes" => {
                sizes = args[i + 1]
                    .split(',')
                    .filter_map(|s| s.trim().parse().ok())
                    .collect();
                i += 2;
            }
            "--out" => {
                out_path = Some(args[i + 1].clone());
                i += 2;
            }
            _ => {
                i += 1;
            }
        }
    }

    // РЎРѕР±РёСЂР°РµРј СЃРїРёСЃРѕРє CSV
    let mut files: Vec<PathBuf> = Vec::new();
    for entry in fs::read_dir(dir)? {
        let entry = entry?;
        let path = entry.path();
        if path.extension().map(|e| e == "csv").unwrap_or(false) {
            if let Some(s) = &symbol_filter {
                let fname = path
                    .file_name()
                    .map(|f| f.to_string_lossy().to_string())
                    .unwrap_or_default();
                if !fname.starts_with(s) {
                    continue;
                }
            }
            files.push(path);
        }
    }
    files.sort();

    if files.is_empty() {
        eprintln!("вќЊ РќРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ CSV-С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ РІ {}", dir);
        std::process::exit(1);
    }

    println!("рџ“‚ РќР°Р№РґРµРЅРѕ {} CSV-С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ РІ {}", files.len(), dir);
    if let Some(s) = &symbol_filter {
        println!("   Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂ РїРѕ СЃРёРјРІРѕР»Сѓ: {}", s);
    }
    println!("   Р Р°Р·РјРµСЂС‹ РѕСЂРґРµСЂРѕРІ: {:?}", sizes);
    println!();

    let start = Instant::now();
    let mut stats: HashMap<String, SymbolStats> = HashMap::new();

    for (idx, file) in files.iter().enumerate() {
        let symbol = file
            .file_stem()
            .unwrap()
            .to_string_lossy()
            .split('-')
            .next()
            .unwrap_or("UNKNOWN")
            .to_string();

        let stat = stats.entry(symbol.clone()).or_insert_with(|| {
            let mut s = SymbolStats::new();
            for &size in &sizes {
                s.buy_slippage.insert(size, StreamingStats::new());
                s.sell_slippage.insert(size, StreamingStats::new());
            }
            s
        });

        let reader = BufReader::new(File::open(file)?);

        for (lineno, line) in reader.lines().enumerate() {
            let line = match line {
                Ok(l) => l,
                Err(_) => continue,
            };
            if lineno == 0
                && !line
                    .chars()
                    .next()
                    .map(|c| c.is_ascii_digit())
                    .unwrap_or(false)
            {
                continue;
            }

            let (bid, bid_qty, ask, ask_qty, _ts) = match parse_line(&line) {
                Some(x) => x,
                None => continue,
            };
            if bid <= 0.0 || ask <= 0.0 || bid_qty < 0.0 || ask_qty < 0.0 {
                continue;
            }

            let mid = (bid + ask) / 2.0;
            if mid <= 0.0 {
                continue;
            }
            let spread_pct = (ask - bid) / mid * 100.0;
            let half_spread_pct = spread_pct / 2.0;
            let bid_depth_usdt = bid_qty * mid;
            let ask_depth_usdt = ask_qty * mid;

            stat.n += 1;
            stat.spreads.add(spread_pct);
            stat.bid_depths.add(bid_depth_usdt);
            stat.ask_depths.add(ask_depth_usdt);
            stat.mids.add(mid);

            // Slippage РґР»СЏ РєР°Р¶РґРѕРіРѕ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂР° РѕСЂРґРµСЂР°
            for &size_usdt in &sizes {
                let size_qty = size_usdt as f64 / mid;

                let buy_slip = estimate_slippage(size_qty, ask_qty, half_spread_pct);
                let sell_slip = estimate_slippage(size_qty, bid_qty, half_spread_pct);

                if let Some(s) = stat.buy_slippage.get_mut(&size_usdt) {
                    s.add(buy_slip);
                }
                if let Some(s) = stat.sell_slippage.get_mut(&size_usdt) {
                    s.add(sell_slip);
                }
            }
        }

        if (idx + 1) % 5 == 0 || idx + 1 == files.len() {
            println!(
                "  [{}] РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РЅРѕ {}/{} С„Р°Р№Р»РѕРІ, {} С‚РёРєРѕРІ РІ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµРј СЃРёРјРІРѕР»Рµ",
                idx + 1,
                idx + 1,
                files.len(),
                stat.n
            );
        }
    }

    let elapsed = start.elapsed();
    println!("\nвЏ± Р“РѕС‚РѕРІРѕ Р·Р° {:.2}s", elapsed.as_secs_f64());

    // === Р’С‹РІРѕРґ СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚РѕРІ ===
    let mut out_writer: Option<std::io::BufWriter<std::fs::File>> = out_path
        .as_ref()
        .and_then(|p| File::create(p).ok())
        .map(std::io::BufWriter::new);

    if let Some(w) = &mut out_writer {
        writeln!(w, "symbol,ticks,spread_mean%,spread_median%,spread_p95%,bid_depth_usdt_median,ask_depth_usdt_median,order_size_usdt,buy_slip_mean%,buy_slip_p95%,sell_slip_mean%,sell_slip_p95%")?;
    }

    // РЎРѕСЂС‚РёСЂСѓРµРј СЃРёРјРІРѕР»С‹ РїРѕ Р°Р»С„Р°РІРёС‚Сѓ РґР»СЏ РїСЂРµРґСЃРєР°Р·СѓРµРјРѕРіРѕ РІС‹РІРѕРґР°
    let mut symbols: Vec<&String> = stats.keys().collect();
    symbols.sort();

    for symbol in symbols {
        let stat = stats.get(symbol).unwrap();

        println!("\n{}", "в•ђ".repeat(70));
        println!("рџ“Љ {} вЂ” {} С‚РёРєРѕРІ", symbol, stat.n);

        if stat.spreads.n == 0 {
            println!("   (РЅРµС‚ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…)");
            continue;
        }

        let spread_mean = stat.spreads.mean();
        let spread_median = stat.spreads.percentile(50.0);
        let spread_p95 = stat.spreads.percentile(95.0);
        let bid_depth_median = stat.bid_depths.percentile(50.0);
        let ask_depth_median = stat.ask_depths.percentile(50.0);
        let mid_median = stat.mids.percentile(50.0);

        println!("   РЎСЂРµРґРЅСЏСЏ С†РµРЅР° (mid, РјРµРґРёР°РЅР°): {:.6}", mid_median);
        println!(
            "   Spread:  mean={:.4}%  median={:.4}%  p95={:.4}%",
            spread_mean, spread_median, spread_p95
        );
        println!(
            "   L1 depth bid (USDT, РјРµРґРёР°РЅР°): {:.2}",
            bid_depth_median
        );
        println!(
            "   L1 depth ask (USDT, РјРµРґРёР°РЅР°): {:.2}",
            ask_depth_median
        );

        println!("\n   рџ“ђ Slippage РїРѕ СЂР°Р·РјРµСЂР°Рј РѕСЂРґРµСЂРѕРІ:");
        println!(
            "   {:>10}  {:>13}  {:>13}  {:>13}  {:>13}",
            "Size USDT", "BUY mean", "BUY p95", "SELL mean", "SELL p95"
        );
        println!("   {}", "в”Ђ".repeat(70));

        for &size in &sizes {
            if let (Some(buys), Some(sells)) =
                (stat.buy_slippage.get(&size), stat.sell_slippage.get(&size))
            {
                let bm = buys.mean();
                let bp = buys.percentile(95.0);
                let sm = sells.mean();
                let sp = sells.percentile(95.0);
                println!(
                    "   {:>10}  {:>12.4}%  {:>12.4}%  {:>12.4}%  {:>12.4}%",
                    size, bm, bp, sm, sp
                );

                if let Some(w) = &mut out_writer {
                    writeln!(
                        w,
                        "{},{},{:.4},{:.4},{:.4},{:.2},{:.2},{},{:.4},{:.4},{:.4},{:.4}",
                        symbol,
                        stat.n,
                        spread_mean,
                        spread_median,
                        spread_p95,
                        bid_depth_median,
                        ask_depth_median,
                        size,
                        bm,
                        bp,
                        sm,
                        sp
                    )?;
                }
            }
        }

        // === РЎРІРѕРґРєР° РґР»СЏ strategy-design СЂРµС€РµРЅРёР№ ===
        println!("\n   рџЋЇ Р РµРєРѕРјРµРЅРґР°С†РёСЏ РґР»СЏ $200-Р±РѕС‚Р° (1 USDT risk per trade):");
        for &size in &sizes {
            if let Some(buys) = stat.buy_slippage.get(&size) {
                let bp95 = buys.percentile(95.0);
                let verdict = if bp95 < 0.05 {
                    "вњ… Р±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅРѕ (slip < 5bps)"
                } else if bp95 < 0.20 {
                    "вљ пёЏ РїСЂРёРµРјР»РµРјРѕ (slip < 20bps)"
                } else if bp95 < 0.50 {
                    "рџ”ґ РІС‹СЃРѕРєРёР№ slip (50bps) вЂ” РЅСѓР¶РµРЅ maker entry"
                } else {
                    "вќЊ РЅРµРїСЂРёРµРјР»РµРјРѕ вЂ” РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ limit"
                };
                println!(
                    "     ${} notional:  p95 slip = {:.4}%  {}",
                    size, bp95, verdict
                );
            }
        }
    }

    if let Some(path) = out_path {
        println!("\nрџ’ѕ РћС‚С‡С‘С‚ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅС‘РЅ: {}", path);
    }

    Ok(())
}

//! Backtest-Р±РёРЅР°СЂРЅРёРє. Р РµР¶РёРјС‹:
//!   --data trades      вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ aggTrades
//!   --data klines      вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ klines
//!   --data spread      вЂ” merged bookTicker + aggTrades (РґР»СЏ spread_capture)
//!   --data book        вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ bookTicker (Р±РµР· aggTrades, fill СЃРёРјСѓР»РёСЂСѓРµС‚СЃСЏ)
//!
//! РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·РѕРІР°РЅРёРµ:
//!   backtest <book_dir> --bot <name> --data book
//!   backtest <trades_dir> --bot <name> --data spread --bookticker <book_dir>
//!   backtest <trades_dir> --bot <name> --data trades
//!   backtest <klines_dir> --bot <name> --data klines

use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use rust_decimal::prelude::ToPrimitive;
use std::env;
use std::fs::{self, File};
use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader, Lines};
use std::iter::Peekable;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::{Arc, RwLock};

use scalping_agents::config::AppConfig;
use scalping_agents::execution::slippage::SlippageCalculator;
use scalping_agents::strategies::{self, OrderBookAnalyzer, OrderBookState, Strategy};
use scalping_agents::websocket::{
    BookTickerMessage, KlineMessage, TradeMessage, WebSocketMessage,
};

const TAKER_FEE_SPOT: f64 = 0.00075;
const TAKER_FEE_FUTURES: f64 = 0.0005;
const MAKER_FEE_FUTURES: f64 = 0.0002;
const MAKER_FEE_SPOT: f64 = 0.0002;
const MIN_NOTIONAL: f64 = 5.0;
const SL_SLIPPAGE_TICKS: f64 = 2.0;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
enum DataType {
    Trades,
    Klines,
    Spread,
    Book, // вњ… РќРћР’РћР•: С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ bookTicker, Р±РµР· aggTrades
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct OpenPosition {
    side: String,
    entry: f64,
    qty: f64,
    sl: Option<f64>,
    tp: Option<f64>,
    opened_at_ms: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct PendingLimit {
    side: String,
    price: f64,
    qty: f64,
    sl: f64,
    tp: f64,
    placed_at_ms: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
struct ClosedTrade {
    side: String,
    entry: f64,
    exit: f64,
    qty: f64,
    pnl_gross: f64,
    fees: f64,
    pnl_net: f64,
    reason: &'static str,
    duration_ms: i64,
    day_key: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default, Clone)]
#[allow(dead_code)]
struct Stats {
    n: usize,
    wins: usize,
    losses: usize,
    win_rate: f64,
    avg_win: f64,
    avg_loss: f64,
    ev: f64,
    profit_factor: f64,
    net_pnl: f64,
    gross_profit: f64,
    gross_loss: f64,
    max_dd: f64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
struct Args {
    primary_path: String,
    bookticker_path: Option<String>,
    bot_name: Option<String>,
    symbol_override: Option<String>,
    config_path: String,
    data_type: Option<DataType>,
}

fn parse_args() -> Result<Args> {
    let argv: Vec<String> = env::args().collect();
    if argv.len() < 2 {
        print_usage();
        std::process::exit(1);
    }
    let mut args = Args {
        primary_path: argv[1].clone(),
        bookticker_path: None,
        bot_name: None,
        symbol_override: None,
        config_path: "config.toml".to_string(),
        data_type: None,
    };
    let mut i = 2;
    while i < argv.len() {
        match argv[i].as_str() {
            "--bot" => { args.bot_name = Some(argv[i + 1].clone()); i += 2; }
            "--symbol" => { args.symbol_override = Some(argv[i + 1].to_uppercase()); i += 2; }
            "--config" => { args.config_path = argv[i + 1].clone(); i += 2; }
            "--bookticker" => { args.bookticker_path = Some(argv[i + 1].clone()); i += 2; }
            "--data" => {
                args.data_type = Some(match argv[i + 1].as_str() {
                    "trades" | "aggTrades" => DataType::Trades,
                    "klines" | "kline" => DataType::Klines,
                    "spread" | "merged" => DataType::Spread,
                    "book" | "bookticker" => DataType::Book,
                    other => return Err(anyhow!("Unknown --data: {}", other)),
                });
                i += 2;
            }
            "-h" | "--help" => { print_usage(); std::process::exit(0); }
            other => return Err(anyhow!("Unknown arg: {}", other)),
        }
    }
    Ok(args)
}

fn print_usage() {
    println!("Usage:");
    println!("  backtest <book_dir> --bot <name> --data book");
    println!("  backtest <trades_path> --bot <name> --data spread --bookticker <book_path>");
    println!("  backtest <trades_dir> --bot <name> --data trades");
    println!("  backtest <klines_dir> --bot <name> --data klines");
}

fn main() -> Result<()> {
    dotenvy::dotenv().ok();
    let args = parse_args()?;

    let config = AppConfig::load_from(&args.config_path)?;
    let global = config.global.clone();

    let bot = match args.bot_name.as_ref() {
        Some(name) => config.bots.iter().find(|b| &b.name == name)
            .ok_or_else(|| {
                let avail: Vec<&str> = config.bots.iter().map(|b| b.name.as_str()).collect();
                anyhow!("Bot '{}' not found. Available: {:?}", name, avail)
            })?.clone(),
        None => config.bots.first().ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("No [[bots]]"))?.clone(),
    };

    let symbol = args.symbol_override.clone()
        .or_else(|| bot.symbol.clone())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| global.symbol.clone());

    let is_futures = bot.market_type == "futures";
    let maker_fee = if is_futures { MAKER_FEE_FUTURES } else { MAKER_FEE_SPOT };
    let taker_fee = if is_futures { TAKER_FEE_FUTURES } else { TAKER_FEE_SPOT };

    let config_fee = bot.fee_rate
        .map(|d| d.to_f64().unwrap_or(taker_fee))
        .unwrap_or(taker_fee);
    if config_fee < taker_fee {
        println!("вљ пёЏ  Р’РќРРњРђРќРР•: config fee_rate = {:.4}% вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ maker fee, РЅРµ taker!", config_fee * 100.0);
        println!("   Р‘СЌРєС‚РµСЃС‚ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚ РїСЂР°РІРёР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ СЃС‚Р°РІРєРё: maker={:.4}%, taker={:.4}%", maker_fee * 100.0, taker_fee * 100.0);
    }

    let data_type = args.data_type.unwrap_or(DataType::Trades);

    println!("в•”в•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•—");
    println!("в•‘   BACKTEST                                                в•‘");
    println!("в• в•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•Ј");
    println!("в•‘   Bot:         {:42} в•‘", bot.name);
    println!("в•‘   Symbol:      {:42} в•‘", symbol);
    println!("в•‘   Strategy:    {:42} в•‘", bot.strategy.name);
    println!("в•‘   Market:      {:42} в•‘", bot.market_type);
    println!("в•‘   Data type:   {:42} в•‘", match data_type {
        DataType::Trades => "trades",
        DataType::Klines => "klines",
        DataType::Spread => "spread (bookTicker + aggTrades)",
        DataType::Book => "bookTicker only (fill simulated)",
    });
    println!("в•‘   Maker fee:  {:>10.4}%                                  в•‘", maker_fee * 100.0);
    println!("в•‘   Taker fee:  {:>10.4}%                                  в•‘", taker_fee * 100.0);
    println!("в•љв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ќ");

    if !bot.strategy.params.is_empty() {
        println!("вљ™пёЏ  Params:");
        let mut keys: Vec<&String> = bot.strategy.params.keys().collect();
        keys.sort();
        for k in keys {
            if let Some(v) = bot.strategy.params.get(k) {
                println!("     {} = {}", k, v);
            }
        }
    }

    let tick_size = if symbol.contains("PEPE") || symbol.contains("DOGS") {
        0.00000001
    } else if symbol.contains("DOGE") {
        0.00001
    } else {
        0.00001
    };
    println!("   Tick size: {}", tick_size);

    let ob_analyzer = OrderBookAnalyzer::new(
        global.orderbook_density_threshold.to_f64().unwrap_or(5.0),
    );
    let ob_state = Arc::new(RwLock::new(OrderBookState::default()));
    let mut strategy = strategies::create_strategy(&bot, &symbol, ob_state, ob_analyzer)?;

    let max_duration_ms = (bot.max_position_duration_sec as i64) * 1000;
    let order_timeout_ms = (bot.order_timeout_sec as i64) * 1000;

    let mut all_trades: Vec<ClosedTrade> = Vec::new();
    let mut total_msgs = 0u64;
    let mut total_signals = 0u64;
    let mut first_ts = 0i64;
    let mut last_ts = 0i64;

    match data_type {
        DataType::Book => {
            let book_files = collect_csv_files(&args.primary_path)?;
            if book_files.is_empty() {
                return Err(anyhow!("No bookTicker CSV files found in {}", args.primary_path));
            }
            println!("\nрџ“љ Found {} bookTicker files", book_files.len());

            for (idx, book_file) in book_files.iter().enumerate() {
                let day_key = extract_date_suffix(book_file)
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| {
                        book_file.file_stem()
                            .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
                            .unwrap_or("unknown")
                            .to_string()
                    });
                println!("\n[{}/{}] {}", idx + 1, book_files.len(), book_file.display());

                let (trades, msgs, sigs, f_ts, l_ts) = run_book_only(
                    book_file, &symbol, maker_fee, taker_fee, tick_size,
                    max_duration_ms, order_timeout_ms, &mut strategy, &day_key,
                )?;

                total_msgs += msgs;
                total_signals += sigs;
                if first_ts == 0 { first_ts = f_ts; }
                last_ts = l_ts;
                all_trades.extend(trades);
            }
        }
        DataType::Spread => {
            let book_path = args.bookticker_path.as_ref()
                .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("--bookticker required for --data spread"))?;

            let trades_files = collect_csv_files(&args.primary_path)?;
            let book_files = collect_csv_files(book_path)?;

            let pairs = match_files_by_date(&trades_files, &book_files)?;
            if pairs.is_empty() {
                return Err(anyhow!("No matching trades/bookticker pairs found"));
            }

            for (trades_file, book_file) in &pairs {
                let day_key = extract_date_suffix(trades_file)
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| {
                        trades_file.file_stem()
                            .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
                            .unwrap_or("unknown")
                            .to_string()
                    });
                println!("\nв–¶ {} + {}", trades_file.display(), book_file.display());

                let (trades, msgs, sigs, f_ts, l_ts) = run_merged(
                    trades_file, book_file, &symbol, maker_fee, taker_fee, tick_size,
                    max_duration_ms, order_timeout_ms, &mut strategy, &day_key,
                )?;

                total_msgs += msgs;
                total_signals += sigs;
                if first_ts == 0 { first_ts = f_ts; }
                last_ts = l_ts;
                all_trades.extend(trades);
            }
        }
        DataType::Trades | DataType::Klines => {
            let files = collect_csv_files(&args.primary_path)?;
            let slippage = SlippageCalculator;

            for (idx, file) in files.iter().enumerate() {
                let day_key = extract_date_suffix(file)
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| {
                        file.file_stem()
                            .and_then(|s| s.to_str())
                            .unwrap_or("unknown")
                            .to_string()
                    });
                println!("\n[{}/{}] {}", idx + 1, files.len(), file.display());

                let (trades, msgs, sigs, f_ts, l_ts) = run_single(
                    file, &symbol, maker_fee, taker_fee, tick_size, max_duration_ms,
                    &mut strategy, &slippage, &day_key, data_type,
                )?;

                total_msgs += msgs;
                total_signals += sigs;
                if first_ts == 0 { first_ts = f_ts; }
                last_ts = l_ts;
                all_trades.extend(trades);
            }
        }
    }

    if let Some(diag) = strategy.diagnostics() {
        println!("\nрџ”Ќ DIAGNOSTICS:");
        println!("   {}", diag);
    }

    let stats = compute_stats(&all_trades);
    let days = if first_ts > 0 {
        ((last_ts - first_ts) as f64 / 86_400_000.0).max(1.0)
    } else {
        1.0
    };

    print_report(&stats, &all_trades, days, total_msgs, total_signals);
    print_breakdown(&all_trades);

    Ok(())
}

fn collect_csv_files(path: &str) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>> {
    let p = Path::new(path);
    if p.is_file() {
        return Ok(vec![p.to_path_buf()]);
    }
    if p.is_dir() {
        let mut files: Vec<_> = fs::read_dir(p)?
            .filter_map(|e| e.ok())
            .map(|e| e.path())
            .filter(|p| p.extension().and_then(|s| s.to_str()) == Some("csv"))
            .collect();
        files.sort();
        return Ok(files);
    }
    Err(anyhow!("Path not found: {}", path))
}

// вњ… РРЎРџР РђР’Р›Р•РќРћ: Р±С‹Р»Рѕ < 5, РґРѕР»Р¶РЅРѕ Р±С‹С‚СЊ < 4
fn extract_date_suffix(path: &Path) -> Option<String> {
    let stem = path.file_stem()?.to_str()?;
    let parts: Vec<&str> = stem.split('-').collect();
    if parts.len() < 4 { return None; } // вњ… Р‘Р«Р›Рћ < 5
    let n = parts.len();
    Some(format!("{}-{}-{}", parts[n-3], parts[n-2], parts[n-1]))
}

fn match_files_by_date(trades: &[PathBuf], books: &[PathBuf]) -> Result<Vec<(PathBuf, PathBuf)>> {
    let mut pairs = Vec::new();
    for t in trades {
        let t_date = match extract_date_suffix(t) { Some(d) => d, None => continue };
        for b in books {
            if let Some(b_date) = extract_date_suffix(b) {
                if b_date == t_date {
                    pairs.push((t.clone(), b.clone()));
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(pairs)
}
// ============================================================
// SINGLE STREAM (trades | klines)
// ============================================================
fn run_single(
    file: &Path,
    symbol: &str,
    maker_fee: f64,
    taker_fee: f64,
    tick_size: f64,
    max_duration_ms: i64,
    strategy: &mut Box<dyn Strategy>,
    slippage_calc: &SlippageCalculator,
    day_key: &str,
    data_type: DataType,
) -> Result<(Vec<ClosedTrade>, u64, u64, i64, i64)> {
    let reader = BufReader::new(File::open(file)?);
    let mut position: Option<OpenPosition> = None;
    let mut trades: Vec<ClosedTrade> = Vec::new();
    let mut msgs = 0u64;
    let mut sigs = 0u64;
    let mut first_ts = 0i64;
    let mut last_ts = 0i64;
    let mut last_price = 0.0f64;

    for (line_no, line) in reader.lines().enumerate() {
        let line = match line { Ok(l) => l, Err(_) => continue };
        if line_no == 0
            && !line.chars().next().map(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()).unwrap_or(false)
        { continue; }

        let (msg, price_now, ts_now) = match data_type {
            DataType::Trades => {
                let t = match parse_agg_trade(&line, symbol) { Some(t) => t, None => continue };
                (WebSocketMessage::Trade(t.clone()), t.price, t.timestamp)
            }
            DataType::Klines => {
                let k = match parse_kline(&line, symbol) { Some(k) => k, None => continue };
                (WebSocketMessage::Kline(k.clone()), k.close, k.timestamp)
            }
            _ => return Err(anyhow!("run_single called with Spread/Book")),
        };

        msgs += 1;
        if first_ts == 0 { first_ts = ts_now; }
        last_ts = ts_now;
        last_price = price_now;

        if let Some(pos) = &position {
            if let Some(reason) = check_exit(pos, price_now, ts_now, max_duration_ms, tick_size) {
                let exit = calc_exit_price(pos, price_now, reason, tick_size);
                let trade = close_position(pos, exit, reason, ts_now, maker_fee, taker_fee, day_key);
                // вњ… РРЎРџР РђР’Р›Р•РќРћ: РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°РµРј notify_position_closed
                strategy.notify_position_closed(trade.pnl_net);
                trades.push(trade);
                position = None;
            }
        }

        if position.is_none() {
            if let Some(signal) = strategy.process(&msg)? {
                sigs += 1;

                // вњ… РќРћР’РћР•: РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° РјРёРЅРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ РЅРѕС‚Р°С†РёРѕРЅР°Р»Р°
                let notional = signal.quantity * signal.price;
                if notional < MIN_NOTIONAL {
                    continue;
                }

                let slip = slippage_calc.calculate_slippage(symbol, signal.price, signal.quantity);
                let fill_price = match signal.side.as_str() {
                    "BUY" => signal.price * (1.0 + slip),
                    "SELL" => signal.price * (1.0 - slip),
                    _ => signal.price,
                };
                position = Some(OpenPosition {
                    side: signal.side.clone(),
                    entry: fill_price,
                    qty: signal.quantity,
                    sl: signal.stop_loss,
                    tp: signal.take_profit,
                    opened_at_ms: ts_now,
                });
                // вњ… РРЎРџР РђР’Р›Р•РќРћ: РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°РµРј notify_position_open
                strategy.notify_position_open();
            }
        }
    }

    if let Some(pos) = position.take() {
        if last_price > 0.0 {
            let trade = close_position(&pos, last_price, "EndOfData", last_ts, maker_fee, taker_fee, day_key);
            strategy.notify_position_closed(trade.pnl_net);
            trades.push(trade);
        }
    }

    Ok((trades, msgs, sigs, first_ts, last_ts))
}

// ============================================================
// MERGED STREAM (bookTicker + aggTrades)
// ============================================================
fn run_merged(
    trades_file: &Path,
    book_file: &Path,
    symbol: &str,
    maker_fee: f64,
    taker_fee: f64,
    tick_size: f64,
    max_duration_ms: i64,
    order_timeout_ms: i64,
    strategy: &mut Box<dyn Strategy>,
    day_key: &str,
) -> Result<(Vec<ClosedTrade>, u64, u64, i64, i64)> {
    let trades_reader: Lines<BufReader<File>> =
        BufReader::new(File::open(trades_file)?).lines();
    let book_reader: Lines<BufReader<File>> =
        BufReader::new(File::open(book_file)?).lines();

    let mut trades_iter = trades_reader.peekable();
    let mut book_iter = book_reader.peekable();

    skip_header(&mut trades_iter);
    skip_header(&mut book_iter);

    let mut position: Option<OpenPosition> = None;
    let mut pending: Option<PendingLimit> = None;
    let mut trades: Vec<ClosedTrade> = Vec::new();
    let mut msgs = 0u64;
    let mut sigs = 0u64;
    let mut first_ts = 0i64;
    let mut last_ts = 0i64;

    loop {
        let t_ts = peek_trade_ts(&mut trades_iter);
        let b_ts = peek_book_ts(&mut book_iter);

        let process_trade = match (t_ts, b_ts) {
            (Some(t), Some(b)) => t <= b,
            (Some(_), None) => true,
            (None, Some(_)) => false,
            (None, None) => break,
        };

        let now_ts;
        let msg: WebSocketMessage;

        if process_trade {
            let line = match trades_iter.next() {
                Some(Ok(l)) => l,
                Some(Err(_)) => continue,
                None => break,
            };
            let t = match parse_agg_trade(&line, symbol) {
                Some(t) => t,
                None => continue,
            };
            now_ts = t.timestamp;
            msg = WebSocketMessage::Trade(t);
        } else {
            let line = match book_iter.next() {
                Some(Ok(l)) => l,
                Some(Err(_)) => continue,
                None => break,
            };
            let bt = match parse_book_ticker(&line, symbol) {
                Some(bt) => bt,
                None => continue,
            };
            now_ts = bt.timestamp;
            msg = WebSocketMessage::BookTicker(bt);
        }

        msgs += 1;
        if first_ts == 0 { first_ts = now_ts; }
        last_ts = now_ts;

        // 1. Fill detection
        if let (Some(order), WebSocketMessage::Trade(t)) = (&pending, &msg) {
            let filled = match order.side.as_str() {
                "BUY" => t.is_buyer_maker && t.price <= order.price,
                "SELL" => !t.is_buyer_maker && t.price >= order.price,
                _ => false,
            };
            if filled {
                position = Some(OpenPosition {
                    side: order.side.clone(),
                    entry: order.price,
                    qty: order.qty,
                    sl: Some(order.sl),
                    tp: Some(order.tp),
                    opened_at_ms: t.timestamp,
                });
                pending = None;
                // вњ… РРЎРџР РђР’Р›Р•РќРћ: РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°РµРј notify_position_open
                strategy.notify_position_open();
            }
        }

        // 2. Timeout pending
        if let Some(order) = &pending {
            if now_ts - order.placed_at_ms > order_timeout_ms {
                pending = None;
            }
        }

        // 3. Exit check
        if let (Some(pos), WebSocketMessage::Trade(t)) = (&position, &msg) {
            if let Some(reason) = check_exit(pos, t.price, t.timestamp, max_duration_ms, tick_size) {
                let exit = calc_exit_price(pos, t.price, reason, tick_size);
                let trade = close_position(pos, exit, reason, t.timestamp, maker_fee, taker_fee, day_key);
                // вњ… РРЎРџР РђР’Р›Р•РќРћ: РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°РµРј notify_position_closed
                strategy.notify_position_closed(trade.pnl_net);
                trades.push(trade);
                position = None;
            }
        }

        // 4. Skip new signals if busy
        if pending.is_some() || position.is_some() {
            continue;
        }

        // 5. Try signal
        if let Some(signal) = strategy.process(&msg)? {
            sigs += 1;

            // вњ… РќРћР’РћР•: РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° РјРёРЅРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ РЅРѕС‚Р°С†РёРѕРЅР°Р»Р°
            let notional = signal.quantity * signal.price;
            if notional < MIN_NOTIONAL {
                continue;
            }

            let sl = signal.stop_loss.unwrap_or(signal.price * 0.998);
            let tp = signal.take_profit.unwrap_or(signal.price * 1.002);
            pending = Some(PendingLimit {
                side: signal.side.clone(),
                price: signal.price,
                qty: signal.quantity,
                sl,
                tp,
                placed_at_ms: now_ts,
            });
        }
    }

    if let Some(pos) = position.take() {
        if last_ts > 0 {
            let trade = close_position(&pos, pos.entry, "EndOfData", last_ts, maker_fee, taker_fee, day_key);
            strategy.notify_position_closed(trade.pnl_net);
            trades.push(trade);
        }
    }

    Ok((trades, msgs, sigs, first_ts, last_ts))
}

// ============================================================
// вњ… РќРћР’РђРЇ Р¤РЈРќРљР¦РРЇ: BOOK-ONLY STREAM (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ bookTicker)
// РЎРёРјСѓР»РёСЂСѓРµС‚ fill РїРѕ bookTicker РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рј, Р±РµР· aggTrades.
// ============================================================
fn run_book_only(
    book_file: &Path,
    symbol: &str,
    maker_fee: f64,
    taker_fee: f64,
    tick_size: f64,
    max_duration_ms: i64,
    _order_timeout_ms: i64,
    strategy: &mut Box<dyn Strategy>,
    day_key: &str,
) -> Result<(Vec<ClosedTrade>, u64, u64, i64, i64)> {
    let reader = BufReader::new(File::open(book_file)?);
    let mut position: Option<OpenPosition> = None;
    let mut trades: Vec<ClosedTrade> = Vec::new();
    let mut msgs = 0u64;
    let mut sigs = 0u64;
    let mut first_ts = 0i64;
    let mut last_ts = 0i64;
    let mut last_price = 0.0f64;

    for (line_no, line) in reader.lines().enumerate() {
        let line = match line { Ok(l) => l, Err(_) => continue };
        if line_no == 0
            && !line.chars().next().map(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()).unwrap_or(false)
        { continue; }

        let bt = match parse_book_ticker(&line, symbol) {
            Some(bt) => bt,
            None => continue,
        };

        if bt.best_bid <= 0.0 || bt.best_ask <= 0.0 { continue; }

        let mid = (bt.best_bid + bt.best_ask) / 2.0;
        let price_now = mid;

        msgs += 1;
        if first_ts == 0 { first_ts = bt.timestamp; }
        last_ts = bt.timestamp;
        last_price = price_now;

        // 1. Exit check (РїРѕ mid price)
        if let Some(pos) = &position {
            if let Some(reason) = check_exit(pos, price_now, bt.timestamp, max_duration_ms, tick_size) {
                let exit = calc_exit_price(pos, price_now, reason, tick_size);
                let trade = close_position(pos, exit, reason, bt.timestamp, maker_fee, taker_fee, day_key);
                strategy.notify_position_closed(trade.pnl_net);
                trades.push(trade);
                position = None;
            }
        }

        // 2. Try signal (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё РЅРµС‚ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё)
        if position.is_none() {
            let msg = WebSocketMessage::BookTicker(bt.clone());
            if let Some(signal) = strategy.process(&msg)? {
                sigs += 1;

                // вњ… РќРћР’РћР•: РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° РјРёРЅРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ РЅРѕС‚Р°С†РёРѕРЅР°Р»Р°
                let notional = signal.quantity * signal.price;
                if notional < MIN_NOTIONAL {
                    continue;
                }

                // вњ… Fill simulation: maker fill = instant at signal price
                position = Some(OpenPosition {
                    side: signal.side.clone(),
                    entry: signal.price,
                    qty: signal.quantity,
                    sl: signal.stop_loss,
                    tp: signal.take_profit,
                    opened_at_ms: bt.timestamp,
                });
                strategy.notify_position_open();
            }
        }
    }

    if let Some(pos) = position.take() {
        if last_price > 0.0 {
            let trade = close_position(&pos, last_price, "EndOfData", last_ts, maker_fee, taker_fee, day_key);
            strategy.notify_position_closed(trade.pnl_net);
            trades.push(trade);
        }
    }

    Ok((trades, msgs, sigs, first_ts, last_ts))
}
fn skip_header<I: Iterator<Item = std::io::Result<String>>>(iter: &mut Peekable<I>) {
    if let Some(Ok(line)) = iter.peek() {
        if !line.chars().next().map(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()).unwrap_or(false) {
            iter.next();
        }
    }
}

fn peek_trade_ts(iter: &mut Peekable<Lines<BufReader<File>>>) -> Option<i64> {
    let line = iter.peek()?.as_ref().ok()?;
    let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split(',').collect();
    if parts.len() < 7 { return None; }
    let ts_raw: i64 = parts[5].trim().parse().ok()?;
    Some(normalize_ts(ts_raw))
}

fn peek_book_ts(iter: &mut Peekable<Lines<BufReader<File>>>) -> Option<i64> {
    let line = iter.peek()?.as_ref().ok()?;
    let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split(',').collect();
    if parts.len() < 7 { return None; }
    let ts_raw: i64 = parts[6].trim().parse().ok()?;
    Some(normalize_ts(ts_raw))
}

fn normalize_ts(ts_raw: i64) -> i64 {
    if ts_raw > 100_000_000_000_000 { ts_raw / 1000 } else { ts_raw }
}

// ============================================================
// Parsers
// ============================================================
fn parse_agg_trade(line: &str, symbol: &str) -> Option<TradeMessage> {
    let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split(',').collect();
    if parts.len() < 7 { return None; }
    let price: f64 = parts[1].trim().parse().ok()?;
    let qty: f64 = parts[2].trim().parse().ok()?;
    let ts_raw: i64 = parts[5].trim().parse().ok()?;
    let ts = normalize_ts(ts_raw);
    let is_buyer_maker = parts[6].trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case("true");
    Some(TradeMessage {
        symbol: symbol.to_string(),
        price, volume: qty,
        timestamp: ts, event_time: ts,
        is_buyer_maker,
    })
}

fn parse_kline(line: &str, symbol: &str) -> Option<KlineMessage> {
    let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split(',').collect();
    if parts.len() < 11 { return None; }
    let open: f64 = parts[1].trim().parse().ok()?;
    let high: f64 = parts[2].trim().parse().ok()?;
    let low: f64 = parts[3].trim().parse().ok()?;
    let close: f64 = parts[4].trim().parse().ok()?;
    let volume: f64 = parts[5].trim().parse().ok()?;
    let close_time_raw: i64 = parts[6].trim().parse().ok()?;
    let taker_buy_volume: f64 = parts[9].trim().parse().unwrap_or(0.0);
    Some(KlineMessage {
        symbol: symbol.to_string(),
        open, high, low, close, volume,
        taker_buy_volume,
        timestamp: normalize_ts(close_time_raw),
        is_closed: true,
        is_backfill: false, // вњ… Р”РћР‘РђР’Р›Р•РќРћ (Р±С‹Р»Рѕ РїСЂРѕРїСѓС‰РµРЅРѕ)
    })
}

fn parse_book_ticker(line: &str, symbol: &str) -> Option<BookTickerMessage> {
    let parts: Vec<&str> = line.split(',').collect();
    if parts.len() < 7 { return None; }
    let best_bid: f64 = parts[1].trim().parse().ok()?;
    let bid_qty: f64 = parts[2].trim().parse().unwrap_or(0.0);
    let best_ask: f64 = parts[3].trim().parse().ok()?;
    let ask_qty: f64 = parts[4].trim().parse().unwrap_or(0.0);
    let timestamp = normalize_ts(parts[6].trim().parse().ok()?);
    Some(BookTickerMessage {
        symbol: symbol.to_string(), best_bid, best_ask, bid_qty, ask_qty, timestamp,
    })
}

// ============================================================
// Exit logic вЂ” вњ… РРЎРџР РђР’Р›Р•РќРћ: РґРѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅ tick_size РґР»СЏ СЃР»РёРїРїРµРґР¶Р°
// ============================================================
fn check_exit(
    pos: &OpenPosition,
    price: f64,
    now_ms: i64,
    max_ms: i64,
    _tick_size: f64,
) -> Option<&'static str> {
    if now_ms - pos.opened_at_ms >= max_ms { return Some("Timeout"); }
    match pos.side.as_str() {
        "BUY" => {
            if let Some(sl) = pos.sl { if price <= sl { return Some("StopLoss"); } }
            if let Some(tp) = pos.tp { if price >= tp { return Some("TakeProfit"); } }
        }
        "SELL" => {
            if let Some(sl) = pos.sl { if price >= sl { return Some("StopLoss"); } }
            if let Some(tp) = pos.tp { if price <= tp { return Some("TakeProfit"); } }
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    None
}

// вњ… РќРћР’РђРЇ Р¤РЈРќРљР¦РРЇ: СЂР°СЃС‡С‘С‚ С†РµРЅС‹ РІС‹С…РѕРґР° СЃ СѓС‡С‘С‚РѕРј СЃР»РёРїРїРµРґР¶Р° СЃС‚РѕРїР°
fn calc_exit_price(
    pos: &OpenPosition,
    market_price: f64,
    reason: &'static str,
    tick_size: f64,
) -> f64 {
    let slippage = SL_SLIPPAGE_TICKS * tick_size;
    match reason {
        "StopLoss" => {
            let sl = pos.sl.unwrap_or(market_price);
            // РЎР»РёРїРїРµРґР¶: СЃС‚РѕРї РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅСЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РҐРЈР–Р• С†РµРЅС‹ С‚СЂРёРіРіРµСЂР°
            match pos.side.as_str() {
                "BUY" => sl - slippage,   // Long SL: РїСЂРѕРґР°С‘Рј РЅРёР¶Рµ СЃС‚РѕРїР°
                "SELL" => sl + slippage,   // Short SL: РїРѕРєСѓРїР°РµРј РІС‹С€Рµ СЃС‚РѕРїР°
                _ => sl,
            }
        }
        "TakeProfit" => pos.tp.unwrap_or(market_price),
        _ => market_price, // Timeout, EndOfData вЂ” РїРѕ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµР№ С†РµРЅРµ
    }
}

// ============================================================
// вњ… РРЎРџР РђР’Р›Р•РќРћ: СЂР°Р·РґРµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ maker/taker РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёРё
// Р’С…РѕРґ РІСЃРµРіРґР° maker (GTX), РІС‹С…РѕРґ maker (TP) РёР»Рё taker (SL/Timeout)
// ============================================================
fn close_position(
    pos: &OpenPosition,
    exit: f64,
    reason: &'static str,
    now_ms: i64,
    maker_fee: f64,
    taker_fee: f64,
    day_key: &str,
) -> ClosedTrade {
    let gross = match pos.side.as_str() {
        "BUY" => (exit - pos.entry) * pos.qty,
        "SELL" => (pos.entry - exit) * pos.qty,
        _ => 0.0,
    };

    // вњ… РРЎРџР РђР’Р›Р•РќРћ: СЂР°Р·РґРµР»СЊРЅС‹Рµ РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёРё
    // Р’С…РѕРґ: РІСЃРµРіРґР° maker (GTX)
    let entry_fee = pos.entry * pos.qty * maker_fee;

    // Р’С‹С…РѕРґ: maker РїСЂРё TP, taker РїСЂРё SL/Timeout
    let exit_fee = match reason {
        "TakeProfit" | "EndOfData" => exit * pos.qty * maker_fee,  // maker exit
        _ => exit * pos.qty * taker_fee,  // taker exit (SL, Timeout)
    };

    let fees = entry_fee + exit_fee;
    let net = gross - fees;

    ClosedTrade {
        side: pos.side.clone(),
        entry: pos.entry, exit, qty: pos.qty,
        pnl_gross: gross, fees, pnl_net: net,
        reason,
        duration_ms: now_ms - pos.opened_at_ms,
        day_key: day_key.to_string(),
    }
}

fn compute_stats(trades: &[ClosedTrade]) -> Stats {
    let n = trades.len();
    if n == 0 { return Stats::default(); }
    let wins: Vec<_> = trades.iter().filter(|t| t.pnl_net > 0.0).collect();
    let losses: Vec<_> = trades.iter().filter(|t| t.pnl_net < 0.0).collect();
    let gross_profit: f64 = wins.iter().map(|t| t.pnl_net).sum();
    let gross_loss: f64 = losses.iter().map(|t| t.pnl_net.abs()).sum();
    let win_rate = wins.len() as f64 / n as f64;
    let avg_win = if !wins.is_empty() { gross_profit / wins.len() as f64 } else { 0.0 };
    let avg_loss = if !losses.is_empty() { gross_loss / losses.len() as f64 } else { 0.0 };
    let ev = win_rate * avg_win - (1.0 - win_rate) * avg_loss;
    let pf = if gross_loss > 1e-9 { gross_profit / gross_loss } else if gross_profit > 0.0 { f64::INFINITY } else { 0.0 };
    let net_pnl: f64 = trades.iter().map(|t| t.pnl_net).sum();

    let mut cum = 0.0; let mut peak = 0.0; let mut max_dd = 0.0;
    for t in trades {
        cum += t.pnl_net;
        if cum > peak { peak = cum; }
        let dd = peak - cum;
        if dd > max_dd { max_dd = dd; }
    }

    Stats { n, wins: wins.len(), losses: losses.len(),
        win_rate, avg_win, avg_loss, ev, profit_factor: pf,
        net_pnl, gross_profit, gross_loss, max_dd }
}

fn print_report(stats: &Stats, trades: &[ClosedTrade], days: f64, msgs: u64, sigs: u64) {
    let mut reasons: std::collections::HashMap<&str, (usize, f64)> = std::collections::HashMap::new();
    for t in trades {
        let e = reasons.entry(t.reason).or_insert((0, 0.0));
        e.0 += 1; e.1 += t.pnl_net;
    }
    let longs: Vec<&ClosedTrade> = trades.iter().filter(|t| t.side == "BUY").collect();
    let shorts: Vec<&ClosedTrade> = trades.iter().filter(|t| t.side == "SELL").collect();
    let (lw, lp) = count_pnl(&longs);
    let (sw, sp) = count_pnl(&shorts);
    let tpd = stats.n as f64 / days.max(1.0);
    let avg_dur = if stats.n > 0 {
        trades.iter().map(|t| t.duration_ms).sum::<i64>() as f64 / stats.n as f64 / 1000.0
    } else { 0.0 };

    println!();
    println!("в•”в•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•—");
    println!("в•‘                    BACKTEST REPORT                        в•‘");
    println!("в• в•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•Ј");
    println!("в•‘   Messages:          {:>15}                       в•‘", msgs);
    println!("в•‘   Signals:           {:>15}                       в•‘", sigs);
    println!("в•‘   Trades closed:     {:>15}                       в•‘", stats.n);
    println!("в•‘   Days:              {:>15.2}                       в•‘", days);
    println!("в•‘   Trades per day:    {:>15.2}                       в•‘", tpd);
    println!("в• в•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•Ј");
    println!("в•‘   Win Rate:          {:>15.2}%                      в•‘", stats.win_rate * 100.0);
    println!("в•‘   Wins / Losses:     {:>7} / {:>7}                  в•‘", stats.wins, stats.losses);
    println!("в•‘   Avg Win:           {:>15.6} USDT                 в•‘", stats.avg_win);
    println!("в•‘   Avg Loss:          {:>15.6} USDT                 в•‘", stats.avg_loss);
    println!("в•‘   EV per trade:      {:>15.6} USDT                 в•‘", stats.ev);
    println!("в•‘   Profit Factor:     {:>15.2}                       в•‘", stats.profit_factor);
    println!("в•‘   Net PnL:           {:>15.4} USDT                 в•‘", stats.net_pnl);
    println!("в•‘   Max Drawdown:      {:>15.4} USDT                 в•‘", stats.max_dd);
    println!("в•‘   Avg Duration:      {:>15.2} sec                  в•‘", avg_dur);
    println!("в• в•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•Ј");
    println!("в•‘   LONG:  {:>5} trades | {:>4} wins | PnL {:>+10.4}    в•‘", longs.len(), lw, lp);
    println!("в•‘   SHORT: {:>5} trades | {:>4} wins | PnL {:>+10.4}    в•‘", shorts.len(), sw, sp);
    println!("в• в•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•Ј");
    println!("в•‘   Exit reasons:                                           в•‘");
    let mut sorted: Vec<_> = reasons.iter().collect();
    sorted.sort_by(|a, b| b.1.0.cmp(&a.1.0));
    for (reason, (count, pnl)) in sorted {
        println!("в•‘     {:<14} {:>5} trades | PnL {:>+10.4} USDT  в•‘", reason, count, pnl);
    }
    println!("в•љв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ќ");

    println!("\nрџ“‹ VERDICT:");
    verdict("Win Rate", stats.win_rate * 100.0, 55.0, "%", true);
    verdict("Profit Factor", stats.profit_factor, 1.3, "", true);
    verdict("EV per trade", stats.ev, 0.000001, " USDT", true);
    verdict("Trades per day", tpd, 20.0, "", true);
}

fn print_breakdown(trades: &[ClosedTrade]) {
    if trades.is_empty() { return; }
    let mut by_day: std::collections::BTreeMap<String, Vec<&ClosedTrade>> = Default::default();
    for t in trades { by_day.entry(t.day_key.clone()).or_default().push(t); }

    println!("\nв•”в•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•—");
    println!("в•‘                    DAILY BREAKDOWN                        в•‘");
    println!("в• в•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•Ј");
    println!("в•‘   {:<28} {:>5} {:>7} {:>10}  в•‘", "Day", "N", "WR%", "PnL");
    println!("в• в•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•Ј");
    for (day, day_trades) in &by_day {
        let n = day_trades.len();
        let wins = day_trades.iter().filter(|t| t.pnl_net > 0.0).count();
        let wr = if n > 0 { wins as f64 / n as f64 * 100.0 } else { 0.0 };
        let pnl: f64 = day_trades.iter().map(|t| t.pnl_net).sum();
        let short = if day.len() > 28 { &day[..28] } else { day };
        println!("в•‘   {:<28} {:>5} {:>6.1}% {:>+10.4}  в•‘", short, n, wr, pnl);
    }
    println!("в•љв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ќ");
}

fn count_pnl(trades: &[&ClosedTrade]) -> (usize, f64) {
    let wins = trades.iter().filter(|t| t.pnl_net > 0.0).count();
    let pnl = trades.iter().map(|t| t.pnl_net).sum();
    (wins, pnl)
}

fn verdict(name: &str, value: f64, threshold: f64, suffix: &str, higher_is_better: bool) {
    let pass = if higher_is_better { value >= threshold } else { value <= threshold };
    let mark = if pass { "вњ…" } else { "вќЊ" };
    let op = if higher_is_better { "в‰Ґ" } else { "в‰¤" };
    println!("   {} {:<18} {:>15.6}{}  (target {} {:.6}{})", mark, name, value, suffix, op, threshold, suffix);
}
=== C:\Users\BASE_PC\scalping_agentsGLM\src\bin\download_bybit_klines.rs ===
//! download_bybit_klines вЂ” СѓС‚РёР»РёС‚Р° СЃРєР°С‡РёРІР°РЅРёСЏ klines СЃ Bybit V5 PROD.

use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::fs::File;
use std::io::{BufWriter, Write};
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::time::Duration;
use chrono::{Utc, TimeZone};

const BYBIT_KLINES: &str = "https://api.bybit.com/v5/market/kline";
const USER_AGENT: &str = "Mozilla/5.0 (scalping_agents/klines-downloader) reqwest/0.12";

fn interval_to_bybit(interval: &str) -> Option<&'static str> {
    match interval {
        "1m" => Some("1"), "5m" => Some("5"), "15m" => Some("15"), "30m" => Some("30"),
        "1h" => Some("60"), "2h" => Some("120"), "4h" => Some("240"),
        "1d" => Some("D"), "1w" => Some("W"), "1M" => Some("M"), _ => None,
    }
}

fn interval_seconds(interval: &str) -> Option<i64> {
    match interval {
        "1m" => Some(60), "5m" => Some(300), "15m" => Some(900), "30m" => Some(1800),
        "1h" => Some(3600), "2h" => Some(7200), "4h" => Some(14400),
        "1d" => Some(86400), "1w" => Some(604800), _ => None,
    }
}

fn parse_args() -> Result<(String, String, i64, PathBuf), String> {
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().collect();
    let mut symbol = String::new();
    let mut interval = String::from("1h");
    let mut days: i64 = 365;
    let mut out = PathBuf::from("data/klines");
    let mut i = 1;
    while i < args.len() {
        match args[i].as_str() {
            "--symbol" => { symbol = args.get(i+1).ok_or("missing symbol")?.clone(); i+=2; }
            "--interval" => { interval = args.get(i+1).ok_or("missing interval")?.clone(); i+=2; }
            "--days" => { days = args.get(i+1).ok_or("missing days")?.parse().map_err(|e| format!("{}",e))?; i+=2; }
            "--out" => { out = PathBuf::from(args.get(i+1).ok_or("missing out")?); i+=2; }
            "-h" | "--help" => {
                eprintln!("Usage: download_bybit_klines --symbol XAUUSDT --interval 1h --days 365 --out data/klines");
                std::process::exit(0);
            }
            _ => { eprintln!("Unknown arg: {}", args[i]); i+=1; }
        }
    }
    if symbol.is_empty() { return Err("--symbol required".into()); }
    Ok((symbol, interval, days, out))
}

async fn fetch_klines(
    client: &Client, symbol: &str, interval_bybit: &str,
    start_ms: i64, end_ms: i64, limit: u32,
) -> Result<Vec<Value>, String> {
    let url = format!(
        "{}/v5/market/kline?category=linear&symbol={}&interval={}&start={}&end={}&limit={}",
        BYBIT_KLINES, symbol.to_uppercase(), interval_bybit, start_ms, end_ms, limit
    );

    // рџ†• Р¤РРљРЎ: РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј .text() РґР»СЏ Р»СѓС‡С€РµР№ РґРёР°РіРЅРѕСЃС‚РёРєРё
    let resp = client.get(&url).send().await.map_err(|e| format!("HTTP: {}", e))?;
    let status = resp.status();
    let text = resp.text().await.map_err(|e| format!("TEXT: {}", e))?;

    if !status.is_success() {
        return Err(format!("HTTP {}: {}", status, &text[..text.len().min(500)]));
    }

    let json: Value = serde_json::from_str(&text)
        .map_err(|e| format!("JSON parse failed: {} | body[:200]: {}", e, &text[..text.len().min(200)]))?;

    let ret_code = json["retCode"].as_i64().unwrap_or(-1);
    if ret_code != 0 {
        return Err(format!("Bybit retCode={} msg={}", ret_code, json["retMsg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")));
    }

    Ok(json["result"]["list"].as_array().cloned().unwrap_or_default())
}

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let (symbol, interval, days, out_dir) = parse_args()?;
    let interval_bybit = interval_to_bybit(&interval).ok_or(format!("Bad interval: {}", interval))?;
    let interval_secs = interval_seconds(&interval).ok_or(format!("Bad interval secs: {}", interval))?;

    let end_date = Utc::now();
    let start_date = end_date - chrono::Duration::days(days);
    let end_ms = end_date.timestamp_millis();
    let start_ms = start_date.timestamp_millis();

    println!("=== Bybit klines downloader (Rust) ===");
    println!("Symbol:    {}", symbol);
    println!("Interval:  {} в†’ Bybit '{}' ({}СЃ)", interval, interval_bybit, interval_secs);
    println!("Range:     {} в†’ {}", start_date.format("%Y-%m-%d"), end_date.format("%Y-%m-%d"));
    println!("Days:      {}", days);
    println!("Out:       {}", out_dir.display());
    println!();

    std::fs::create_dir_all(&out_dir)?;
    let out_file = out_dir.join(format!(
        "{}-{}-{}_{}.csv",
        symbol.to_uppercase(), interval,
        start_date.format("%Y%m%d").to_string(),
        end_date.format("%Y%m%d").to_string()
    ));

    // рџ†• Р¤РРљРЎ: СЏРІРЅС‹Р№ User-Agent + Accept header вЂ” Bybit Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂСѓРµС‚ РґРµС„РѕР»С‚РЅС‹Р№ UA
    let mut headers = reqwest::header::HeaderMap::new();
    headers.insert("Accept", reqwest::header::HeaderValue::from_static("application/json"));
    let client = Client::builder()
        .timeout(Duration::from_secs(30))
        .user_agent(USER_AGENT)
        .default_headers(headers)
        .pool_idle_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(20)).tcp_keepalive(std::time::Duration::from_secs(15))/* P19-POOL */.build()?;

    let mut all_rows: Vec<(i64, String, String, String, String, String, i64, String, i64, String, String, i64)> = Vec::new();
    let mut cursor_end_ms = end_ms;
    let mut request_count = 0;

    while cursor_end_ms > start_ms {
        request_count += 1;
        let list = match fetch_klines(&client, &symbol, interval_bybit, start_ms, cursor_end_ms, 1000).await {
            Ok(v) => v,
            Err(e) => {
                println!("  вљ  Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ {}: РѕС€РёР±РєР°: {}", request_count, e);
                break;
            }
        };
        if list.is_empty() {
            println!("  Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ {}: 0 СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ вЂ” РєРѕРЅРµС†", request_count);
            break;
        }

        let mut sorted: Vec<Value> = list.clone();
        sorted.reverse();

        let mut new_count = 0;
        let mut oldest_ms = i64::MAX;
        let mut newest_ms = i64::MIN;
        for row in &sorted {
            let start_str = row[0].as_str().unwrap_or("0");
            let start_ms_val: i64 = start_str.parse().unwrap_or(0);
            if start_ms_val < start_ms || start_ms_val > end_ms { continue; }
            let open_time_ms = start_ms_val;
            let close_time_ms = open_time_ms + interval_secs * 1000 - 1;
            if all_rows.iter().any(|r| r.0 == open_time_ms) { continue; }

            let open = row[1].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
            let high = row[2].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
            let low = row[3].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
            let close = row[4].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
            let volume = row[5].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
            let turnover = row[6].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();

            all_rows.push((open_time_ms, open, high, low, close, volume, close_time_ms, turnover, 0, "0".to_string(), "0".to_string(), 0));
            new_count += 1;
            if open_time_ms < oldest_ms { oldest_ms = open_time_ms; }
            if open_time_ms > newest_ms { newest_ms = open_time_ms; }
        }

        let oldest_dt = Utc.timestamp_millis_opt(oldest_ms).unwrap();
        let newest_dt = Utc.timestamp_millis_opt(newest_ms).unwrap();
        println!("  Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ {}: {} СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ | {} в†’ {} | РёС‚РѕРіРѕ {}",
                 request_count, new_count,
                 oldest_dt.format("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"),
                 newest_dt.format("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"),
                 all_rows.len());

        if new_count == 0 || sorted.len() < 1000 { break; }
        cursor_end_ms = oldest_ms - 1;
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(300)).await;
    }

    if all_rows.is_empty() {
        eprintln!("вќЊ РќРµС‚ СЃРІРµС‡РµР№");
        std::process::exit(1);
    }

    all_rows.sort_by_key(|r| r.0);

    let mut f = BufWriter::new(File::create(&out_file)?);
    writeln!(f, "open_time,open,high,low,close,volume,close_time,quote_volume,count,taker_buy_volume,taker_buy_quote_volume,ignore")?;
    for r in &all_rows {
        writeln!(f, "{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{}",
                 r.0, r.1, r.2, r.3, r.4, r.5, r.6, r.7, r.8, r.9, r.10, r.11)?;
    }
    drop(f);

    println!();
    println!("вњ… РЎРєР°С‡Р°РЅРѕ {} СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ в†’ {}", all_rows.len(), out_file.display());
    let first_dt = Utc.timestamp_millis_opt(all_rows[0].0).unwrap();
    let last_dt = Utc.timestamp_millis_opt(all_rows[all_rows.len()-1].0).unwrap();
    println!("   РџРµСЂРёРѕРґ: {} в†’ {}",
             first_dt.format("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"),
             last_dt.format("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M"));

    let closes: Vec<f64> = all_rows.iter().filter_map(|r| r.4.parse().ok()).collect();
    if !closes.is_empty() {
        let min = closes.iter().cloned().fold(f64::INFINITY, f64::min);
        let max = closes.iter().cloned().fold(f64::NEG_INFINITY, f64::max);
        let avg = closes.iter().sum::<f64>() / closes.len() as f64;
        println!("   Р¦РµРЅР°: min={:.2} max={:.2} avg={:.2}", min, max, avg);
    }

    Ok(())
}
