//! Recorder РґР»СЏ Binance Futures bookTicker С‡РµСЂРµР· WebSocket.
//! РџРёС€РµС‚ РїРѕС‚РѕРє best_bid/best_ask РІ CSV РїРѕ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚Сѓ archives:
//!   update_id,best_bid_price,best_bid_qty,best_ask_price,best_ask_qty,transaction_time,event_time
//!
//! Р—Р°РїСѓСЃРє:
//!   cargo run --release --bin record_bookticker -- DOGEUSDT
//!
//! Р¤Р°Р№Р» СЂР°СЃС‚С‘С‚ РІ data/bookticker_live/DOGEUSDT-YYYY-MM-DD.csv

use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use futures_util::StreamExt;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::fs::{create_dir_all, OpenOptions};
use std::io::Write;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use tokio_tungstenite::connect_async;

const WS_BASE: &str = "wss://fstream.binance.com/ws";
const DATA_DIR: &str = "data/bookticker_live";

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> Result<()> {
    let symbol = std::env::args()
        .nth(1)
        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("Usage: record_bookticker <SYMBOL> (e.g. DOGEUSDT)"))?;
    let symbol_upper = symbol.to_uppercase();
    let symbol_lower = symbol.to_lowercase();

    create_dir_all(DATA_DIR)?;

    tracing_subscriber::fmt()
        .with_target(false)
        .with_env_filter("info")
        .init();

    let url = format!("{}/{}@bookTicker", WS_BASE, symbol_lower);
    tracing::info!("Recorder starting: {}", url);

    let mut current_file: Option<(String, std::fs::File)> = None;
    let mut rows_written: u64 = 0;
    let mut last_log = Instant::now();
    let mut reconnect_count: u32 = 0;

    loop {
        tracing::info!("Connecting to {}", url);
        let connect = connect_async(&url).await;

        let ws = match connect {
            Ok((ws, _)) => {
                tracing::info!("вњ… Connected");
                reconnect_count = 0;
                ws
            }
            Err(e) => {
                reconnect_count += 1;
                let backoff = (2u64.pow(reconnect_count.min(6))).min(60);
                tracing::error!("Connection failed: {}. Retry in {}s", e, backoff);
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(backoff)).await;
                continue;
            }
        };

        let (_, mut read) = ws.split();

        while let Some(msg) = read.next().await {
            let msg = match msg {
                Ok(m) => m,
                Err(e) => {
                    tracing::error!("WS read error: {}", e);
                    break;
                }
            };

            if !msg.is_text() {
                continue;
            }

            let text = match msg.into_text() {
                Ok(t) => t,
                Err(_) => continue,
            };

            let json: Value = match serde_json::from_str(&text) {
                Ok(v) => v,
                Err(_) => continue,
            };

            // РџРѕР»СЏ: e, u (update_id), s (symbol), b, B, a, A, T, E
            let update_id = json.get("u").and_then(|v| v.as_u64()).unwrap_or(0);
            let best_bid = json.get("b").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("0");
            let bid_qty = json.get("B").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("0");
            let best_ask = json.get("a").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("0");
            let ask_qty = json.get("A").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or("0");
            let trans_time = json.get("T").and_then(|v| v.as_i64()).unwrap_or(0);
            let event_time = json.get("E").and_then(|v| v.as_i64()).unwrap_or(trans_time);

            // РћРїСЂРµРґРµР»СЏРµРј РґР°С‚Сѓ РїРѕ event_time (UTC)
            let date = chrono::DateTime::from_timestamp_millis(event_time)
                .map(|dt| dt.format("%Y-%m-%d").to_string())
                .unwrap_or_else(|| "unknown".to_string());

            // Roll С„Р°Р№Р»Р° РїСЂРё СЃРјРµРЅРµ РґР°С‚С‹
            let need_new_file = match &current_file {
                Some((d, _)) => d != &date,
                None => true,
            };

            if need_new_file {
                if let Some((old_date, _)) = current_file.take() {
                    tracing::info!("Rolling file from {} to {}", old_date, date);
                }
                let path = PathBuf::from(DATA_DIR).join(format!("{}-{}.csv", symbol_upper, date));
                let new_file = OpenOptions::new()
                    .create(true)
                    .append(true)
                    .open(&path)?;
                tracing::info!("Writing to {}", path.display());
                current_file = Some((date, new_file));
            }

            let line = format!(
                "{},{},{},{},{},{},{}\n",
                update_id, best_bid, bid_qty, best_ask, ask_qty, trans_time, event_time
            );

             if let Some((_, f)) = current_file.as_mut() {
                f.write_all(line.as_bytes())?;
                f.flush()?;              // вњ… Windows: СЃР±СЂРѕСЃ Р±СѓС„РµСЂР° РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РєР°Р¶РґРѕР№ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё
                rows_written += 1;
            }

            // РџРµСЂРёРѕРґРёС‡РµСЃРєРёР№ Р»РѕРі + flush
            if last_log.elapsed() >= Duration::from_secs(10) {
                if let Some((_, f)) = current_file.as_mut() {
                    f.flush()?;
                }
                tracing::info!(
                    "Recorded {} rows | current file: {}",
                    rows_written,
                    current_file.as_ref().map(|(d, _)| d.as_str()).unwrap_or("вЂ”")
                );
                last_log = Instant::now();
            }
        }

        tracing::warn!("WS disconnected. Reconnecting...");
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(2)).await;
    }
}

//! resample_klines вЂ” СЂРµСЃСЌРјРїР»РёРЅРі СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ РёР· 1h РІ 4h/1d.
//! Р—Р°РїСѓСЃРє: cargo run --release --bin resample_klines -- data/klines/XAUUSDT-1h-...csv --to 4h --out data/klines/XAUUSDT-4h-...csv

use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::fs::File;
use std::io::{BufRead, BufWriter, Write};
use std::path::PathBuf;

fn interval_seconds(interval: &str) -> Option<i64> {
    match interval {
        "1m" => Some(60), "5m" => Some(300), "15m" => Some(900), "30m" => Some(1800),
        "1h" => Some(3600), "2h" => Some(7200), "4h" => Some(14400),
        "1d" => Some(86400), "1w" => Some(604800), _ => None,
    }
}

fn parse_args() -> Result<(PathBuf, String, PathBuf), String> {
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().collect();
    let mut input: Option<PathBuf> = None;
    let mut target: Option<String> = None;
    let mut out: Option<PathBuf> = None;
    let mut i = 1;
    while i < args.len() {
        match args[i].as_str() {
            "--to" => { target = Some(args.get(i+1).ok_or("missing target")?.clone()); i+=2; }
            "--out" => { out = Some(PathBuf::from(args.get(i+1).ok_or("missing out")?)); i+=2; }
            x if !x.starts_with('-') => { input = Some(PathBuf::from(x)); i+=1; }
            _ => { i+=1; }
        }
    }
    let input = input.ok_or("input file required")?;
    let target = target.ok_or("--to required")?;
    let out_path = out.unwrap_or_else(|| {
        let name = input.file_name().unwrap().to_str().unwrap();
        let mut new_name = String::new();
        for src_tf in &["1m","5m","15m","30m","1h","2h","4h","1d","1w"] {
            let token = format!("-{}-", src_tf);
            if name.contains(&token) {
                new_name = name.replace(&token, &format!("-{}-", target));
                break;
            }
        }
        if new_name.is_empty() { new_name = format!("{}-{}.csv", name.trim_end_matches(".csv"), target); }
        input.with_file_name(new_name)
    });
    Ok((input, target, out_path))
}

fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
    let (in_path, target_str, out_path) = parse_args()?;
    let target_secs = interval_seconds(&target_str).ok_or(format!("Bad target: {}", target_str))?;

    println!("Resampling: {} в†’ {} ({})", in_path.display(), out_path.display(), target_str);
    println!("Target interval: {} = {}СЃ", target_str, target_secs);

    let f = File::open(&in_path)?;
    let reader = std::io::BufReader::new(f);
    let mut lines = reader.lines();
    let header = lines.next().ok_or("empty file")??;

    let mut rows: Vec<Vec<String>> = Vec::new();
    for line in lines {
        let line = line?;
        let cols: Vec<String> = line.split(',').map(|s| s.to_string()).collect();
        if cols.len() >= 7 { rows.push(cols); }
    }

    println!("Input: {} candles", rows.len());
    if rows.len() < 2 { return Err("Not enough data".into()); }

    let t0: i64 = rows[0][0].parse().unwrap_or(0);
    let t1: i64 = rows[1][0].parse().unwrap_or(t0 + 3600000);
    let src_secs = ((t1 - t0) / 1000).max(60);
    println!("Source interval: {}СЃ", src_secs);

    if target_secs <= src_secs {
        return Err(format!("Target {} must be > source {}СЃ", target_str, src_secs).into());
    }

    let bucket_size_ms = target_secs * 1000;
    let mut groups: HashMap<i64, (String, f64, f64, String, f64, f64, i64, String, i64)> = HashMap::new();

    for r in &rows {
        let open_time: i64 = r[0].parse().unwrap_or(0);
        let bucket_start = (open_time / bucket_size_ms) * bucket_size_ms;
        let entry = groups.entry(bucket_start).or_insert_with(|| {
            (r[1].clone(), f64::NEG_INFINITY, f64::INFINITY, r[4].clone(), 0.0, 0.0, 0, String::new(), 0)
        });
        let h: f64 = r[2].parse().unwrap_or(0.0);
        if h > entry.1 { entry.1 = h; }
        let l: f64 = r[3].parse().unwrap_or(f64::INFINITY);
        if l < entry.2 { entry.2 = l; }
        entry.3 = r[4].clone();  // close = last
        entry.4 += r[5].parse::<f64>().unwrap_or(0.0);  // volume sum
        entry.5 += r.get(7).and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);  // quote_volume
        entry.7 = r.get(7).cloned().unwrap_or_default();  // keep turnover as is for simplicity
        entry.8 += r.get(8).and_then(|s| s.parse::<i64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0);  // count
    }

    let mut sorted: Vec<_> = groups.into_iter().collect();
    sorted.sort_by_key(|(k, _)| *k);

    let mut f = BufWriter::new(File::create(&out_path)?);
    writeln!(f, "{}", header)?;
    for (bucket_start, g) in &sorted {
        let close_time = bucket_start + target_secs * 1000 - 1;
        writeln!(f, "{},{},{:.8},{:.8},{},{:.8},{},{:.8},{},{:.8},{:.8},0",
                 bucket_start, g.0, g.1, g.2, g.3, g.4, close_time, g.5, g.8, 0.0, 0.0)?;
    }

    println!();
    println!("вњ… Resampled {} candles {} в†’ {}", sorted.len(), target_str, out_path.display());
    Ok(())
}

//! P30 v2: Walk-Forward Analysis BounceTrader (BTCUSDT 1h) вЂ” Rust-РїРѕСЂС‚ РїР°РєРµС‚Р° P28.

//! 16 РєРѕРЅС„РёРіРѕРІ РїРµС‡Р°С‚Р°Р»РёСЃСЊ 8 СѓРЅРёРєР°Р»СЊРЅС‹РјРё РјРµС‚РєР°РјРё вЂ” search 0.012/0.020 РЅРµ

//! СЃРѕРІРјРµСЃС‚РёРј СЃ CPython random.Random, СЃСѓРјРјР° вЂ” РєР°Рє sum() РІ Python 3.12+).

use std::env;
use std::fs::{self, File};
use std::io::{BufRead, BufReader, Write};
use std::path::Path;
use std::process::exit;

// ---------- РєРѕРЅСЃС‚Р°РЅС‚С‹ СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёРё (bounce_trader.rs) ----------
const FRACTAL_LB: usize = 5;
const MAX_FRACTALS: usize = 20;
const LEVEL_BUFFER: f64 = 0.0005;
const MIN_SL_PCT: f64 = 0.003;
const MAX_HISTORY: usize = 200;

fn die(msg: &str) -> ! {
    eprintln!("{}", msg);
    exit(1);
}

// С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚ С‡РёСЃР»Р° РєР°Рє repr() РІ Python: 1.0 -> "1.0", 1.5 -> "1.5", 1.2 -> "1.2"
fn py_f64(v: f64) -> String {
    if v == v.trunc() {
        format!("{:.1}", v)
    } else {
        format!("{}", v)
    }
}

// СЃСѓРјРјР° РєР°Рє РІ Python 3.12+: sum() РґР»СЏ float РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚ РєРѕРјРїРµРЅСЃРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅСѓСЋ СЃСѓРјРјСѓ
// РќРѕР№РјР°Р№РµСЂР° (gh-100425); РЅР°РёРІРЅС‹Р№ С„РѕР»Рґ РІ Rust РґР°С‘С‚ СЂР°СЃС…РѕР¶РґРµРЅРёРµ РІ РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРµРј Р±РёС‚Рµ
fn py_sum(iter: impl Iterator<Item = f64>) -> f64 {
    let mut s = 0.0f64;
    let mut c = 0.0f64;
    for x in iter {
        let t = s + x;
        if s.abs() >= x.abs() {
            c += (s - t) + x;
        } else {
            c += (x - t) + s;
        }
        s = t;
    }
    s + c
}

// ---------- CLI ----------
struct Args {
    data: String,
    out: String,
    fee: f64,
    spread: f64,
    funding: f64,
    train: usize,
    test: usize,
    step: usize,
    warm: usize,
    seed: u64,
}

fn usage() {
    println!("P30 v2 WFA BounceTrader (Rust, С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ std) вЂ” РјРµС‚РєРё РєРѕРЅС„РёРіРѕРІ РІРєР»СЋС‡Р°СЋС‚ search");
    println!("Р—Р°РїСѓСЃРє: cargo run --release --bin wfa_bounce [-- РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂС‹]");
    println!("  --data РџРЈРўР¬    CSV РєР»Р°Р№РЅРѕРІ 1h (default: BTCUSDT-1h-full.csv, fallback klines/)");
    println!("  --out РљРђРўРђР›РћР“  СЂРµР·СѓР»СЊС‚Р°С‚С‹ (default: wfa_out)");
    println!("  --fee F        taker РЅР° СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅСѓ, РґРѕР»СЏ (default 0.0005)");
    println!("  --spread F     СЃРїСЂРµРґ РїРѕР»РЅС‹Р№ РєСЂСѓРі, РґРѕР»СЏ (default 0.0002)");
    println!("  --funding F    funding РѕРґРёРЅ РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» 8h, РґРѕР»СЏ (default 0.0001)");
    println!("  --train N      train-РѕРєРЅРѕ, Р±Р°СЂРѕРІ (default 2160 = 90 РґРЅРµР№ РЅР° 1h)");
    println!("  --test N       test-РѕРєРЅРѕ OOS, Р±Р°СЂРѕРІ (default 720 = 30 РґРЅРµР№)");
    println!("  --step N       С€Р°Рі РєР°СЂСѓСЃРµР»Рё, Р±Р°СЂРѕРІ (default 720 = 30 РґРЅРµР№)");
    println!("  --warm N       РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµРІ РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂРѕРІ, Р±Р°СЂРѕРІ (default 250)");
    println!("  --seed N       seed bootstrap-CI (default 42)");
}

fn parse_f64(args: &mut impl Iterator<Item = String>, flag: &str) -> f64 {
    let v = args
        .next()
        .unwrap_or_else(|| die(&format!("[!] {}: РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ", flag)));
    v.parse::<f64>()
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| die(&format!("[!] {}: РїР»РѕС…РѕРµ С‡РёСЃР»Рѕ '{}'", flag, v)))
}

fn parse_usize(args: &mut impl Iterator<Item = String>, flag: &str) -> usize {
    let v = args
        .next()
        .unwrap_or_else(|| die(&format!("[!] {}: РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ", flag)));
    v.parse::<usize>()
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| die(&format!("[!] {}: РїР»РѕС…РѕРµ С‡РёСЃР»Рѕ '{}'", flag, v)))
}

fn parse_args() -> Args {
    let mut a = Args {
        data: String::from("BTCUSDT-1h-full.csv"),
        out: String::from("wfa_out"),
        fee: 0.0005,
        spread: 0.0002,
        funding: 0.0001,
        train: 2160,
        test: 720,
        step: 720,
        warm: 250,
        seed: 42,
    };
    let mut it = env::args().skip(1);
    while let Some(k) = it.next() {
        match k.as_str() {
            "-h" | "--help" => {
                usage();
                exit(0);
            }
            "--data" => {
                a.data = it
                    .next()
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| die("[!] --data: РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ"));
            }
            "--out" => {
                a.out = it
                    .next()
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| die("[!] --out: РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ"));
            }
            "--fee" => a.fee = parse_f64(&mut it, "--fee"),
            "--spread" => a.spread = parse_f64(&mut it, "--spread"),
            "--funding" => a.funding = parse_f64(&mut it, "--funding"),
            "--train" => a.train = parse_usize(&mut it, "--train"),
            "--test" => a.test = parse_usize(&mut it, "--test"),
            "--step" => a.step = parse_usize(&mut it, "--step"),
            "--warm" => a.warm = parse_usize(&mut it, "--warm"),
            "--seed" => {
                let v = it
                    .next()
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| die("[!] --seed: РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ"));
                a.seed = v
                    .parse::<u64>()
                    .unwrap_or_else(|_| die(&format!("[!] --seed: РїР»РѕС…РѕРµ С‡РёСЃР»Рѕ '{}'", v)));
            }
            other => die(&format!(
                "[!] РЅРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅС‹Р№ Р°СЂРіСѓРјРµРЅС‚ '{}', СЃРј. --help",
                other
            )),
        }
    }
    // fallback: РµСЃР»Рё РґРµС„РѕР»С‚РЅРѕРіРѕ С„Р°Р№Р»Р° РЅРµС‚, СЃРјРѕС‚СЂРёРј klines/ СЂСЏРґРѕРј СЃ Р·Р°РїСѓСЃРєРѕРј
    if !Path::new(&a.data).exists() && a.data == "BTCUSDT-1h-full.csv" {
        let alt = Path::new("klines").join("BTCUSDT-1h-full.csv");
        if alt.exists() {
            a.data = alt.to_string_lossy().into_owned();
        }
    }
    a
}

// ---------- РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ ----------
struct Bar {
    ts: i64,
    o: f64,
    h: f64,
    l: f64,
    c: f64,
}

fn load_bars(path: &str) -> Vec<Bar> {
    let f = File::open(path)
        .unwrap_or_else(|e| die(&format!("[!] РЅРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ {}: {}", path, e)));
    let mut bars = Vec::new();
    for (i, line) in BufReader::new(f).lines().enumerate() {
        let line = line.unwrap_or_else(|e| die(&format!("[!] РѕС€РёР±РєР° С‡С‚РµРЅРёСЏ: {}", e)));
        if i == 0 || line.trim().is_empty() {
            continue; // Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРѕРє / РїСѓСЃС‚С‹Рµ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё
        }
        let cols: Vec<&str> = line.split(',').collect();
        if cols.len() < 5 {
            die(&format!("[!] СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР° {}: РѕР¶РёРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ >= 5 РєРѕР»РѕРЅРѕРє", i + 1));
        }
        let parse_f = |s: &str, name: &str| -> f64 {
            s.trim()
                .parse::<f64>()
                .unwrap_or_else(|_| die(&format!("[!] СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР° {}: РїР»РѕС…РѕРµ {} = '{}'", i + 1, name, s)))
        };
        bars.push(Bar {
            ts: cols[0]
                .trim()
                .parse::<i64>()
                .unwrap_or_else(|_| die(&format!("[!] СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР° {}: РїР»РѕС…РѕР№ close_time", i + 1))),
            o: parse_f(cols[1], "open"),
            h: parse_f(cols[2], "high"),
            l: parse_f(cols[3], "low"),
            c: parse_f(cols[4], "close"),
        });
    }
    bars.sort_by_key(|b| b.ts);
    bars
}

// ---------- РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂС‹ ----------
struct RsiWilder {
    p: usize,
    prev: Option<f64>,
    avg_g: f64,
    avg_l: f64,
    sg: f64,
    sl: f64,
    count: usize,
    value: f64,
}

impl RsiWilder {
    fn new(p: usize) -> Self {
        RsiWilder {
            p,
            prev: None,
            avg_g: 0.0,
            avg_l: 0.0,
            sg: 0.0,
            sl: 0.0,
            count: 0,
            value: 50.0,
        }
    }

    fn next(&mut self, close: f64) -> f64 {
        let prev = match self.prev {
            None => {
                self.prev = Some(close);
                return self.value;
            }
            Some(v) => v,
        };
        let d = close - prev;
        self.prev = Some(close);
        let g = d.max(0.0);
        let l = (-d).max(0.0);
        self.count += 1;
        if self.count <= self.p {
            self.sg += g;
            self.sl += l;
            if self.count == self.p {
                self.avg_g = self.sg / self.p as f64;
                self.avg_l = self.sl / self.p as f64;
                let rs = if self.avg_l == 0.0 {
                    f64::INFINITY
                } else {
                    self.avg_g / self.avg_l
                };
                self.value = 100.0 - 100.0 / (1.0 + rs);
            }
        } else {
            let pf = self.p as f64;
            self.avg_g = (self.avg_g * (pf - 1.0) + g) / pf;
            self.avg_l = (self.avg_l * (pf - 1.0) + l) / pf;
            let rs = if self.avg_l == 0.0 {
                f64::INFINITY
            } else {
                self.avg_g / self.avg_l
            };
            self.value = 100.0 - 100.0 / (1.0 + rs);
        }
        self.value
    }
}

// ---------- РєРѕРЅС„РёРіРё ----------
#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
struct Cfg {
    rr: f64,
    min_rr: f64,
    rsi_lo: f64,
    rsi_hi: f64,
    trend_thr: f64,
    trend_win: usize,
    search: f64,
}

fn prod_cfg() -> Cfg {
    Cfg {
        rr: 2.0,
        min_rr: 1.2,
        rsi_lo: 30.0,
        rsi_hi: 70.0,
        trend_thr: 1.5,
        trend_win: 12,
        search: 0.012,
    }
}

fn build_grid() -> Vec<Cfg> {
    let mut grid = Vec::new();
    for &(lo, hi) in &[(30.0f64, 70.0f64), (25.0, 75.0)] {
        for &tt in &[1.5f64, 1.0] {
            for &mr in &[1.2f64, 1.5] {
                for &se in &[0.012f64, 0.020] {
                    let mut g = prod_cfg();
                    g.rsi_lo = lo;
                    g.rsi_hi = hi;
                    g.trend_thr = tt;
                    g.min_rr = mr;
                    g.search = se;
                    grid.push(g);
                }
            }
        }
    }
    grid
}

// ---------- СЃРёРјСѓР»СЏС‚РѕСЂ СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёРё ----------
#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
struct Pos {
    side: &'static str,
    entry: f64,
    sl: f64,
    tp: f64,
    sl_dist: f64,
    ts_in: i64,
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
struct Pend {
    side: &'static str,
    sl: f64,
    tp: f64,
    sl_dist: f64,
}

#[derive(Clone)]
struct Trade {
    side: &'static str,
    ts_in: i64,
    ts_out: i64,
    entry: f64,
    sl: f64,
    tp: f64,
    exit: f64,
    why: &'static str,
    sl_dist: f64,
    net_r: f64,
}

fn run(bars: &[Bar], cfg: Cfg, sig_from: usize, sig_to: usize, warmup_from: usize, cost_unit: f64) -> Vec<Trade> {
    let mut highs: Vec<f64> = Vec::new();
    let mut lows: Vec<f64> = Vec::new();
    let mut closes: Vec<f64> = Vec::new();
    let mut rsi = RsiWilder::new(14);
    let mut fh: Vec<f64> = Vec::new(); // С„СЂР°РєС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ СѓСЂРѕРІРЅРё (С†РµРЅС‹)
    let mut fl: Vec<f64> = Vec::new();
    #[allow(unused_assignments)] // 50.0 вЂ” Р·РµСЂРєР°Р»Рѕ Python-РёРЅРёС†РёР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёРё, РїРµСЂРµР·Р°РїРёСЃС‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РЅР° РїРµСЂРІРѕРј Р±Р°СЂРµ
    let mut rsi_val: f64 = 50.0;
    let mut trades: Vec<Trade> = Vec::new();
    let mut pos: Option<Pos> = None;
    let mut pending: Option<Pend> = None;
    let mut just_closed = false;

    let end = sig_to.min(bars.len());
    for i in warmup_from..end {
        let b = &bars[i];
        let (ts, o, h, l, c) = (b.ts, b.o, b.h, b.l, b.c);
        let in_sig = sig_from <= i && i < sig_to;

        // --- 1) РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРёРµ РѕР¶РёРґР°СЋС‰РµРіРѕ РІС…РѕРґР° РїРѕ open СЌС‚РѕР№ СЃРІРµС‡Рё ---
        if pending.is_some() && pos.is_none() {
            let p = pending.take().unwrap();
            pos = Some(Pos {
                side: p.side,
                entry: o, // РІС…РѕРґ РїРѕ open (Р±РµР· look-ahead); С‚Р° Р¶Рµ СЃРІРµС‡Р° РјРѕР¶РµС‚ РґР°С‚СЊ РІС‹С…РѕРґ
                sl: p.sl,
                tp: p.tp,
                sl_dist: p.sl_dist,
                ts_in: ts,
            });
        }

        // --- 2) РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєРё РІС‹С…РѕРґР° РЅР° СЌС‚РѕР№ СЃРІРµС‡Рµ ---
        if let Some(p) = pos {
            let mut exit_px = f64::NAN;
            let mut why = "";
            let mut hit = false;
            if p.side == "long" {
                if o <= p.sl {
                    exit_px = o; why = "gap_sl"; hit = true;
                } else if o >= p.tp {
                    exit_px = o; why = "gap_tp"; hit = true;
                } else if l <= p.sl {
                    exit_px = p.sl; why = "sl"; hit = true;
                } else if h >= p.tp {
                    exit_px = p.tp; why = "tp"; hit = true;
                }
            } else {
                if o >= p.sl {
                    exit_px = o; why = "gap_sl"; hit = true;
                } else if o <= p.tp {
                    exit_px = o; why = "gap_tp"; hit = true;
                } else if h >= p.sl {
                    exit_px = p.sl; why = "sl"; hit = true;
                } else if l <= p.tp {
                    exit_px = p.tp; why = "tp"; hit = true;
                }
            }
            if hit {
                let d = if p.side == "long" { 1.0 } else { -1.0 };
                let cost = p.entry * cost_unit;
                let net_r = (d * (exit_px - p.entry) - cost) / p.sl_dist;
                trades.push(Trade {
                    side: p.side,
                    ts_in: p.ts_in,
                    ts_out: ts,
                    entry: p.entry,
                    sl: p.sl,
                    tp: p.tp,
                    exit: exit_px,
                    why,
                    sl_dist: p.sl_dist,
                    net_r,
                });
                pos = None;
                just_closed = true;
            }
        }

        // --- 3) РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂРѕРІ (РєР°Рє push_candle РІ Rust) ---
        highs.push(h);
        lows.push(l);
        closes.push(c);
        if highs.len() > MAX_HISTORY {
            highs.remove(0);
            lows.remove(0);
            closes.remove(0);
        }
        rsi_val = rsi.next(c);
        let n = highs.len();
        if n >= FRACTAL_LB * 2 + 1 {
            let ci = n - 1 - FRACTAL_LB;
            let hc = highs[ci];
            let lc = lows[ci];
            let mut up = true;
            for j in (ci - FRACTAL_LB)..=(ci + FRACTAL_LB) {
                if j != ci && !(hc > highs[j]) {
                    up = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if up {
                fh.push(hc);
                if fh.len() > MAX_FRACTALS {
                    fh.remove(0);
                }
            }
            let mut dn = true;
            for j in (ci - FRACTAL_LB)..=(ci + FRACTAL_LB) {
                if j != ci && !(lc < lows[j]) {
                    dn = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
            if dn {
                fl.push(lc);
                if fl.len() > MAX_FRACTALS {
                    fl.remove(0);
                }
            }
        }

        // --- 4) РіРµРЅРµСЂР°С†РёСЏ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»Р° (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РІ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРј РѕРєРЅРµ) ---
        if !in_sig || pos.is_some() || pending.is_some() {
            continue;
        }
        if just_closed {
            just_closed = false; // cooldown 120СЃ РёСЃС‚С‘Рє Рє СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РµР№ СЃРІРµС‡Рµ (1h)
        }
        if closes.len() < cfg.trend_win + 1 {
            continue;
        }
        let recent = closes.len() - 1;
        let past = closes.len() - 1 - cfg.trend_win;
        let mom = (closes[recent] - closes[past]).abs() / closes[past] * 100.0;
        if mom >= cfg.trend_thr {
            continue;
        }
        let price = c;

        if rsi_val < cfg.rsi_lo {
            let mut best = 0.0f64;
            let mut bd = f64::INFINITY;
            let mut found = false;
            for &lv in &fl {
                let dist = (price - lv).abs() / price;
                if dist <= cfg.search && dist < bd {
                    bd = dist;
                    best = lv;
                    found = true;
                }
            }
            if !found {
                continue;
            }
            let buffer = price * LEVEL_BUFFER;
            let sd = ((price - best) + buffer).max(price * MIN_SL_PCT);
            let np_ = cfg.rr * sd - price * cost_unit;
            let nl_ = sd + price * cost_unit;
            if nl_ <= 0.0 || np_ <= 0.0 || np_ / nl_ < cfg.min_rr {
                continue;
            }
            pending = Some(Pend {
                side: "long",
                sl: price - sd,
                tp: price + sd * cfg.rr,
                sl_dist: sd,
            });
        } else if rsi_val > cfg.rsi_hi {
            let mut best = 0.0f64;
            let mut bd = f64::INFINITY;
            let mut found = false;
            for &lv in &fh {
                let dist = (price - lv).abs() / price;
                if dist <= cfg.search && dist < bd {
                    bd = dist;
                    best = lv;
                    found = true;
                }
            }
            if !found {
                continue;
            }
            let buffer = price * LEVEL_BUFFER;
            let sd = ((best - price) + buffer).max(price * MIN_SL_PCT);
            let np_ = cfg.rr * sd - price * cost_unit;
            let nl_ = sd + price * cost_unit;
            if nl_ <= 0.0 || np_ <= 0.0 || np_ / nl_ < cfg.min_rr {
                continue;
            }
            pending = Some(Pend {
                side: "short",
                sl: price + sd,
                tp: price - sd * cfg.rr,
                sl_dist: sd,
            });
        }
    }
    trades
}

// ---------- СЃС‚Р°С‚РёСЃС‚РёРєР° ----------
struct St {
    n: usize,
    wr: f64,
    total_r: f64,
    pf: f64,
    maxdd: f64,
    avg_r: f64,
}

fn stats(trades: &[Trade]) -> St {
    if trades.is_empty() {
        return St { n: 0, wr: 0.0, total_r: 0.0, pf: 0.0, maxdd: 0.0, avg_r: 0.0 };
    }
    let n = trades.len();
    let rs: Vec<f64> = trades.iter().map(|t| t.net_r).collect();
    let wins = rs.iter().filter(|&&x| x > 0.0).count();
    let gw: f64 = py_sum(rs.iter().filter(|&&x| x > 0.0).copied());
    let gl: f64 = py_sum(rs.iter().filter(|&&x| x <= 0.0).map(|x| -x));
    let mut eq = 0.0f64;
    let mut peak = 0.0f64;
    let mut maxdd = 0.0f64;
    for &x in &rs {
        eq += x;
        if eq > peak {
            peak = eq;
        }
        if peak - eq > maxdd {
            maxdd = peak - eq;
        }
    }
    St {
        n,
        wr: 100.0 * wins as f64 / n as f64,
        total_r: py_sum(rs.iter().copied()),
        pf: if gl > 0.0 { gw / gl } else { f64::INFINITY },
        maxdd,
        avg_r: py_sum(rs.iter().copied()) / n as f64,
    }
}

fn fmt_st(s: &St) -> String {
    if s.n == 0 {
        return String::from("n=0");
    }
    format!(
        "n={} WR={:.1}% R={:+.2} PF={:.2} maxDD={:.2}R avg={:+.3}R",
        s.n, s.wr, s.total_r, s.pf, s.maxdd, s.avg_r
    )
}

// ---------- Р“РџРЎР§ MT19937 (Р±РёС‚-РІ-Р±РёС‚ СЃРѕРІРјРµСЃС‚РёРј СЃ random.Random РёР· CPython) ----------
struct Mt19937 {
    mt: [u32; 624],
    idx: usize,
}

impl Mt19937 {
    fn init_genrand(&mut self, s: u32) {
        self.mt[0] = s;
        for i in 1..624 {
            let prev = self.mt[i - 1];
            self.mt[i] = 1812433253u32
                .wrapping_mul(prev ^ (prev >> 30))
                .wrapping_add(i as u32);
        }
        self.idx = 624;
    }

    // РєР°Рє random_seed РІ CPython: init_by_array СЃ 32-Р±РёС‚РЅС‹РјРё СЃР»РѕРІР°РјРё С‡РёСЃР»Р° (LE)
    fn new(seed: u64) -> Self {
        let mut key: Vec<u32> = Vec::new();
        if seed == 0 {
            key.push(0);
        } else {
            key.push((seed & 0xffff_ffff) as u32);
            let hi = (seed >> 32) as u32;
            if hi != 0 {
                key.push(hi);
            }
        }
        let mut m = Mt19937 { mt: [0u32; 624], idx: 624 };
        m.init_genrand(19650218);
        let mut i = 1usize;
        let mut j = 0usize;
        let mut k = if 624 > key.len() { 624 } else { key.len() };
        while k > 0 {
            let prev = m.mt[i - 1];
            m.mt[i] = (m.mt[i] ^ ((prev ^ (prev >> 30)).wrapping_mul(1664525)))
                .wrapping_add(key[j])
                .wrapping_add(j as u32);
            i += 1;
            j += 1;
            if i >= 624 {
                m.mt[0] = m.mt[623];
                i = 1;
            }
            if j >= key.len() {
                j = 0;
            }
            k -= 1;
        }
        k = 623;
        while k > 0 {
            let prev = m.mt[i - 1];
            m.mt[i] = (m.mt[i] ^ ((prev ^ (prev >> 30)).wrapping_mul(1566083941)))
                .wrapping_sub(i as u32);
            i += 1;
            if i >= 624 {
                m.mt[0] = m.mt[623];
                i = 1;
            }
            k -= 1;
        }
        m.mt[0] = 0x8000_0000;
        m
    }

    fn genrand_u32(&mut self) -> u32 {
        if self.idx >= 624 {
            for i in 0..624 {
                let y = (self.mt[i] & 0x8000_0000) | (self.mt[(i + 1) % 624] & 0x7fff_ffff);
                let mut nxt = self.mt[(i + 397) % 624] ^ (y >> 1);
                if y & 1 != 0 {
                    nxt ^= 0x9908_b0df;
                }
                self.mt[i] = nxt;
            }
            self.idx = 0;
        }
        let mut y = self.mt[self.idx];
        self.idx += 1;
        y ^= y >> 11;
        y ^= (y << 7) & 0x9d2c_5680;
        y ^= (y << 15) & 0xefc6_0000;
        y ^= y >> 18;
        y
    }

    // getrandbits(k) РґР»СЏ k<=32: РєР°Рє РІ CPython
    fn getrandbits(&mut self, k: u32) -> u32 {
        self.genrand_u32() >> (32 - k)
    }

    // _randbelow_with_getrandbits(n): РІС‹Р±РѕСЂ РёР· [0, n) вЂ” РєР°Рє random.choice
    fn below(&mut self, n: usize) -> usize {
        let n = n as u32;
        let k = 32 - n.leading_zeros(); // bit_length
        let mut r = self.getrandbits(k);
        while r >= n {
            r = self.getrandbits(k);
        }
        r as usize
    }
}

struct FoldRow {
    fold: usize,
    train: String,
    test: String,
    chosen: String,
    train_stats: St,
    oos_chosen: St,
    oos_prod: St,
}

fn main() {
    let args = parse_args();
    let cost_unit = 2.0 * args.fee + args.spread + args.funding;
    let seed = args.seed;

    if !Path::new(&args.data).exists() {
        die(&format!(
            "[!] С„Р°Р№Р» РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ: {}\n    РїРѕР»РѕР¶Рё BTCUSDT-1h-full.csv РІ РєРѕСЂРµРЅСЊ РїСЂРѕРµРєС‚Р° РёР»Рё СѓРєР°Р¶Рё: cargo run --release --bin wfa_bounce -- --data РџРЈРўР¬",
            args.data
        ));
    }
    let mut bars = load_bars(&args.data);
    bars.sort_by_key(|b| b.ts);
    let n = bars.len();
    if n < args.train + args.test + args.warm {
        die(&format!(
            "[!] РјР°Р»Рѕ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С…: {} Р±Р°СЂРѕРІ, РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ >= train+test+warm = {} (РЅР° 1h: {} РґРЅРµР№)",
            n,
            args.train + args.test + args.warm,
            (args.train + args.test + args.warm) / 24
        ));
    }

    println!("[P30] data = {}", args.data);
    println!(
        "[P30] bars={} | fee={} spread={} funding={} | bootstrap seed={}",
        n, args.fee, args.spread, args.funding, args.seed
    );

    // ---------- WFA ----------
    let (train, test, step, warm) = (args.train, args.test, args.step, args.warm);
    let mut folds: Vec<usize> = Vec::new();
    let mut s = 0usize;
    while s + train + test <= n {
        folds.push(s);
        s += step;
    }
    if folds.is_empty() {
        die(&format!(
            "[!] С„РѕР»РґРѕРІ 0: РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… {} Р±Р°СЂРѕРІ РЅРµ С…РІР°С‚Р°РµС‚ РґР°Р¶Рµ РЅР° РѕРґРёРЅ С„РѕР»Рґ (train {} + test {})",
            n, train, test
        ));
    }
    println!("bars={} folds={} train={} test={}", n, folds.len(), train, test);

    let grid = build_grid();
    let prod = prod_cfg();
    let mut per_fold: Vec<FoldRow> = Vec::new();
    let mut chosen_oos: Vec<Trade> = Vec::new();
    let mut prod_oos: Vec<Trade> = Vec::new();
    let mut grid_oos: Vec<Vec<Trade>> = vec![Vec::new(); grid.len()];
    let mut last_cfg = prod;

    for (k, &s0) in folds.iter().enumerate() {
        let (t0, t1, e0, e1) = (s0, s0 + train, s0 + train, s0 + train + test);
        let sig_train_from = if s0 == 0 { t0 + warm } else { t0 };
        // --- train: РІС‹Р±РѕСЂ РєРѕРЅС„РёРіР° ---
        let mut best_cfg = last_cfg;
        let mut best_r = f64::NEG_INFINITY;
        let mut best_stats: Option<St> = None;
        for g in &grid {
            let tr = run(&bars, *g, sig_train_from, t1, t0, cost_unit);
            let st = stats(&tr);
            if st.n >= 8 && st.total_r > best_r {
                best_cfg = *g;
                best_r = st.total_r;
                best_stats = Some(st);
            }
        }
        if best_stats.is_none() {
            best_cfg = last_cfg; // РЅРµС‡РµРіРѕ РІС‹Р±СЂР°С‚СЊ вЂ” РєР°С‚РёРј РїСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰РёР№
            best_stats = Some(stats(&run(&bars, best_cfg, sig_train_from, t1, t0, cost_unit)));
        }
        last_cfg = best_cfg;
        let train_st = best_stats.unwrap();
        // --- OOS: РІС‹Р±СЂР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ + РїСЂРѕРґ + РІСЃСЏ СЃРµС‚РєР° (РїР»Р°С‚Рѕ) ---
        let oos_ch = run(&bars, best_cfg, e0, e1, e0 - warm, cost_unit);
        let oos_pr = run(&bars, prod, e0, e1, e0 - warm, cost_unit);
        let oos_ch_st = stats(&oos_ch);
        let oos_pr_st = stats(&oos_pr);
        chosen_oos.extend(oos_ch);
        prod_oos.extend(oos_pr);
        for (gi, g) in grid.iter().enumerate() {
            let tr = run(&bars, *g, e0, e1, e0 - warm, cost_unit);
            grid_oos[gi].extend(tr);
        }
        let chosen_tag = format!(
            "rsi{:.0}/{:.0} tr{} rr{} se{:.1}",
            best_cfg.rsi_lo,
            best_cfg.rsi_hi,
            py_f64(best_cfg.trend_thr),
            py_f64(best_cfg.min_rr),
            best_cfg.search * 100.0
        );
        per_fold.push(FoldRow {
            fold: k + 1,
            train: format!("{}-{}", t0, t1),
            test: format!("{}-{}", e0, e1),
            chosen: chosen_tag.clone(),
            train_stats: train_st,
            oos_chosen: oos_ch_st,
            oos_prod: oos_pr_st,
        });
        println!(
            "fold {:2}: train[{}:{}] -> {} | train {} | OOS* {} | OOSprod {}",
            k + 1,
            t0,
            t1,
            chosen_tag,
            fmt_st(&per_fold.last().unwrap().train_stats),
            fmt_st(&per_fold.last().unwrap().oos_chosen),
            fmt_st(&per_fold.last().unwrap().oos_prod)
        );
    }

    // ---------- Р°РіСЂРµРіР°С‚С‹ РїРѕ РїСЂРѕРґ-РєРѕРЅС„РёРіСѓ ----------
    let st_prod = stats(&prod_oos);
    let rs: Vec<f64> = prod_oos.iter().map(|t| t.net_r).collect();
    let avg_sl = if !prod_oos.is_empty() {
        py_sum(prod_oos.iter().map(|t| t.sl_dist / t.entry)) / prod_oos.len() as f64
    } else {
        0.0
    };
    let cost_r = if avg_sl != 0.0 { cost_unit / avg_sl } else { 0.0 };
    let be_wr = if !rs.is_empty() {
        (1.0 + cost_r) / (prod.rr + cost_r) * 100.0
    } else {
        0.0
    };
    let days = (bars[folds[folds.len() - 1] + train].ts - bars[folds[0] + train].ts) as f64
        / 86400000.0;
    let trades_per_day = if days != 0.0 {
        prod_oos.len() as f64 / days
    } else {
        0.0
    };

    let mut rng = Mt19937::new(seed);
    let mut boots: Vec<f64> = Vec::with_capacity(5000);
    for _ in 0..5000 {
        if rs.is_empty() {
            boots.push(0.0);
            continue;
        }
        // sum(rng.choice(rs) for _ in rs) вЂ” РІС‹Р±РѕСЂ Рё СЃСѓРјРјР° РєР°Рє РІ Python
        let mut acc = 0.0f64;
        let mut c = 0.0f64;
        for _ in 0..rs.len() {
            let x = rs[rng.below(rs.len())];
            let t = acc + x;
            if acc.abs() >= x.abs() {
                c += (acc - t) + x;
            } else {
                c += (x - t) + acc;
            }
            acc = t;
        }
        boots.push(acc + c);
    }
    boots.sort_by(|a, b| a.partial_cmp(b).unwrap());
    let ci_lo = boots[(0.025 * boots.len() as f64) as usize];
    let ci_hi = boots[((0.975 * boots.len() as f64) as usize) - 1];
    let mean_r = if !rs.is_empty() {
        st_prod.total_r / rs.len() as f64
    } else {
        0.0
    };
    let sd_r = if rs.len() > 1 {
        (py_sum(rs.iter().map(|x| (x - mean_r) * (x - mean_r))) / (rs.len() - 1) as f64).sqrt()
    } else {
        0.0
    };
    let sharpe = if sd_r > 0.0 && days > 0.0 {
        mean_r / sd_r * (rs.len() as f64 / days).sqrt()
    } else {
        0.0
    };

    println!();
    println!("=== PRODUCTION CONFIG OOS (fix, 1:1 СЃ Р¶РёРІС‹Рј) ===");
    println!("{}", fmt_st(&st_prod));
    println!(
        "breakeven WR ~ {:.1}% (cost {:.2}R РїСЂРё avg SL {:.2}%)",
        be_wr,
        cost_r,
        avg_sl * 100.0
    );
    println!(
        "95% CI total R: [{:+.2}; {:+.2}] | sharpe(РіРѕРґРѕРІРѕР№, РїРѕ СЃРґРµР»РєР°Рј) {:.2} | trades/day {:.2}",
        ci_lo, ci_hi, sharpe, trades_per_day
    );

    println!();
    println!("=== РџР›РђРўРћ (РІСЃРµ РєРѕРЅС„РёРіРё СЃРµС‚РєРё, OOS С†РµР»РёРєРѕРј) ===");
    for (gi, g) in grid.iter().enumerate() {
        let tag = format!(
            "rsi{:.0}/{:.0} trend<{}% minRR {} se{:.1}%",
            g.rsi_lo,
            g.rsi_hi,
            py_f64(g.trend_thr),
            py_f64(g.min_rr),
            g.search * 100.0
        );
        println!("{:<44} {}", tag, fmt_st(&stats(&grid_oos[gi])));
    }

    // ---------- С„Р°Р№Р»С‹ ----------
    fs::create_dir_all(&args.out)
        .unwrap_or_else(|e| die(&format!("[!] РЅРµ СЃРѕР·РґР°С‚СЊ РєР°С‚Р°Р»РѕРі {}: {}", args.out, e)));

    // per_fold.csv (CRLF вЂ” РєР°Рє csv.writer РІ Python)
    {
        let mut f = File::create(format!("{}/per_fold.csv", args.out))
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| die(&format!("[!] per_fold.csv: {}", e)));
        write!(f, "fold,train_bars,test_bars,chosen_cfg,train_n,train_total_r,train_wr,train_pf,oos_chosen_n,oos_chosen_r,oos_prod_n,oos_prod_r,oos_prod_wr,oos_prod_pf\r\n")
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| die(&format!("[!] per_fold.csv: {}", e)));
        for p in &per_fold {
            let train_pf = if p.train_stats.n > 0 {
                format!("{:.2}", p.train_stats.pf)
            } else {
                String::new()
            };
            let oos_prod_pf = if p.oos_prod.n > 0 {
                format!("{:.2}", p.oos_prod.pf)
            } else {
                String::new()
            };
            write!(
                f,
                "{},{},{},{},{},{:.3},{:.1},{},{},{:.3},{},{:.3},{:.1},{}\r\n",
                p.fold,
                p.train,
                p.test,
                p.chosen,
                p.train_stats.n,
                p.train_stats.total_r,
                p.train_stats.wr,
                train_pf,
                p.oos_chosen.n,
                p.oos_chosen.total_r,
                p.oos_prod.n,
                p.oos_prod.total_r,
                p.oos_prod.wr,
                oos_prod_pf
            )
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| die(&format!("[!] per_fold.csv: {}", e)));
        }
    }

    // oos_trades_production.csv
    {
        let mut f = File::create(format!("{}/oos_trades_production.csv", args.out))
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| die(&format!("[!] oos_trades_production.csv: {}", e)));
        write!(f, "ts_in,ts_out,side,entry,sl,tp,exit,why,sl_dist,net_r\r\n")
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| die(&format!("[!] oos_trades_production.csv: {}", e)));
        for t in &prod_oos {
            write!(
                f,
                "{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{:.6},{:.4}\r\n",
                t.ts_in,
                t.ts_out,
                t.side,
                py_f64(t.entry),
                py_f64(t.sl),
                py_f64(t.tp),
                py_f64(t.exit),
                t.why,
                t.sl_dist,
                t.net_r
            )
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| die(&format!("[!] oos_trades_production.csv: {}", e)));
        }
    }

    // summary.json (layout РєР°Рє json.dump(indent=1) РІ Python)
    {
        let pf_json = if st_prod.pf.is_finite() {
            format!("{:?}", st_prod.pf)
        } else {
            String::from("Infinity")
        };
        let json = format!(
            "{{\n \"n\": {},\n \"wr\": {:?},\n \"total_r\": {:?},\n \"pf\": {},\n \"maxdd\": {:?},\n \"be_wr\": {:?},\n \"ci_lo\": {:?},\n \"ci_hi\": {:?},\n \"sharpe\": {:?},\n \"trades_per_day\": {:?},\n \"days\": {:?},\n \"cost_r\": {:?},\n \"avg_sl_pct\": {:?},\n \"meta\": {{\n  \"data\": \"{}\",\n  \"bars\": {},\n  \"fee\": {:?},\n  \"spread\": {:?},\n  \"funding\": {:?},\n  \"train\": {},\n  \"test\": {},\n  \"step\": {},\n  \"warm\": {},\n  \"seed\": {}\n }}\n}}",
            st_prod.n,
            st_prod.wr,
            st_prod.total_r,
            pf_json,
            st_prod.maxdd,
            be_wr,
            ci_lo,
            ci_hi,
            sharpe,
            trades_per_day,
            days,
            cost_r,
            avg_sl * 100.0,
            Path::new(&args.data)
                .file_name()
                .map(|s| s.to_string_lossy().into_owned())
                .unwrap_or_else(|| args.data.clone()),
            n,
            args.fee,
            args.spread,
            args.funding,
            args.train,
            args.test,
            args.step,
            args.warm,
            args.seed
        );
        let mut f = File::create(format!("{}/summary.json", args.out))
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| die(&format!("[!] summary.json: {}", e)));
        f.write_all(json.as_bytes())
            .unwrap_or_else(|e| die(&format!("[!] summary.json: {}", e)));
    }

    println!();
    println!(
        "saved: per_fold.csv, oos_trades_production.csv, summary.json -> {}",
        args.out
    );
}
=== C:\Users\BASE_PC\scalping_agentsGLM\src\bin\ws_probe.rs ===
//! вњ… Р”РёР°РіРЅРѕСЃС‚РёРєР°: РєР°РєР°СЏ С„РѕСЂРјР° РїРѕРґРїРёСЃРєРё СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ РЅР° fstream.binance.com.
//! Р—Р°РїСѓСЃРє: cargo run --release --bin ws_probe -- [symbol]   (РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ solusdt)

use futures_util::{SinkExt, StreamExt};
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio_tungstenite::{connect_async, tungstenite::Message};

const SECS: u64 = 15;

async fn probe(name: &str, url: &str, subscribe: Option<&str>) {
    tracing::info!("[{}] в†’ {}", name, url);
    let ws = match tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(10), connect_async(url)).await {
        Ok(Ok((ws, _))) => ws,
        Ok(Err(e)) => { tracing::error!("[{}] connect: {}", name, e); return; }
        Err(_) => { tracing::error!("[{}] connect: С‚Р°Р№РјР°СѓС‚ 10СЃ", name); return; }
    };
    let (mut write, mut read) = ws.split();

    if let Some(payload) = subscribe {
        if let Err(e) = write.send(Message::Text(payload.to_string())).await {
            tracing::error!("[{}] subscribe send: {}", name, e);
            return;
        }
    }

    let (mut agg, mut book, mut kline, mut other) = (0u64, 0u64, 0u64, 0u64);
    let mut first: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
    let deadline = tokio::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(SECS);

    while let Ok(msg) = tokio::time::timeout_at(deadline, read.next()).await {
        let msg = match msg { Some(Ok(m)) => m, _ => break };
        let text = match msg {
            Message::Text(t) => t,
            Message::Binary(_) => { tracing::warn!("[{}] Binary-РєР°РґСЂ (РїСЂРѕРїСѓС‰РµРЅ)", name); continue; }
            _ => continue,
        };
        let v: serde_json::Value = match serde_json::from_str(&text) {
            Ok(v) => v,
            Err(_) => { other += 1; continue; }
        };
        if v.get("result").is_some() { continue; } // ack РЅР° SUBSCRIBE
        let ev = v["data"]["e"].as_str().or_else(|| v["e"].as_str()).unwrap_or("?");
        if first.len() < 2 {
            let label = v.get("stream").and_then(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or("<Р±РµР· РѕР±С‘СЂС‚РєРё>");
            first.push(format!("stream={} e={}", label, ev));
        }
        match ev {
            "aggTrade" => agg += 1,
            "bookTicker" => book += 1,
            "kline" => kline += 1,
            _ => other += 1,
        }
    }
    tracing::info!("[{}] РРўРћР“ Р·Р° {}СЃ: aggTrade={} bookTicker={} kline={} РґСЂСѓРіРѕРµ={}",
        name, SECS, agg, book, kline, other);
    for f in first { tracing::info!("[{}]   РєР°РґСЂ: {}", name, f); }
}

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {
    tracing_subscriber::fmt().with_env_filter("info").init();

    let sym = std::env::args().nth(1).unwrap_or_else(|| "solusdt".to_string());
    let s = sym.to_lowercase();
    tracing::info!("РџСЂРѕР±РЅРёРє fstream РґР»СЏ {}", s);

        // ===== Р‘Р°С‚Р°СЂРµСЏ 2: РґРёС„С„РµСЂРµРЅС†РёР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ РґРёР°РіРЅРѕСЃС‚РёРєР° =====

    // A) РљРѕРЅС‚СЂРѕР»СЊ: СЃРїРѕС‚РѕРІС‹Р№ aggTrade вЂ” РІ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°СЋС‰РµРј Р±РѕС‚Рµ РѕРЅ РїСЂРёС…РѕРґРёС‚
    probe("A-СЃРїРѕС‚-aggTrade-РљРћРќРўР РћР›Р¬",
        &format!("wss://stream.binance.com:9443/stream?streams={s}@aggTrade"),
        None).await;

    // B) Р¤СЊСЋС‡РµСЂСЃРЅС‹Р№ aggTrade РїРѕ BTCUSDT вЂ” Р·Р°РІРёСЃРёС‚ Р»Рё РѕС‚ СЃРёРјРІРѕР»Р°
    probe("B-fstream-btcusdt-aggTrade",
        "wss://fstream.binance.com/stream?streams=btcusdt@aggTrade",
        None).await;

    // C) Р¤СЊСЋС‡РµСЂСЃРЅС‹Р№ markPrice вЂ” РґСЂСѓРіРѕР№ С‚РёРї СЃС‚СЂРёРјР°
    probe("C-fstream-markPrice",
        &format!("wss://fstream.binance.com/stream?streams={s}@markPrice"),
        None).await;

    // D) Р¤СЊСЋС‡РµСЂСЃРЅС‹Р№ miniTicker вЂ” РµС‰С‘ РѕРґРёРЅ С‚РёРї
    probe("D-fstream-miniTicker",
        &format!("wss://fstream.binance.com/stream?streams={s}@miniTicker"),
        None).await;

    Ok(())
}

use anyhow::{Context, Result};
use rust_decimal::Decimal;
use serde::Deserialize;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::fs;
use std::path::Path;

// ============================================================
// Global defaults
// ============================================================
fn default_symbol() -> String { "BTCUSDT".to_string() }
fn default_base_currency() -> String { "USDT".to_string() }
fn default_db_path() -> String { "data/state.db".to_string() }
fn default_sync_interval() -> u64 { 5 }
fn default_max_concurrent_positions() -> usize { 3 }
fn default_rest_url() -> String { "https://testnet.binance.vision".to_string() }
fn default_ws_url() -> String { "wss://stream.binance.com:9443".to_string() }
fn default_market_type() -> String { "spot".to_string() }
fn default_kline_interval() -> String { "1m".to_string() }
fn default_orderbook_density() -> Decimal { Decimal::from(5) }

// ============================================================
// Bot defaults
// ============================================================
fn default_initial_balance() -> Decimal { Decimal::from(1000) }
fn default_max_daily_loss_amount() -> Decimal { Decimal::from(30) }
fn default_circuit_breaker_window() -> u64 { 1800 }
fn default_circuit_breaker_cooldown() -> u64 { 600 }
fn default_order_timeout() -> u64 { 15 }
fn default_leverage() -> u32 { 1 }
fn default_risk_per_trade() -> Decimal { Decimal::new(5, 1) }
fn default_trailing_activation() -> Decimal { Decimal::new(15, 2) }
fn default_break_even_offset() -> Decimal { Decimal::new(5, 2) }
// [P9:mode-field] trailing regime: "atr" | "percent" | "tightest"
fn default_trailing_mode() -> String { "atr".to_string() }
fn default_max_position_duration() -> u64 { 60 }
fn default_simulate() -> bool { true }
fn default_params() -> HashMap<String, Decimal> { HashMap::new() }
fn default_maker_fee() -> Decimal { Decimal::new(2, 4) }     // 0.0002
fn default_min_ev_pct() -> Decimal { Decimal::ZERO }
fn default_min_net_rr() -> Decimal { Decimal::new(12, 1) }   // 1.2
fn default_max_concurrent_risk_pct() -> Decimal { Decimal::new(20, 1) } // 2.0%

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct GlobalConfig {
    pub exchange: String,

    #[serde(default = "default_symbol")]
    pub symbol: String,

    #[serde(default = "default_base_currency")]
    pub base_currency: String,

    #[serde(default = "default_rest_url")]
    pub rest_url: String,

    #[serde(default = "default_ws_url")]
    pub ws_url: String,

    #[serde(default = "default_db_path")]
    pub db_path: String,

    #[serde(default = "default_sync_interval")]
    pub sync_interval_sec: u64,

    #[serde(default = "default_max_concurrent_positions")]
    pub max_concurrent_positions: usize,

    #[serde(default = "default_orderbook_density")]
    pub orderbook_density_threshold: Decimal,
}

// ============================================================
// BotConfig вЂ” СЃ РїРѕР»СЏРјРё РёР·РѕР»СЏС†РёРё
// ============================================================
#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct BotConfig {
    pub name: String,

    /// РЎРёРјРІРѕР» Р±РѕС‚Р°. Р•СЃР»Рё РЅРµ Р·Р°РґР°РЅ вЂ” РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ РіР»РѕР±Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub symbol: Option<String>,

    /// РР—РћР›РЇР¦РРЇ: РµСЃР»Рё true вЂ” СЃРІРѕР№ WS hub, СЃРІРѕР№ REST, СЃРІРѕСЏ Р‘Р”.
    #[serde(default)]
    pub isolated: bool,

    /// РўРёРї СЂС‹РЅРєР°: "spot" РёР»Рё "futures"
    #[serde(default = "default_market_type")]
    pub market_type: String,

    /// вњ… 6: РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёРё ("1m", "5m", "15m" ...).
    /// Р”Р»СЏ futures-Р±РѕС‚РѕРІ Р·Р°РґР°С‘С‚ РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» REST-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂР° СЃРІРµС‡РµР№.
    #[serde(default = "default_kline_interval")]
    pub kline_interval: String,

    /// РЎРІРѕР№ REST URL (РґР»СЏ futures)
    #[serde(default)]
    pub rest_url_override: Option<String>,

    /// РЎРІРѕР№ WS URL (РґР»СЏ futures)
    #[serde(default)]
    pub ws_url_override: Option<String>,

    /// РЎРІРѕСЏ Р‘Р” (РґР»СЏ РёР·РѕР»СЏС†РёРё СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёСЏ)
    #[serde(default)]
    pub db_path_override: Option<String>,

    #[serde(default)]
    pub subaccount_id: Option<String>,

    pub api_key: String,
    pub api_secret: String,

    /// v4.0: OKX passphrase (required for OKX; absent for Binance/Bybit)
    #[serde(default)]
    pub passphrase: Option<String>,

    #[serde(default = "default_initial_balance")]
    pub initial_balance: Decimal,

    #[serde(default = "default_sync_interval")]
    pub sync_interval_sec: u64,

    #[serde(default = "default_max_daily_loss_amount")]
    pub max_daily_loss_amount: Decimal,

    #[serde(default = "default_circuit_breaker_window")]
    pub circuit_breaker_window_sec: u64,

    #[serde(default = "default_circuit_breaker_cooldown")]
    pub circuit_breaker_cooldown_sec: u64,

    #[serde(default = "default_order_timeout")]
    pub order_timeout_sec: u64,

    #[serde(default = "default_leverage")]
    pub leverage: u32,

    #[serde(default = "default_risk_per_trade")]
    pub risk_per_trade_pct: Decimal,

    #[serde(default = "default_trailing_activation")]
    pub trailing_stop_activation_percent: Decimal,

    #[serde(default = "default_break_even_offset")]
    pub break_even_offset_percent: Decimal,

    // [P9:mode-field] trailing regime switch
    #[serde(default = "default_trailing_mode")]
    pub trailing_mode: String,

    #[serde(default)]
    pub fee_rate: Option<Decimal>,

    #[serde(default = "default_maker_fee")]
    pub maker_fee: Decimal,

    #[serde(default = "default_min_ev_pct")]
    pub min_ev_pct: Decimal,

    #[serde(default = "default_min_net_rr")]
    pub min_net_rr: Decimal,

    #[serde(default = "default_max_concurrent_risk_pct")]
    pub max_concurrent_risk_pct: Decimal,

    #[serde(default = "default_max_position_duration")]
    pub max_position_duration_sec: u64,

    #[serde(default = "default_simulate")]
    pub simulate: bool,

    pub strategy: StrategyConfig,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct StrategyConfig {
    pub name: String,

    #[serde(default = "default_params")]
    pub params: HashMap<String, Decimal>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Deserialize)]
pub struct AppConfig {
    pub global: GlobalConfig,

    #[serde(default)]
    pub bots: Vec<BotConfig>,
}

// AppConfig impl
// ============================================================
impl AppConfig {
    pub fn load_from(path: &str) -> Result<Self> {
        if !Path::new(path).exists() {
            anyhow::bail!(
                "config.toml РЅРµ РЅР°Р№РґРµРЅ РїРѕ РїСѓС‚Рё '{path}'. \
                 РЎРѕР·РґР°Р№С‚Рµ РІСЂСѓС‡РЅСѓСЋ (СЃРј. config.example.toml). \
                 Р‘РѕС‚ РќР• РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ СЃРѕР·РґР°РІР°С‚СЊ РєРѕРЅС„РёРі Р°РІС‚РѕРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРё вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ РЅРµР±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅРѕ."
            );
        }
        let content = fs::read_to_string(path)?;
        let mut config: Self = toml::from_str(&content)?;

        // вњ… РџРѕРґСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєР° РїРµСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅС‹С… РѕРєСЂСѓР¶РµРЅРёСЏ РІ API-РєР»СЋС‡Рё вЂ” fail-loud:
        // РµСЃР»Рё VAR РЅРµ Р·Р°РґР°РЅР° РёР»Рё РїСѓСЃС‚Р° вЂ” bail СЃ РїРѕРЅСЏС‚РЅС‹Рј СЃРѕРѕР±С‰РµРЅРёРµРј.
        for bot in config.bots.iter_mut() {
            bot.api_key = resolve_env_required(
                &bot.api_key,
                &format!("bots.{}.api_key", bot.name),
            )?;
            bot.api_secret = resolve_env_required(
                &bot.api_secret,
                &format!("bots.{}.api_secret", bot.name),
            )?;
            if let Some(p) = bot.passphrase.clone() {
                bot.passphrase = Some(resolve_env_required(
                    &p,
                    &format!("bots.{}.passphrase", bot.name),
                )?);
            }
        }

        // [P9:mode-validation] fail-loud on unknown trailing_mode
        for bot in config.bots.iter() {
            match bot.trailing_mode.as_str() {
                "atr" | "percent" | "tightest" => {}
                other => anyhow::bail!(
                    "bots.{}: trailing_mode = \"{}\" - must be atr | percent | tightest",
                    bot.name, other
                ),
            }
        }

        Ok(config)
    }
}
  // ============================================================
 // РЎРІРѕР±РѕРґРЅР°СЏ С„СѓРЅРєС†РёСЏ вЂ” РїРѕРґСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєР° ${VAR} РёР· РѕРєСЂСѓР¶РµРЅРёСЏ.
 // fail-loud: bail РµСЃР»Рё VAR РЅРµ Р·Р°РґР°РЅР° РёР»Рё РїСѓСЃС‚Р°.
 // ============================================================
 fn resolve_env_required(value: &str, field: &str) -> Result<String> {
    let v = value.trim();
    if v.starts_with("${") && v.ends_with('}') {
        let key = &v[2..v.len() - 1];
        let resolved = std::env::var(key).with_context(|| {
            format!(
                "РџРµСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅР°СЏ РѕРєСЂСѓР¶РµРЅРёСЏ {key} РЅРµ Р·Р°РґР°РЅР° вЂ” Р·Р°РїРѕР»РЅРёС‚Рµ .env (РїРѕР»Рµ {field}). \
                 Р‘РµР· РЅРµС‘ Р±РѕС‚ РЅРµ СЃРјРѕР¶РµС‚ РїРѕРґРїРёСЃР°С‚СЊ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃС‹ Рє Binance."
            )
        })?;
        if resolved.trim().is_empty() {
            anyhow::bail!(
                "РџРµСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅР°СЏ РѕРєСЂСѓР¶РµРЅРёСЏ {key} Р·Р°РґР°РЅР° РїСѓСЃС‚РѕР№ вЂ” РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЊС‚Рµ .env (РїРѕР»Рµ {field})"
            );
        }
        return Ok(resolved);
    }
    if v.is_empty() {
        anyhow::bail!(
            "РџРѕР»Рµ {field} РїСѓСЃС‚Рѕ Рё РЅРµ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚ ${{VAR}} вЂ” Р·Р°РґР°Р№С‚Рµ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРµ РІ config.toml"
        );
    }
    Ok(value.to_string())
}

use super::time_sync::TimeSync;
use super::traits::{ExchangeClient, ExchangePosition, OrderResult};
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use async_trait::async_trait;
use hmac::{Hmac, Mac};
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::Value;
use sha2::Sha256;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

type HmacSha256 = Hmac<Sha256>;

pub struct BinanceFuturesClient {
    api_key: String,
    api_secret: String,
    client: Client,
    base_url: String,
    time_sync: Arc<TimeSync>,
}

impl BinanceFuturesClient {
    pub fn new(
        api_key: &str,
        api_secret: &str,
        base_url: &str,
        time_sync: Arc<TimeSync>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            api_key: api_key.to_string(),
            api_secret: api_secret.to_string(),
            client: Client::builder()
                .timeout(Duration::from_secs(10))
                .build()
                .unwrap_or_default(),
            base_url: base_url.trim_end_matches('/').to_string(),
            time_sync,
        }
    }

    fn now_ms(&self) -> i64 {
        self.time_sync.now_ms()
    }

    fn sign(&self, query: &str) -> String {
        let mut mac = HmacSha256::new_from_slice(self.api_secret.as_bytes()).unwrap();
        mac.update(query.as_bytes());
        hex::encode(mac.finalize().into_bytes())
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl ExchangeClient for BinanceFuturesClient {
    async fn sync_time(&self) -> Result<()> {
        let url = format!("{}/fapi/v1/time", self.base_url);
        let json: Value = self.client.get(&url).send().await?.json().await?;
        let server_ms = json["serverTime"].as_i64().unwrap_or(0);
        let local_ms = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis();
        let offset = server_ms - local_ms;
        self.time_sync.set_offset(offset);
        tracing::info!("вЏ° Futures clock offset: {} ms", offset);
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn place_market_order(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        side: &str,
        quantity: f64,
        client_order_id: Option<String>,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let qty_str = format!("{}", quantity);
        let mut query = format!(
            "symbol={}&side={}&type=MARKET&quantity={}&timestamp={}",
            symbol, side, qty_str, ts
        );
        if let Some(id) = client_order_id {
            query.push_str(&format!("&newClientOrderId={}", id));
        }
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/fapi/v1/order?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.post(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;
        let json: Value = resp.json().await?;
        if let Some(err) = json.get("msg") {
            return Err(anyhow!("Binance Futures error: {}", err));
        }
        Ok(OrderResult {
            order_id: json["orderId"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0),
            symbol: json["symbol"].as_str().unwrap_or(symbol).to_string(),
            side: json["side"].as_str().unwrap_or(side).to_string(),
            price: json["avgPrice"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
            quantity: json["origQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(quantity),
            executed_qty: json["executedQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
            status: json["status"].as_str().unwrap_or("UNKNOWN").to_string(),
            commission: 0.0,
        })
    }

    async fn place_limit_order(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        side: &str,
        quantity: f64,
        price: f64,
        client_order_id: Option<String>,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let qty_str = format!("{}", quantity);
        let price_str = format!("{}", price);
        let mut query = format!(
            "symbol={}&side={}&type=LIMIT&timeInForce=GTX&quantity={}&price={}&timestamp={}",
            symbol, side, qty_str, price_str, ts
        );
        if let Some(id) = client_order_id {
            query.push_str(&format!("&newClientOrderId={}", id));
        }
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/fapi/v1/order?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.post(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;
        let json: Value = resp.json().await?;
        if let Some(err) = json.get("msg") {
            return Err(anyhow!("Binance Futures error: {}", err));
        }
        Ok(OrderResult {
            order_id: json["orderId"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0),
            symbol: symbol.to_string(),
            side: side.to_string(),
            price,
            quantity,
            executed_qty: 0.0,
            status: json["status"].as_str().unwrap_or("NEW").to_string(),
            commission: 0.0,
        })
    }

    // ============================================================
    // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B v3: Р·Р°С‰РёС‚РЅС‹Рµ РѕСЂРґРµСЂР° (STOP_MARKET / TAKE_PROFIT_MARKET).
    // РўРµСЃС‚РЅРµС‚ РїСЂРёРЅРёРјР°РµС‚ РёС… С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ С‡РµСЂРµР· Algo Order API.
    // РСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ РѕС€РёР±РѕРє API вЂ” С‚СЂРё РєСЂСѓРіР° РІР°Р»РёРґР°С†РёРё, РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ РЅР°Р·С‹РІР°Р» СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РёР№

    //   РєСЂСѓРі 1: РЅСѓР¶РµРЅ  'type'          в†’ РґРѕР±Р°РІРёР»Рё type=
    //   РєСЂСѓРі 2: РЅСѓР¶РµРЅ  'algotype'      в†’ РґРѕР±Р°РІРёР»Рё algotype=CONDITIONAL

    // РљР»Р°СЃСЃРёС‡РµСЃРєРёР№ /fapi/v1/order РѕСЃС‚Р°С‘С‚СЃСЏ fallback'РѕРј (РѕРЅ РїРѕРЅРёРјР°РµС‚ stopPrice).

    async fn place_protective_order(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        side: &str,
        stop: bool,
        trigger_price: f64,
        client_order_id: Option<String>,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let ord_type = if stop { "STOP_MARKET" } else { "TAKE_PROFIT_MARKET" };

        // ---------- Р¦РµРїРѕС‡РєР° С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕСЃС‚РµР№ РЅР° Algo Order API ----------
        for &dec in &[8usize, 7, 6] {
            let tp_str = format!("{:.*}", dec, trigger_price);
            let ts = self.now_ms();
            let mut q = format!(
                "symbol={}&side={}&type={}&algotype=CONDITIONAL&triggerprice={}&closePosition=true&timestamp={}",
                symbol, side, ord_type, tp_str, ts
            );
            if let Some(id) = &client_order_id {
                q.push_str(&format!("&newClientOrderId={}", id));
            }
            let sig = self.sign(&q);
            let url = format!("{}/fapi/v1/algoOrder?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, q, sig);
            let resp = self.client.post(&url)
                .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
                .send().await?;
            let json: Value = resp.json().await?;

            // РЈСЃРїРµС… = РІ РѕС‚РІРµС‚Рµ РЅРµС‚ "code" (РѕС€РёР±РєР° РїСЂРёС…РѕРґРёС‚ РєР°Рє {"code":..,"msg":..})
            if json["code"].as_i64().is_none() {
                let oid = json["algoId"].as_u64()
                    .or_else(|| json["orderId"].as_u64())
                    .unwrap_or(0);
                tracing::info!(
                    "[{}] рџ›Ў Р·Р°С‰РёС‚РЅС‹Р№ {} РїРѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»РµРЅ С‡РµСЂРµР· Algo API (id={}, trigger={}, С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ={} Р·РЅ.)",
                    symbol, ord_type, oid, tp_str, dec
                );
                return Ok(OrderResult {
                    order_id: oid,
                    symbol: symbol.to_string(),
                    side: side.to_string(),
                    price: trigger_price,
                    quantity: 0.0,
                    executed_qty: 0.0,
                    status: json["algoStatus"].as_str()
                        .or_else(|| json["status"].as_str())
                        .unwrap_or("NEW").to_string(),
                    commission: 0.0,
                });
            }

            let code = json["code"].as_i64().unwrap_or(0);
            let msg = json["msg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?").to_string();
            tracing::warn!(
                "[{}] Algo API (С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ {} Р·РЅ., С†РµРЅР° '{}') РѕС‚РєР°Р·Р°Р» (code={}): {}",

            // Р›СЋР±Р°СЏ РґСЂСѓРіР°СЏ РѕС€РёР±РєР° в†’ С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ РЅРµ РїРѕРјРѕР¶РµС‚, РІС‹С…РѕРґРёРј РІ fallback.
            if code != -1111 {
                break;
            }
        }

        // ---------- РџРѕРїС‹С‚РєР° 2: РєР»Р°СЃСЃРёС‡РµСЃРєРёР№ /fapi/v1/order (fallback) ----------
        let ts2 = self.now_ms();
        let tp_str2 = format!("{:.8}", trigger_price);
        // AUDIT-P8: workingType=MARK_PRICE вЂ” РґРµС„РѕР»С‚ РєР»Р°СЃСЃРёРєРё CONTRACT_PRICE,
        // С‚СЂРёРіРіРµСЂ РїРѕ last price: СЃРїР°Р№Рє last price РЅР° С‚РµСЃС‚РЅРµС‚Рµ СЃСЂС‹РІР°РµС‚ СЃС‚РѕРї
        // Р±РµР· СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ РґРІРёР¶РµРЅРёСЏ mark price. Mark price СѓСЃС‚РѕР№С‡РёРІ Рє РІСЃРїР»РµСЃРєР°Рј.
        // Р’ Algo-РїСѓС‚СЊ РІС‹С€Рµ РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂ РќР• РґРѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅ СЃРѕР·РЅР°С‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ: СЌРЅРґРїРѕРёРЅС‚
        // РЅРµСЃС‚Р°РЅРґР°СЂС‚РЅС‹Р№, РіР»Р°РІРЅС‹Р№ РїСѓС‚СЊ SL РЅРµ Р»РѕРјР°РµРј РґРѕ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРёСЏ С‚РѕРіРѕ,
        // С‡С‚Рѕ Р»РёС€РЅРёР№ РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂ С‚Р°Рј Р±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРµРЅ.
        let mut q2 = format!(
            "symbol={}&side={}&type={}&stopPrice={}&closePosition=true&workingType=MARK_PRICE&timestamp={}",
            symbol, side, ord_type, tp_str2, ts2
        );
        if let Some(id) = client_order_id {
            q2.push_str(&format!("&newClientOrderId={}", id));
        }
        let sig2 = self.sign(&q2);

        let resp2 = self.client.post(&url2)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;
        let json2: Value = resp2.json().await?;
        if let Some(err) = json2.get("msg") {
