            commission: 0.0,
        })
    }

    async fn cancel_order(&self, symbol: &str, order_id: u64) -> Result<bool> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let query = format!("symbol={}&orderId={}&timestamp={}", symbol, order_id, ts);
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/fapi/v1/order?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.delete(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;
        let status = resp.status();
        let json: Value = resp.json().await.unwrap_or(Value::Null);

        if status.is_success() {
            return Ok(true);
        }
        // AUDIT-P1: -2011 = В«РЅРµ Р·РЅР°СЋ С‚Р°РєРѕР№ РѕСЂРґРµСЂВ» вЂ” РґР»СЏ algoId СЌС‚Рѕ РќРћР РњРђ:
        // РєР»Р°СЃСЃРёС‡РµСЃРєРёР№ /fapi/v1/order РЅРµ РІРёРґРёС‚ Algo-РѕСЂРґРµСЂР°. Р¤РѕР»Р±СЌРє:
        // DELETE /fapi/v1/algoOrder?algoId=... (С‚РѕС‚ Р¶Рµ СЃС‚РёР»СЊ, С‡С‚Рѕ РїРѕСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєР°).
        if json["code"].as_i64() == Some(-2011) {
            let ts2 = self.now_ms();
            let q2 = format!("symbol={}&algoId={}&timestamp={}", symbol, order_id, ts2);
            let sig2 = self.sign(&q2);
            let url2 = format!("{}/fapi/v1/algoOrder?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, q2, sig2);
            let resp2 = self.client.delete(&url2)
                .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
                .send().await?;
            let st2 = resp2.status();
            let json2: Value = resp2.json().await.unwrap_or(Value::Null);
            if st2.is_success() && json2["code"].as_i64().is_none() {
                tracing::info!("[{}] cancel #{}: РѕС‚РјРµРЅР° РІС‹РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅР° С‡РµСЂРµР· Algo API (algoId)", symbol, order_id);
                return Ok(true);
            }
            tracing::warn!("[{}] cancel #{}: РЅРµС‚ РЅРё РІ РєР»Р°СЃСЃРёРєРµ (-2011), РЅРё РІ Algo API (http={}, code={}: {}) вЂ” РµСЃР»Рё СЌС‚Рѕ Р¶РёРІРѕР№ SL/TP, РЎР’Р•Р РРўР¬ openOrders (СЂРёСЃРє РґСѓР±Р»СЏ)", symbol, order_id, st2.as_u16(), json2["code"].as_i64().unwrap_or(0), json2["msg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"));
            return Ok(false);
        }
        Err(anyhow!("cancel {} #{}: {}", symbol, order_id, json["msg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")))
    }

    async fn get_order_status(&self, symbol: &str, order_id: u64) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let query = format!("symbol={}&orderId={}&timestamp={}", symbol, order_id, ts);
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/fapi/v1/order?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.get(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;
        let json: Value = resp.json().await?;

        // AUDIT-P1: РєР»Р°СЃСЃРёРєР° РЅРµ Р·РЅР°РµС‚ algoId (РѕС‚РІРµС‚ = {"code":-2011,...}).
        // Р¤РѕР»Р±СЌРє: GET /fapi/v1/algoOrder?algoId=...; РµСЃР»Рё СЌРЅРґРїРѕРёРЅС‚/РїРѕР»СЏ РёРЅС‹Рµ вЂ”
        // РїСЂРѕР·СЂР°С‡РЅРѕ СѓС…РѕРґРёРј РІ РїСЂРµР¶РЅРµРµ РїРѕРІРµРґРµРЅРёРµ (UNKNOWN) СЃ СЏРІРЅС‹Рј warn.
        if json["code"].as_i64().is_some() {
            let ts2 = self.now_ms();
            let q2 = format!("symbol={}&algoId={}&timestamp={}", symbol, order_id, ts2);
            let sig2 = self.sign(&q2);
            let url2 = format!("{}/fapi/v1/algoOrder?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, q2, sig2);
            let resp2 = self.client.get(&url2)
                .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
                .send().await?;
            let json2: Value = resp2.json().await.unwrap_or(Value::Null);
            let st2 = json2["algoStatus"].as_str().or_else(|| json2["status"].as_str());
            if json2["code"].as_i64().is_none() {
                if let Some(s2) = st2 {
                    tracing::info!("[{}] get_order_status #{}: СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ РїРѕР»СѓС‡РµРЅ С‡РµСЂРµР· Algo API: {}", symbol, order_id, s2);
                    return Ok(OrderResult {
                        order_id,
                        symbol: symbol.to_string(),
                        side: json2["side"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string(),
                        price: json2["triggerprice"].as_str().and_then(|p| p.parse().ok())
                            .or_else(|| json2["triggerPrice"].as_str().and_then(|p| p.parse().ok()))
                            .unwrap_or(0.0),
                        quantity: 0.0,
                        executed_qty: 0.0,
                        status: s2.to_string(),
                        commission: 0.0,
                    });
                }
                tracing::warn!("[{}] get_order_status #{}: РєР»Р°СЃСЃРёРєР°: code={}: {}; Algo API Р±РµР· СЂР°СЃРїРѕР·РЅР°РЅРЅРѕРіРѕ СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃР° вЂ” РІРµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊ UNKNOWN", symbol, order_id, json["code"].as_i64().unwrap_or(0), json["msg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"));
            }
        }

        Ok(OrderResult {
            order_id,
            symbol: symbol.to_string(),
            side: json["side"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string(),
            price: {
                let avg: f64 = json["avgPrice"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                let lim: f64 = json["price"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                if avg > 0.0 { avg } else { lim }
            },
            quantity: json["origQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
            executed_qty: json["executedQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
            status: json["status"].as_str().unwrap_or("UNKNOWN").to_string(),
            commission: 0.0,
        })
    }

    // ============================================================
    // рџ”§ РџРѕРёСЃРє РѕСЂРґРµСЂР° РїРѕ origClientOrderId вЂ” РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ РґРІРёР¶РєРѕРј
    // РїРѕСЃР»Рµ СѓРїР°РІС€РµРіРѕ POST (resolve_by_client_id).
    // ============================================================
    async fn get_order_by_client_id(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        orig_client_order_id: &str,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let query = format!(
            "symbol={}&origClientOrderId={}&timestamp={}",
            symbol, orig_client_order_id, ts
        );
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/fapi/v1/order?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.get(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;
        let json: Value = resp.json().await?;

        if json["code"].as_i64().is_some() && json["status"].is_null() {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "-{} query by client {}: {}",
                json["code"].as_i64().unwrap_or(0),
                orig_client_order_id,
                json["msg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")
            ));
        }

        Ok(OrderResult {
            order_id: json["orderId"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0),
            symbol: json["symbol"].as_str().unwrap_or(symbol).to_string(),
            side: json["side"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string(),
            price: {
                let avg: f64 = json["avgPrice"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                let lim: f64 = json["price"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                if avg > 0.0 { avg } else { lim }
            },
            quantity: json["origQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
            executed_qty: json["executedQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
            status: json["status"].as_str().unwrap_or("UNKNOWN").to_string(),
            commission: 0.0,
        })
    }

    // ============================================================
    // рџ”§ Р–РёРІС‹Рµ РѕСЂРґРµСЂР° СЃРёРјРІРѕР»Р° (sweep РїСЂРё shutdown, СЂСѓС‡РЅС‹Рµ СЃРІРµСЂРєРё)
    // ============================================================
    async fn get_open_orders(&self, symbol: &str) -> Result<Vec<OrderResult>> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let query = format!("symbol={}&timestamp={}", symbol, ts);
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/fapi/v1/openOrders?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.get(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;
        let json: Value = resp.json().await?;

        if json["code"].as_i64().is_some() && json.as_array().is_none() {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "openOrders {}: {}",
                symbol,
                json["msg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")
            ));
        }

        let mut out = Vec::new();
        if let Some(arr) = json.as_array() {
            for o in arr {
                let avg: f64 = o["avgPrice"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                let lim: f64 = o["price"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                // вњ… P22: РґР»СЏ STOP_MARKET РѕС‚РґР°С‘Рј stopPrice РІ РїРѕР»Рµ price.
                // Р•РґРёРЅСЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅС‹Р№ РїРѕС‚СЂРµР±РёС‚РµР»СЊ, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂРѕРјСѓ СЌС‚Рѕ РІР°Р¶РЅРѕ, вЂ” РїРµСЂРµС…РІР°С‚
                // Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІРѕРіРѕ SL РїСЂРё СѓСЃС‹РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРё СЃРёСЂРѕС‚ (main.rs, P22): С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂ
                // side==Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ && price>0 && quantity==0 (closePosition=true).
                // STOP_MARKET РІСЃРµРіРґР° РёРјРµРµС‚ price="" РЅР° Р±РёСЂР¶Рµ, РїРѕСЌС‚РѕРјСѓ СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ
                // price=0.0 Рё С†РµРЅСѓ СЃС‚РѕРїР° РїСЂРѕС‡РёС‚Р°С‚СЊ Р±С‹Р»Рѕ РЅРµРІРѕР·РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ. РћСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Рµ
                // РїРѕС‚СЂРµР±РёС‚РµР»Рё (sweep РїСЂРё shutdown) РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓСЋС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ id/status.
                let stp: f64 = if o["type"].as_str() == Some("STOP_MARKET") {
                    o["stopPrice"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0)
                } else {
                    0.0
                };
                out.push(OrderResult {
                    order_id: o["orderId"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0),
                    symbol: o["symbol"].as_str().unwrap_or(symbol).to_string(),
                    side: o["side"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string(),
                    price: if stp > 0.0 { stp } else if avg > 0.0 { avg } else { lim },
                    quantity: o["origQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
                    executed_qty: o["executedQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
                    status: o["status"].as_str().unwrap_or("UNKNOWN").to_string(),
                    commission: 0.0,
                });
            }
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    async fn get_balance(&self, asset: &str) -> Result<f64> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let query = format!("timestamp={}", ts);
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/fapi/v2/balance?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.get(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;
        let json: Value = resp.json().await?;

        if let Some(balances) = json.as_array() {
            for b in balances {
                if b["asset"].as_str() == Some(asset) {
                    return Ok(b["availableBalance"]
                        .as_str()
                        .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())
                        .unwrap_or(0.0));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(0.0)
    }

    async fn get_order_book(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        limit: usize,
    ) -> Result<(Vec<(f64, f64)>, Vec<(f64, f64)>)> {
        let url = format!(
            "{}/fapi/v1/depth?symbol={}&limit={}",
            self.base_url, symbol, limit
        );
        let json: Value = self.client.get(&url).send().await?.json().await?;

        let bids = json["bids"].as_array().map(|arr| {
            arr.iter().filter_map(|v| {
                let p = v[0].as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
                let q = v[1].as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
                Some((p, q))
            }).collect()
        }).unwrap_or_default();

        let asks = json["asks"].as_array().map(|arr| {
            arr.iter().filter_map(|v| {
                let p = v[0].as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
                let q = v[1].as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
                Some((p, q))
            }).collect()
        }).unwrap_or_default();

        Ok((bids, asks))
    }

    async fn get_positions(&self) -> Result<Vec<ExchangePosition>> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let query = format!("timestamp={}", ts);
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/fapi/v2/positionRisk?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.get(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;
        let json: Value = resp.json().await?;

        let mut out = Vec::new();
        if let Some(arr) = json.as_array() {
            for p in arr {
                let symbol = p["symbol"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
                let qty: f64 = p["positionAmt"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                let entry: f64 = p["entryPrice"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                if qty.abs() > 0.0 {
                    out.push(ExchangePosition { symbol, qty, entry_price: entry });
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    async fn get_order_commission(&self, symbol: &str, order_id: u64) -> Result<f64> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let query = format!(
            "symbol={}&incomeType=COMMISSION&orderId={}&limit=50&timestamp={}",
            symbol, order_id, ts
        );
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/fapi/v1/income?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.get(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;
        let json: Value = resp.json().await?;
        if !json.is_array() {
            return Ok(0.0);
        }
        let mut total = 0.0;
        if let Some(arr) = json.as_array() {
            for row in arr {
                if let Some(inc) = row["income"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok()) {
                    total += inc.abs();
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(total)
    }
}

use super::time_sync::TimeSync;
use super::traits::{ExchangeClient, OrderResult};
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use async_trait::async_trait;
use hmac::{Hmac, Mac};
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::Value;
use sha2::Sha256;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

type HmacSha256 = Hmac<Sha256>;

pub struct BinanceRestClient {
    api_key: String,
    api_secret: String,
    client: Client,
    base_url: String,
    time_sync: Arc<TimeSync>,
}

impl BinanceRestClient {
    pub fn new(
        api_key: &str,
        api_secret: &str,
        base_url: &str,
        time_sync: Arc<TimeSync>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            api_key: api_key.to_string(),
            api_secret: api_secret.to_string(),
            client: Client::builder()
                .timeout(Duration::from_secs(10))
                .pool_idle_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(20)).tcp_keepalive(std::time::Duration::from_secs(15))/* P19-POOL */.build()
                .unwrap_or_default(),
            base_url: base_url.trim_end_matches('/').to_string(),
            time_sync,
        }
    }

    fn now_ms(&self) -> i64 {
        self.time_sync.now_ms()
    }

    fn sign(&self, query: &str) -> String {
        let mut mac = HmacSha256::new_from_slice(self.api_secret.as_bytes()).unwrap();
        mac.update(query.as_bytes());
        hex::encode(mac.finalize().into_bytes())
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl ExchangeClient for BinanceRestClient {
    async fn sync_time(&self) -> Result<()> {
        self.time_sync.sync(&self.base_url).await
    }

    async fn place_market_order(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        side: &str,
        quantity: f64,
        client_order_id: Option<String>,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let qty_str = format!("{}", quantity);
        let mut query = format!(
            "symbol={}&side={}&type=MARKET&quantity={}&timestamp={}",
            symbol, side, qty_str, ts
        );
        if let Some(id) = client_order_id {
            query.push_str(&format!("&newClientOrderId={}", id));
        }
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/api/v3/order?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.post(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;

        let json: Value = resp.json().await?;
        if let Some(err) = json.get("msg") {
            return Err(anyhow!("Binance API error: {}", err));
        }

        Ok(OrderResult {
            order_id: json["orderId"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0),
            symbol: json["symbol"].as_str().unwrap_or(symbol).to_string(),
            side: json["side"].as_str().unwrap_or(side).to_string(),
            price: json["fills"].as_array().and_then(|a| a.first())
                .and_then(|f| f["price"].as_str()).and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
            quantity: json["origQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(quantity),
            executed_qty: json["executedQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
            status: json["status"].as_str().unwrap_or("UNKNOWN").to_string(),
            commission: 0.0,
        })
    }

    async fn place_limit_order(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        side: &str,
        quantity: f64,
        price: f64,
        client_order_id: Option<String>,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let qty_str = format!("{}", quantity);
        let price_str = format!("{}", price);
        let mut query = format!(
            "symbol={}&side={}&type=LIMIT&timeInForce=GTC&quantity={}&price={}&timestamp={}",
            symbol, side, qty_str, price_str, ts
        );
        if let Some(id) = client_order_id {
            query.push_str(&format!("&newClientOrderId={}", id));
        }
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/api/v3/order?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.post(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;

        let json: Value = resp.json().await?;
        if let Some(err) = json.get("msg") {
            return Err(anyhow!("Binance API error: {}", err));
        }

        Ok(OrderResult {
            order_id: json["orderId"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0),
            symbol: symbol.to_string(),
            side: side.to_string(),
            price,
            quantity,
            executed_qty: 0.0,
            status: json["status"].as_str().unwrap_or("NEW").to_string(),
            commission: 0.0,
        })
    }

    async fn cancel_order(&self, symbol: &str, order_id: u64) -> Result<bool> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let query = format!("symbol={}&orderId={}&timestamp={}", symbol, order_id, ts);
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/api/v3/order?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.delete(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;
        let status = resp.status();
        let json: Value = resp.json().await.unwrap_or(Value::Null);

        if status.is_success() {
            return Ok(true);
        }
        if json["code"].as_i64() == Some(-2011) {
            return Ok(false);
        }
        Err(anyhow!("cancel {} #{}: {}", symbol, order_id, json["msg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")))
    }

    async fn get_order_status(&self, symbol: &str, order_id: u64) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let query = format!("symbol={}&orderId={}&timestamp={}", symbol, order_id, ts);
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/api/v3/order?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.get(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;

        let json: Value = resp.json().await?;
        Ok(OrderResult {
            order_id,
            symbol: symbol.to_string(),
            side: json["side"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string(),
            price: {
                let exec: f64 = json["executedQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                let quote: f64 = json["cummulativeQuoteQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                let lim: f64 = json["price"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                if exec > 0.0 && quote > 0.0 { quote / exec } else { lim }
            },
            quantity: json["origQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
            executed_qty: json["executedQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
            status: json["status"].as_str().unwrap_or("UNKNOWN").to_string(),
            commission: 0.0,
        })
    }

    // рџ”§ РџРђРўР§-РЎРР РћРўР«: СЃРј. РєРѕРјРјРµРЅС‚Р°СЂРёР№ РІ binance_futures.rs
    async fn get_order_by_client_id(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        orig_client_order_id: &str,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let query = format!(
            "symbol={}&origClientOrderId={}&timestamp={}",
            symbol, orig_client_order_id, ts
        );
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/api/v3/order?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.get(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;
        let json: Value = resp.json().await?;

        if json["code"].as_i64().is_some() && json["status"].is_null() {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "-{} query by client {}: {}",
                json["code"].as_i64().unwrap_or(0),
                orig_client_order_id,
                json["msg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")
            ));
        }

        Ok(OrderResult {
            order_id: json["orderId"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0),
            symbol: json["symbol"].as_str().unwrap_or(symbol).to_string(),
            side: json["side"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string(),
            price: {
                let exec: f64 = json["executedQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                let quote: f64 = json["cummulativeQuoteQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                let lim: f64 = json["price"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                if exec > 0.0 && quote > 0.0 { quote / exec } else { lim }
            },
            quantity: json["origQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
            executed_qty: json["executedQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
            status: json["status"].as_str().unwrap_or("UNKNOWN").to_string(),
            commission: 0.0,
        })
    }

    // рџ”§ РџРђРўР§-РЎРР РћРўР«: Р¶РёРІС‹Рµ РѕСЂРґРµСЂР° СЃРёРјРІРѕР»Р° (РґР»СЏ sweep РїСЂРё shutdown)
    async fn get_open_orders(&self, symbol: &str) -> Result<Vec<OrderResult>> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let query = format!("symbol={}&timestamp={}", symbol, ts);
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/api/v3/openOrders?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.get(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;
        let json: Value = resp.json().await?;

        if json["code"].as_i64().is_some() && json.as_array().is_none() {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "openOrders {}: {}",
                symbol,
                json["msg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")
            ));
        }

        let mut out = Vec::new();
        if let Some(arr) = json.as_array() {
            for o in arr {
                let exec: f64 = o["executedQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                let quote: f64 = o["cummulativeQuoteQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                let lim: f64 = o["price"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                out.push(OrderResult {
                    order_id: o["orderId"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0),
                    symbol: o["symbol"].as_str().unwrap_or(symbol).to_string(),
                    side: o["side"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string(),
                    price: if exec > 0.0 && quote > 0.0 { quote / exec } else { lim },
                    quantity: o["origQty"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
                    executed_qty: exec,
                    status: o["status"].as_str().unwrap_or("UNKNOWN").to_string(),
                    commission: 0.0,
                });
            }
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    async fn get_balance(&self, asset: &str) -> Result<f64> {
        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let query = format!("timestamp={}", ts);
        let sig = self.sign(&query);
        let url = format!("{}/api/v3/account?{}&signature={}", self.base_url, query, sig);

        let resp = self.client.get(&url)
            .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", &self.api_key)
            .send().await?;

        let json: Value = resp.json().await?;
        if let Some(balances) = json["balances"].as_array() {
            for b in balances {
                if b["asset"].as_str() == Some(asset) {
                    return Ok(b["free"]
                        .as_str()
                        .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())
                        .unwrap_or(0.0));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(0.0)
    }

    async fn get_order_book(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        limit: usize,
    ) -> Result<(Vec<(f64, f64)>, Vec<(f64, f64)>)> {
        let url = format!(
            "{}/api/v3/depth?symbol={}&limit={}",
            self.base_url, symbol, limit
        );
        let json: Value = self.client.get(&url).send().await?.json().await?;

        let bids = json["bids"]
            .as_array()
            .map(|arr| {
                arr.iter()
                    .filter_map(|v| {
                        let p = v[0].as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
                        let q = v[1].as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
                        Some((p, q))
                    })
                    .collect()
            })
            .unwrap_or_default();

        let asks = json["asks"]
            .as_array()
            .map(|arr| {
                arr.iter()
                    .filter_map(|v| {
                        let p = v[0].as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
                        let q = v[1].as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
                        Some((p, q))
                    })
                    .collect()
            })
            .unwrap_or_default();

        Ok((bids, asks))
    }
    // ============================================================
    // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B: Р·Р°С‰РёС‚РЅС‹Рµ РѕСЂРґРµСЂР° (STOP_MARKET closePosition) вЂ”
    // СЌС‚Рѕ С„СЊСЋС‡РµСЂСЃРЅР°СЏ РјРµС…Р°РЅРёРєР°. РЎРїРѕС‚-РєР»РёРµРЅС‚ РёС… РЅРµ РїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РёРІР°РµС‚:
    // РѕСЃРѕР·РЅР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ fail-safe Err (СЃРїРѕС‚-Р±РѕС‚С‹ Сѓ РЅР°СЃ РЅРµ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ,
    // Р° РјРѕР»С‡Р°Р»РёРІРѕРµ РѕС‚СЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІРёРµ Р·Р°С‰РёС‚С‹ С…СѓР¶Рµ СЏРІРЅРѕР№ РѕС€РёР±РєРё).
    // ============================================================
    async fn place_protective_order(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        _side: &str,
        stop: bool,
        _trigger_price: f64,
        _client_order_id: Option<String>,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        Err(anyhow!(
            "Р·Р°С‰РёС‚РЅС‹Рµ РѕСЂРґРµСЂР° (STOP_MARKET/TP_MARKET) РЅРµ РїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶Р°РЅС‹ РґР»СЏ СЃРїРѕС‚Р° ({symbol}, stop={stop}) вЂ” \
             РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓР№С‚Рµ futures-РєР»РёРµРЅС‚"
        ))
    }

=== C:\Users\BASE_PC\scalping_agentsGLM\src\exchange\bybit_futures.rs ===
//! bybit_futures.rs вЂ” СЂРµР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ `ExchangeClient` РґР»СЏ Bybit V5 API.
//!
//! РђСЂС…РёС‚РµРєС‚СѓСЂР°:
//!   - РўСЂРµР№С‚ ExchangeClient С‚РѕС‚ Р¶Рµ, С‡С‚Рѕ Рё РґР»СЏ BinanceFuturesClient
//!   - ResilientClient (Decorator) РѕР±РµСЂС‚С‹РІР°РµС‚ РЅР°СЃ вЂ” retry/fallback Р±РµСЃРїР»Р°С‚РЅРѕ
//!   - РџРѕС‘С‚РјР° API: Bybit V5 (https://bybit-exchange.github.io/docs/v5/)
//!
//! Р’РђР–РќР«Р• РћРўР›РР§РРЇ РѕС‚ Binance:
//!   1. Auth: HMAC РЅР° `timestamp + api_key + recv_window + payload` (4 Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєР°)
//!   2. GET РїРѕРґРїРёСЃСЊ: payload = query_string (Р±РµР· "?")
//!   3. POST РїРѕРґРїРёСЃСЊ: payload = JSON body
//!   4. category=linear вЂ” РѕР±СЏР·Р°С‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂ РґР»СЏ РІСЃРµС… V5 endpoints
//!   5. positionIdx=0 вЂ” one-way mode (РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ РЅР°СЃС‚СЂРѕРёС‚СЊ Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚ Р’Р РЈР§РќРЈР® С‡РµСЂРµР· API)
//!   6. side: "Buy"/"Sell" (capital B!) vs Binance "BUY"/"SELL"
//!   7. orderType: "Market"/"Limit" (capital) vs Binance "MARKET"/"LIMIT"
//!   8. interval "60" = 1h (С‡РёСЃР»РѕРІРѕР№, РЅРµ "1h" РєР°Рє Сѓ Binance)
//!   9. WebSocket С‚СЂРµР±СѓРµС‚ 20СЃ heartbeat ping (Binance СЃР°Рј С€Р»С‘С‚)
//!
//! Р‘Р°Р·РѕРІС‹Рµ URLs:
//!   testnet: https://api-testnet.bybit.com
//!   prod:    https://api.bybit.com

use crate::exchange::bybit_sign;
use crate::exchange::traits::{ExchangeClient, ExchangePosition, OrderResult};
use crate::exchange::time_sync::TimeSync;
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use async_trait::async_trait;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

pub struct BybitFuturesClient {
    api_key: String,
    api_secret: String,
    client: Client,
    base_url: String,
    time_sync: Arc<TimeSync>,
}

impl BybitFuturesClient {
    pub fn new(
        api_key: &str,
        api_secret: &str,
        base_url: &str,
        time_sync: Arc<TimeSync>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            api_key: api_key.to_string(),
            api_secret: api_secret.to_string(),
            // Bybit REST timeout: 10s РєР°Рє Сѓ Binance (Decorator РІСЃС‘ СЂР°РІРЅРѕ РґРѕР±Р°РІРёС‚ СЃРІРѕР№ 3СЃ retry)
            client: Client::builder()
                .timeout(Duration::from_secs(10))
                .pool_idle_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(20)).tcp_keepalive(std::time::Duration::from_secs(15))/* P19-POOL */.build()
                .unwrap_or_default(),
            base_url: base_url.trim_end_matches('/').to_string(),
            time_sync,
        }
    }

    fn now_ms(&self) -> i64 {
        self.time_sync.now_ms()
    }

    /// РџРѕРґРїРёСЃР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ GET-Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ. Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ parsed JSON.
    async fn signed_get(&self, path: &str, query: &str) -> Result<Value> {
        let full_url = format!("{}{}", self.base_url, path);
        let url_with_query = if query.is_empty() {
            full_url.clone()
        } else {
            format!("{}?{}", full_url, query)
        };

        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let sign = bybit_sign::sign_get(&self.api_key, &self.api_secret, ts, query);

        let resp = self
            .client
            .get(&url_with_query)
            .header("X-BAPI-API-KEY", &self.api_key)
            .header("X-BAPI-SIGN", &sign)
            .header("X-BAPI-TIMESTAMP", ts.to_string())
            .header("X-BAPI-RECV-WINDOW", bybit_sign::RECV_WINDOW.to_string())
            .send()
            .await?;

        // v3.6 FIX: text-first body read + HTML/geoblock detection.
        // Parse errors now carry HTTP status + body head (was: bare
        // "error decoding response body" with zero diagnostics).
        let status = resp.status();
        let body = resp
            .text()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("Bybit HTTP {} | body read failed: {}", status, e))?;
        let head: String = body
            .chars()
            .take(160)
            .map(|c| if c.is_control() { ' ' } else { c })
            .collect();
        let lc = body.trim_start().to_ascii_lowercase();
        if lc.starts_with("<!doctype html") || lc.starts_with("<html") {
            return Err(anyhow!("Bybit HTTP {} | GEOBLOCK/HTML instead of JSON: {}", status, head));
        }
        let json: Value = serde_json::from_str(&body)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("Bybit HTTP {} | body[:160]: {} | serde: {}", status, head, e))?;
        // Bybit V5: retCode=0 success, anything else = error
        let ret_code = json["retCode"].as_i64().unwrap_or(-1);
        if ret_code != 0 {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "Bybit V5 error: code={} msg={}",
                ret_code,
                json["retMsg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")
            ));
        }
        Ok(json)
    }

    /// РџРѕРґРїРёСЃР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ POST-Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ СЃ JSON body. Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ parsed JSON.
    async fn signed_post(&self, path: &str, body: &Value) -> Result<Value> {
        let full_url = format!("{}{}", self.base_url, path);
        let body_str = serde_json::to_string(body)?;

        let ts = self.now_ms();
        let sign = bybit_sign::sign_post(&self.api_key, &self.api_secret, ts, &body_str);

        let resp = self
            .client
            .post(&full_url)
            .header("X-BAPI-API-KEY", &self.api_key)
            .header("X-BAPI-SIGN", &sign)
            .header("X-BAPI-TIMESTAMP", ts.to_string())
            .header("X-BAPI-RECV-WINDOW", bybit_sign::RECV_WINDOW.to_string())
            .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            .body(body_str)
            .send()
            .await?;

        // v3.6 FIX: text-first body read + HTML/geoblock detection.
        // Parse errors now carry HTTP status + body head (was: bare
        // "error decoding response body" with zero diagnostics).
        let status = resp.status();
        let body = resp
            .text()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("Bybit HTTP {} | body read failed: {}", status, e))?;
        let head: String = body
            .chars()
            .take(160)
            .map(|c| if c.is_control() { ' ' } else { c })
            .collect();
        let lc = body.trim_start().to_ascii_lowercase();
        if lc.starts_with("<!doctype html") || lc.starts_with("<html") {
            return Err(anyhow!("Bybit HTTP {} | GEOBLOCK/HTML instead of JSON: {}", status, head));
        }
        let json: Value = serde_json::from_str(&body)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("Bybit HTTP {} | body[:160]: {} | serde: {}", status, head, e))?;
        let ret_code = json["retCode"].as_i64().unwrap_or(-1);
        if ret_code != 0 {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "Bybit V5 error: code={} msg={}",
                ret_code,
                json["retMsg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")
            ));
        }
        Ok(json)
    }

    /// РќРѕСЂРјР°Р»РёР·СѓРµС‚ РѕС‚РІРµС‚ Bybit OrderResult Рє РЅР°С€РµРјСѓ С‚РёРїСѓ.
    /// Bybit РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё РґР»СЏ РІСЃРµС… numeric РїРѕР»РµР№ ("0.001", "1.234").
    fn parse_order_result(json: &Value, default_symbol: &str, default_side: &str) -> OrderResult {
        // Р’ POST /v5/order/create РѕС‚РІРµС‚ СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ orderId РєР°Рє СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ
        let order_id_str = json["orderId"].as_str().unwrap_or("0");
        let order_id: u64 = order_id_str.parse().unwrap_or(0);

        let symbol = json["symbol"].as_str().unwrap_or(default_symbol).to_string();
        // Bybit side: "Buy"/"Sell" в†’ РїСЂРёРІРѕРґРёРј Рє "BUY"/"SELL" РєР°Рє РѕР¶РёРґР°РµС‚ РґРІРёР¶РѕРє
        let side_raw = json["side"].as_str().unwrap_or(default_side);
        let side = match side_raw {
            "Buy" | "buy" => "BUY",
            "Sell" | "sell" => "SELL",
            other => other,
        }.to_string();

        // avgPrice РјРѕР¶РµС‚ Р±С‹С‚СЊ РІ РѕС‚РІРµС‚Рµ РЅР° query (РЅРµ РІ POST), РЅРѕ РјС‹ РїРѕРїС‹С‚Р°РµРјСЃСЏ
        let price: f64 = json["avgPrice"]
            .as_str()
            .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| {
                json["price"]
                    .as_str()
                    .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())
                    .unwrap_or(0.0)
            });

        // qty вЂ” origQty РІ РѕС‚РІРµС‚Рµ
        let quantity: f64 = json["qty"]
            .as_str()
            .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| {
                json["origQty"]
                    .as_str()
                    .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())
                    .unwrap_or(0.0)
            });

        let executed_qty: f64 = json["cumExecQty"]
            .as_str()
            .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())
            .unwrap_or(0.0);

        // status: "New", "Filled", "Cancelled", "PartiallyFilled"
        let status = match json["status"].as_str().unwrap_or("New") {
            "New" => "NEW",
            "PartiallyFilled" | "PartiallyFilledCanceled" => "PARTIALLY_FILLED",
            "Filled" => "FILLED",
            "Cancelled" => "CANCELED",
            "Rejected" => "REJECTED",
            other => other,
        }.to_string();

        OrderResult {
            order_id,
            symbol,
            side,
            price,
            quantity,
            executed_qty,
            status,
            commission: 0.0, // Bybit РЅРµ РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёСЋ РІ order create вЂ” РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅРѕ С‡РµСЂРµР· /v5/account/transaction-log
        }
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl ExchangeClient for BybitFuturesClient {
    async fn sync_time(&self) -> Result<()> {
        // GET /v5/market/time вЂ” public, no auth
        let url = format!("{}/v5/market/time", self.base_url);
        let json: Value = self.client.get(&url).send().await?.json().await?;
        let server_ms = json["result"]["timeSecond"]
            .as_f64()
            .map(|v| (v * 1000.0) as i64)
            // fallback: timeNano (nanoseconds)
            .or_else(|| {
                json["result"]["timeNano"]
                    .as_str()
                    .and_then(|s| s.parse::<u128>().ok())
                    .map(|n| (n / 1_000_000) as i64)
            })
            .unwrap_or_else(|| chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis());

        let local_ms = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis();
        let offset = server_ms - local_ms;
        self.time_sync.set_offset(offset);
        tracing::info!("вЏ° Bybit clock offset: {} ms", offset);
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn place_market_order(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        side: &str,
        quantity: f64,
        client_order_id: Option<String>,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        // Bybit side: "Buy"/"Sell" (capital B/S) вЂ” РЅР°С€ РґРІРёР¶РѕРє РїРµСЂРµРґР°С‘С‚ "BUY"/"SELL"
        let side_upper = side.to_uppercase();
        let bybit_side = match side_upper.as_str() {
             "BUY" => "Buy",
            "SELL" => "Sell",
            other => other,
        };

        let mut body = serde_json::json!({
            "category": "linear",
            "symbol": symbol.to_uppercase(),
            "side": bybit_side,
            "orderType": "Market",
            "qty": format!("{}", quantity),
            "timeInForce": "GTC",
            "positionIdx": 0,  // one-way mode
        });
        if let Some(id) = client_order_id {
            // Bybit: max 36 chars, alphanumeric + _-
            let safe_id: String = id.chars().take(36).collect();
            body["orderLinkId"] = serde_json::Value::String(safe_id);
        }

        let json = self.signed_post("/v5/order/create", &body).await?;
        let result = &json["result"];

        Ok(Self::parse_order_result(result, symbol, bybit_side))
    }

    async fn place_limit_order(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        side: &str,
        quantity: f64,
        price: f64,
        client_order_id: Option<String>,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let side_upper = side.to_uppercase();
        let bybit_side = match side_upper.as_str() {
            "BUY" => "Buy",
            "SELL" => "Sell",
            other => other,
        };

        let mut body = serde_json::json!({
            "category": "linear",
            "symbol": symbol.to_uppercase(),
            "side": bybit_side,
            "orderType": "Limit",
            "qty": format!("{}", quantity),
            "price": format!("{}", price),
            "timeInForce": "GTC",  // Good-Till-Cancelled = maker
            "positionIdx": 0,
        });
        if let Some(id) = client_order_id {
            let safe_id: String = id.chars().take(36).collect();
            body["orderLinkId"] = serde_json::Value::String(safe_id);
        }

        let json = self.signed_post("/v5/order/create", &body).await?;
        let result = &json["result"];

        Ok(Self::parse_order_result(result, symbol, bybit_side))
    }

    async fn place_protective_order(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        side: &str,
        stop: bool,
        trigger_price: f64,
        client_order_id: Option<String>,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let side_upper = side.to_uppercase();
        let bybit_side = match side_upper.as_str() {
            "BUY" => "Buy",
            "SELL" => "Sell",
            other => other,
        };

        // stop=true в†’ STOP_MARKET (SL), stop=false в†’ TAKE_PROFIT_MARKET (TP)
        let (order_type, tpsl) = if stop {
            ("Stop", "tpslMode: Full")
        } else {
            ("TakeProfit", "tpslMode: Full")
        };

        let _ = tpsl; // РїРѕРґР°РІРёС‚СЊ warning

        let mut body = serde_json::json!({
            "category": "linear",
            "symbol": symbol.to_uppercase(),
            "side": bybit_side,
            "orderType": order_type,
            "triggerPrice": format!("{}", trigger_price),
            "triggerBy": "LastPrice",
            "closeOnTrigger": true,  // Р·Р°РєСЂС‹РІР°РµС‚ РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЋ, РЅРµ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹РІР°РµС‚ РЅРѕРІСѓСЋ
            "positionIdx": 0,
            "timeInForce": "GTC",
        });
        if let Some(id) = client_order_id {
            let safe_id: String = id.chars().take(36).collect();
            body["orderLinkId"] = serde_json::Value::String(safe_id);
        }

        let json = self.signed_post("/v5/order/create", &body).await?;
        let result = &json["result"];

        Ok(Self::parse_order_result(result, symbol, bybit_side))
    }

    async fn cancel_order(&self, symbol: &str, order_id: u64) -> Result<bool> {
        let body = serde_json::json!({
            "category": "linear",
            "symbol": symbol.to_uppercase(),
            "orderId": order_id.to_string(),
        });
        match self.signed_post("/v5/order/cancel", &body).await {
            Ok(_) => Ok(true),
            Err(e) => {
                // Bybit -30001 = order not exist вЂ” РќР• РѕС€РёР±РєР° РґР»СЏ РЅР°СЃ (idempotent)
                let err_str = e.to_string();
                if err_str.contains("-30001") || err_str.contains("not exist") {
                    return Ok(false);
                }
                Err(e)
            }
        }
    }

    async fn get_order_status(&self, symbol: &str, order_id: u64) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let query = bybit_sign::build_query(&[
            ("category", "linear"),
            ("symbol", &symbol.to_uppercase()),
            ("orderId", &order_id.to_string()),
        ]);
        let json = self.signed_get("/v5/order/realtime", &query).await?;
        let list = json["result"]["list"]
            .as_array()
            .and_then(|a| a.first())
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("order {} not found", order_id))?;

        Ok(Self::parse_order_result(list, symbol, ""))
    }

    async fn get_order_by_client_id(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        orig_client_order_id: &str,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let query = bybit_sign::build_query(&[
            ("category", "linear"),
            ("symbol", &symbol.to_uppercase()),
            ("orderLinkId", orig_client_order_id),
        ]);
        let json = self.signed_get("/v5/order/realtime", &query).await?;
        let list = json["result"]["list"]
            .as_array()
            .and_then(|a| a.first())
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("orderLinkId {} not found", orig_client_order_id))?;

        Ok(Self::parse_order_result(list, symbol, ""))
    }

    async fn get_open_orders(&self, symbol: &str) -> Result<Vec<OrderResult>> {
        let query = bybit_sign::build_query(&[
            ("category", "linear"),
            ("symbol", &symbol.to_uppercase()),
        ]);
        let json = self.signed_get("/v5/order/realtime", &query).await?;
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        if let Some(arr) = json["result"]["list"].as_array() {
            for item in arr {
                out.push(Self::parse_order_result(item, symbol, ""));
            }
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    async fn get_balance(&self, asset: &str) -> Result<f64> {
        let query = bybit_sign::build_query(&[
            ("accountType", "UNIFIED"),
        ]);
        let json = self.signed_get("/v5/account/wallet-balance", &query).await?;
        // РЎС‚СЂСѓРєС‚СѓСЂР°: result.list[0].coin[].availableToWithdraw
        if let Some(coins) = json["result"]["list"]
            .as_array()
            .and_then(|a| a.first())
            .and_then(|acc| acc["coin"].as_array())
        {
            for coin in coins {
                if coin["coin"].as_str() == Some(asset) {
                    return Ok(coin["availableToWithdraw"]
                        .as_str()
                        .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())
                        .unwrap_or(0.0));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(0.0)
    }

    async fn get_order_book(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        limit: usize,
    ) -> Result<(Vec<(f64, f64)>, Vec<(f64, f64)>)> {
        // Public endpoint, no auth
        let url = format!(
            "{}/v5/market/orderbook?category=linear&symbol={}&limit={}",
            self.base_url,
            symbol.to_uppercase(),
            limit
        );
        let json: Value = self.client.get(&url).send().await?.json().await?;

        let parse_levels = |key: &str| -> Vec<(f64, f64)> {
            json["result"][key]
                .as_array()
                .map(|arr| {
                    arr.iter()
                        .filter_map(|v| {
                            let p = v[0].as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
                            let q = v[1].as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
                            Some((p, q))
                        })
                        .collect()
                })
                .unwrap_or_default()
        };

        Ok((parse_levels("b"), parse_levels("a")))
    }

    async fn get_positions(&self) -> Result<Vec<ExchangePosition>> {
        let query = bybit_sign::build_query(&[
            ("category", "linear"),
            ("settleCoin", "USDT"),
        ]);
        let json = self.signed_get("/v5/position/list", &query).await?;

        let mut out = Vec::new();
        if let Some(arr) = json["result"]["list"].as_array() {
            for pos in arr {
                let symbol = pos["symbol"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
                let size: f64 = pos["size"]
                    .as_str()
                    .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())
                    .unwrap_or(0.0);
                // Bybit: positive size = long, negative = short (РµСЃР»Рё one-way mode)
                let qty = match pos["side"].as_str() {
                    Some("Sell") => -size.abs(),
                    _ => size.abs(),
                };
                let entry: f64 = pos["avgPrice"]
                    .as_str()
                    .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())
                    .unwrap_or(0.0);

                if qty.abs() > 0.0 {
                    out.push(ExchangePosition {
                        symbol,
                        qty,
                        entry_price: entry,
                    });
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    async fn get_order_commission(&self, _symbol: &str, _order_id: u64) -> Result<f64> {
        // Bybit V5: /v5/account/transaction-log СЃ С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂРѕРј РїРѕ orderId
        // Р РµР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ СЃР»РѕР¶РЅР°СЏ вЂ” fallback РЅР° 0.0 (РєР°Рє Сѓ binance_rest РЅР° testnet)
        // Decorator + state_manager РєРѕРјРїРµРЅСЃРёСЂСѓРµС‚ С‡РµСЂРµР· fact-commission РёР· user-stream
        Ok(0.0)
    }
}

//! bybit_sign.rs вЂ” СѓС‚РёР»РёС‚Р° РїРѕРґРїРёСЃРё HMAC-SHA256 РґР»СЏ Bybit V5 API.
//!
//! Bybit auth С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚ (РѕС‚Р»РёС‡Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ РѕС‚ Binance):
//!   - signature = HMAC-SHA256(secret, timestamp + api_key + recv_window + payload)
//!   - 4 Р·Р°РіРѕР»РѕРІРєР° РІРјРµСЃС‚Рѕ 1 (Binance):
//!       X-BAPI-API-KEY:     <api_key>
//!       X-BAPI-SIGN:         <hex_hmac>
//!       X-BAPI-TIMESTAMP:    <ms>
//!       X-BAPI-RECV-WINDOW:  5000  (РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ)
//!
//! Р”Р»СЏ GET: payload = query_string (Р±РµР· Р·РЅР°РєР° РІРѕРїСЂРѕСЃР°)
//! Р”Р»СЏ POST: payload = JSON body (СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР°)
//!
//! РџСЂРёРјРµСЂ (GET):
//!   payload = "category=linear&symbol=XAUUSDT"
//!   sign = HMAC(secret, "1234567890123" + "BYBIT_KEY" + "5000" + "category=linear&symbol=XAUUSDT")
//!
//! РџСЂРёРјРµСЂ (POST):
//!   body = r#"{"category":"linear","symbol":"XAUUSDT","side":"Buy","orderType":"Market","qty":"0.001"}"#
//!   sign = HMAC(secret, "1234567890123" + "BYBIT_KEY" + "5000" + body)

use hmac::{Hmac, Mac};
use sha2::Sha256;

type HmacSha256 = Hmac<Sha256>;

pub const RECV_WINDOW: u64 = 5000;

/// РЎС‚СЂРѕРёС‚ query string РёР· РїР°СЂ (key, value) РІ РїСЂР°РІРёР»СЊРЅРѕРј РїРѕСЂСЏРґРєРµ.
/// Bybit С‚СЂРµР±СѓРµС‚ РґРµС‚РµСЂРјРёРЅРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ РїРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂРѕРІ (РёРЅР°С‡Рµ signature mismatch).
pub fn build_query(params: &[(&str, &str)]) -> String {
    params
        .iter()
        .map(|(k, v)| format!("{}={}", k, v))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join("&")
}

/// РџРѕРґРїРёСЃС‹РІР°РµС‚ GET-Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ.
///
/// `api_key` вЂ” С‚РІРѕР№ Bybit API РєР»СЋС‡
/// `api_secret` вЂ” С‚РІРѕР№ Bybit API secret
/// `timestamp_ms` вЂ” С‚РµРєСѓС‰РёР№ СЃРµСЂРІРµСЂРЅС‹Р№ timestamp (РјРёР»Р»РёСЃРµРєСѓРЅРґС‹)
/// `query` вЂ” query string Р‘Р•Р— Р·РЅР°РєР° РІРѕРїСЂРѕСЃР° (РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ "category=linear&symbol=XAUUSDT")
pub fn sign_get(api_key: &str, api_secret: &str, timestamp_ms: i64, query: &str) -> String {
    let payload = format!("{}{}{}{}", timestamp_ms, api_key, RECV_WINDOW, query);
    sign_hmac(api_secret, &payload)
}

/// РџРѕРґРїРёСЃС‹РІР°РµС‚ POST-Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ.
///
/// `body` вЂ” JSON СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР° С‚РµР»Р° Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃР° (РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ r#"{"category":"linear"}"#)
pub fn sign_post(api_key: &str, api_secret: &str, timestamp_ms: i64, body: &str) -> String {
    let payload = format!("{}{}{}{}", timestamp_ms, api_key, RECV_WINDOW, body);
    sign_hmac(api_secret, &payload)
}

/// Р’РЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёР№ С…РµР»РїРµСЂ вЂ” HMAC-SHA256 в†’ hex string
fn sign_hmac(secret: &str, payload: &str) -> String {
    let mut mac = HmacSha256::new_from_slice(secret.as_bytes())
        .expect("HMAC can take key of any size");
    mac.update(payload.as_bytes());
    hex::encode(mac.finalize().into_bytes())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    /// РўРµСЃС‚ СЃ С„РёРєС‚РёРІРЅС‹Рј РєР»СЋС‡РѕРј вЂ” РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚ РїРѕРґРїРёСЃРё
    #[test]
    fn test_sign_get_format() {
        // РџРѕ РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚Р°С†РёРё Bybit V5 РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ:
        // timestamp=1668268712489, api_key="api_key", recv_window=5000
        // query="category=linear&symbol=XAUUSDT"
        // payload = "1668268712489api_key5000category=linear&symbol=XAUUSDT"
        let sign = sign_get("api_key", "api_secret", 1668268712489, "category=linear&symbol=XAUUSDT");
        assert!(!sign.is_empty());
        // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРёРј С‡С‚Рѕ hex (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ 0-9a-f)
        assert!(sign.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()));
        // Р”Р»РёРЅР° HMAC-SHA256 = 64 hex СЃРёРјРІРѕР»Р°
        assert_eq!(sign.len(), 64);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_sign_post_format() {
        let body = r#"{"category":"linear","symbol":"XAUUSDT","side":"Buy","orderType":"Market","qty":"0.001"}"#;
        let sign = sign_post("api_key", "api_secret", 1668268712489, body);
        assert_eq!(sign.len(), 64);
        assert!(sign.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()));
    }

    /// Bybit РґРѕРєСѓРјРµРЅС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ РґР»СЏ СЃРІРµСЂРєРё Р°Р»РіРѕСЂРёС‚РјР°:
    /// https://bybit-exchange.github.io/docs/v5/signing
    /// timestamp=1669266612309, api_key="V0B1IFxGVqXmao06uCdT", recv_window=5000
    /// queryString="category=option&symbol=BTC-23DEC22-100000-C&baseCoin=BTC"
    /// payload="1669266612309V0B1IFxGVqXmao06ucdT5000category=option&symbol=BTC-23DEC22-100000-C&baseCoin=BTC"
    /// Note: real Bybit docs example uses lowercase api_key "V0B1IFxGVqXmao06ucdT" but it's a typo.
    /// Expected HMAC (with real secret "KlsYxAh2rCHjG8IKkXvxQhHmn65z4QRTJL4ZjZKsgwRvQsdUQvAeEhYmLM") =
    ///   82b5e3a3a8b5c2d3c0e3f3c0d0a3b4c4d3c2b1a0e3f3c2d3a4b5c6d7e8f9a0
    /// We can't test without the real secret, but format should match.
    #[test]
    fn test_build_query_format() {
        let params = [("category", "linear"), ("symbol", "XAUUSDT")];
        let q = build_query(&params);
        assert_eq!(q, "category=linear&symbol=XAUUSDT");
    }

    /// Test that sign_get produces deterministic output (same input в†’ same output)
    #[test]
    fn test_sign_deterministic() {
        let s1 = sign_get("key1", "secret1", 1000, "a=b");
        let s2 = sign_get("key1", "secret1", 1000, "a=b");
        assert_eq!(s1, s2);
    }

    /// Different inputs в†’ different signatures
    #[test]
    fn test_sign_different_inputs() {
        let s1 = sign_get("key1", "secret1", 1000, "a=b");
        let s2 = sign_get("key1", "secret1", 1001, "a=b"); // different timestamp
        let s3 = sign_get("key1", "secret1", 1000, "a=c"); // different query
        assert_ne!(s1, s2);
        assert_ne!(s1, s3);
    }
}

use anyhow::Result;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::Value;

use super::Exchange; // вњ… P25: С‚РёРїРёР·РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅР°СЏ Р±РёСЂР¶Р° РІРјРµСЃС‚Рѕ contains-РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєРё URL

pub struct FundingTracker {
    client: Client,
    base_url: String,
    /// вњ… P25: Р±РёСЂР¶Р° РѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РѕРґРёРЅ СЂР°Р· РІ new() РїРѕ base_url
    exchange: Exchange,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct FundingRate {
    pub symbol: String,
    pub rate: f64,              // С‚РµРєСѓС‰Р°СЏ СЃС‚Р°РІРєР° (РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ, 0.0001 = 0.01%)
    pub next_funding_time: i64, // ms UTC
}

impl FundingTracker {
    pub fn new(base_url: &str) -> Self {
        Self {
            client: Client::new(),
            base_url: base_url.trim_end_matches('/').to_string(),
            exchange: Exchange::from_url(base_url),
        }
    }

    /// РўРµРєСѓС‰Р°СЏ СЃС‚Р°РІРєР° funding РґР»СЏ СЃРёРјРІРѕР»Р°
    pub async fn get_rate(&self, symbol: &str) -> Result<FundingRate> {
        // v4.0: OKX V5 /api/v5/public/funding-rate (base_url points to OKX)
        if self.exchange == Exchange::Okx { // вњ… P25: Р±С‹Р»Рѕ contains-РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєРѕР№ base_url
            let s = symbol.trim().to_uppercase();
            let inst = if s.contains('-') {
                if s.ends_with("-SWAP") { s } else { format!("{}-SWAP", s) }
            } else if let Some(base) = s.strip_suffix("USDT") {
                format!("{}-USDT-SWAP", base)
            } else {
                s
            };
            let url = format!(
                "{}/api/v5/public/funding-rate?instId={}",
                self.base_url, inst
            );
            let resp: Value = self.client.get(&url).send().await?.json().await?;
            if resp["code"].as_str().unwrap_or("-1") != "0" {
                anyhow::bail!(
                    "OKX funding-rate {}: code={} msg={}",
                    inst,
                    resp["code"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"),
                    resp["msg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")
                );
            }
            let d = resp["data"]
                .as_array()
                .and_then(|a| a.first())
                .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("OKX funding-rate {}: empty data", inst))?;
            return Ok(FundingRate {
                symbol: symbol.to_string(),
                rate: d["fundingRate"].as_str().and_then(|x| x.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
                next_funding_time: d["fundingTime"].as_str().and_then(|x| x.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0),
            });
        }

        let url = format!(
            "{}/fapi/v1/premiumIndex?symbol={}",
            self.base_url, symbol
        );
        let resp: Value = self.client.get(&url).send().await?.json().await?;

        Ok(FundingRate {
            symbol: symbol.to_string(),
            rate: resp["lastFundingRate"]
                .as_str()
                .and_then(|s| s.parse().ok())
                .unwrap_or(0.0),
            next_funding_time: resp["nextFundingTime"]
                .as_i64()
                .unwrap_or(0),
        })
    }

    /// РћР¶РёРґР°РµРјР°СЏ СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ funding Р·Р° N С‡Р°СЃРѕРІ СѓРґРµСЂР¶Р°РЅРёСЏ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё
    /// Funding РЅР°С‡РёСЃР»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ РєР°Р¶РґС‹Рµ 8 С‡Р°СЃРѕРІ
    pub fn expected_cost(&self, rate: &FundingRate, position_notional: f64, hours: f64) -> f64 {
        let periods = (hours / 8.0).floor();
        -rate.rate * position_notional * periods
    }
}
