//! вњ… Р¤Р°Р·Р° 3Рі: СЃРІРµС‡Рё РґР»СЏ futures С‡РµСЂРµР· REST-РїРѕР»Р»РёРЅРі.
//! fstream РѕС‚РґР°С‘С‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ bookTicker (РґРѕРєР°Р·Р°РЅРѕ РїСЂРѕР±РЅРёРєРѕРј), REST fapi СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ РїРѕР»РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊСЋ.
//! вњ… Р¤Р°Р·Р° 6: РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» РЅР°СЃС‚СЂР°РёРІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ per-bot (kline_interval: "1m", "15m"...).
//! вњ… Рџ3: РїРµСЂРІС‹Р№ СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅС‹Р№ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ вЂ” backfill (limit=250).
//! рџ”§ РђРќРўР-Р‘РђРќ v2: РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» 10СЃ, Р±СЌРєРѕС„С„ 10в†’60СЃ, Р±Р°РЅ-РїР°СѓР·Р° 120СЃ,
//! С‚РµР»РµРјРµС‚СЂРёСЏ РІРѕР·СЂР°СЃС‚Р° REST-С‚РёС€РёРЅС‹.
//! рџ›ЎпёЏ RESILIENT v3: 3 РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёРµ РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєРё СЃ backoff 500РјСЃ+1СЃ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё
//! РѕРґРЅРѕР№ poll-РёС‚РµСЂР°С†РёРё. Р›РѕРІРёС‚ С‚СЂР°РЅР·РёРµРЅС‚РЅС‹Рµ TCP-РѕР±СЂС‹РІС‹ (os error 10054),
//! РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ testnet Binance РІС‹РґР°С‘С‚ РєР°Р¶РґС‹Рµ ~50СЃ. Р’РЅРµС€РЅСЏСЏ pause_secs РЅРµ
//! С‚СЂРѕРЅСѓС‚Р° вЂ” РѕРЅР° РґР»СЏ IP-Р±Р°РЅРѕРІ Рё Р·Р°С‚СЏР¶РЅС‹С… РїР°РґРµРЅРёР№.
//!
//! в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
//! рџ”§ FIX 23.10.2026 вЂ” BUG#1 (gold_dca candles=0 РЅР° РїСЂРѕС‚СЏР¶РµРЅРёРё 48С‡):
//!   1. Bybit: close_time_ms Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ РќР• С…Р°СЂРґРєРѕРґ В«+1 С‡Р°СЃВ» вЂ” РІС‹С‡РёСЃР»СЏРµС‚СЃСЏ
//!      РёР· РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р»Р° (4h в†’ +14_400_000). Р Р°РЅСЊС€Рµ РЅРµР·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚Р°СЏ 4h-СЃРІРµС‡Р°
//!      РїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°Р»Р°СЃСЊ С‡РµСЂРµР· С‡Р°СЃ РєР°Рє is_closed=true, Р° РµС‘ СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ
//!      Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ РїРѕС‚РѕРј С‚РµСЂСЏР»РѕСЃСЊ (open_time <= last_open_time в†’ skip).
//!   2. Bybit: resp.text() + РґРµС‚РµРєС‚ HTML Р”Рћ РїР°СЂСЃРёРЅРіР° JSON. Р“РµРѕР±Р»РѕРє-СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р°
//!      СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ РїР°РґР°Р»Р° РІ "error decoding response body" Р±РµР· РґРёР°РіРЅРѕСЃС‚РёРєРё вЂ”
//!      РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ РјРѕР»РѕС‚РёР» РІРїСѓСЃС‚СѓСЋ Рё РЅРё СЂР°Р·Сѓ РЅРµ РїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°Р» СЃРІРµС‡Сѓ.
//!   3. Binance-РІРµС‚РєР° РќР• С‚СЂРѕРЅСѓС‚Р° (СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ СЃС‚Р°Р±РёР»СЊРЅРѕ).
//! в”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђв”Ђ
use crate::websocket::{KlineMessage, WebSocketHub, WebSocketMessage};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

/// рџ›ЎпёЏ RESILIENT: СЃРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїРѕРїС‹С‚РѕРє РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё РѕРґРЅРѕР№ poll-РёС‚РµСЂР°С†РёРё.
const INNER_ATTEMPTS: u32 = 3;
/// рџ›ЎпёЏ RESILIENT: РїР°СѓР·С‹ РјРµР¶РґСѓ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёРјРё РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєР°РјРё (РјСЃ).
const INNER_BACKOFF_MS: &[u64] = &[500, 1000];

/// рџ”§ FIX: РїР°СѓР·Р° РїСЂРё РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅРѕРј РіРµРѕР±Р»РѕРєРµ (HTML РІРјРµСЃС‚Рѕ JSON).
/// Р РµС‚СЂР°РёС‚СЊ Р±РµСЃСЃРјС‹СЃР»РµРЅРЅРѕ вЂ” HTML РЅРµ В«РїРѕР¶РµР»С‚РµРµС‚В» РІ JSON, РїР°СѓР·Р° РґР»РёРЅРЅР°СЏ.
const GEOBLOCK_PAUSE_SECS: u64 = 120;

pub fn spawn(
    children: &mut tokio::task::JoinSet<()>,
    hub: Arc<WebSocketHub>,
    rest_base: String,
    symbol: String,
    interval: String,
) {
    children.spawn(async move {
        let client = reqwest::Client::builder()
            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(10))
            .pool_idle_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(20)).tcp_keepalive(std::time::Duration::from_secs(15))/* P19-POOL */.build()
            .unwrap_or_default();
        let base = rest_base.trim_end_matches('/').to_string();
        let mut last_open_time: i64 = 0;
        let mut backfilled = false; // вњ… Рџ3: РґРѕ РїРµСЂРІРѕРіРѕ СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕРіРѕ РѕС‚РІРµС‚Р° вЂ” С€РёСЂРѕРєРёР№ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ
        let mut poll = tokio::time::interval(Duration::from_secs(10));
        poll.tick().await; // РїРµСЂРІС‹Р№ С‚РёРє РјРіРЅРѕРІРµРЅРЅС‹Р№ вЂ” РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµРј

        // рџ”§ РђРќРўР-Р‘РђРќ v2: РґРёРЅР°РјРёС‡РµСЃРєР°СЏ РїР°СѓР·Р°
        let mut pause_secs: u64 = 0;
        let mut consec_fails: u64 = 0;
        // рџ”§ РўР•Р›Р•РњР•РўР РРЇ: РјРѕРјРµРЅС‚ РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРµРіРѕ СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕРіРѕ REST (0 = РµС‰С‘ РЅРµ Р±С‹Р»Рѕ)
        let mut last_ok_ms: i64 = 0;

        loop {
            poll.tick().await;

            // рџ”§ РђРќРўР-Р‘РђРќ: РїР°СѓР·Р° РїРѕСЃР»Рµ Р±Р°РЅР°/СЃРµСЂРёРё С„РµР№Р»РѕРІ
            if pause_secs > 0 {
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(pause_secs)).await;
            }

            // вњ… B2: РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ вЂ” non-critical: РїСЂРё РїРµСЂРµРіСЂСѓР·РєРµ РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµРј С‚РёРє
            if crate::utils::rate_limiter::LIMITER.acquire(false).await
                == crate::utils::rate_limiter::Acquire::Throttle
            {
                continue;
            }

            let limit = if backfilled { 2 } else { 250 };
            let url = format!(
                "{}/fapi/v1/klines?symbol={}&interval={}&limit={}",
                base,
                symbol.to_uppercase(),
                interval,
                limit
            );

            // рџ›ЎпёЏ RESILIENT: 3 РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєРё СЃ backoff 500РјСЃ+1СЃ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё РѕРґРЅРѕР№
            // poll-РёС‚РµСЂР°С†РёРё. Р›РѕРІРёС‚ С‚СЂР°РЅР·РёРµРЅС‚РЅС‹Рµ TCP-РѕР±СЂС‹РІС‹, РєРѕС‚РѕСЂС‹Рµ testnet
            // РІС‹РґР°С‘С‚ РєР°Р¶РґС‹Рµ ~50СЃ (os error 10054). РџРѕСЃР»Рµ 3 РїСЂРѕРІР°Р»РѕРІ вЂ”
            // РґРµР»РµРіРёСЂСѓРµРј РІРѕ РІРЅРµС€РЅСЋСЋ pause_secs Р»РѕРіРёРєСѓ (IP-Р±Р°РЅ / Р·Р°С‚СЏР¶РЅРѕР№ СЃР±РѕР№).
            let v_opt: Option<serde_json::Value> = {
                let mut attempts: u32 = 0;
                loop {
                    attempts += 1;
                    let resp_result = client.get(&url).send().await;
                    match resp_result {
                        Ok(r) => match read_body_json(r).await {
                            Ok(v) => break Some(v),
                            Err(e) if attempts < INNER_ATTEMPTS => {
                                tracing::warn!(
                                    "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ: JSON РЅРµ СЂР°Р·РѕР±СЂР°РЅ, РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєР° {}/{}: {}",
                                    symbol, attempts, INNER_ATTEMPTS, e
                                );
                                let backoff = INNER_BACKOFF_MS
                                    [(attempts - 1) as usize % INNER_BACKOFF_MS.len()];
                                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(backoff)).await;
                                continue;
                            }
                            Err(e) => {
                                consec_fails += 1;
                                pause_secs = (10u64.saturating_mul(consec_fails.min(6))).min(60);
                                tracing::warn!(
                                    "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ: РѕС‚РІРµС‚ РЅРµ СЂР°Р·РѕР±СЂР°РЅ \
                                     (С„РµР№Р» {}, РїР°СѓР·Р° {}СЃ, REST-С‚РёС€РёРЅР° {}СЃ): {}",
                                    symbol, consec_fails, pause_secs,
                                    rest_silence(last_ok_ms), e
                                );
                                break None;
                            }
                        },
                        Err(e) if attempts < INNER_ATTEMPTS => {
                            tracing::warn!(
                                "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ: Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ РЅРµ РїСЂРѕС€С‘Р», РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєР° {}/{}: {}",
                                symbol, attempts, INNER_ATTEMPTS, e
                            );
                            let backoff = INNER_BACKOFF_MS
                                [(attempts - 1) as usize % INNER_BACKOFF_MS.len()];
                            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(backoff)).await;
                            continue;
                        }
                        Err(e) => {
                            consec_fails += 1;
                            pause_secs = (10u64.saturating_mul(consec_fails.min(6))).min(60);
                            tracing::warn!(
                                "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ: Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ РЅРµ РїСЂРѕС€С‘Р» \
                                 (С„РµР№Р» {}, РїР°СѓР·Р° {}СЃ, REST-С‚РёС€РёРЅР° {}СЃ): {}",
                                symbol, consec_fails, pause_secs,
                                rest_silence(last_ok_ms), e
                            );
                            break None;
                        }
                    }
                }
            };

            // рџ›ЎпёЏ Р’СЃРµ 3 РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРµРЅРЅРёРµ РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєРё РїСЂРѕРІР°Р»РёР»РёСЃСЊ вЂ” РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµРј РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєСѓ,
            // РІРЅРµС€РЅСЏСЏ pause_secs СЃСЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ РЅР° СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РµРј С‚РёРєРµ.
            let Some(v) = v_opt else { continue };

            // рџ”§ РђРќРўР-Р‘РђРќ: -1003 = IP ban вЂ” РїР°СѓР·Р° 120СЃ, Р±Р°РЅ РЅРµ РїСЂРѕРґР»РµРІР°РµРј
            if v["code"].as_i64() == Some(-1003) {
                pause_secs = 120;
                consec_fails += 1;
                tracing::error!(
                    "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ: рџљЁ IP BANNED (-1003): {} вЂ” РїР°СѓР·Р° 120СЃ",
                    symbol,
                    v["msg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")
                );
                continue;
            }
            // РџСЂРѕС‡РёРµ РѕС€РёР±РєРё API (РЅРµ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ)
            if v.as_array().is_none() {
                consec_fails += 1;
                pause_secs = (10u64.saturating_mul(consec_fails.min(6))).min(60);
                tracing::warn!(
                    "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ: РѕС‚РІРµС‚ РЅРµ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ \
                     (С„РµР№Р» {}, РїР°СѓР·Р° {}СЃ, REST-С‚РёС€РёРЅР° {}СЃ): {}",
                    symbol, consec_fails, pause_secs,
                    rest_silence(last_ok_ms), v
                );
                continue;
            }

            // РЈСЃРїРµС…: СЃР±СЂРѕСЃ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРєРѕРІ
            consec_fails = 0;
            pause_secs = 0;
            last_ok_ms = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis();

            let rows = v.as_array().unwrap();

            for row in rows {
                let Some(open_time) = row[0].as_i64() else { continue };
                let Some(close_time) = row[6].as_i64() else { continue };
                if close_time >= chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis() {
                    continue; // РµС‰С‘ С„РѕСЂРјРёСЂСѓРµС‚СЃСЏ
                }
                if open_time <= last_open_time {
                    continue; // СѓР¶Рµ РїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°Р»Рё
                }
                let p = |i: usize| row[i].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                last_open_time = open_time;

                let close = p(4);
                hub.publish(WebSocketMessage::Kline(KlineMessage {
                    symbol: symbol.to_uppercase(),
                    open: p(1),
                    high: p(2),
                    low: p(3),
                    close,
                    volume: p(5),
                    taker_buy_volume: p(9),
                    timestamp: close_time,
                    is_closed: true,
                    is_backfill: !backfilled,
                }));
                if backfilled {
                    tracing::info!(
                        "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ: СЃРІРµС‡Р° РѕРїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°РЅР° (open_time={}, close={})",
                        symbol, open_time, close
                    );
                }
            }

            if !rows.is_empty() {
                if !backfilled {
                    tracing::info!(
                        "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ: backfill Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€С‘РЅ ({} СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёРё)",
                        symbol, rows.len()
                    );
                }
                backfilled = true;
            }
        }
    });
}

/// рџ”Ќ V3.2: С‚РµР»Рѕ РѕС‚РІРµС‚Р° РєР°Рє С‚РµРєСЃС‚ + СЂСѓС‡РЅРѕР№ РїР°СЂСЃРёРЅРі. РџСЂРё РѕС€РёР±РєРµ РІ СЃРѕРѕР±С‰РµРЅРёРµ
/// РїРѕРїР°РґР°РµС‚ HTTP-СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ Рё РїРµСЂРІС‹Рµ 160 СЃРёРјРІРѕР»РѕРІ С‚РµР»Р° вЂ” СЃСЂР°Р·Сѓ РІРёРґРЅРѕ, Р§РўРћ
/// РІРµСЂРЅСѓР» С‚РµСЃС‚РЅРµС‚ (HTML-СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р°, JSON-РѕС€РёР±РєР°, РїСѓСЃС‚РѕС‚Р°) РІРјРµСЃС‚Рѕ РіРѕР»РѕРіРѕ
/// "error decoding response body".
async fn read_body_json(r: reqwest::Response) -> Result<serde_json::Value, String> {
    let status = r.status();
    let body = match r.text().await {
        Ok(b) => b,
        Err(e) => return Err(format!("HTTP {} | С‚РµР»Рѕ РЅРµ РїСЂРѕС‡РёС‚Р°РЅРѕ: {}", status, e)),
    };
    match serde_json::from_str::<serde_json::Value>(&body) {
        Ok(v) => Ok(v),
        Err(e) => Err(format!(
            "HTTP {} | С‚РµР»Рѕ[:160]: {:?} | serde: {}",
            status,
            body.chars().take(160).collect::<String>(),
            e
        )),
    }
}

/// рџ†• Phase B: Bybit V5 kline poller.
///
/// РћС‚Р»РёС‡РёСЏ РѕС‚ Binance:
///   1. URL: /v5/market/kline?category=linear&symbol=XAUUSDT&interval=60&limit=2
///   2. interval = "60" (С‡РёСЃР»Рѕ РјРёРЅСѓС‚), РЅРµ "1h" РєР°Рє Сѓ Binance
///   3. Response: result.list вЂ” array of arrays, РќРћ Р Р•Р’Р•Р РЎРќРЈРўР«Р™ (РЅРѕРІРµР№С€РёРµ РїРµСЂРІС‹РјРё!)
///      Format: [start, open, high, low, close, volume, turnover, ...]
///      Р’СЂРµРјСЏ вЂ” РІ РЎР•РљРЈРќР”РђРҐ (РЅРµ РјРёР»Р»РёСЃРµРєСѓРЅРґР°С… РєР°Рє Сѓ Binance!)
///   4. confirm=true вЂ” СЃРІРµС‡Р° Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚Р°
pub fn spawn_bybit(
    children: &mut tokio::task::JoinSet<()>,
    hub: Arc<WebSocketHub>,
    rest_base: String,
    symbol: String,
    interval: String,
) {
    children.spawn(async move {
        let client = reqwest::Client::builder()
            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(10))
            .pool_idle_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(20)).tcp_keepalive(std::time::Duration::from_secs(15))/* P19-POOL */.build()
            .unwrap_or_default();
        let base = rest_base.trim_end_matches('/').to_string();

        // РљРѕРЅРІРµСЂС‚Р°С†РёСЏ interval СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё РІ Bybit-С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚:
        //   "1h" в†’ "60", "4h" в†’ "240", "15m" в†’ "15", "1d" в†’ "D", "1w" в†’ "W"
        let bybit_interval = match interval.as_str() {
            "1m" => "1".to_string(),
            "5m" => "5".to_string(),
            "15m" => "15".to_string(),
            "30m" => "30".to_string(),
            "1h" => "60".to_string(),
            "4h" => "240".to_string(),
            "1d" => "D".to_string(),
            "1w" => "W".to_string(),
            "1M" => "M".to_string(),
            other => other.to_string(),
        };

        // рџ”§ FIX BUG#1: РґР»РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р»Р° РІ РјСЃ вЂ” РґР»СЏ С‡РµСЃС‚РЅРѕРіРѕ close_time.
        // Р Р°РЅСЊС€Рµ: close_time_ms = open_time_ms + 3600_000 (С…Р°СЂРґРєРѕРґ 1h!),
        // Р° gold_dca СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ РЅР° 4h в†’ РЅРµР·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚С‹Рµ 4h-СЃРІРµС‡Рё РїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°Р»РёСЃСЊ
        // С‡РµСЂРµР· С‡Р°СЃ РєР°Рє is_closed=true, СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ С‚РµСЂСЏР»РѕСЃСЊ.
        let bybit_interval_ms: i64 = match bybit_interval.as_str() {
            "1" => 60_000,
            "5" => 300_000,
            "15" => 900_000,
            "30" => 1_800_000,
            "60" => 3_600_000,
            "120" => 7_200_000,
            "240" => 14_400_000,
            "360" => 21_600_000,
            "480" => 28_800_000,
            "720" => 43_200_000,
            "D" => 86_400_000,
            "W" => 604_800_000,
            // РћС†РµРЅРєР° 31 РґРµРЅСЊ: РµСЃР»Рё С‡СѓС‚СЊ РЅРµРґРѕР·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚Р° вЂ” РѕРїСѓР±Р»РёРєСѓРµС‚СЃСЏ РЅР°
            // СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РµРј С†РёРєР»Рµ (10СЃ), Р±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРЅРѕ РІ РѕР±Рµ СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅС‹.
            "M" => 2_678_400_000,
            _ => 3_600_000,
        };

        let mut last_open_time: i64 = 0;
        let mut backfilled = false;
        let mut poll = tokio::time::interval(Duration::from_secs(10));
        poll.tick().await;

        let mut pause_secs: u64 = 0;
        let mut consec_fails: u64 = 0;

        loop {
            poll.tick().await;

            if pause_secs > 0 {
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(pause_secs)).await;
            }

            if crate::utils::rate_limiter::LIMITER.acquire(false).await
                == crate::utils::rate_limiter::Acquire::Throttle
            {
                continue;
            }

            let limit = if backfilled { 2 } else { 250 };
            let url = format!(
                "{}/v5/market/kline?category=linear&symbol={}&interval={}&limit={}",
                base,
                symbol.to_uppercase(),
                bybit_interval,
                limit
            );

            let resp = match client.get(&url).send().await {
                Ok(r) => r,
                Err(e) => {
                    consec_fails += 1;
                    pause_secs = (10u64.saturating_mul(consec_fails.min(6))).min(60);
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ (Bybit): Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ РЅРµ РїСЂРѕС€С‘Р» (С„РµР№Р» {}, РїР°СѓР·Р° {}СЃ): {}",
                        symbol, consec_fails, pause_secs, e
                    );
                    continue;
                }
            };

            // рџ”§ FIX BUG#1: СЃРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° РўР•РљРЎРў Рё РґРµС‚РµРєС‚ HTML вЂ” РіРµРѕР±Р»РѕРє Bybit СЃ RU-IP
            // РѕС‚РґР°С‘С‚ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Сѓ РІРјРµСЃС‚Рѕ JSON. Р Р°РЅСЊС€Рµ resp.json() РїР°РґР°Р» СЃ
            // "error decoding response body" Р±РµР· РґРёР°РіРЅРѕСЃС‚РёРєРё, РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ РєСЂСѓС‚РёР»СЃСЏ
            // 48С‡ РІРїСѓСЃС‚СѓСЋ Рё РЅРё СЂР°Р·Сѓ РЅРµ РїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°Р» СЃРІРµС‡Сѓ (= candles=0).
            let body = match resp.text().await {
                Ok(b) => b,
                Err(e) => {
                    consec_fails += 1;
                    pause_secs = (10u64.saturating_mul(consec_fails.min(6))).min(60);
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ (Bybit): С‚РµР»Рѕ РЅРµ РїСЂРѕС‡РёС‚Р°РЅРѕ \
                         (С„РµР№Р» {}, РїР°СѓР·Р° {}СЃ): {}",
                        symbol, consec_fails, pause_secs, e
                    );
                    continue;
                }
            };
            if looks_like_html(None, &body) {
                // HTML РЅРµ СЃС‚Р°РЅРµС‚ JSON РѕС‚ СЂРµС‚СЂР°РµРІ вЂ” РґР»РёРЅРЅР°СЏ РїР°СѓР·Р°, РІРЅСЏС‚РЅС‹Р№ Р»РѕРі
                consec_fails += 1;
                pause_secs = GEOBLOCK_PAUSE_SECS;
                tracing::error!(
                    "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ (Bybit): рџљЁ GEOBLOCK вЂ” HTML РІРјРµСЃС‚Рѕ JSON \
                     (С„РµР№Р» {}, РїР°СѓР·Р° {}СЃ): {}",
                    symbol, consec_fails, pause_secs, body_head(&body)
                );
                continue;
            }
            let v: serde_json::Value = match serde_json::from_str(&body) {
                Ok(v) => v,
                Err(e) => {
                    consec_fails += 1;
                    pause_secs = (10u64.saturating_mul(consec_fails.min(6))).min(60);
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ (Bybit): РѕС‚РІРµС‚ РЅРµ СЂР°Р·РѕР±СЂР°РЅ \
                         (С„РµР№Р» {}, РїР°СѓР·Р° {}СЃ): {} | С‚РµР»Рѕ: {}",
                        symbol, consec_fails, pause_secs, e, body_head(&body)
                    );
                    continue;
                }
            };

            // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј Bybit V5 retCode
            let ret_code = v["retCode"].as_i64().unwrap_or(-1);
            if ret_code != 0 {
                consec_fails += 1;
                pause_secs = (10u64.saturating_mul(consec_fails.min(6))).min(60);
                tracing::warn!(
                    "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ (Bybit): retCode={} msg={}",
                    symbol, ret_code,
                    v["retMsg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")
                );
                continue;
            }

            let rows = match v["result"]["list"].as_array() {
                Some(a) => a,
                None => {
                    consec_fails += 1;
                    pause_secs = (10u64.saturating_mul(consec_fails.min(6))).min(60);
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ (Bybit): result.list РЅРµ РјР°СЃСЃРёРІ: {}",
                        symbol, v
                    );
                    continue;
                }
            };

            // РЈСЃРїРµС…
            consec_fails = 0;
            pause_secs = 0;

            // Bybit РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ СЃРІРµС‡Рё Р’ РћР‘Р РђРўРќРћРњ РџРћР РЇР”РљР• (РЅРѕРІРµР№С€РёРµ РїРµСЂРІС‹РјРё).
            // Р Р°Р·РІРѕСЂР°С‡РёРІР°РµРј РґР»СЏ РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРѕРІР°С‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕР№ РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєРё.
            let mut sorted_rows: Vec<&serde_json::Value> = rows.iter().collect();
            sorted_rows.reverse();

            for row in sorted_rows {
                // Bybit kline row format:
                // [start (sec), open, high, low, close, volume, turnover]
                // 0     1     2     3    4      5       6
                let Some(start_str) = row[0].as_str() else { continue };
                let Ok(start_raw) = start_str.parse::<i64>() else { continue };
                let open_time_ms = if start_raw > 100_000_000_000 { start_raw } else { start_raw * 1000 }; // v3.6 FIX: Bybit TESTNET gives ms (13 digits), MAINNET gives sec - autodetect // sec в†’ ms
                // рџ”§ FIX BUG#1: Р±С‹Р»Рѕ `+ 3600_000` (С…Р°СЂРґРєРѕРґ 1h вЂ” Р±РёС‚С‹Рµ 4h-СЃРІРµС‡Рё!)
                let close_time_ms = open_time_ms + bybit_interval_ms;

                // РџСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµРј РЅРµР·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚С‹Рµ СЃРІРµС‡Рё
                if close_time_ms >= chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis() {
                    continue;
                }
                if open_time_ms <= last_open_time {
                    continue;
                }

                let p = |i: usize| row[i].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                last_open_time = open_time_ms;

                let close = p(4);
                hub.publish(WebSocketMessage::Kline(KlineMessage {
                    symbol: symbol.to_uppercase(),
                    open: p(1),
                    high: p(2),
                    low: p(3),
                    close,
                    volume: p(5),
                    taker_buy_volume: 0.0, // Bybit РЅРµ РѕС‚РґР°С‘С‚ taker_buy РІ СЌС‚РѕРј endpoint
                    timestamp: close_time_ms,
                    is_closed: true,
                    is_backfill: !backfilled,
                }));
                if backfilled {
                    tracing::info!(
                        "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ (Bybit): СЃРІРµС‡Р° РѕРїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°РЅР° (open_time={}, close={})",
                        symbol, open_time_ms, close
                    );
                }
            }

            if !rows.is_empty() {
                if !backfilled {
                    tracing::info!(
                        "рџ“€ [{}] kline-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ (Bybit): backfill Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€С‘РЅ ({} СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёРё)",
                        symbol, rows.len()
                    );
                }
                backfilled = true;
            }
        }
    });
}

/// рџ”§ FIX: РґРµС‚РµРєС‚ HTML-РѕС‚РІРµС‚Р° (РіРµРѕР±Р»РѕРє-СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° Bybit) Р”Рћ РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєРё JSON-РїР°СЂСЃРёРЅРіР°
fn looks_like_html(content_type: Option<&str>, body: &str) -> bool {
    if let Some(ct) = content_type {
        if ct.to_ascii_lowercase().contains("text/html") {
            return true;
        }
    }
    let head = body.trim_start().to_ascii_lowercase();
    head.starts_with("<!doctype html") || head.starts_with("<html")
}

/// рџ”§ FIX: РіРѕР»РѕРІР° С‚РµР»Р° РґР»СЏ РґРёР°РіРЅРѕСЃС‚РёРєРё (РїРµСЂРІС‹Рµ 160 СЃРёРјРІРѕР»РѕРІ, Р±РµР· РїРµСЂРµРЅРѕСЃРѕРІ)
fn body_head(body: &str) -> String {
    body.chars()
        .take(160)
        .map(|c| if c.is_control() { ' ' } else { c })
        .collect()
}

/// РЎРµРєСѓРЅРґС‹ СЃ РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРµРіРѕ СѓСЃРїРµС€РЅРѕРіРѕ REST (РґР»СЏ СЃС‚СЂРѕРє РґРёР°РіРЅРѕСЃС‚РёРєРё)
fn rest_silence(last_ok_ms: i64) -> i64 {
    if last_ok_ms == 0 {
        return -1; // РµС‰С‘ РЅРё РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ СѓСЃРїРµС…Р°
    }
    chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis() / 1000 - last_ok_ms / 1000
}

/// v4.0: OKX V5 kline poller (public /api/v5/market/candles).
/// bar: 1m/3m/5m/15m/30m/1H/2H/4H/6H/12H. Rows NEWEST-first:
/// [ts, o, h, l, c, vol_contracts, vol_base, vol_quote, confirm]
pub fn spawn_okx(
    children: &mut tokio::task::JoinSet<()>,
    hub: Arc<WebSocketHub>,
    rest_base: String,
    symbol: String,
    interval: String,
) {
    children.spawn(async move {
        let client = reqwest::Client::builder()
            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(10))
            .pool_idle_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(20)).tcp_keepalive(std::time::Duration::from_secs(15))/* P19-POOL */.build()
            .unwrap_or_default();
        let base = rest_base.trim_end_matches('/').to_string();

        let bar = match interval.as_str() {
            "1h" => "1H".to_string(),
            "2h" => "2H".to_string(),
            "4h" => "4H".to_string(),
            "6h" => "6H".to_string(),
            "12h" => "12H".to_string(),
            other => other.to_string(),
        };
        let interval_ms: i64 = match bar.as_str() {
            "1m" => 60_000,
            "3m" => 180_000,
            "5m" => 300_000,
            "15m" => 900_000,
            "30m" => 1_800_000,
            "1H" => 3_600_000,
            "2H" => 7_200_000,
            "4H" => 14_400_000,
            "6H" => 21_600_000,
            "12H" => 43_200_000,
            _ => 3_600_000,
        };

        let inst_id = okx_inst_id_from_symbol(&symbol);

        let mut last_open_time: i64 = 0;
        let mut backfilled = false;
        let mut consec_fails: u64 = 0;
        let mut poll = tokio::time::interval(Duration::from_secs(10));
        poll.tick().await;

        loop {
            poll.tick().await;

            let limit = if backfilled { 3 } else { 250 };
            let url = format!(
                "{}/api/v5/market/candles?instId={}&bar={}&limit={}",
                base, inst_id, bar, limit
            );

            let body = match client.get(&url).send().await {
                Ok(r) => match r.text().await {
                    Ok(b) => b,
                    Err(e) => {
                        consec_fails += 1;
                        let pause = (10u64 * consec_fails.min(6)).min(60);
                        tracing::warn!(
                            "[{}] kline-poller (OKX): body read failed (fail {}, pause {}s): {}",
                            symbol, consec_fails, pause, e
                        );
                        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(pause)).await;
                        continue;
                    }
                },
                Err(e) => {
                    consec_fails += 1;
                    let pause = (10u64 * consec_fails.min(6)).min(60);
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "[{}] kline-poller (OKX): request failed (fail {}, pause {}s): {}",
                        symbol, consec_fails, pause, e
                    );
                    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(pause)).await;
                    continue;
                }
            };

            if body.trim_start().starts_with('<') {
                tracing::error!(
                    "[{}] kline-poller (OKX): GEOBLOCK/HTML instead of JSON: {}",
                    symbol,
                    body.chars().take(120).collect::<String>()
                );
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(300)).await;
                continue;
            }

            let v: serde_json::Value = match serde_json::from_str(&body) {
                Ok(v) => v,
                Err(e) => {
                    consec_fails += 1;
                    let pause = (10u64 * consec_fails.min(6)).min(60);
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "[{}] kline-poller (OKX): parse failed (fail {}, pause {}s): {} | body: {}",
                        symbol,
                        consec_fails,
                        pause,
                        e,
                        body.chars().take(120).collect::<String>()
                    );
                    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(pause)).await;
                    continue;
                }
            };

            if v["code"].as_str().unwrap_or("-1") != "0" {
                consec_fails += 1;
                tracing::warn!(
                    "[{}] kline-poller (OKX): code={} msg={}",
                    symbol,
                    v["code"].as_str().unwrap_or("?"),
                    v["msg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")
                );
                tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(10)).await;
                continue;
            }

            let rows = match v["data"].as_array() {
                Some(a) => a,
                None => {
                    consec_fails += 1;
                    tracing::warn!("[{}] kline-poller (OKX): data is not an array", symbol);
                    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(10)).await;
                    continue;
                }
            };

            consec_fails = 0;

            let mut sorted_rows: Vec<&serde_json::Value> = rows.iter().collect();
            sorted_rows.reverse(); // oldest first

            for row in sorted_rows {
                let Some(open_ms) = row[0].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse::<i64>().ok()) else {
                    continue;
                };
                let close_ms = open_ms + interval_ms;
                if close_ms >= chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis() {
                    continue; // candle still forming
                }
                if open_ms <= last_open_time {
                    continue; // already published
                }
                let p = |i: usize| row[i].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                last_open_time = open_ms;

                hub.publish(WebSocketMessage::Kline(KlineMessage {
                    symbol: symbol.to_uppercase(),
                    open: p(1),
                    high: p(2),
                    low: p(3),
                    close: p(4),
                    volume: p(6), // volCcy = base-asset volume (Binance-like)
                    taker_buy_volume: 0.0,
                    timestamp: close_ms,
                    is_closed: true,
                    is_backfill: !backfilled,
                }));
            }

            if !rows.is_empty() {
                if !backfilled {
                    tracing::info!(
                        "[{}] kline-poller (OKX): backfill done ({} candles)",
                        symbol,
                        rows.len()
                    );
                }
                backfilled = true;
            }
        }
    });
}

/// v4.0: "XAUUSDT" -> "XAU-USDT-SWAP"
fn okx_inst_id_from_symbol(symbol: &str) -> String {
    let s = symbol.trim().to_uppercase();
    if s.contains('-') {
        if s.ends_with("-SWAP") { s } else { format!("{}-SWAP", s) }
    } else if let Some(base) = s.strip_suffix("USDT") {
        format!("{}-USDT-SWAP", base)
    } else {
        s
    }
}

// вњ… P25: С‚РёРїРёР·РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅР°СЏ Р±РёСЂР¶Р° РІРјРµСЃС‚Рѕ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРѕРІС‹С… РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРѕРє URL
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// Р›РёС‡РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ Р±РёСЂР¶Рё СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ РѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»СЏР»Р°СЃСЊ СЂРѕСЃСЃС‹РїСЊСЋ contains-РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРѕРє РїРѕ URL
// Рё СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРѕРІС‹Рј match РїРѕ global.exchange РІ 4 С„Р°Р№Р»Р°С… (main.rs,
// websocket/mod.rs, exchange/mod.rs, funding.rs). РўРµРїРµСЂСЊ РѕРґРёРЅ С‚РёРї Рё
// РѕРґРЅР° С‚РѕС‡РєР° РѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅРёСЏ. РџРѕРІРµРґРµРЅРёРµ 1:1: РїСЂРёРѕСЂРёС‚РµС‚ URL (bybit, Р·Р°С‚РµРј
// okx), Р·Р°С‚РµРј global.exchange ("binance"/"bybit"/"okx"/"okx-demo");
// РЅРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅР°СЏ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєР° -> None (create_client РѕС‚РІРµС‡Р°РµС‚ РїСЂРµР¶РЅРµР№ РѕС€РёР±РєРѕР№
// В«РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅР°СЏ Р±РёСЂР¶Р°В»), from_url РґР»СЏ WS/REST-С…Р°Р±РѕРІ РїРѕ СѓРјРѕР»С‡Р°РЅРёСЋ
// Binance вЂ” РєР°Рє РїСЂРµР¶РЅРёРµ else-РІРµС‚РєРё is_bybit=false/is_okx=false.
// ============================================================
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub enum Exchange {
    Binance,
    Bybit,
    Okx,
}

impl Exchange {
    /// РљР°РЅРѕРЅРёС‡РµСЃРєРѕРµ РЅРёР¶РЅРµСЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂРѕРІРѕРµ РёРјСЏ (Р»РѕРіРё, РјРµС‚СЂРёРєРё Prometheus).
    pub fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Exchange::Binance => "binance",
            Exchange::Bybit => "bybit",
            Exchange::Okx => "okx",
        }
    }

    /// РћРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅРёРµ Р±РёСЂР¶Рё РїРѕ URL + СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРµ global.exchange РёР· РєРѕРЅС„РёРіР°.
    /// None = РЅРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅР°СЏ Р±РёСЂР¶Р° (РїСЂРµР¶РґРµ вЂ” Err В«РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅР°СЏ Р±РёСЂР¶Р°: вЂ¦В»).
    pub fn detect(url: &str, global_exchange: &str) -> Option<Exchange> {
        let u = url.to_lowercase();
        if u.contains("bybit") {
            return Some(Exchange::Bybit);
        }
        if u.contains("okx") {
            return Some(Exchange::Okx);
        }
        match global_exchange.trim().to_lowercase().as_str() {
            "binance" => Some(Exchange::Binance),
            "bybit" => Some(Exchange::Bybit),
            "okx" | "okx-demo" => Some(Exchange::Okx),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    /// РћРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅРёРµ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїРѕ URL (WS-С…Р°Р±С‹, РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂС‹, FundingTracker).
    /// РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅРѕ -> Binance: СЂРѕРІРЅРѕ РєР°Рє РїСЂРµР¶РЅРёРµ РІРµС‚РєРё
    /// is_bybit=false / is_okx=false / else-Р±РёРЅС‹ Binance.
    pub fn from_url(url: &str) -> Exchange {
        Self::detect(url, "").unwrap_or(Exchange::Binance)
    }
}

pub fn create_time_sync() -> SharedTimeSync {
    Arc::new(TimeSync::new())
}

pub fn create_client(
    global: &GlobalConfig,
    config: &BotConfig,
    time_sync: SharedTimeSync,
) -> Result<Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>> {
    // вњ… РџСЂРёРѕСЂРёС‚РµС‚: rest_url_override РёР· BotConfig в†’ РіР»РѕР±Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№
    let rest_url = config
        .rest_url_override
        .as_ref()
        .unwrap_or(&global.rest_url);

    let is_futures = config.market_type == "futures"
        || rest_url.contains("futures")
        || rest_url.contains("binancefuture")
        || rest_url.contains("bybit");

    tracing::info!(
        "[{}] РЎРѕР·РґР°СЋ РєР»РёРµРЅС‚ | market={} | rest_url={}",
        config.name,
        if is_futures { "FUTURES" } else { "SPOT" },
        rest_url
    );

    // вњ… P25: С‚РёРїРёР·РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅР°СЏ Р±РёСЂР¶Р° РІРјРµСЃС‚Рѕ СЃС‚СЂРѕРє exchange_detected/match.
    // РћС€РёР±РєР° В«РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅР°СЏ Р±РёСЂР¶Р°В» вЂ” СЃ С‚РѕР№ Р¶Рµ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРѕР№, С‡С‚Рѕ РїСЂРµР¶РґРµ (global.exchange РІ РЅРёР¶РЅРµРј СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂРµ).
    let ex = Exchange::detect(rest_url, &global.exchange)
        .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("РќРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РЅР°СЏ Р±РёСЂР¶Р°: {}", global.exchange.to_lowercase()))?;
    // v4.0: demo/prod OKX СЂРµС€Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРѕР№ global.exchange ("okx-demo" vs "okx")
    let okx_demo = global.exchange.to_lowercase() == "okx-demo";

    match ex {
        // === v4.0: OKX V5 (demo when global.exchange = "okx-demo") ===
        Exchange::Okx => {
            let passphrase = config.passphrase.as_deref().ok_or_else(|| {
                anyhow::anyhow!("[{}] OKX: passphrase not set (bots.{}.passphrase)", config.name, config.name)
            })?;
            tracing::info!("[{}] OKX client | demo={} | rest_url={}", config.name, okx_demo, rest_url);
            Ok(Arc::new(okx_futures::OkxFuturesClient::new(
                &config.api_key,
                &config.api_secret,
                passphrase,
                rest_url,
                okx_demo,
                time_sync,
            )) as Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>)
        }

        // === Binance ===
        Exchange::Binance => {
            if is_futures {
                Ok(Arc::new(binance_futures::BinanceFuturesClient::new(
                    &config.api_key,
                    &config.api_secret,
                    rest_url,
                    time_sync,
                )) as Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>)
            } else {
                Ok(Arc::new(binance_rest::BinanceRestClient::new(
                    &config.api_key,
                    &config.api_secret,
                    rest_url,
                    time_sync,
                )) as Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>)
            }
        }

        // === рџ†• Phase B: Bybit V5 ===
        Exchange::Bybit => {
            // Bybit РЅРµ СЂР°Р·РґРµР»СЏРµС‚ spot/futures РІ API СЃС‚СЂСѓРєС‚СѓСЂРµ РєР°Рє Binance вЂ”
            // РґР»СЏ USDT perp (XAUUSDT, BTCUSDT) РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј BybitFuturesClient.
            // Spot СЂРµРґРєРёР№ СЃР»СѓС‡Р°Р№ вЂ” РїРѕРєР° РЅРµ РїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РёРІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ (bybit_spot.rs РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ РґРѕР±Р°РІРёС‚СЊ РїРѕР·Р¶Рµ).
            if is_futures {
                Ok(Arc::new(bybit_futures::BybitFuturesClient::new(
                    &config.api_key,
                    &config.api_secret,
                    rest_url,
                    time_sync,
                )) as Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>)
            } else {
                // Р•СЃР»Рё РІ config market_type != "futures" РЅРѕ exchange=bybit вЂ” РїРѕРєР° fallback РЅР° futures
                tracing::warn!(
                    "[{}] Bybit SPOT РїРѕРєР° РЅРµ СЂРµР°Р»РёР·РѕРІР°РЅ вЂ” РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓСЋ Futures РєР»РёРµРЅС‚ (РґР»СЏ perp СЃРёРјРІРѕР»РѕРІ СЌС‚Рѕ OK)",
                    config.name
                );
                Ok(Arc::new(bybit_futures::BybitFuturesClient::new(
                    &config.api_key,
                    &config.api_secret,
                    rest_url,
                    time_sync,
                )) as Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>)
            }
        }
    }
}
=== C:\Users\BASE_PC\scalping_agentsGLM\src\exchange\okx_futures.rs ===
//! okx_futures.rs - ExchangeClient for OKX V5 (USDT-margined linear SWAP).
//! Differences vs Bybit/Binance:
//!   1. OK-ACCESS-* headers; sign = base64(HMAC-SHA256(ts_iso + METHOD + path(+query) + body))
//!   2. ts = ISO8601 UTC string (not ms)
//!   3. passphrase required (3rd credential)
//!   4. sz in LOTS: lots = qty_base / ctVal (fetched from /public/instruments, cached)
//!   5. "XAUUSDT" -> instId "XAU-USDT-SWAP"; emitted symbols converted back
//!   6. envelope {"code":"0","data":[...]}, code is STRING
//!   7. demo mode: header x-simulated-trading: 1 on PRIVATE endpoints (same hosts)

use crate::exchange::okx_sign;
use crate::exchange::time_sync::TimeSync;
use crate::exchange::traits::{ExchangeClient, ExchangePosition, OrderResult};
use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};
use async_trait::async_trait;
use reqwest::Client;
use serde_json::Value;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::Duration;

/// "XAUUSDT" -> "XAU-USDT-SWAP"; already-hyphened input passes through.
fn to_inst_id(symbol: &str) -> String {
    let s = symbol.trim().to_uppercase();
    if s.contains('-') {
        if s.ends_with("-SWAP") { s } else { format!("{}-SWAP", s) }
    } else if let Some(base) = s.strip_suffix("USDT") {
        format!("{}-USDT-SWAP", base)
    } else {
        s
    }
}

/// "XAU-USDT-SWAP" -> "XAUUSDT" (engine-side canonical symbol).
fn from_inst_id(inst_id: &str) -> String {
    let s = inst_id.trim().to_uppercase();
    if let Some(base) = s.strip_suffix("-USDT-SWAP") {
        format!("{}USDT", base)
    } else if let Some(base) = s.strip_suffix("-SWAP") {
        base.to_string()
    } else {
        s
    }
}

pub struct OkxFuturesClient {
    api_key: String,
    api_secret: String,
    passphrase: String,
    demo: bool,
    client: Client,
    base_url: String,
    time_sync: Arc<TimeSync>,
    /// instId -> (ctVal, lotSz, minSz, tickSz), lazily fetched
    contracts: Mutex<HashMap<String, (f64, f64, f64, f64)>>,
}

impl OkxFuturesClient {
    pub fn new(
        api_key: &str,
        api_secret: &str,
        passphrase: &str,
        base_url: &str,
        demo: bool,
        time_sync: Arc<TimeSync>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            api_key: api_key.to_string(),
            api_secret: api_secret.to_string(),
            passphrase: passphrase.to_string(),
            demo,
            client: Client::builder().timeout(Duration::from_secs(10)).pool_idle_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(20)).tcp_keepalive(std::time::Duration::from_secs(15))/* P19-POOL */.build().unwrap_or_default(),
            base_url: base_url.trim_end_matches('/').to_string(),
            time_sync,
            contracts: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()),
        }
    }

    async fn parse_envelope(resp: reqwest::Response) -> Result<Value> {
        let status = resp.status();
        let body = resp
            .text()
            .await
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("OKX HTTP {} | body read failed: {}", status, e))?;
        let head: String = body.chars().take(160).map(|c| if c.is_control() { ' ' } else { c }).collect();
        let lc = body.trim_start().to_ascii_lowercase();
        if lc.starts_with("<!doctype html") || lc.starts_with("<html") {
            return Err(anyhow!("OKX HTTP {} | GEOBLOCK/HTML instead of JSON: {}", status, head));
        }
        let json: Value = serde_json::from_str(&body)
            .map_err(|e| anyhow!("OKX HTTP {} | body[:160]: {} | serde: {}", status, head, e))?;
        let code = json["code"].as_str().unwrap_or("-1").to_string();
        if code != "0" {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "OKX V5 error: code={} msg={}",
                code,
                json["msg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")
            ));
        }
        Ok(json)
    }

    async fn public_get(&self, path: &str, query: &str) -> Result<Value> {
        let url = if query.is_empty() {
            format!("{}{}", self.base_url, path)
        } else {
            format!("{}{}?{}", self.base_url, path, query)
        };
        let resp = self.client.get(&url).send().await?;
        Self::parse_envelope(resp).await
    }

    async fn signed_get(&self, path: &str, query: &str) -> Result<Value> {
        let request_path = if query.is_empty() {
            path.to_string()
        } else {
            format!("{}?{}", path, query)
        };
        let url = format!("{}{}", self.base_url, request_path);
        let ts = okx_sign::iso_timestamp();
        let sign = okx_sign::sign(&self.api_secret, &ts, "GET", &request_path, "");
        let mut req = self
            .client
            .get(&url)
            .header("OK-ACCESS-KEY", &self.api_key)
            .header("OK-ACCESS-SIGN", sign)
            .header("OK-ACCESS-TIMESTAMP", ts)
            .header("OK-ACCESS-PASSPHRASE", &self.passphrase);
        if self.demo {
            req = req.header(okx_sign::DEMO_HEADER, "1");
        }
        let resp = req.send().await?;
        Self::parse_envelope(resp).await
    }

    async fn signed_post(&self, path: &str, body: &Value) -> Result<Value> {
        let body_str = serde_json::to_string(body)?;
        let ts = okx_sign::iso_timestamp();
        let sign = okx_sign::sign(&self.api_secret, &ts, "POST", path, &body_str);
        let mut req = self
            .client
            .post(&format!("{}{}", self.base_url, path))
            .header("OK-ACCESS-KEY", &self.api_key)
            .header("OK-ACCESS-SIGN", sign)
            .header("OK-ACCESS-TIMESTAMP", ts)
            .header("OK-ACCESS-PASSPHRASE", &self.passphrase)
            .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            .body(body_str);
        if self.demo {
            req = req.header(okx_sign::DEMO_HEADER, "1");
        }
        let resp = req.send().await?;
        Self::parse_envelope(resp).await
    }

    async fn ensure_contract(&self, inst_id: &str) -> Result<(f64, f64, f64, f64)> {
        if let Some(c) = self.contracts.lock().unwrap().get(inst_id) {
            return Ok(*c);
        }
        let json = self
            .public_get("/api/v5/public/instruments", &format!("instType=SWAP&instId={}", inst_id))
            .await?;
        let d = json["data"]
            .as_array()
            .and_then(|a| a.first())
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("OKX: instrument {} not found", inst_id))?;
        let ctval: f64 = d["ctVal"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
        let lotsz: f64 = d["lotSz"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(1.0);
        let minsz: f64 = d["minSz"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(1.0);
        let ticksz: f64 = d["tickSz"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.1);
        if ctval <= 0.0 {
            return Err(anyhow!("OKX: bad ctVal for {}", inst_id));
        }
        let c = (ctval, lotsz, minsz, ticksz);
        self.contracts.lock().unwrap().insert(inst_id.to_string(), c);
        tracing::info!("[okx] РєРѕРЅС‚СЂР°РєС‚ {}: ctVal={} lotSz={} minSz={} tickSz={}", inst_id, ctval, lotsz, minsz, ticksz);
        Ok(c)
    }

fn qty_to_lots(qty: f64, c: (f64, f64, f64, f64)) -> f64 {
    let (ctval, lotsz, minsz, _) = c;
    let lotsz = if lotsz > 0.0 { lotsz } else { 1.0 };
    let raw = qty / ctval;
    let mut lots = (raw / lotsz).round() * lotsz;
    if lots < minsz { lots = minsz; }
    lots
}

fn lots_str(lots: f64) -> String {
    let s = format!("{:.10}", lots);
    let s = s.trim_end_matches('0').trim_end_matches('.');
    if s.is_empty() { "0".to_string() } else { s.to_string() }
}

    fn px_str(p: f64, tick: f64) -> String {
        let t = if tick > 0.0 { tick } else { 0.1 };
        let v = (p / t).round() * t;
        let s = format!("{:.8}", v);
        s.trim_end_matches('0').trim_end_matches('.').to_string()
    }

    fn check_scode(result: &Value) -> Result<()> {
        let scode = result[0]["sCode"].as_str().unwrap_or("0");
        if scode != "0" {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "OKX order rejected: sCode={} sMsg={}",
                scode,
                result[0]["sMsg"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")
            ));
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn parse_order(o: &Value, ctval: f64, default_symbol: &str) -> OrderResult {
        let order_id = o["ordId"]
            .as_str()
            .or_else(|| o["algoId"].as_str())
            .and_then(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(0);
        let symbol = from_inst_id(o["instId"].as_str().unwrap_or(default_symbol));
        let side = match o["side"].as_str().unwrap_or("") {
            "buy" => "BUY".to_string(),
            "sell" => "SELL".to_string(),
            other => other.to_string(),
        };
        let price: f64 = ["avgPx", "fillPx", "px", "slTriggerPx", "tpTriggerPx"]
            .iter()
            .find_map(|k| o[*k].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok()))
            .unwrap_or(0.0);
        let sz: f64 = o["sz"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
        let acc: f64 = o["accFillSz"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
        let status = match o["state"].as_str().unwrap_or("live") {
            "live" => "NEW",
            "partially_filled" => "PARTIALLY_FILLED",
            "filled" => "FILLED",
            "canceled" | "cancelled" => "CANCELED",
            other => other,
        }
        .to_string();
        let commission = o["fee"]
            .as_str()
            .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
            .map(|f| f.abs())
            .unwrap_or(0.0);
        OrderResult {
            order_id,
            symbol,
            side,
            price,
            quantity: sz * ctval,
            executed_qty: acc * ctval,
            status,
            commission,
        }
    }

    fn client_id_to_clord(id: &Option<String>) -> Option<Value> {
        id.as_ref().map(|s| {
            let safe: String = s.chars().filter(|ch| ch.is_ascii_alphanumeric()).take(32).collect();
            Value::String(safe)
        })
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl ExchangeClient for OkxFuturesClient {
    async fn sync_time(&self) -> Result<()> {
        let json = self.public_get("/api/v5/public/time", "").await?;
        let server_ms = json["data"][0]["ts"]
            .as_str()
            .and_then(|s| s.parse::<i64>().ok())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis());
        let local_ms = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis();
        let offset = server_ms - local_ms;
        self.time_sync.set_offset(offset);
        tracing::info!("OKX clock offset: {} ms", offset);
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn place_market_order(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        side: &str,
        quantity: f64,
        client_order_id: Option<String>,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let inst_id = to_inst_id(symbol);
        let c = self.ensure_contract(&inst_id).await?;
        let lots = Self::qty_to_lots(quantity, c);
        tracing::info!("[okx] market {}: {} {} base -> {} lots (ctVal={})", inst_id, side, quantity, lots, c.0);
        let mut body = serde_json::json!({
            "instId": inst_id,
            "tdMode": "cross",
            "side": side.to_lowercase(),
            "ordType": "market",
            "sz": Self::lots_str(lots),
        });
        if let Some(v) = Self::client_id_to_clord(&client_order_id) {
            body["clOrdId"] = v;
        }
        let json = self.signed_post("/api/v5/trade/order", &body).await?;
        Self::check_scode(&json["data"])?;
        Ok(OrderResult {
            order_id: json["data"][0]["ordId"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),
            symbol: from_inst_id(&inst_id),
            side: side.to_uppercase(),
            price: 0.0,
            quantity: lots * c.0,
            executed_qty: 0.0,
            status: "NEW".to_string(),
            commission: 0.0,
        })
    }

    async fn place_limit_order(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        side: &str,
        quantity: f64,
        price: f64,
        client_order_id: Option<String>,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let inst_id = to_inst_id(symbol);
        let c = self.ensure_contract(&inst_id).await?;
        let lots = Self::qty_to_lots(quantity, c);
        let mut body = serde_json::json!({
            "instId": inst_id,
            "tdMode": "cross",
            "side": side.to_lowercase(),
            "ordType": "limit",
            "sz": Self::lots_str(lots),
            "px": Self::px_str(price, c.3),
            "tif": "GTC",
        });
        if let Some(v) = Self::client_id_to_clord(&client_order_id) {
            body["clOrdId"] = v;
        }
        let json = self.signed_post("/api/v5/trade/order", &body).await?;
        Self::check_scode(&json["data"])?;
        Ok(OrderResult {
            order_id: json["data"][0]["ordId"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),
            symbol: from_inst_id(&inst_id),
            side: side.to_uppercase(),
            price,
            quantity: lots * c.0,
            executed_qty: 0.0,
            status: "NEW".to_string(),
            commission: 0.0,
        })
    }

    async fn place_protective_order(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        side: &str,
        stop: bool,
        trigger_price: f64,
        client_order_id: Option<String>,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        // Conditional algo order, closes 100% of position (OKX analog of closeOnTrigger).
        let inst_id = to_inst_id(symbol);
        let c = self.ensure_contract(&inst_id).await?;
        let trig = Self::px_str(trigger_price, c.3);
        let mut body = if stop {
            serde_json::json!({
                "instId": inst_id, "tdMode": "cross",
                "side": side.to_lowercase(), "ordType": "conditional",
                "closeFraction": "1", "reduceOnly": true,
                "slTriggerPx": trig, "slOrdPx": "-1",
            })
        } else {
            serde_json::json!({
                "instId": inst_id, "tdMode": "cross",
                "side": side.to_lowercase(), "ordType": "conditional",
                "closeFraction": "1", "reduceOnly": true,
                "tpTriggerPx": trig, "tpOrdPx": "-1",
            })
        };
        if let Some(v) = Self::client_id_to_clord(&client_order_id) {
            body["clOrdId"] = v;
        }
        let json = self.signed_post("/api/v5/trade/order-algo", &body).await?;
        Self::check_scode(&json["data"])?;
        Ok(OrderResult {
            order_id: json["data"][0]["algoId"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse::<u64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0),
            symbol: from_inst_id(&inst_id),
            side: side.to_uppercase(),
            price: trigger_price,
            quantity: 0.0,
            executed_qty: 0.0,
            status: "NEW".to_string(),
            commission: 0.0,
        })
    }

    async fn cancel_order(&self, symbol: &str, order_id: u64) -> Result<bool> {
        let inst_id = to_inst_id(symbol);
        let not_exist = |e: &anyhow::Error| {
            let s = e.to_string();
            s.contains("51400") || s.contains("51503") || s.contains("51603")
                || s.contains("not exist") || s.contains("does not exist") || s.contains("doesn't exist")
        };
        let body = serde_json::json!({ "instId": inst_id, "ordId": order_id.to_string() });
        match self.signed_post("/api/v5/trade/cancel-order", &body).await {
            Ok(_) => Ok(true),
            Err(e) if not_exist(&e) => {
                // maybe it is an algo (SL/TP) order -> try cancel-algo
                let body2 = serde_json::json!({ "instId": inst_id, "algoId": order_id.to_string() });
                match self.signed_post("/api/v5/trade/cancel-algo", &body2).await {
                    Ok(_) => Ok(true),
                    Err(e2) if not_exist(&e2) => Ok(false),
                    Err(e2) => Err(e2),
                }
            }
            Err(e) => Err(e),
        }
    }

    async fn get_order_status(&self, symbol: &str, order_id: u64) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let inst_id = to_inst_id(symbol);
        let c = self.ensure_contract(&inst_id).await?;
        let json = self
            .signed_get("/api/v5/trade/order", &format!("instId={}&ordId={}", inst_id, order_id))
            .await?;
        let o = json["data"]
            .as_array()
            .and_then(|a| a.first())
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("order {} not found", order_id))?;
        Ok(Self::parse_order(o, c.0, symbol))
    }

    async fn get_order_by_client_id(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        orig_client_order_id: &str,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let inst_id = to_inst_id(symbol);
        let c = self.ensure_contract(&inst_id).await?;
        let json = self
            .signed_get("/api/v5/trade/order", &format!("instId={}&clOrdId={}", inst_id, orig_client_order_id))
            .await?;
        let o = json["data"]
            .as_array()
            .and_then(|a| a.first())
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow!("clOrdId {} not found", orig_client_order_id))?;
        Ok(Self::parse_order(o, c.0, symbol))
    }

    async fn get_open_orders(&self, symbol: &str) -> Result<Vec<OrderResult>> {
        let inst_id = to_inst_id(symbol);
        let c = self.ensure_contract(&inst_id).await?;
        let json = self
            .signed_get("/api/v5/trade/orders-pending", &format!("instId={}", inst_id))
            .await?;
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        if let Some(arr) = json["data"].as_array() {
            for o in arr {
                out.push(Self::parse_order(o, c.0, symbol));
            }
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    async fn get_balance(&self, asset: &str) -> Result<f64> {
        let json = self
            .signed_get("/api/v5/account/balance", &format!("ccy={}", asset.to_uppercase()))
            .await?;
        if let Some(details) = json["data"]
            .as_array()
            .and_then(|a| a.first())
            .and_then(|d| d["details"].as_array())
        {
            for d in details {
                if d["ccy"].as_str() == Some(asset.to_uppercase().as_str()) {
                    let v = d["availBal"]
                        .as_str()
                        .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
                        .or_else(|| d["availEq"].as_str().filter(|s| !s.is_empty()))
                        .or_else(|| d["eq"].as_str().filter(|s| !s.is_empty()));
                    if let Some(v) = v {
                        if let Ok(f) = v.parse::<f64>() {
                            return Ok(f);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(0.0)
    }

    async fn get_order_book(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        limit: usize,
    ) -> Result<(Vec<(f64, f64)>, Vec<(f64, f64)>)> {
        let inst_id = to_inst_id(symbol);
        let sz = limit.clamp(1, 400);
        let json = self
            .public_get("/api/v5/market/books", &format!("instId={}&sz={}", inst_id, sz))
            .await?;
        let d = json["data"][0].clone();
        let parse_levels = |key: &str| -> Vec<(f64, f64)> {
            d[key]
                .as_array()
                .map(|arr| {
                    arr.iter()
                        .filter_map(|v| {
                            let p = v[0].as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
                            let q = v[1].as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
                            Some((p, q))
                        })
                        .collect()
                })
                .unwrap_or_default()
        };
        Ok((parse_levels("bids"), parse_levels("asks")))
    }

    async fn get_positions(&self) -> Result<Vec<ExchangePosition>> {
        let json = self.signed_get("/api/v5/account/positions", "instType=SWAP").await?;
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        if let Some(arr) = json["data"].as_array() {
            for p in arr {
                let pos: f64 = p["pos"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                if pos.abs() <= 0.0 {
                    continue;
                }
                let inst_id = p["instId"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_string();
                let ctval = match self.ensure_contract(&inst_id).await {
                    Ok(c) => c.0,
                    Err(_) => {
                        tracing::warn!("[okx] РЅРµС‚ ctVal РґР»СЏ {} вЂ” РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ РїСЂРѕРїСѓС‰РµРЅР°", inst_id);
                        continue;
                    }
                };
                out.push(ExchangePosition {
                    symbol: from_inst_id(&inst_id),
                    qty: pos * ctval,
                    entry_price: p["avgPx"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0),
                });
            }
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    async fn get_order_commission(&self, symbol: &str, order_id: u64) -> Result<f64> {
        let inst_id = to_inst_id(symbol);
        let json = self
            .signed_get("/api/v5/trade/order", &format!("instId={}&ordId={}", inst_id, order_id))
            .await?;
        if let Some(o) = json["data"].as_array().and_then(|a| a.first()) {
            if let Some(fee) = o["fee"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok()) {
                return Ok(fee.abs());
            }
        }
        Ok(0.0)
    }
}
=== C:\Users\BASE_PC\scalping_agentsGLM\src\exchange\okx_sign.rs ===
//! okx_sign.rs - OKX V5 HMAC-SHA256 signing (base64 output).
//! prehash = timestamp(ISO8601) + METHOD + requestPath(+query) + body
//! headers: OK-ACCESS-KEY / OK-ACCESS-SIGN / OK-ACCESS-TIMESTAMP / OK-ACCESS-PASSPHRASE
//! demo mode adds: x-simulated-trading: 1

use base64::Engine;
use hmac::{Hmac, Mac};
use sha2::Sha256;

type HmacSha256 = Hmac<Sha256>;

pub const DEMO_HEADER: &str = "x-simulated-trading";

/// ISO8601 UTC with milliseconds: 2026-01-01T00:00:00.123Z
pub fn iso_timestamp() -> String {
    chrono::Utc::now()
        .format("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%.3fZ")
        .to_string()
}

pub fn sign(secret: &str, timestamp_iso: &str, method: &str, request_path: &str, body: &str) -> String {
    let payload = format!("{}{}{}{}", timestamp_iso, method.to_uppercase(), request_path, body);
    sign_hmac(secret, &payload)
}

pub fn sign_hmac(secret: &str, payload: &str) -> String {
    let mut mac = HmacSha256::new_from_slice(secret.as_bytes())
        .expect("HMAC can take key of any size");
    mac.update(payload.as_bytes());
    base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(mac.finalize().into_bytes())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_sign_format() {
        let s = sign("secret", "2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z", "GET", "/api/v5/account/balance?ccy=USDT", "");
        assert!(!s.is_empty());
        assert!(s.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '+' || c == '/' || c == '='));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_iso_format() {
        let ts = iso_timestamp();
        assert_eq!(ts.len(), 24);
        assert!(ts.ends_with('Z'));
        assert_eq!(&ts[10..11], "T");
    }
}
