use crate::config::BotConfig;
use crate::exchange::ExchangeClient;
use crate::risk::GlobalRiskManager;
use crate::state::StateManager;
use crate::strategies::Signal;
use crate::exchange::symbol_filters::FilterRegistry;
use crate::exchange::traits::OrderResult;
use crate::exchange::user_stream::OrderEventsRx;
use anyhow::Result;
use rust_decimal::prelude::*;
use std::sync::Arc;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum ExecOutcome {
    Exit(f64),
    ExitUnconfirmed,
    Entry { price: f64, qty: f64, order_id: u64 },
    Missed(String),
}

pub struct ExecutionEngine {
    config: BotConfig,
    state_manager: Arc<StateManager>,
    global_risk: Arc<GlobalRiskManager>,
    rest_client: Option<Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>>,
    simulate: bool,
    filters: Option<Arc<FilterRegistry>>,
    order_events: Option<OrderEventsRx>,
    last_close_attempt_ms: std::sync::Mutex<std::collections::HashMap<String, i64>>,

    // рџ”§ РџРђРўР§-РЎРР РћРўР«: РіРµР№С‚С‹ РІС…РѕРґР° РїРѕ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёСЋ СЃРµС‚Рё
    last_entry_net_fail: std::sync::Mutex<Option<std::time::Instant>>,
    net_fails: std::sync::Mutex<std::collections::VecDeque<std::time::Instant>>,
    entries_blocked_until: std::sync::Mutex<Option<std::time::Instant>>,

    // рџ”§ РђРќРўР-Р‘РђРќ: throttle pre-entry reconcile (РЅРµ С‡Р°С‰Рµ СЂР°Р·Р° РІ 30СЃ)
    last_reconcile_attempt: std::sync::Mutex<Option<std::time::Instant>>,
}

impl ExecutionEngine {
    pub fn new(
        config: &BotConfig,
        client: Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>,
        state_manager: Arc<StateManager>,
        global_risk: Arc<GlobalRiskManager>,
        filters: Option<Arc<FilterRegistry>>,
        order_events: Option<OrderEventsRx>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            config: config.clone(),
            state_manager,
            global_risk,
            rest_client: Some(client),
            simulate: config.simulate,
            filters,
            order_events,
            last_close_attempt_ms: std::sync::Mutex::new(std::collections::HashMap::new()),
            last_entry_net_fail: std::sync::Mutex::new(None),
            net_fails: std::sync::Mutex::new(std::collections::VecDeque::new()),
            entries_blocked_until: std::sync::Mutex::new(None),
            last_reconcile_attempt: std::sync::Mutex::new(None),
        }
    }

    // ============================================================
    // рџ”§ РџРђРўР§-РЎРР РћРўР«: СЃРµС‚РµРІС‹Рµ РіРµР№С‚С‹ РІС…РѕРґРѕРІ.
    // ============================================================
    fn record_net_fail(&self) {
        let now = std::time::Instant::now();
        *self.last_entry_net_fail.lock().unwrap() = Some(now);

        let mut q = self.net_fails.lock().unwrap();
        q.push_back(now);
        while q.front().map_or(false, |t| now.duration_since(*t).as_secs() > 600) {
            q.pop_front();
        }
        if q.len() >= 5 {
            *self.entries_blocked_until.lock().unwrap() =
                Some(now + std::time::Duration::from_secs(900));
            tracing::error!(
                "[{}] рџЊђ 5 СЃРµС‚РµРІС‹С… СЃР±РѕРµРІ Р·Р° 10 РјРёРЅ вЂ” Р’РҐРћР”Р« Р—РђР‘Р›РћРљРР РћР’РђРќР« РЅР° 15 РјРёРЅ",
                self.config.name
            );
        }
    }

    fn entries_allowed(&self) -> bool {
        self.entries_blocked_until
            .lock()
            .unwrap()
            .map_or(true, |t| std::time::Instant::now() >= t)
    }

    fn entry_backoff_active(&self) -> bool {
        self.last_entry_net_fail
            .lock()
            .unwrap()
            .map_or(false, |t| t.elapsed() < std::time::Duration::from_secs(180))
    }

    fn taker_fee(&self) -> f64 {
        if let Some(fee) = self.config.fee_rate {
            return fee.to_f64().unwrap_or(0.00075);
        }
        match self.config.market_type.as_str() {
            "futures" => 0.0005,
            _ => 0.00075,
        }
    }

    fn maker_fee(&self) -> f64 {
        self.config
            .maker_fee
            .to_f64()
            .unwrap_or(if self.config.market_type == "futures" { 0.0002 } else { 0.00075 })
    }

    fn apply_filters(&self, symbol: &str, price: f64, qty: f64) -> Option<(f64, f64)> {
        let f = self.filters.as_ref()?.get(symbol).ok()?;
        let p = Decimal::from_f64(price)?;
        let q = Decimal::from_f64(qty)?;
        Some((f.round_price(p).to_f64()?, f.floor_qty(q).to_f64()?))
    }

    // ============================================================
    // рџ”§ РџРђРўР§-РЎРР РћРўР«: РґРѕРІРѕРґРєР° РѕСЂРґРµСЂР° РґРѕ С‚РµСЂРјРёРЅР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃР°.
    // ============================================================
    async fn ensure_terminal(
        &self,
        rest: &Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>,
        symbol: &str,
        order_id: u64,
        known_commission: f64,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        for attempt in 1..=5u32 {
            match rest.get_order_status(symbol, order_id).await {
                Ok(mut st) => {
                    if st.commission < known_commission {
                        st.commission = known_commission;
                    }
                    if matches!(st.status.as_str(), "FILLED" | "CANCELED" | "EXPIRED") {
                        return Ok(st);
                    }
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "[{}] РѕСЂРґРµСЂ #{} РµС‰С‘ Р¶РёРІ ({}) РїРѕСЃР»Рµ С‚Р°Р№РјР°СѓС‚Р° вЂ” РѕС‚РјРµРЅСЏСЋ",
                        self.config.name, order_id, st.status
                    );
                    if let Err(ce) = rest.cancel_order(symbol, order_id).await {
                        tracing::warn!(
                            "[{}] РѕС‚РјРµРЅР° #{} (ensure_terminal) РЅРµ СѓРґР°Р»Р°СЃСЊ: {}",
                            self.config.name, order_id, ce
                        );
                    }
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "[{}] СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ #{} РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРµРЅ ({}/5): {}",
                        self.config.name, order_id, attempt, e
                    );
                    self.record_net_fail();
                }
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(500 * attempt as u64)).await;
        }

        let _ = rest.cancel_order(symbol, order_id).await;
        for attempt in 1..=5u32 {
            match rest.get_order_status(symbol, order_id).await {
                Ok(st) if matches!(st.status.as_str(), "FILLED" | "CANCELED" | "EXPIRED") => {
                    return Ok(st);
                }
                Ok(st) => {
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "[{}] РѕСЂРґРµСЂ #{} РІСЃС‘ РµС‰С‘ Р¶РёРІ ({}) вЂ” РѕС‚РјРµРЅСЏСЋ РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРЅРѕ",
                        self.config.name, order_id, st.status
                    );
                    let _ = rest.cancel_order(symbol, order_id).await;
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "[{}] СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ #{} РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРµРЅ (С„РёРЅР°Р» {}/5): {}",
                        self.config.name, order_id, attempt, e
                    );
                    self.record_net_fail();
                }
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(500 * attempt as u64)).await;
        }

        Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
            "СЃСѓРґСЊР±Р° РѕСЂРґРµСЂР° {} РЅРµ РІС‹СЏСЃРЅРµРЅР° РїРѕСЃР»Рµ 10+ РїРѕРїС‹С‚РѕРє вЂ” РЎР’Р•Р Р¬ openOrders Р’Р РЈР§РќРЈР®",
            order_id
        ))
    }

    async fn resolve_by_client_id(
        &self,
        rest: &Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>,
        symbol: &str,
        client_id: &str,
    ) -> Option<OrderResult> {
        for attempt in 1..=6u32 {
            match rest.get_order_by_client_id(symbol, client_id).await {
                Ok(st) => {
                    if matches!(st.status.as_str(), "FILLED" | "CANCELED" | "EXPIRED") {
                        if st.executed_qty > 0.0 {
                            tracing::warn!(
                                "[{}] вљ пёЏ РѕСЂРґРµСЂ client={} (id={}) РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРµРЅ ({:.5}) РІРѕРїСЂРµРєРё СЃР±РѕСЋ POST вЂ” РђР”РћРџРўРР РЈР® РєР°Рє РІС…РѕРґ",
                                self.config.name, client_id, st.order_id, st.executed_qty
                            );
                            return Some(st);
                        }
                        tracing::info!(
                            "[{}] РѕСЂРґРµСЂ client={} (id={}) РјС‘СЂС‚РІ ({}) вЂ” СѓРїСѓС‰РµРЅРёРµ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРѕ",
                            self.config.name, client_id, st.order_id, st.status
                        );
                        return None;
                    }
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "[{}] РѕСЂРґРµСЂ client={} (id={}) Р¶РёРІ ({}) вЂ” РѕС‚РјРµРЅСЏСЋ",
                        self.config.name, client_id, st.order_id, st.status
                    );
                    let _ = rest.cancel_order(symbol, st.order_id).await;
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    if e.to_string().contains("-2013") {
                        tracing::info!(
                            "[{}] РѕСЂРґРµСЂ client={} РЅРµ СЃСѓС‰РµСЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚ РЅР° Р±РёСЂР¶Рµ вЂ” POST РЅРµ РґРѕС€С‘Р»",
                            self.config.name, client_id
                        );
                        return None;
                    }
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "[{}] РїРѕРёСЃРє client={} ({}/6): {}",
                        self.config.name, client_id, attempt, e
                    );
                }
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(500 * attempt as u64)).await;
        }
        tracing::error!(
            "[{}] рџљЁ CRITICAL: СЃСѓРґСЊР±Р° client={} РЅРµ РІС‹СЏСЃРЅРµРЅР° Р·Р° 6 РїРѕРїС‹С‚РѕРє вЂ” \
             РЎР’Р•Р Р¬ openOrders/positionRisk Р’Р РЈР§РќРЈР®",
            self.config.name, client_id
        );
        None
    }

    async fn place_and_wait<F, Fut>(
        &self,
        rest: &Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>,
        symbol: &str,
        place: F,
        timeout_ms: u64,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult>
    where
        F: FnOnce() -> Fut,
        Fut: std::future::Future<Output = Result<OrderResult>>,
    {
        let Some(rx_arc) = &self.order_events else {
            let order = place().await?;
            return self.wait_plain(rest, symbol, order.order_id, timeout_ms).await;
        };

        let mut rx = rx_arc.lock().await;
        let deadline =
            std::time::Instant::now() + std::time::Duration::from_millis(timeout_ms + 2000);

        let order = place().await?;

        let mut commission_sum: f64 = 0.0;
        loop {
            let Some(remain) = deadline.checked_duration_since(std::time::Instant::now()) else {
                drop(rx);
                return self
                    .ensure_terminal(rest, symbol, order.order_id, commission_sum)
                    .await;
            };
            match tokio::time::timeout(remain, rx.recv()).await {
                Ok(Ok(ev)) if ev.order_id == order.order_id => {
                    commission_sum += ev.commission;
                    if matches!(ev.status.as_str(), "FILLED" | "CANCELED" | "EXPIRED") {
                        let mut res = ev.to_order_result();
                        res.commission = commission_sum;
                        return Ok(res);
                    }
                    continue;
                }
                Ok(Ok(_)) => continue,
                Ok(Err(tokio::sync::broadcast::error::RecvError::Lagged(_))) => continue,
                Ok(Err(tokio::sync::broadcast::error::RecvError::Closed)) => {
                    drop(rx);
                    return self.wait_plain(rest, symbol, order.order_id, timeout_ms).await;
                }
                Err(_) => {
                    drop(rx);
                    return self
                        .ensure_terminal(rest, symbol, order.order_id, commission_sum)
                        .await;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    async fn wait_plain(
        &self,
        rest: &Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>,
        symbol: &str,
        order_id: u64,
        timeout_ms: u64,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let deadline =
            std::time::Instant::now() + std::time::Duration::from_millis(timeout_ms);
        let mut consec_err = 0u32;
        loop {
            if std::time::Instant::now() >= deadline {
                return self.ensure_terminal(rest, symbol, order_id, 0.0).await;
            }
            let remain = deadline
                .checked_duration_since(std::time::Instant::now())
                .unwrap_or_default();
            let step = remain.min(std::time::Duration::from_millis(400));
            tokio::time::sleep(step).await;
            match rest.get_order_status(symbol, order_id).await {
                Ok(st) => {
                    consec_err = 0;
                    if matches!(st.status.as_str(), "FILLED" | "CANCELED" | "EXPIRED") {
                        return Ok(st);
                    }
                }
                Err(e) => {
                    consec_err += 1;
                    self.record_net_fail();
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "[{}] СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ #{} РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРµРЅ ({}/10): {}",
                        self.config.name, order_id, consec_err, e
                    );
                    if consec_err >= 10 {
                        return self.ensure_terminal(rest, symbol, order_id, 0.0).await;
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    async fn enter_maker_no_chase(
        &self,
        rest: &Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>,
        signal: &Signal,
        safe_price: f64,
        safe_qty: f64,
    ) -> Result<Option<OrderResult>> {
        let wait_ms = (self.config.order_timeout_sec as u64).saturating_mul(1000).max(500);

        let client_id = self.new_client_id();

        let status = match self
            .place_and_wait(
                rest,
                &signal.symbol,
                || {
                    let cid = client_id.clone();
                    async move {
                        rest.place_limit_order(
                            &signal.symbol,
                            &signal.side,
                            safe_qty,
                            safe_price,
                            Some(cid),
                        )
                        .await
                    }
                },
                wait_ms,
            )
            .await
        {
            Ok(s) => s,
            Err(e) => {
                let msg = e.to_string();
                if msg.contains("Binance") {
                    tracing::info!(
                        "[{}] maker-РІС…РѕРґ РѕС‚РєР»РѕРЅС‘РЅ Р±РёСЂР¶РµР№: {}",
                        self.config.name, msg
                    );
                    return Ok(None);
                }
                tracing::warn!(
                    "[{}] СЃРµС‚РµРІРѕР№ СЃР±РѕР№ POST РІС…РѕРґР°: {} | client={} вЂ” РІС‹СЏСЃРЅСЏСЋ СЃСѓРґСЊР±Сѓ",
                    self.config.name, msg, client_id
                );
                self.record_net_fail();
                return Ok(self
                    .resolve_by_client_id(rest, &signal.symbol, &client_id)
                    .await);
            }
        };

        if status.status == "FILLED" {
            tracing::info!("[{}] вњ… maker РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРµРЅ (id={}, РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёРё={:.6})",
                self.config.name, status.order_id, status.commission);
            return Ok(Some(status));
        }

        match rest.cancel_order(&signal.symbol, status.order_id).await {
            Ok(true) => {
                match rest.get_order_status(&signal.symbol, status.order_id).await {
                    Ok(mut final_st) => {
                        if final_st.commission < status.commission {
                            final_st.commission = status.commission;
                        }
                        if final_st.executed_qty > 0.0 {
                            tracing::warn!(
                                "[{}] maker С‡Р°СЃС‚РёС‡РЅРѕ ({:.5} РёР· {:.5}) вЂ” Р±РµСЂС‘Рј РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРЅСѓСЋ С‡Р°СЃС‚СЊ",
                                self.config.name, final_st.executed_qty, safe_qty
                            );
                            Ok(Some(final_st))
                        } else {
                            tracing::info!(
                                "[{}] maker РЅРµ РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРµРЅ Р·Р° {} РјСЃ вЂ” РѕС‚РјРµРЅС‘РЅ (Р±РµР· РґРѕРіРѕРЅР°)",
                                self.config.name, wait_ms
                            );
                            Ok(None)
                        }
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        tracing::error!(
                            "[{}] СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РѕС‚РјРµРЅС‹ #{} РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРµРЅ: {} вЂ” resolve",
                            self.config.name, status.order_id, e
                        );
                        self.resolve_order_fate(
                            rest, &signal.symbol, status.order_id, safe_qty, status.commission,
                        )
                        .await
                    }
                }
            }
            Ok(false) => {
                tracing::warn!("[{}] РѕСЂРґРµСЂ РёСЃС‡РµР· РїСЂРё РѕС‚РјРµРЅРµ вЂ” РїРµСЂРµС‡РёС‚С‹РІР°РµРј СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ", self.config.name);
                match rest.get_order_status(&signal.symbol, status.order_id).await {
                    Ok(mut st) => {
                        if st.commission < status.commission {
                            st.commission = status.commission;
                        }
                        Ok(Some(st))
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        tracing::error!(
                            "[{}] СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ РёСЃС‡РµР·РЅСѓРІС€РµРіРѕ #{} РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРµРЅ: {} вЂ” resolve",
                            self.config.name, status.order_id, e
                        );
                        self.resolve_order_fate(
                            rest, &signal.symbol, status.order_id, safe_qty, status.commission,
                        )
                        .await
                    }
                }
            }
            Err(e) => {
                tracing::error!(
                    "[{}] cancel РїРѕСЃР»Рµ С‚Р°Р№РјР°СѓС‚Р°: {} вЂ” РІС‹СЏСЃРЅСЏСЋ СЃСѓРґСЊР±Сѓ РѕСЂРґРµСЂР° {}",
                    self.config.name, e, status.order_id
                );
                self.resolve_order_fate(
                    rest, &signal.symbol, status.order_id, safe_qty, status.commission,
                )
                .await
            }
        }
    }

    async fn resolve_order_fate(
        &self,
        rest: &Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>,
        symbol: &str,
        order_id: u64,
        safe_qty: f64,
        known_commission: f64,
    ) -> Result<Option<OrderResult>> {
        for attempt in 1..=3u32 {
            if let Err(ce) = rest.cancel_order(symbol, order_id).await {
                tracing::warn!(
                    "[{}] resolve: РѕС‚РјРµРЅР° {}/3 РЅРµ СѓРґР°Р»Р°СЃСЊ: {}",
                    self.config.name, attempt, ce
                );
            }
            match rest.get_order_status(symbol, order_id).await {
                Ok(mut st) if matches!(st.status.as_str(), "FILLED" | "CANCELED" | "EXPIRED") => {
                    if st.commission < known_commission {
                        st.commission = known_commission;
                    }
                    if st.executed_qty > 0.0 {
                        tracing::warn!(
                            "[{}] вљ пёЏ РѕСЂРґРµСЂ {} РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРµРЅ ({:.5} РёР· {:.5}) РІРѕРїСЂРµРєРё СЃР±РѕСЋ вЂ” Р±РµСЂС‘Рј РєР°Рє РІС…РѕРґ",
                            self.config.name, order_id, st.executed_qty, safe_qty
                        );
                        return Ok(Some(st));
                    }
                    tracing::info!(
                        "[{}] РѕСЂРґРµСЂ {} РјС‘СЂС‚РІ ({}) вЂ” СѓРїСѓС‰РµРЅРёРµ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРѕ",
                        self.config.name, order_id, st.status
                    );
                    return Ok(None);
                }
                Ok(st) => {
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "[{}] РѕСЂРґРµСЂ {} РµС‰С‘ Р¶РёРІ ({}) вЂ” РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєР° {}/3",
                        self.config.name, order_id, st.status, attempt
                    );
                }
                Err(se) => {
                    tracing::error!(
                        "[{}] СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ РѕСЂРґРµСЂР° {} РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРµРЅ: {} (РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєР° {}/3)",
                        self.config.name, order_id, se, attempt
                    );
                }
            }
            if attempt < 3 {
                tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(500)).await;
            }
        }
        tracing::error!(
            "[{}] рџљЁ CRITICAL: СЃСѓРґСЊР±Р° РѕСЂРґРµСЂР° {} РЅРµ РІС‹СЏСЃРЅРµРЅР° вЂ” РІРѕР·РјРѕР¶РЅР° Р¶РёРІР°СЏ РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ; \
             РїРѕРґС…РІР°С‚РёС‚ РїРµСЂРёРѕРґРёС‡РµСЃРєРёР№ reconciliation (в‰¤10 РјРёРЅ)",
            self.config.name, order_id
        );
        Ok(None)
    }

    async fn enter_market_confirmed(
        &self,
        rest: &Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>,
        signal: &Signal,
        qty: f64,
    ) -> Result<OrderResult> {
        let status = match self
            .place_and_wait(
                rest,
                &signal.symbol,
                || async {
                    rest.place_market_order(
                        &signal.symbol,
                        &signal.side,
                        qty,
                        Some(self.new_client_id()),
                    )
                    .await
                },
                3000,
            )
            .await
        {
            Ok(s) => s,
            Err(e) => return Err(anyhow::anyhow!("РЎРЈР”Р¬Р‘Рђ РќР•РР—Р’Р•РЎРўРќРђ: {}", e)),
        };

        if status.status == "FILLED" || status.executed_qty > 0.0 {
            if status.executed_qty < qty {
                tracing::warn!(
                    "[{}] market РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРµРЅ С‡Р°СЃС‚РёС‡РЅРѕ: {:.5} РёР· {:.5}",
                    self.config.name, status.executed_qty, qty
                );
            }
            return Ok(status);
        }
        Err(anyhow::anyhow!(
            "market-РѕСЂРґРµСЂ {} РЅРµ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґС‘РЅ (СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ {})",
            signal.symbol, status.status
        ))
    }

    async fn recover_commissions(
        &self,
        rest: &Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>,
        symbol: &str,
        order_ids: &[u64],
    ) {
        for oid in order_ids {
            match rest.get_order_commission(symbol, *oid).await {
                Ok(c) if c > 0.0 => {
                    self.state_manager.add_fees_paid(symbol, c);
                    tracing::info!(
                        "[{}] рџ©№ РґРѕРєР°РїС‡РёРІР°РЅРёРµ: РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёРё #{} = {:.6} (РёР· income)",
                        self.config.name, oid, c
                    );
                }
                Ok(_) => {}
                Err(e) => {
                    tracing::debug!(
                        "[{}] РґРѕРєР°РїС‡РёРІР°РЅРёРµ #{} РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРЅРѕ: {}",
                        self.config.name, oid, e
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // ============================================================
    // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B: СЃРЅСЏС‚СЊ Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІС‹Рµ Р·Р°С‰РёС‚РЅС‹Рµ РѕСЂРґРµСЂР° Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РѕР№ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё.
    // ============================================================
    async fn remove_protective_orders(&self, symbol: &str) {
        let (sl_id, tp_id) = self.state_manager.get_protective_ids(symbol);
        if let Some(rest) = &self.rest_client {
            for (tag, id_str) in [("SL", sl_id), ("TP", tp_id)] {
                if let Some(s) = id_str {
                    let oid: u64 = s.parse().unwrap_or(0);
                    if oid > 0 {
                        match rest.cancel_order(symbol, oid).await {
                            Ok(_) => tracing::info!(
                                "[{}] рџ§№ Р·Р°С‰РёС‚РЅС‹Р№ {} #{} СЃРЅСЏС‚",
                                self.config.name, tag, oid
                            ),
                            Err(e) => {
                                match rest.get_order_status(symbol, oid).await {
                                    Ok(st) => tracing::warn!(
                                        "[{}] Р·Р°С‰РёС‚РЅС‹Р№ {} #{} СѓР¶Рµ {} вЂ” РѕРє",
                                        self.config.name, tag, oid, st.status
                                    ),
                                    Err(_) => tracing::error!(
                                        "[{}] рџљЁ РЅРµ СЃРЅСЏС‚СЊ Р·Р°С‰РёС‚РЅС‹Р№ {} #{}: {}",
                                        self.config.name, tag, oid, e
                                    ),
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // ============================================================
    // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B: РїРѕСЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ Р·Р°С‰РёС‚РЅС‹Рµ РѕСЂРґРµСЂР° РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РІС…РѕРґР°.
    // ============================================================
    async fn place_protective_orders(
        &self,
        rest: &Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>,
        signal: &Signal,
        entry_order_id: u64,
    ) {
        let close_side = if signal.side == "BUY" { "SELL" } else { "BUY" };

        // P18: protective triggers must be multiples of tickSize, otherwise
        // -1111 rejects them (TAOUSDT 2026-10-18: SL/TP rejected 3x, position
        // 110+ sec unprotected). Decimal round to nearest tick, no float dust.
        let p18_tick = |v: f64| -> f64 {
            match self.filters.as_ref().and_then(|r| r.get(&signal.symbol).ok()) {
                Some(f) => match Decimal::from_f64(v) {
                    Some(d) => f.round_price(d).to_f64().unwrap_or(v),
                    None => v,
                },
                None => v,
            }
        };
        let sl_f = signal.stop_loss.map(|v| p18_tick(v));
        let tp_f = signal.take_profit.map(|v| p18_tick(v));

        if sl_f != signal.stop_loss || tp_f != signal.take_profit {
            tracing::info!(
                "[{}] P18: protective triggers tick-aligned: sl {:?} -> {:?}, tp {:?} -> {:?}",
                self.config.name, signal.stop_loss, sl_f, signal.take_profit, tp_f
            );
        }

        if let Some(sl) = sl_f {
            let mut placed = false;
            for attempt in 1..=2u32 {
                                // рџ”§ Р•СЃР»Рё shutdown СѓР¶Рµ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹Р» РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЋ вЂ” Р·Р°С‰РёС‚С‹ РЅРµ РЅСѓР¶РЅС‹
                if self.state_manager.get_positions().is_empty() {
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "[{}] РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ СѓР¶Рµ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚Р° (shutdown) вЂ” РїСЂРµСЂС‹РІР°СЋ РїРѕСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєСѓ Р·Р°С‰РёС‚",
                        self.config.name
                    );
                    return;
                }
                match rest
                    .place_protective_order(
                        &signal.symbol,
                        &close_side,
                        true,
                        sl,
                        Some(format!("pr_sl_{}", entry_order_id)),
                    )
                    .await
                {
                    Ok(o) => {
                        self.state_manager
                            .set_sl_order_id(&signal.symbol, o.order_id.to_string())
                            .await;
                        tracing::info!(
                            "[{}] рџ›Ў Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІРѕР№ STOP_MARKET #{} @ {:.8} РїРѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»РµРЅ",
                            self.config.name, o.order_id, sl
                        );
                        placed = true;
                        break;
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        tracing::warn!(
                            "[{}] рџ›Ў STOP_MARKET РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєР° {}/2 РЅРµ СѓРґР°Р»Р°СЃСЊ: {}",
                            self.config.name, attempt, e
                        );
                        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(
                            1000 * attempt as u64,
                        ))
                        .await;
                    }
                }
            }
            if !placed {
                tracing::error!(
                    "[{}] рџљЁ РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ Р‘Р•Р— Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІРѕРіРѕ СЃС‚РѕРїР° вЂ” СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ СЃРѕС„С‚-Р·Р°С‰РёС‚Р°",
                    self.config.name
                );
            }
        }

        if let Some(tp) = tp_f {
            let mut placed = false;
            for attempt in 1..=2u32 {
                                // рџ”§ Р•СЃР»Рё shutdown СѓР¶Рµ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹Р» РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЋ вЂ” Р·Р°С‰РёС‚С‹ РЅРµ РЅСѓР¶РЅС‹
                if self.state_manager.get_positions().is_empty() {
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "[{}] РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ СѓР¶Рµ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚Р° (shutdown) вЂ” РїСЂРµСЂС‹РІР°СЋ РїРѕСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРєСѓ Р·Р°С‰РёС‚",
                        self.config.name
                    );
                    return;
                }
                match rest
                    .place_protective_order(
                        &signal.symbol,
                        &close_side,
                        false,
                        tp,
                        Some(format!("pr_tp_{}", entry_order_id)),
                    )
                    .await
                {
                    Ok(o) => {
                        self.state_manager
                            .set_tp_order_id(&signal.symbol, o.order_id.to_string())
                            .await;
                        tracing::info!(
                            "[{}] рџ›Ў Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІРѕР№ TAKE_PROFIT_MARKET #{} @ {:.8} РїРѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»РµРЅ",
                            self.config.name, o.order_id, tp
                        );
                        placed = true;
                        break;
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        tracing::warn!(
                            "[{}] рџ›Ў TP_MARKET РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєР° {}/2 РЅРµ СѓРґР°Р»Р°СЃСЊ: {}",
                            self.config.name, attempt, e
                        );
                        tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(
                            1000 * attempt as u64,
                        ))
                        .await;
                    }
                }
            }
            if !placed {
                tracing::error!(
                    "[{}] рџљЁ РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ Р‘Р•Р— Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІРѕРіРѕ TP вЂ” СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ СЃРѕС„С‚-TP",
                    self.config.name
                );
            }
        }
    }

    fn exit_allows_maker(&self, strategy_name: &str) -> bool {
        !matches!(
            strategy_name,
            "Exit_StopLoss" | "Exit_Timeout" | "Exit_Panic" | "Exit_Shutdown_Market"
        )
    }

    async fn close_position_real(
        &self,
        rest: &Arc<dyn ExchangeClient>,
        signal: &Signal,
    ) -> Option<f64> {
        // anti-hammer
        {
            let key = signal.symbol.clone();
            let now = std::time::SystemTime::now()
                .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
                .map(|d| d.as_millis() as i64)
                .unwrap_or(0);
            let mut map = self.last_close_attempt_ms.lock().unwrap();
            if let Some(&t) = map.get(&key) {
                if now - t < 5000 {
                    tracing::debug!(
                        "[{}] Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ {}: throttle вЂ” РјРµРЅРµРµ 5СЃ СЃ РїСЂРѕС€Р»РѕР№ РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєРё",
                        self.config.name, key
                    );
                    return None;
                }
            }
            map.insert(key, now);
        }

        let qty = self
            .apply_filters(&signal.symbol, signal.price, signal.quantity)
            .map(|(_, q)| q)
            .unwrap_or(signal.quantity);

        // РџР«Р›Р¬
        if let Some(f) = self.filters.as_ref().and_then(|r| r.get(&signal.symbol).ok()) {
            let min_n = f.min_notional.to_f64().unwrap_or(0.0);
            if qty * signal.price < min_n {
                tracing::warn!(
                    "[{}] рџ§№ РџР«Р›Р¬: {} Г— {:.8} = ${:.4} < minNotional {} вЂ” РїРѕРіР°С€РµРЅР° РІ state Р±РµР· РѕСЂРґРµСЂР°",
                    self.config.name, qty, signal.price, qty * signal.price, min_n
                );
                let pnl = self
                    .state_manager
                    .close_position(&signal.symbol, signal.price, qty)
                    .await;
                return Some(pnl);
            }
        }

        let origin = self
            .state_manager
            .origin_strategy_for(&signal.symbol)
            .unwrap_or_default();

        let use_maker_exit = (origin == "SpreadCapture" || origin == "SpreadCapture_MOMENTUM")
            && self.config.market_type == "futures"
            && self.exit_allows_maker(&signal.strategy_name);

        if use_maker_exit {
            if let Some(price) = self
                .apply_filters(&signal.symbol, signal.price, signal.quantity)
                .map(|(p, _)| p)
            {
                tracing::info!(
                    "[{}] maker-РІС‹С…РѕРґ: limit {} {:.5} @ {:.8}",
                    self.config.name, signal.side, qty, price
                );
                let status_res = self
                    .place_and_wait(
                        rest,
                        &signal.symbol,
                        || async {
                            rest.place_limit_order(
                                &signal.symbol,
                                &signal.side,
                                qty,
                                price,
                                Some(self.new_client_id()),
                            )
                            .await
                        },
                        (self.config.order_timeout_sec as u64).saturating_mul(1000).max(500),
                    )
                    .await;
                if let Ok(status) = status_res {
                    if status.status == "FILLED" {
                        if status.commission > 0.0 {
                            self.state_manager
                                .add_fees_paid(&signal.symbol, status.commission);
                        }
                        let exit_price = if status.price > 0.0 { status.price } else { price };
                        let pnl = self
                            .state_manager
                            .close_position(&signal.symbol, exit_price, qty)
                            .await;
                        if status.commission <= 0.0 {
                            self.recover_commissions(rest, &signal.symbol, &[status.order_id])
                                .await;
                        }
                        return Some(pnl);
                    }

                    let mut maker_filled = 0.0;
                    let mut maker_price = 0.0;
                    let mut maker_commission = status.commission;
                    let maker_order_id = status.order_id;
                    let mut resolved = false;
                    for attempt in 1..=3u32 {
                        if let Err(ce) = rest.cancel_order(&signal.symbol, status.order_id).await {
                            tracing::warn!(
                                "[{}] РѕС‚РјРµРЅР° maker-РІС‹С…РѕРґР° {}/3: {}",
                                self.config.name, attempt, ce
                            );
                        }
                        match rest.get_order_status(&signal.symbol, status.order_id).await {
                            Ok(st) if matches!(st.status.as_str(), "FILLED" | "CANCELED" | "EXPIRED") => {
                                maker_filled = st.executed_qty;
                                maker_price = st.price;
                                if st.commission > maker_commission {
                                    maker_commission = st.commission;
                                }
                                resolved = true;
                                break;
                            }
                            Ok(st) => {
                                tracing::warn!(
                                    "[{}] maker-РІС‹С…РѕРґ #{} РµС‰С‘ Р¶РёРІ ({}) вЂ” РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєР° {}/3",
                                    self.config.name, status.order_id, st.status, attempt
                                );
                            }
                            Err(se) => {
                                tracing::error!(
                                    "[{}] СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ maker-РІС‹С…РѕРґР° #{} РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРµРЅ: {} (РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєР° {}/3)",
                                    self.config.name, status.order_id, se, attempt
                                );
                            }
                        }
                        if attempt < 3 {
                            tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_millis(500)).await;
                        }
                    }
                    if !resolved {
                        tracing::error!(
                            "[{}] вќЊ СЃСѓРґСЊР±Р° maker-РІС‹С…РѕРґР° #{} РЅРµ РІС‹СЏСЃРЅРµРЅР° вЂ” Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ РѕС‚Р»РѕР¶РµРЅРѕ",
                            self.config.name, status.order_id
                        );
                        return None;
                    }

                    if maker_filled >= qty {
                        if maker_commission > 0.0 {
                            self.state_manager
                                .add_fees_paid(&signal.symbol, maker_commission);
                        }
                        let exit_price = if maker_price > 0.0 { maker_price } else { price };
                        let pnl = self
                            .state_manager
                            .close_position(&signal.symbol, exit_price, qty)
                            .await;
                        if maker_commission <= 0.0 {
                            self.recover_commissions(rest, &signal.symbol, &[maker_order_id])
                                .await;
                        }
                        return Some(pnl);
                    }
                    let remaining = qty - maker_filled;
                    match self.enter_market_confirmed(rest, signal, remaining).await {
                        Ok(mkt) => {
                            if maker_commission > 0.0 {
                                self.state_manager
                                    .add_fees_paid(&signal.symbol, maker_commission);
                            }
                            if mkt.commission > 0.0 {
                                self.state_manager
                                    .add_fees_paid(&signal.symbol, mkt.commission);
                            }
                            let total = maker_filled + remaining;
                            let blended = if total > 0.0 {
                                (maker_filled * maker_price + remaining * mkt.price) / total
                            } else {
                                signal.price
                            };
                            let pnl = self
                                .state_manager
                                .close_position(&signal.symbol, blended, total)
                                .await;
                            if maker_commission <= 0.0 && mkt.commission <= 0.0 {
                                self.recover_commissions(
                                    rest,
                                    &signal.symbol,
                                    &[maker_order_id, mkt.order_id],
                                )
                                .await;
                            }
                            return Some(pnl);
                        }
                        Err(e) => {
                            tracing::error!(
                                "[{}] вљ пёЏ CRITICAL: РѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕРє Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёСЏ РЅРµ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґС‘РЅ ({}) вЂ” РЎР’Р•Р РРўР¬ РџРћР—РР¦РР® РќРђ Р‘РР Р–Р•",
                                self.config.name, e
                            );
                            if maker_filled > 0.0 {
                                if maker_commission > 0.0 {
                                    self.state_manager
                                        .add_fees_paid(&signal.symbol, maker_commission);
                                }
                                return Some(
                                    self.state_manager
                                        .close_position(&signal.symbol, maker_price, maker_filled)
                                        .await,
                                );
                            }
                            return None;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // вњ… SL/Timeout вЂ” РјР°СЂРєРµС‚РѕРј, Р±РµР· GTX
        match self.enter_market_confirmed(rest, signal, qty).await {
            Ok(s) => {
                if s.commission > 0.0 {
                    self.state_manager
                        .add_fees_paid(&signal.symbol, s.commission);
                }
                let exit_price = if s.price > 0.0 { s.price } else { signal.price };
                let pnl = self
                    .state_manager
                    .close_position(&signal.symbol, exit_price, qty)
                    .await;
                if s.commission <= 0.0 {
                    self.recover_commissions(rest, &signal.symbol, &[s.order_id]).await;
                }
                Some(pnl)
            }
            Err(e) => {
                tracing::error!(
                    "[{}] вќЊ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ {} РЅРµ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРѕ: {} вЂ” РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ РѕСЃС‚Р°С‘С‚СЃСЏ, РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂ (throttled)",
                    self.config.name, signal.symbol, e
                );
                None
            }
        }
    }

    fn new_client_id(&self) -> String {
        static COUNTER: std::sync::atomic::AtomicU64 = std::sync::atomic::AtomicU64::new(0);
        let n = COUNTER.fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
        let ms = std::time::SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
            .map(|d| d.as_millis())
            .unwrap_or(0);
        format!("sa{}x{}", ms, n)
    }

    pub async fn execute(&self, signal: Signal) -> Result<ExecOutcome> {
        if signal.strategy_name.starts_with("Exit_") {
            // ============================================================
            // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B: СЃРёРЅС…СЂРѕРЅРёР·Р°С†РёСЏ Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІРѕРіРѕ СЃС‚РѕРїР° (РїРѕРґС‚СЏР¶РєР°).
            // ============================================================
            if signal.strategy_name == "Exit_UpdateSL" {
                if let Some(rest) = &self.rest_client {
                    let (sl_id, _tp_id) = self.state_manager.get_protective_ids(&signal.symbol);
                    // P18: trail trigger must be a tick multiple, otherwise -1111
                    // kills BOTH stops: the old one is already cancelled here.
                    let new_sl = match self
                        .filters
                        .as_ref()
                        .and_then(|r| r.get(&signal.symbol).ok())
                        .and_then(|f| {
                            Decimal::from_f64(signal.price)
                                .map(|d| f.round_price(d).to_f64().unwrap_or(signal.price))
                        })
                    {
                        Some(v) if v > 0.0 => v,
                        _ => signal.price,
                    };
                    if (new_sl - signal.price).abs() > f64::EPSILON {
                        tracing::info!(
                            "[{}] P18: UpdateSL trigger {} -> {} (tickSize)",
                            self.config.name, signal.price, new_sl
                        );
                    }
                    if let Some(old_id_str) = sl_id {
                        let old_id: u64 = old_id_str.parse().unwrap_or(0);
                        if old_id > 0 {
                            let _ = rest.cancel_order(&signal.symbol, old_id).await;
                        }
                    }
                    let close_side = signal.side.clone();
                    match rest
                        .place_protective_order(
                            &signal.symbol,
                            &close_side,
                            true,
                            new_sl,
                            Some(format!(
                                "pr_sl_t{}",
                                chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis()
                            )),
                        )
                        .await
                    {
                        Ok(o) => {
                            self.state_manager
                                .set_sl_order_id(&signal.symbol, o.order_id.to_string())
                                .await;
                            tracing::info!(
                                "[{}] рџЋ— Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІРѕР№ СЃС‚РѕРї РїРѕРґС‚СЏРЅСѓС‚: #{} в†’ {:.8}",
                                self.config.name, o.order_id, new_sl
                            );
                        }
                        Err(e) => {
                            tracing::error!(
                                "[{}] рџљЁ РїРѕРґС‚СЏР¶РєР° СЃС‚РѕРїР° РЅРµ СѓРґР°Р»Р°СЃСЊ ({}): РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂРЅР°СЏ РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєР°",
                                self.config.name, e
                            );
                            if let Ok(o) = rest
                                .place_protective_order(
                                    &signal.symbol, &close_side, true, new_sl, None,
                                )
                                .await
                            {
                                self.state_manager
                                    .set_sl_order_id(&signal.symbol, o.order_id.to_string())
                                    .await;
                                tracing::warn!(
                                    "[{}] рџ›Ў СЃС‚РѕРї РїРѕСЃС‚Р°РІР»РµРЅ СЃРѕ 2-Р№ РїРѕРїС‹С‚РєРё #{}",
                                    self.config.name, o.order_id
                                );
                            } else {
                                tracing::error!(
                                    "[{}] рџљЁрџљЁ РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ Р±РµР· Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІРѕРіРѕ СЃС‚РѕРїР° РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РїРѕРґС‚СЏР¶РєРё вЂ” \
                                     СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ СЃРѕС„С‚-СЃС‚РѕРї; РЎР’Р•Р РРўР¬ openOrders",
                                    self.config.name
                                );
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
                return Ok(ExecOutcome::Missed("Exit_UpdateSL РІС‹РїРѕР»РЅРµРЅ".into()));
            }

            if self.simulate {
                let pnl = self
                    .state_manager
                    .close_position(&signal.symbol, signal.price, signal.quantity)
                    .await;
                if !signal.strategy_name.starts_with("Exit_Partial") {
                    self.global_risk
                        .unregister_position(&self.config.name, &signal.symbol);
                }
                return Ok(ExecOutcome::Exit(pnl));
            }
            if let Some(rest) = &self.rest_client {
                return match self.close_position_real(rest, &signal).await {
                    Some(pnl) => {
                        self.remove_protective_orders(&signal.symbol).await;
                        if !signal.strategy_name.starts_with("Exit_Partial") {
                            self.global_risk
                                .unregister_position(&self.config.name, &signal.symbol);
                        }
                        Ok(ExecOutcome::Exit(pnl))
                    }
                    None => Ok(ExecOutcome::ExitUnconfirmed),
                };
            }
            return Ok(ExecOutcome::Exit(0.0));
        }

        if self.simulate {
            self.execute_simulated(signal).await
        } else {
            self.execute_real(signal).await
        }
    }

    async fn execute_simulated(&self, signal: Signal) -> Result<ExecOutcome> {
        let sl_str = signal
            .stop_loss
            .map(|v| format!("{:.8}", v))
            .unwrap_or("вЂ”".into());
        let tp_str = signal
            .take_profit
            .map(|v| format!("{:.8}", v))
            .unwrap_or("вЂ”".into());

        let risk = signal.quantity
            * (signal.price - signal.stop_loss.unwrap_or(signal.price)).abs();
        let expected_profit = signal.quantity
            * (signal.take_profit.unwrap_or(signal.price) - signal.price).abs();

        let fee_rate = self.taker_fee();
        let expected_fees = signal.quantity * signal.price * fee_rate * 2.0;
        let net_profit = expected_profit - expected_fees;

        if expected_profit <= expected_fees {
            tracing::warn!(
                "вљ пёЏ [РЎРРњРЈР›РЇР¦РРЇ] {}: РґРІРёР¶РµРЅРёРµ {:.6} в‰¤ РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёР№ {:.6} вЂ” РІС…РѕРґ РѕС‚РєР»РѕРЅС‘РЅ",
                signal.symbol, expected_profit, expected_fees
            );
            return Ok(ExecOutcome::Missed("sim: РґРІРёР¶РµРЅРёРµ в‰¤ РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёР№".into()));
        }

        let rr = if risk > 0.0 { expected_profit / risk } else { 0.0 };
        let net_rr = if risk > 0.0 {
            net_profit / (risk + expected_fees)
        } else {
            0.0
        };

        tracing::info!("в•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђ");
        tracing::info!(
            "рџ“ќ [{}] [РЎРРњРЈР›РЇР¦РРЇ] {} {:.5} {} @ {:.8}",
            self.config.name, signal.side, signal.quantity, signal.symbol, signal.price
        );
        tracing::info!("   рџ›‘ SL: {} | рџЋЇ TP: {}", sl_str, tp_str);
        tracing::info!(
            "   вљ пёЏ Р РёСЃРє: {:.4} USDT | рџ’° Р±СЂСѓС‚С‚Рѕ: {:.4} | fee({:.4}%): {:.4} | РЅРµС‚С‚Рѕ: {:.4}",
            risk, expected_profit, fee_rate * 100.0, expected_fees, net_profit
        );
        tracing::info!("   рџ“Љ gross R:R = {:.2} | net R:R = {:.2}", rr, net_rr);
        tracing::info!("в•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђ");

        // AUDIT-P3: Р±РµР· SL СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂРёСЂСѓРµРј РєРѕРЅСЃРµСЂРІР°С‚РёРІРЅС‹Рµ 2% РѕС‚ РЅРѕРјРёРЅР°Р»Р° (РЅРµ 0).
        let sl_ref = signal.stop_loss.unwrap_or(signal.price * 0.98);
        let risk_usdt = signal.quantity * (signal.price - sl_ref).abs();

        self.global_risk
            .register_position(&self.config.name, &signal.symbol, risk_usdt);

        self.state_manager
            .add_position_with_risk(
                signal.symbol.clone(),
                signal.side.clone(),
                signal.price,
                signal.quantity,
                signal.stop_loss,
                signal.take_profit,
                signal.strategy_name.clone(),
                // AUDIT-P7: per-unit risk С‚СЂРµР№Р»РёРЅРіР° вЂ” РєР°Рє РІ P3: РЅРµС‚ SL = 2% (РЅРµ 0).
                (signal.price - signal.stop_loss.unwrap_or(signal.price * 0.98)).abs(),
                signal.atr_hint,
                signal.entry_spread
            )
            .await;

        Ok(ExecOutcome::Entry { price: signal.price, qty: signal.quantity, order_id: 0 })
    }

    async fn execute_real(&self, signal: Signal) -> Result<ExecOutcome> {
        // ============================================================
        // рџ”§ РџРђРўР§-РЎРР РћРўР«: РіРµР№С‚С‹ РІС…РѕРґР°.
        // ============================================================
        if !self.entries_allowed() {
            return Ok(ExecOutcome::Missed(
                "gate: СЃРµС‚РµРІС‹Рµ СЃР±РѕРё вЂ” РІС…РѕРґС‹ Р·Р°Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅС‹ РЅР° 15 РјРёРЅ".into(),
            ));
        }
        if self.entry_backoff_active() {
            return Ok(ExecOutcome::Missed(
                "backoff 60СЃ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ СЃРµС‚РµРІРѕРіРѕ СЃР±РѕСЏ РІС…РѕРґР°".into(),
            ));
        }

        let (safe_price, safe_qty) = match self
            .apply_filters(&signal.symbol, signal.price, signal.quantity)
        {
            Some(v) => v,
            None => {
                tracing::error!(
                    "[{}] РЅРµС‚ С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂРѕРІ РґР»СЏ {} вЂ” РѕСЂРґРµСЂ РѕС‚РєР»РѕРЅС‘РЅ (fail-safe)",
                    self.config.name, signal.symbol
                );
                return Ok(ExecOutcome::Missed("РЅРµС‚ С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂРѕРІ СЃРёРјРІРѕР»Р°".into()));
            }
        };

        if safe_qty <= 0.0 {
            tracing::error!("РћР±СЉС‘Рј СЃС‚Р°Р» 0 РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РѕРєСЂСѓРіР»РµРЅРёСЏ: {}", signal.quantity);
            return Ok(ExecOutcome::Missed("qty=0 РїРѕСЃР»Рµ С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂРѕРІ".into()));
        }

        if let Some(f) = self.filters.as_ref().and_then(|r| r.get(&signal.symbol).ok()) {
            let notional = safe_price * safe_qty;
            let min_n = f.min_notional.to_f64().unwrap_or(0.0);
            if notional < min_n {
                tracing::warn!(
                    "[{}] РЅРѕС‚Р°С†РёРѕРЅР°Р» {:.4} < min {} вЂ” РІС…РѕРґ РѕС‚РєР»РѕРЅС‘РЅ",
                    self.config.name, notional, min_n
                );
                return Ok(ExecOutcome::Missed(format!("notional {:.4} < min {}", notional, min_n)));
            }
        }

        let rest = self
            .rest_client
            .as_ref()
            .ok_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("REST not init"))?;

        // ============================================================
        // рџ”§ РђРќРўР-Р‘РђРќ: pre-entry reconcile РЅРµ С‡Р°С‰Рµ СЂР°Р·Р° РІ 30СЃ.
        // РџСЂРё РїР°С‡РєРµ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»РѕРІ (РєР°Рє 19:03вЂ“19:05) РЅРµ С€С‚РѕСЂРјРёРј Р±РёСЂР¶Сѓ
        // РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР°РјРё РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ Рё РЅР°РєРѕРїРёР»Рѕ -1003 Р±Р°РЅ.
        // ============================================================
        {
            let mut last = self.last_reconcile_attempt.lock().unwrap();
            if let Some(t) = *last {
                if t.elapsed() < std::time::Duration::from_secs(120) {
                    return Ok(ExecOutcome::Missed(
                        "reconcile-РєСЌС€: СЃР»РёС€РєРѕРј С‡Р°СЃС‚Рѕ вЂ” РІС…РѕРґ РїСЂРѕРїСѓС‰РµРЅ".into(),
                    ));
                }
            }
            *last = Some(std::time::Instant::now());
        }

        // ============================================================
        // рџ”§ РџРђРўР§-РЎРР РћРўР«: СЃРІРµСЂРєР° Р±РёСЂР¶Р°/СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ РџР•Р Р•Р” РІС…РѕРґРѕРј.
        // IP-ban (-1003) в†’ Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІРєР° РІС…РѕРґРѕРІ РЅР° 60 РјРёРЅ (Р¶С‘СЃС‚РєРёР№ РІР°СЂРёР°РЅС‚ вЂ”
        // Р±РµР· С…СЂСѓРїРєРѕРіРѕ РїР°СЂСЃРёРЅРіР° РІСЂРµРјРµРЅРё Р±Р°РЅР°).
        // ============================================================
        match rest.get_positions().await {
            Ok(all) => {
                if let Some(p) = all
                    .iter()
                    .find(|p| p.symbol.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&signal.symbol))
                {
                    if p.qty.abs() > 0.0 && !self.state_manager.has_position(&signal.symbol) {
                        tracing::error!(
                            "[{}] рџљЁ RECONCILE-PRE-ENTRY: РЅР° Р±РёСЂР¶Рµ РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ {:.5} {}, Р±РѕС‚ flat вЂ” \
                             РІС…РѕРґ Р—РђРџР Р•Р©РЃРќ (СЃРёСЂРѕС‚СЃРєР°СЏ/С‡СѓР¶Р°СЏ РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ)",
                            self.config.name, p.qty.abs(), p.symbol
                        );
                        return Ok(ExecOutcome::Missed(
                            "reconcile: РЅР° Р±РёСЂР¶Рµ РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ, Р±РѕС‚РѕРј РЅРµ СѓРїСЂР°РІР»СЏРµРјР°СЏ".into(),
                        ));
                    }
                }
            }
            Err(e) => {
                let msg = e.to_string();
                if msg.contains("-1003") || msg.contains("banned") {
                    // рџ”§ IP-BAN: Р±РёСЂР¶Р° Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‚РёР»Р° REST вЂ” РІС…РѕРґС‹ РІРЅРёР· РЅР° 60 РјРёРЅСѓС‚
                    *self.entries_blocked_until.lock().unwrap() = Some(
                        std::time::Instant::now() + std::time::Duration::from_secs(3600),
                    );
                    tracing::error!(
                        "[{}] рџљЁ IP BANNED Р±РёСЂР¶РµР№ (-1003) вЂ” РІС…РѕРґС‹ Р·Р°Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅС‹ РЅР° 60 РјРёРЅ. \
                         РќР• СЂРµСЃС‚Р°СЂС‚РёС‚СЊ СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ: РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ РїСЂРѕРґР»РµРІР°РµС‚ Р±Р°РЅ",
                        self.config.name
                    );
                } else {
                    tracing::warn!(
                        "[{}] pre-entry reconcile РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРµРЅ ({}) вЂ” РІС…РѕРґ РїСЂРѕРїСѓС‰РµРЅ",
                        self.config.name, msg
                    );
                }
                self.record_net_fail();
                return Ok(ExecOutcome::Missed("reconcile РЅРµРґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРµРЅ/Р±Р°РЅ".into()));
            }
        }

        // ============================================================
        // рџ”§ РџРР’РћРў B-MARKET: РјР°СЂС€СЂСѓС‚РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ РІС…РѕРґР°.
        // ============================================================
        let is_passive =
            self.config.market_type == "futures" && signal.strategy_name == "SpreadCapture";
        let is_momentum = signal.strategy_name.ends_with("_MOMENTUM");

        let fill: Option<OrderResult> = if is_momentum {
            match self.enter_market_confirmed(rest, &signal, safe_qty).await {
                Ok(s) => Some(s),
                Err(e) => {
                    let msg = e.to_string();
                    if msg.contains("РЎРЈР”Р¬Р‘Рђ РќР•РР—Р’Р•РЎРўРќРђ") {
                        tracing::error!(
                            "[{}] рџљЁ momentum market-РІС…РѕРґ РЅРµ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґС‘РЅ (СЃРµС‚СЊ): {} вЂ” \
                             СЃР»РµРґРёС‚ periodic reconciliation + pre-entry reconcile",
                            self.config.name, msg
                        );
                        self.record_net_fail();
                    } else if msg.contains("Binance") {
                        tracing::warn!(
                            "[{}] momentum market-РІС…РѕРґ РѕС‚РєР»РѕРЅС‘РЅ Р±РёСЂР¶РµР№: {}",
                            self.config.name, msg
                        );
                    } else {
                        tracing::warn!(
                            "[{}] momentum market-РІС…РѕРґ РЅРµ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏР»СЃСЏ: {}",
                            self.config.name, msg
                        );
                    }
                    None
                }
            }
        } else if is_passive {
            self.enter_maker_no_chase(rest, &signal, safe_price, safe_qty)
                .await?
        } else {
            match self.enter_market_confirmed(rest, &signal, safe_qty).await {
                Ok(s) => Some(s),
                Err(e) => {
                    let msg = e.to_string();
                    if msg.contains("РЎРЈР”Р¬Р‘Рђ РќР•РР—Р’Р•РЎРўРќРђ") {
                        tracing::error!(
                            "[{}] рџљЁ market-РІС…РѕРґ РЅРµ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґС‘РЅ (СЃРµС‚СЊ): {} вЂ” \
                             СЃР»РµРґРёС‚ РїРµСЂРёРѕРґРёС‡РµСЃРєРёР№ reconciliation + pre-entry reconcile",
                            self.config.name, msg
                        );
                        self.record_net_fail();
                    } else if msg.contains("Binance") {
                        tracing::warn!(
                            "[{}] market-РІС…РѕРґ РѕС‚РєР»РѕРЅС‘РЅ Р±РёСЂР¶РµР№: {}",
                            self.config.name, msg
                        );
                    } else {
                        tracing::warn!(
                            "[{}] market-РІС…РѕРґ РЅРµ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏР»СЃСЏ: {}",
                            self.config.name, msg
                        );
                    }
                    None
                }
            }
        };

        let Some(order) = fill else {
            return Ok(ExecOutcome::Missed("РІС…РѕРґ РЅРµ РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРµРЅ".into()));
        };

        if order.executed_qty <= 0.0 && order.status != "FILLED" {
            return Ok(ExecOutcome::Missed(format!(
                "РѕСЂРґРµСЂ {} Р±РµР· РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРёСЏ (СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ {})",
                order.order_id, order.status
            )));
        }

        let fill_price = if order.price > 0.0 { order.price } else { safe_price };
        let mut fill_qty = if order.executed_qty > 0.0 { order.executed_qty } else { safe_qty };   // в†ђ mut
        let fee = if is_momentum || !is_passive { self.taker_fee() } else { self.maker_fee() };

        tracing::info!(
            "вњ… Р’РҐРћР” РРЎРџРћР›РќР•Рќ [{}] {} {:.5} {} @ {:.8} (СЃРёРіРЅР°Р» {:.8}) | feeв‰€{:.4}% | id={} | С„Р°РєС‚-РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёСЏ={:.6}",
            self.config.name,
            signal.side,
            fill_qty,
            signal.symbol,
            fill_price,
            safe_price,
            fee * 100.0,
            order.order_id,
            order.commission
        );

        // ============================================================
        // рџ”§ SLIPPAGE GUARD: СЃР»РёРїРїРµРґР¶ > 0.3% = РіРµРѕРјРµС‚СЂРёСЏ СЃРґРµР»РєРё РёСЃРїРѕСЂС‡РµРЅР°
        // (СЂРёСЃРє/РµРґ СЂР°Р·РґСѓР»Рѕ, TP/SL РЅРµ РЅР° С‚РµС… РґРёСЃС‚Р°РЅС†РёСЏС… вЂ” РєРµР№СЃ 06:01:
        //  SELL РїРѕ 0.00428010 РїСЂРё TP 0.00429960 Р’Р«РЁР• РІС…РѕРґР° в†’ Р»РѕР¶РЅС‹Р№ TP).
        // РќРµРјРµРґР»РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ РІСЃС‚СЂРµС‡РЅС‹Р№ MARKET С‚РµРј Р¶Рµ РѕР±СЉС‘РјРѕРј, Р·Р°С‚РµРј
        // positionRisk-СЃРІРµСЂРєР°: flat в†’ РІС‹С…РѕРґ; РѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕРє в†’ РІРµРґС‘Рј РґР°Р»СЊС€Рµ.
        // ============================================================
        let slip = if signal.price > 0.0 {
            (fill_price - signal.price).abs() / signal.price
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        if slip > 0.003 {
            let rev_side = if signal.side == "BUY" { "SELL" } else { "BUY" };
            tracing::error!(
                "[{}] рџљЁ SLIPPAGE {:.2}% > 0.3% (СЃРёРіРЅР°Р» {:.8} в†’ РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРѕ {:.8}) вЂ” \
                 РѕС‚РєР°С‚ РІСЃС‚СЂРµС‡РЅС‹Рј market {} {}",
                self.config.name, slip * 100.0, signal.price, fill_price,
                rev_side, fill_qty
            );

            let mut rev = signal.clone();
            rev.side = rev_side.to_string();
            rev.price = fill_price;
            rev.quantity = fill_qty;
            rev.strategy_name = format!("{}_SLIP_CANCEL", signal.strategy_name);

            // Р’СЃС‚СЂРµС‡РЅС‹Р№ market: РїР°РґРµРЅРёРµ = РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ РѕСЃС‚Р°С‘С‚СЃСЏ (РІРµРґС‘Рј СЃРѕС„С‚-РјРµРЅРµРґР¶РјРµРЅС‚РѕРј)
            if let Err(e) = self.enter_market_confirmed(rest, &rev, fill_qty).await {
                tracing::error!(
                    "[{}] рџљЁрџљЁ РѕС‚РєР°С‚ РќР• РџРћР”РўР’Р•Р Р–Р”РЃРќ ({}): РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ {} {} РѕСЃС‚Р°С‘С‚СЃСЏ РЅР° Р±РёСЂР¶Рµ вЂ” \
                     РІРµРґС‘С‚ СЃРѕС„С‚-РјРµРЅРµРґР¶РјРµРЅС‚ + РїРµСЂРёРѕРґРёС‡РµСЃРєРёР№ reconciliation",
                    self.config.name, e, signal.side, fill_qty
                );
            }

            // Р¤Р°РєС‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРёР№ РѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕРє Сѓ Р±РёСЂР¶Рё (РІСЃС‚СЂРµС‡РЅС‹Р№ РјРѕРі РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚СЊСЃСЏ С‡Р°СЃС‚РёС‡РЅРѕ)
            let actual = match rest.get_positions().await {
                Ok(all) => all
                    .iter()
                    .find(|p| p.symbol.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&signal.symbol))
                    .map(|p| p.qty.abs())
                    .unwrap_or(0.0),
                Err(_) => fill_qty, // РЅРµ СЃРјРѕРіР»Рё СЃРїСЂРѕСЃРёС‚СЊ вЂ” РєРѕРЅСЃРµСЂРІР°С‚РёРІРЅРѕ СЃС‡РёС‚Р°РµРј РІСЃС‘ Р¶РёРІС‹Рј
            };

            if actual <= 0.0 {
                tracing::warn!(
                    "[{}] вњ… РѕС‚РєР°С‚ Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€С‘РЅ вЂ” РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё РЅРµС‚, РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёРё СЃРїРёСЃР°РЅС‹ Р±РёСЂР¶РµР№",
                    self.config.name
                );
                return Ok(ExecOutcome::Missed(format!(
                    "SLIPPAGE {:.2}% вЂ” РІС…РѕРґ РѕС‚РєР°С‡РµРЅ РІСЃС‚СЂРµС‡РЅС‹Рј market",
                    slip * 100.0
                )));
            }

            tracing::warn!(
                "[{}] РѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕРє РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РѕС‚РєР°С‚Р°: {:.5} вЂ” СЂРµРіРёСЃС‚СЂРёСЂСѓСЋ СЃ С„Р°РєС‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРёРј РѕР±СЉС‘РјРѕРј",
                self.config.name, actual
            );
            fill_qty = actual;
        }

        // ============================================================
        // рџ”§ RE-ANCHOR: TP/SL РїРµСЂРµСЃС‡РёС‚С‹РІР°СЋС‚СЃСЏ РѕС‚ Р¤РђРљРўРР§Р•РЎРљРћР™ С†РµРЅС‹
        // РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРёСЏ (РѕС„С„СЃРµС‚С‹ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»Р° РїРµСЂРµРЅРѕСЃСЏС‚СЃСЏ РЅР° fill). РљРµР№СЃ 06:01
        // Р±РµР· СЌС‚РѕРіРѕ: TP С€РѕСЂС‚Р° РѕРєР°Р·Р°Р»СЃСЏ Р’Р«РЁР• С„Р°РєС‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРѕРіРѕ РІС…РѕРґР° в†’
        // РјРіРЅРѕРІРµРЅРЅС‹Р№ Р»РѕР¶РЅС‹Р№ TakeProfit.
        // ============================================================
        let mut signal = signal;
        if is_momentum && signal.price > 0.0 && fill_price > 0.0 {
            let sig_entry = signal.price;
            let sl_off = ((sig_entry - signal.stop_loss.unwrap_or(sig_entry)).abs()) / sig_entry;
            let tp_off = ((signal.take_profit.unwrap_or(sig_entry) - sig_entry).abs()) / sig_entry;
            let is_long = signal.side == "BUY";
            let (new_sl, new_tp) = if is_long {
                (fill_price * (1.0 - sl_off), fill_price * (1.0 + tp_off))
            } else {
                (fill_price * (1.0 + sl_off), fill_price * (1.0 - tp_off))
            };
            tracing::warn!(
                "[{}] рџ“Џ RE-ANCHOR: fill {:.8} vs СЃРёРіРЅР°Р» {:.8} (СЃРјРµС‰РµРЅРёРµ {:+.3}%) | SL в†’ {:.8}, TP в†’ {:.8}",
                self.config.name, fill_price, sig_entry,
                (fill_price - sig_entry) / sig_entry * 100.0, new_sl, new_tp
            );
            signal.stop_loss = Some(new_sl);
            signal.take_profit = Some(new_tp);
        }

        // AUDIT-P3: Р±РµР· SL вЂ” РєРѕРЅСЃРµСЂРІР°С‚РёРІРЅС‹Рµ 2% РѕС‚ РЅРѕРјРёРЅР°Р»Р° fill (РЅРµ 0).
        let sl_ref = signal.stop_loss.unwrap_or(fill_price * 0.98);
        let risk_usdt = fill_qty * (fill_price - sl_ref).abs();

        self.global_risk
            .register_position(&self.config.name, &signal.symbol, risk_usdt);

        let initial_risk = (fill_price - signal.stop_loss.unwrap_or(fill_price * 0.98)).abs(); // AUDIT-P3: РЅРµС‚ SL = 2% (РЅРµ 0)
        self.state_manager
            .add_position_with_risk(
                signal.symbol.clone(),
                signal.side.clone(),
                fill_price,
                fill_qty,
                signal.stop_loss,
                signal.take_profit,
                signal.strategy_name.clone(),
                initial_risk,
                signal.atr_hint,
                signal.entry_spread,
            )
            .await;

        if order.commission > 0.0 {
            self.state_manager
                .add_fees_paid(&signal.symbol, order.commission);
        } else {
            self.recover_commissions(rest, &signal.symbol, &[order.order_id])
                .await;
        }

        // ============================================================
        // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B: Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІС‹Рµ Р·Р°С‰РёС‚РЅС‹Рµ РѕСЂРґРµСЂР° (STOP_MARKET + TP_MARKET)
        // вЂ” РЅР° Р Р•-РђРќРљР•Р Р•РќРќР«РҐ С†РµРЅР°С….
        // ============================================================
        self.place_protective_orders(rest, &signal, order.order_id).await;

        Ok(ExecOutcome::Entry { price: fill_price, qty: fill_qty, order_id: order.order_id })
    }
}

/// РћС†РµРЅРєР° slippage РІ РґРѕР»СЏС… (РЅРµ bps). Р”Р»СЏ РјРµРј-РєРѕРёРЅРѕРІ РЅР° РїРѕСЂСЏРґРѕРє РІС‹С€Рµ BTC.
pub struct SlippageCalculator;

impl SlippageCalculator {
    /// Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ РѕР¶РёРґР°РµРјС‹Р№ slippage РІ РґРѕР»СЏС… С†РµРЅС‹.
    ///
    /// Р—РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏ РїРѕ СЃРёРјРІРѕР»Р°Рј (РЅР° РѕСЃРЅРѕРІРµ Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІРѕР№ СЃС‚Р°С‚РёСЃС‚РёРєРё Рё РѕС‚С‡С‘С‚Р°):
    /// - BTC/ETH:  1 bps  (0.0001) вЂ” РіР»СѓР±РѕРєР°СЏ Р»РёРєРІРёРґРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ
    /// - SOL/XRP:  3 bps  (0.0003)
    /// - PEPE/DOGS: 50 bps (0.005)  вЂ” РЅРёР·РєР°СЏ Р»РёРєРІРёРґРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ, С‚РѕРЅРєРёР№ СЃС‚Р°РєР°РЅ
    /// - default:  5 bps  (0.0005)
    pub fn calculate_slippage(&self, symbol: &str, _price: f64, _quantity: f64) -> f64 {
        let s = symbol.to_uppercase();
        if s.contains("BTC") || s.contains("ETH") {
            0.0001
        } else if s.contains("SOL") || s.contains("XRP") || s.contains("BNB") {
            0.0003
            // РЎРўРђР›Рћ вЂ” РєР°Р»РёР±СЂРѕРІР°РЅРѕ РЅР° 51M С‚РёРєРѕРІ СЃ РїСЂРѕРґР°:
        } else if s.contains("PEPE") || s.contains("DOGS") || s.contains("SHIB") || s.contains("BONK") {
            0.0002  // 2 bps вЂ” СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ РґР»СЏ РјР°Р»С‹С… РѕСЂРґРµСЂРѕРІ (p95 = 0.0206% РЅР° $100 notional)
        } else {
            0.0005
        }
    }
}
