pub fn spawn_metrics_server() -> tokio::task::JoinHandle<()> {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        use tokio::io::AsyncWriteExt;
        use tokio::net::TcpListener;
        let listener = TcpListener::bind("0.0.0.0:9090").await.unwrap();
        tracing::info!("Metrics server on 0.0.0.0:9090");
        loop {
            let (mut socket, _) = listener.accept().await.unwrap();
            let mut buffer = [0u8; 4096];
            let _ = tokio::io::AsyncReadExt::read(&mut socket, &mut buffer).await;
            let mut body = Vec::new();
            let encoder = TextEncoder::new();
            let metric_families = REGISTRY.gather();
            encoder.encode(&metric_families, &mut body).unwrap();
            let response = format!(
                "HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Type: text/plain; version=0.0.4\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n",
                body.len()
            );
            let _ = socket.write_all(response.as_bytes()).await;
            let _ = socket.write_all(&body).await;
            let _ = socket.shutdown().await;
        }
    })
}
=== C:\Users\BASE_PC\scalping_agentsGLM\src\risk\circuit_breaker.rs ===
use parking_lot::Mutex;
use std::collections::VecDeque;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

#[derive(Clone)]
pub struct CircuitBreaker {
    inner: Arc<Mutex<Inner>>,
}

struct Inner {
    losses: VecDeque<Instant>,
    max_losses: usize,
    window: Duration,
    /// вњ… bk: cooldown РёР· РєРѕРЅС„РёРіР° (СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ Р±С‹Р» Р·Р°С…Р°СЂРґРєРѕР¶РµРЅ 300 СЃРµРє)
    cooldown: Duration,
    is_open: bool,
    last_trip: Option<Instant>,
}

impl Default for CircuitBreaker {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new(5, 3600, 600)
    }
}

impl CircuitBreaker {
    /// * `max_losses` вЂ” СЃРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ СѓР±С‹С‚РєРѕРІ РїРѕРґСЂСЏРґ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹РІР°СЋС‚ breaker
    /// * `window_secs` вЂ” РѕРєРЅРѕ, Р·Р° РєРѕС‚РѕСЂРѕРµ СЃС‡РёС‚Р°СЋС‚СЃСЏ СѓР±С‹С‚РєРё
    /// * `cooldown_secs` вЂ” СЃРєРѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґРµСЂР¶Р°С‚СЊ breaker РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚С‹Рј РїРѕСЃР»Рµ СЃСЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°РЅРёСЏ
    pub fn new(max_losses: usize, window_secs: u64, cooldown_secs: u64) -> Self {
        Self {
            inner: Arc::new(Mutex::new(Inner {
                losses: VecDeque::new(),
                max_losses,
                window: Duration::from_secs(window_secs),
                cooldown: Duration::from_secs(cooldown_secs),
                is_open: false,
                last_trip: None,
            })),
        }
    }

    pub fn record_loss(&self) {
        let mut i = self.inner.lock();
        let now = Instant::now();

        // Р§РёСЃС‚РёРј СѓСЃС‚Р°СЂРµРІС€РёРµ РїРѕС‚РµСЂРё Р·Р° РѕРєРЅРѕРј
        while let Some(&t) = i.losses.front() {
            if now.duration_since(t) > i.window {
                i.losses.pop_front();
            } else {
                break;
            }
        }

        // рџ”§ РџРђРўР§: Р»РѕРі РїСЂРѕРґР»РµРЅРёСЏ РєСѓР»РґР°СѓРЅР° (СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ РјРѕР»С‡Р° РґРІРёРіР°Р» last_trip)
        if i.is_open {
            tracing::warn!(
                "вЏі CIRCUIT BREAKER: СѓР±С‹С‚РѕРє РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё cooldown вЂ” С‚Р°Р№РјРµСЂ РїСЂРѕРґР»С‘РЅ"
            );
        }

        i.losses.push_back(now);

        if i.losses.len() >= i.max_losses {
            if !i.is_open {
                tracing::warn!(
                    "рџ”ґ CIRCUIT BREAKER TRIP: {} СѓР±С‹С‚РєРѕРІ Р·Р° {:?}",
                    i.losses.len(),
                    i.window
                );
            }
            i.is_open = true;
            i.last_trip = Some(now);
        }
    }

    /// РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР°: РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚ Р»Рё breaker.
    ///
    /// РџСЂРё РёСЃС‚РµС‡РµРЅРёРё cooldown вЂ” РїРѕР»РЅС‹Р№ СЃР±СЂРѕСЃ СЃРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёСЏ.
    pub fn is_open(&self) -> bool {
        let mut i = self.inner.lock();

        if i.is_open {
            if let Some(t) = i.last_trip {
                if t.elapsed() >= i.cooldown {
                    tracing::info!(
                        "рџџў CIRCUIT BREAKER RESET (cooldown {:?} РёСЃС‚С‘Рє)",
                        i.cooldown
                    );
                    i.is_open = false;
                    i.losses.clear();
                    i.last_trip = None;
                    return false;
                }
            }
            return true;
        }

        false
    }

    /// РџСЂРёРЅСѓРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СЃР±СЂРѕСЃ (РЅР°РїСЂРёРјРµСЂ, СЂСѓС‡РЅРѕР№ РёР»Рё РїРѕ РёСЃС‚РµС‡РµРЅРёРё РґРЅСЏ)
    pub fn reset(&self) {
        let mut i = self.inner.lock();
        i.is_open = false;
        i.losses.clear();
        i.last_trip = None;
        tracing::info!("рџџў CIRCUIT BREAKER СЃР±СЂРѕС€РµРЅ РІСЂСѓС‡РЅСѓСЋ");
    }

    /// Р“РµС‚С‚РµСЂС‹ РґР»СЏ РґРёР°РіРЅРѕСЃС‚РёРєРё
    pub fn losses_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.inner.lock().losses.len()
    }

    pub fn cooldown_remaining(&self) -> Option<Duration> {
        let i = self.inner.lock();
        if let Some(t) = i.last_trip {
            let elapsed = t.elapsed();
            if elapsed < i.cooldown {
                return Some(i.cooldown - elapsed);
            }
        }
        None
    }

    // ============================================================
    // рџ”§ РџРђРўР§: РїРµСЂСЃРёСЃС‚ РєСѓР»РґР°СѓРЅР° вЂ” СЂРµСЃС‚Р°СЂС‚ РїСЂРѕС†РµСЃСЃР° Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ РЅРµ СЃР±СЂР°СЃС‹РІР°РµС‚
    // РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚С‹Р№ breaker (СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ СЂРµСЃС‚Р°СЂС‚ = РїРѕР»РЅС‹Р№ СЃР±СЂРѕСЃ Р·Р°С‰РёС‚С‹)
    // ============================================================

    /// РћСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕРє РєСѓР»РґР°СѓРЅР° РІ РјСЃ (0 = breaker РЅРµ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚). РџРёС€РµС‚СЃСЏ РІ risk_stats.
    pub fn cooldown_remaining_ms(&self) -> u64 {
        let i = self.inner.lock();
        if i.is_open {
            if let Some(t) = i.last_trip {
                let elapsed = t.elapsed();
                if elapsed < i.cooldown {
                    return (i.cooldown - elapsed).as_millis() as u64;
                }
            }
        }
        0
    }

    /// Р’РѕСЃСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚РѕРіРѕ breaker РїРѕСЃР»Рµ СЂРµСЃС‚Р°СЂС‚Р° СЃ РѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РєРѕРј РєСѓР»РґР°СѓРЅР°.
    pub fn restore_open_with_remaining(&self, remaining_ms: u64) {
        if remaining_ms == 0 {
            return;
        }
        let mut i = self.inner.lock();
        let back = i.cooldown.saturating_sub(Duration::from_millis(remaining_ms));
        // checked_sub: Instant РЅРµ РјРѕР¶РµС‚ СѓР№С‚Рё В«РґРѕ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚Р° РїСЂРѕС†РµСЃСЃР°В» вЂ”
        // РµСЃР»Рё Р°РїС‚Р°Р№Рј РјРµРЅСЊС€Рµ РѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РєР° РєСѓР»РґР°СѓРЅР°, СЃС‡РёС‚Р°РµРј С‚СЂРёРї С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ С‡С‚Рѕ.
        i.last_trip = Some(Instant::now().checked_sub(back).unwrap_or_else(Instant::now));
        i.is_open = true;
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::thread::sleep;

    #[test]
    fn test_breaker_trips_after_max_losses() {
        let cb = CircuitBreaker::new(3, 3600, 60);
        assert!(!cb.is_open());

        cb.record_loss();
        assert!(!cb.is_open(), "1 loss вЂ” breaker Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚");

        cb.record_loss();
        assert!(!cb.is_open(), "2 losses вЂ” breaker Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚");

        cb.record_loss();
        assert!(cb.is_open(), "3 losses вЂ” breaker РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊСЃСЏ");

        assert_eq!(cb.losses_count(), 3);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_breaker_ignores_old_losses_outside_window() {
        let cb = CircuitBreaker::new(3, 1, 60);

        cb.record_loss();
        cb.record_loss();
        sleep(Duration::from_millis(1100));
        cb.record_loss();

        assert!(!cb.is_open(), "СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Рµ РїРѕС‚РµСЂРё РІС‹С€Р»Рё РёР· РѕРєРЅР°");
        assert!(cb.losses_count() <= 2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_breaker_manual_reset() {
        let cb = CircuitBreaker::new(2, 3600, 600);
        cb.record_loss();
        cb.record_loss();
        assert!(cb.is_open());

        cb.reset();
        assert!(!cb.is_open());
        assert_eq!(cb.losses_count(), 0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_breaker_cooldown_from_config() {
        let cb = CircuitBreaker::new(1, 3600, 0);
        cb.record_loss();
        assert!(!cb.is_open(), "cooldown 0 РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ СЃСЂР°Р·Сѓ СЃР±СЂРѕСЃРёС‚СЊ breaker");
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_cooldown_remaining() {
        let cb = CircuitBreaker::new(1, 3600, 600);
        cb.record_loss();
        let remaining = cb.cooldown_remaining();
        assert!(remaining.is_some());
        assert!(remaining.unwrap() <= Duration::from_secs(600));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_persist_restore_roundtrip() {
        let cb = CircuitBreaker::new(1, 3600, 600);
        cb.record_loss();
        let saved = cb.cooldown_remaining_ms();
        assert!(saved > 590_000 && saved <= 600_000);

        // В«Р РµСЃС‚Р°СЂС‚В»: РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ breaker + РІРѕСЃСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёРµ
        let cb2 = CircuitBreaker::new(1, 3600, 600);
        assert!(!cb2.is_open());
        cb2.restore_open_with_remaining(saved);
        assert!(cb2.is_open(), "РїРѕСЃР»Рµ restore breaker РґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅ Р±С‹С‚СЊ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚");
        let left = cb2.cooldown_remaining_ms();
        assert!(left <= saved, "РѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕРє РЅРµ РјРѕР¶РµС‚ РІС‹СЂР°СЃС‚Рё");
    }
}

use dashmap::DashMap;
use std::sync::Arc;

/// вњ… 4Р± (Рљ-1): РћР”РРќ СЌРєР·РµРјРїР»СЏСЂ РЅР° РІРµСЃСЊ РїСЂРѕС†РµСЃСЃ (СЃРѕР·РґР°С‘С‚СЃСЏ РІ main).
/// РљР»СЋС‡ вЂ” В«Р‘РћРў/РЎРРњР’РћР›В»: РґРІР° Р±РѕС‚Р° РЅР° РѕРґРЅРѕРј СЃРёРјРІРѕР»Рµ РЅРµ Р·Р°С‚РёСЂР°СЋС‚ Р·Р°РїРёСЃРё РґСЂСѓРі РґСЂСѓРіР°.
pub struct GlobalRiskManager {
    /// В«Р‘РћРў/РЎРРњР’РћР›В» в†’ СЂРёСЃРє РІ USDT
    positions: Arc<DashMap<String, f64>>,
}

impl Default for GlobalRiskManager {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self::new()
    }
}

impl GlobalRiskManager {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            positions: Arc::new(DashMap::new()),
        }
    }

    fn key(bot: &str, symbol: &str) -> String {
        format!("{}/{}", bot.to_uppercase(), symbol.to_uppercase())
    }

    /// Р РµРіРёСЃС‚СЂРёСЂСѓРµРј РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЋ РѕС‚ РёРјРµРЅРё РєРѕРЅРєСЂРµС‚РЅРѕРіРѕ Р±РѕС‚Р°
    pub fn register_position(&self, bot: &str, symbol: &str, risk_usdt: f64) {
        self.positions.insert(Self::key(bot, symbol), risk_usdt.max(0.0));
    }

    pub fn unregister_position(&self, bot: &str, symbol: &str) {
        self.positions.remove(&Self::key(bot, symbol));
    }

    /// Р’СЃРµРіРѕ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚С‹С… РїРѕР·РёС†РёР№ РџРћ Р’РЎР•Рњ Р±РѕС‚Р°Рј
    pub fn active_positions_count(&self) -> usize {
        self.positions.len()
    }

    /// РЎСѓРјРјР°СЂРЅС‹Р№ СЂРёСЃРє РџРћ Р’РЎР•Рњ Р±РѕС‚Р°Рј, USDT
    pub fn total_risk(&self) -> f64 {
        self.positions.iter().map(|e| *e.value()).sum()
    }
}
// ==== PATCH P15: portfolio daily stop + KILL file (appended by patch_p15d) ====
// State lives in a process-wide static: no changes to the original struct.
// KILL file: create an empty file named KILL in the project root to block entries.
#[derive(Default)]
struct P15PortfolioState {
    equity_base: f64,
    realized_today: f64,
    day_no: u64,
    stop_pct_cfg: f64,
}

static P15_PORTFOLIO: std::sync::Mutex<Option<P15PortfolioState>> = std::sync::Mutex::new(None);

impl GlobalRiskManager {
    fn p15_day_no() -> u64 {
        std::time::SystemTime::now()
            .duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH)
            .map(|d| d.as_secs() / 86400)
            .unwrap_or(0)
    }

    fn p15_lock() -> std::sync::MutexGuard<'static, Option<P15PortfolioState>> {
        match P15_PORTFOLIO.lock() {
            Ok(g) => g,
            Err(poisoned) => poisoned.into_inner(),
        }
    }

    fn p15_day_file() -> &'static str {
        "data/p15_portfolio_day.txt"
    }

    pub fn set_equity_base(&self, equity: f64) {
        let mut g = Self::p15_lock();
        let s = g.get_or_insert_with(P15PortfolioState::default);
        s.equity_base = equity;
        if s.stop_pct_cfg == 0.0 {
            s.stop_pct_cfg = 5.0; // default portfolio daily stop: -5% of equity base
        }
    }

    pub fn load_persisted_day(&self) {
        let mut g = Self::p15_lock();
        let s = g.get_or_insert_with(P15PortfolioState::default);
        s.day_no = Self::p15_day_no();
        if let Ok(txt) = std::fs::read_to_string(Self::p15_day_file()) {
            let parts: Vec<&str> = txt.trim().split_whitespace().collect();
            if parts.len() >= 2 {
                if let Ok(day) = parts[0].parse::<u64>() {
                    if day == s.day_no {
                        if let Ok(pnl) = parts[1].parse::<f64>() {
                            s.realized_today = pnl;
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        tracing::info!(
            "[P15] portfolio daily-stop armed | day={} | realized_today={:.4} | stop=-{:.1}% of {:.2}",
            s.day_no, s.realized_today, s.stop_pct_cfg, s.equity_base
        );
    }

    pub fn stop_pct(&self) -> f64 {
        let g = Self::p15_lock();
        match g.as_ref() {
            Some(s) => s.stop_pct_cfg,
            None => 5.0,
        }
    }

    pub fn entries_allowed(&self) -> Result<(), String> {
        if std::path::Path::new("KILL").exists() {
            return Err(String::from("KILL file present - entries disabled"));
        }
        let g = Self::p15_lock();
        if let Some(s) = g.as_ref() {
            if s.equity_base > 0.0 && s.stop_pct_cfg > 0.0 {
                let limit = s.equity_base * s.stop_pct_cfg / 100.0;
                if s.realized_today <= -limit {
                    return Err(format!(
                        "portfolio daily stop hit: realized {:.4} <= -{:.4}",
                        s.realized_today, limit
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn add_closed_pnl(&self, pnl: f64) {
        let snapshot;
        {
            let mut g = Self::p15_lock();
            let s = g.get_or_insert_with(P15PortfolioState::default);
            let today = Self::p15_day_no();
            if s.day_no != today {
                s.day_no = today;
                s.realized_today = 0.0; // new UTC day - reset
            }
            s.realized_today += pnl;
            snapshot = (s.day_no, s.realized_today);
        }
        let _ = std::fs::create_dir_all("data");
        let _ = std::fs::write(
            Self::p15_day_file(),
            format!("{} {:.6}\n", snapshot.0, snapshot.1),
        );
    }
}

pub mod circuit_breaker;

use crate::config::{BotConfig, GlobalConfig};

/// вњ… bk9: РјРёРЅРёРјСѓРј СЃРґРµР»РѕРє, РїРѕСЃР»Рµ РєРѕС‚РѕСЂРѕРіРѕ РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј EV.
const MIN_TRADES_FOR_EV_CHECK: u32 = 30;

/// вњ… bk9: РїРѕСЂРѕРі EV (РІ USDT РЅР° СЃРґРµР»РєСѓ), РЅРёР¶Рµ РєРѕС‚РѕСЂРѕРіРѕ СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ РїСЂРёР·РЅР°С‘С‚СЃСЏ СѓР±С‹С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕР№.
const MIN_EV_USDT: f64 = 0.0;

/// рџ”§ PROP-GUARD: Р»РёРјРёС‚ РїСЂРѕСЃР°РґРєРё СЌРєРІРёС‚Рё РѕС‚ РїРёРєР° (РґРѕР»СЏ). Р”РµС„РѕР»С‚ 0.20
/// РґР»СЏ С‚РµСЃС‚РЅРµС‚-СЌРєСЃРїРµСЂРёРјРµРЅС‚Р°. РџСЂРѕРї-РЅРѕСЂРјР° РґР»СЏ СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ СЃС‡С‘С‚Р°: 0.06вЂ“0.10 вЂ”
/// РІС‹СЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЊ С‡РµСЂРµР· .env PROP_MAX_DD_PCT РїСЂРё РїРµСЂРµС…РѕРґРµ РЅР° Р±РѕРµРІРѕР№ СЂРµР¶РёРј.
const PROP_DD_DEFAULT: f64 = 0.20;

// ============================================================
// рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ C: СЂРµР¶РёРјС‹ РІС‹Р¶РёРІР°РЅРёСЏ (GROWTH / SURVIVAL / HALT)
// ============================================================
/// РћРєРЅРѕ СЃРєРѕР»СЊР·СЏС‰РµРіРѕ EV РґР»СЏ РѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅРёСЏ СЂРµР¶РёРјР°.
const MODE_EV_WINDOW: usize = 20;
/// РџРѕСЂРѕРі EV (USDT/СЃРґРµР»РєСѓ), РІС‹С€Рµ РєРѕС‚РѕСЂРѕРіРѕ СЂРµР¶РёРј GROWTH (РїРѕР»РЅС‹Р№ СЂРёСЃРє).
/// EV20 в‰Ґ +0.02 = СЌРґР¶ РїРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґС‘РЅ РЅР° СЃРєРѕР»СЊР·СЏС‰РµРј РѕРєРЅРµ.
const MODE_EV_GROWTH: f64 = 0.02;
/// РџСЂРѕСЃР°РґРєР° РѕС‚ РїРёРєР°, РїСЂРё РєРѕС‚РѕСЂРѕР№ GROWTH РїСЂРёРЅСѓРґРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ РґРµРіСЂР°РґРёСЂСѓРµС‚
/// РІ SURVIVAL (РјСЏРіРєРёР№ Р·Р°РЅРѕСЃ РїРµСЂРµРґ PROP-GUARD-РєР°Р·РЅСЊСЋ РЅР° 20%).
const MODE_DD_SOFT: f64 = 0.08;

pub struct RiskManager {
    config: BotConfig,
    global: GlobalConfig,
    daily_loss: f64,
    circuit_breaker: CircuitBreaker,
    global_risk: Arc<GlobalRiskManager>,

    realized_pnl: Decimal,
    total_trades: u32,
    win_trades: u32,
    loss_trades: u32,
    gross_profit: Decimal,
    gross_loss: Decimal,

    suspended: bool,
    suspend_reason: Option<String>,
    pool: SqlitePool,
    stat_day: String,

    // --- рџ”§ PROP-GUARD: РїРёРє СЌРєРІРёС‚Рё Рё Р»РёРјРёС‚ РїСЂРѕСЃР°РґРєРё ---
    equity_peak: f64,
    prop_dd_pct: f64,

    // P16: live equity mirror (initial + realized PnL), read by strategies
    live_equity: Arc<RwLock<f64>>,

    // --- рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ C: СЂРµР¶РёРјС‹ РІС‹Р¶РёРІР°РЅРёСЏ ---
    /// РЎРєРѕР»СЊР·СЏС‰РµРµ РѕРєРЅРѕ РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРёС… net_pnl (РґР»СЏ EV20)
    recent_ev: std::collections::VecDeque<f64>,
    /// РњРЅРѕР¶РёС‚РµР»СЊ СЂРёСЃРєР°: 1.0 = GROWTH, 0.5 = SURVIVAL, 0.0 = HALT
    risk_multiplier: f64,
}

impl RiskManager {
    pub async fn new(
        config: &BotConfig,
        global: &GlobalConfig,
        global_risk: Arc<GlobalRiskManager>,
    ) -> Result<Self> {
        let db_path = config
            .db_path_override
            .as_ref()
            .unwrap_or(&global.db_path);
        let pool = SqlitePool::connect(&format!("sqlite:{}?mode=rwc", db_path)).await?;
        sqlx::query(
            r#"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS risk_stats (
                bot_name TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                realized_pnl TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
                total_trades INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                win_trades INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                loss_trades INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                gross_profit TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
                gross_loss TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
                daily_loss TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0',
                stat_day TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
                suspended INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                suspend_reason TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT ''
            )"#,
        )
        .execute(&pool)
        .await?;

        let _ = sqlx::query(
            "ALTER TABLE risk_stats ADD COLUMN cb_remaining_ms INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
        )
        .execute(&pool)
        .await;

        // рџ”§ PROP-GUARD: РїРёРє СЌРєРІРёС‚Рё РїРµСЂСЃРёСЃС‚РёС‚СЃСЏ вЂ” СЂРµСЃС‚Р°СЂС‚ РЅРµ СЃР±СЂР°СЃС‹РІР°РµС‚
        let _ = sqlx::query(
            "ALTER TABLE risk_stats ADD COLUMN equity_peak REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
        )
        .execute(&pool)
        .await;

        let today = Utc::now().format("%Y-%m-%d").to_string();

        // ============================================================
        // рџ”§ RESET_STATS: С‡РёСЃС‚С‹Р№ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚ СЌРєСЃРїРµСЂРёРјРµРЅС‚Р° (.env, РѕРґРёРЅ СЂР°Р·).
        // ============================================================
        let mut force_reset = false;
        if std::env::var("RESET_STATS")
            .map(|v| v.trim() == "1")
            .unwrap_or(false)
        {
            let _ = sqlx::query("DELETE FROM risk_stats WHERE bot_name = ?")
                .bind(&config.name)
                .execute(&pool)
                .await;
            force_reset = true;
            tracing::warn!(
                "[risk:{}] рџ§№ RESET_STATS=1: СЃС‚Р°С‚РёСЃС‚РёРєР° СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅР° вЂ” С‡РёСЃС‚С‹Р№ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚ РіРёРїРѕС‚РµР·С‹",
                config.name
            );
        }

        let mut realized_pnl = Decimal::ZERO;
        let (mut total_trades, mut win_trades, mut loss_trades) = (0u32, 0u32, 0u32);
        let (mut gross_profit, mut gross_loss) = (Decimal::ZERO, Decimal::ZERO);
        let mut daily_loss = 0.0f64;
        let mut suspended = false;
        let mut suspend_reason: Option<String> = None;
        let mut cb_remaining_ms: i64 = 0;
        let mut equity_peak = 0.0f64;

        if !force_reset {
            if let Ok(row) = sqlx::query(
                "SELECT realized_pnl, total_trades, win_trades, loss_trades, gross_profit, \
                 gross_loss, daily_loss, stat_day, suspended, suspend_reason, cb_remaining_ms, \
                 equity_peak FROM risk_stats WHERE bot_name = ?",
            )
            .bind(&config.name)
            .fetch_one(&pool)
            .await
            {
                use sqlx::Row;
                realized_pnl = row.try_get::<String, _>("realized_pnl").ok()
                    .and_then(|s| s.parse::<Decimal>().ok()).unwrap_or(Decimal::ZERO);
                total_trades = row.try_get::<u32, _>("total_trades").unwrap_or(0);
                win_trades = row.try_get::<u32, _>("win_trades").unwrap_or(0);
                loss_trades = row.try_get::<u32, _>("loss_trades").unwrap_or(0);
                gross_profit = row.try_get::<String, _>("gross_profit").ok()
                    .and_then(|s| s.parse::<Decimal>().ok()).unwrap_or(Decimal::ZERO);
                gross_loss = row.try_get::<String, _>("gross_loss").ok()
                    .and_then(|s| s.parse::<Decimal>().ok()).unwrap_or(Decimal::ZERO);
                suspended = row.try_get::<i64, _>("suspended").unwrap_or(0) != 0;
                let reason = row.try_get::<String, _>("suspend_reason").unwrap_or_default();
                if suspended && !reason.is_empty() {
                    suspend_reason = Some(reason);
                }
                let saved_day: String = row.try_get::<String, _>("stat_day").unwrap_or_default();
                if saved_day == today {
                    daily_loss = row.try_get::<String, _>("daily_loss").ok()
                        .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
                }
                cb_remaining_ms = row.try_get::<i64, _>("cb_remaining_ms").unwrap_or(0);
                equity_peak = row.try_get::<f64, _>("equity_peak").unwrap_or(0.0);

                tracing::info!(
                    "[risk:{}] рџ“Љ СЃС‚Р°С‚РёСЃС‚РёРєР° РІРѕСЃСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅР° РёР· Р‘Р”: trades={} | WR={:.1}% | pnl={} | daily_loss={:.4}{}",
                    config.name, total_trades,
                    if total_trades > 0 { win_trades as f64 / total_trades as f64 * 100.0 } else { 0.0 },
                    realized_pnl, daily_loss,
                    if suspended { " | SUSPENDED" } else { "" }
                );
            }
        }

        // ============================================================
        // рџ”§ PROP-GUARD: Р»РёРјРёС‚ РїСЂРѕСЃР°РґРєРё РёР· .env PROP_MAX_DD_PCT (0.01вЂ“0.50)
        // ============================================================
        let mut prop_dd_pct = PROP_DD_DEFAULT;
        if let Ok(v) = std::env::var("PROP_MAX_DD_PCT") {
            if let Ok(x) = v.trim().parse::<f64>() {
                if (0.01..=0.50).contains(&x) {
                    prop_dd_pct = x;
                    tracing::info!(
                        "[risk:{}] рџ›Ў PROP_MAX_DD_PCT РёР· .env = {:.2} (РґРµС„РѕР»С‚ {:.2})",
                        config.name, x, PROP_DD_DEFAULT
                    );
                }
            }
        }

        // РџРёРє СЌРєРІРёС‚Рё: РёР· Р‘Р”; 0 (РїРѕСЃР»Рµ RESET РёР»Рё РїРµСЂРІР°СЏ РєРѕР»РѕРЅРєР°) = С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµРµ СЌРєРІРёС‚Рё
        let initial_balance = config.initial_balance.to_f64().unwrap_or(1000.0);
        let current_equity = initial_balance + realized_pnl.to_f64().unwrap_or(0.0);
        let live_equity = Arc::new(RwLock::new(current_equity)); // P16
        if equity_peak <= 0.0 {
            equity_peak = current_equity;
        }
        if equity_peak < current_equity {
            equity_peak = current_equity;
        }

        // рџ”§ PROP-GUARD: РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° РџРћРЎР›Р• РІРѕСЃСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРёСЏ вЂ” РґРµС‚РµСЂРјРёРЅРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Р№ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚
        let mut dd_at_start = 0.0f64;
        if equity_peak > 0.0 {
            dd_at_start = (equity_peak - current_equity) / equity_peak;
            if dd_at_start < 0.0 {
                dd_at_start = 0.0;
            }
        }
        if dd_at_start >= prop_dd_pct && !suspended {
            suspended = true;
            suspend_reason = Some(format!(
                "PROP-GUARD РїСЂРё СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚Рµ: РїСЂРѕСЃР°РґРєР° {:.1}% РѕС‚ РїРёРєР° {:.2} (Р»РёРјРёС‚ {:.1}%)",
                dd_at_start * 100.0, equity_peak, prop_dd_pct * 100.0
            ));
            tracing::error!("рџљЁ PROP-GUARD: {}", suspend_reason.as_deref().unwrap_or(""));
        }

        let circuit_breaker = CircuitBreaker::new(
            3,
            config.circuit_breaker_window_sec,
            config.circuit_breaker_cooldown_sec,
        );
        if cb_remaining_ms > 0 {
            circuit_breaker.restore_open_with_remaining(cb_remaining_ms as u64);
            tracing::warn!(
                "[risk:{}] рџ›Ў Circuit Breaker РІРѕСЃСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅ РёР· Р‘Р” вЂ” РєСѓР»РґР°СѓРЅ РµС‰С‘ {} РјСЃ",
                config.name, cb_remaining_ms
            );
        }

        tracing::info!(
            "[risk:{}] рџ›Ў PROP-GUARD: РїРёРє СЌРєРІРёС‚Рё={:.2} | С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµРµ={:.2} | DD={:.1}% | Р»РёРјРёС‚={:.1}%",
            config.name, equity_peak, current_equity,
            dd_at_start * 100.0, prop_dd_pct * 100.0
        );

        // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ C: СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚ РІСЃРµРіРґР° РІ SURVIVAL вЂ” РґРѕРєР°Р¶Рё СЌРґР¶, РїРѕР»СѓС‡Рё РїРѕР»РЅС‹Р№ СЂРёСЃРє
        tracing::info!(
            "[risk:{}] рџ§­ СЂРµР¶РёРј РІС‹Р¶РёРІР°РЅРёСЏ: СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚ РІ SURVIVAL (РјРЅРѕР¶РёС‚РµР»СЊ СЂРёСЃРєР° 0.5) вЂ” \
             GROWTH РїРѕСЃР»Рµ EV20 в‰Ґ +{:.2} РЅР° {} СЃРґРµР»РєР°С…",
            config.name, MODE_EV_GROWTH, MODE_EV_WINDOW
        );

        Ok(Self {
            config: config.clone(),
            global: global.clone(),
            daily_loss,
            circuit_breaker,
            global_risk,
            realized_pnl,
            total_trades,
            win_trades,
            loss_trades,
            gross_profit,
            gross_loss,
            suspended,
            suspend_reason,
            pool,
            stat_day: today,
            // рџ”§ PROP-GUARD
            equity_peak,
            prop_dd_pct,
            live_equity,
            // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ C
            recent_ev: std::collections::VecDeque::with_capacity(MODE_EV_WINDOW + 1),
            risk_multiplier: 0.5,
        })
    }

    // ============================================================
    // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєРё РїРµСЂРµРґ РІС…РѕРґРѕРј
    // ============================================================
    pub fn check(&mut self, signal: &Signal) -> bool { // AUDIT-P4: &mut РґР»СЏ rollover РґРЅСЏ
        if signal.strategy_name.starts_with("Exit_") {
            return true;
        }

        // [P11:kill] Kill-switch: KILL file in project root = exits-only mode.
        if crate::utils::killfile::kill_switch_active() {
            tracing::warn!("KILL file active - entry rejected (exits-only mode)");
            return false;
        }

        if self.suspended {
            tracing::warn!(
                "в›” Р‘РѕС‚ РІ SUSPENDED: {}",
                self.suspend_reason.as_deref().unwrap_or("EV в‰¤ 0")
            );
            return false;
        }

        if self.circuit_breaker.is_open() {
            tracing::warn!("Circuit breaker Р°РєС‚РёРІРµРЅ вЂ” РІС…РѕРґ Р·Р°Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅ");
            return false;
        }

        // AUDIT-P4: rollover РґРЅСЏ Р—Р”Р•РЎР¬ (Р° РЅРµ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РІ record_close).
        // РРЅР°С‡Рµ deadlock: РґРЅРµРІРЅРѕР№ СЃС‚РѕРї -> РІС…РѕРґРѕРІ РЅРµС‚ -> СЃРґРµР»РѕРє РЅРµС‚ ->
        // record_close РЅРµ Р·РѕРІС‘С‚СЃСЏ -> daily_loss РЅРёРєРѕРіРґР° РЅРµ СЃР±СЂРѕСЃРёС‚СЃСЏ.
        let today = Utc::now().format("%Y-%m-%d").to_string();
        if self.stat_day != today {
            tracing::info!(
                "[risk:{}] рџ“… РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ РґРµРЅСЊ вЂ” РґРЅРµРІРЅРѕР№ СѓР±С‹С‚РѕРє СЃР±СЂРѕС€РµРЅ (Р±С‹Р»Рѕ {:.4} USDT)",
                self.config.name, self.daily_loss
            );
            self.stat_day = today;
            self.daily_loss = 0.0;
        }
        let max_loss = self.config.max_daily_loss_amount.to_f64().unwrap_or(30.0);
        if self.daily_loss >= max_loss {
            tracing::warn!(
                "Р”РЅРµРІРЅРѕР№ Р»РёРјРёС‚ СѓР±С‹С‚РєР° РґРѕСЃС‚РёРіРЅСѓС‚: {:.4} >= {:.4} USDT",
                self.daily_loss, max_loss
            );
            return false;
        }

        if self.global_risk.active_positions_count() >= self.global.max_concurrent_positions {
            tracing::warn!(
                "РџСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ Р»РёРјРёС‚ РѕРґРЅРѕРІСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅС‹С… РїРѕР·РёС†РёР№: {} >= {}",
                self.global_risk.active_positions_count(),
                self.global.max_concurrent_positions
            );
            return false;
        }

        // AUDIT-P3: Р±РµР· SL СЃРёРіРЅР°Р» СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ РёРјРµР» СЂРёСЃРє 0 (unwrap_or(price)) Рё
        // РѕР±С…РѕРґРёР» Р»РёРјРёС‚ СЃРѕРІРѕРєСѓРїРЅРѕРіРѕ СЂРёСЃРєР°. РќРµС‚ SL = 2% adverse move.
        let stop = signal.stop_loss.unwrap_or(signal.price * 0.98);
        let new_risk = signal.quantity * (signal.price - stop).abs();

        let initial = self.config.initial_balance.to_f64().unwrap_or(1000.0);
        let realized = self.realized_pnl.to_f64().unwrap_or(0.0);
        let balance = initial + realized;
        // AUDIT-P5: Р±Р°Р·Р° СЃРѕРІРѕРєСѓРїРЅРѕРіРѕ СЂРёСЃРєР° вЂ” floor РїРѕ equity_peak. РРЅР°С‡Рµ
        // РїРѕСЃР»Рµ СЃРµСЂРёРё СѓР±С‹С‚РєРѕРІ Р»РёРјРёС‚ СЃС…Р»РѕРїС‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ, Р° СЃР°Р№Р·РёРЅРі РёРґС‘С‚ РѕС‚
        // initial_balance: РіРµР№С‚ РјРѕР»С‡Р° Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂСѓРµС‚ РІСЃРµ РЅРѕРІС‹Рµ РІС…РѕРґС‹ РїСЂРё
        // РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚РѕР№ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё (deadlock в„–2, Р±РѕС‚ РЅРµ suspended). DD-СЂРµР¶РёРј вЂ”
        // СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р° PROP-GUARD Рё SURVIVAL-РјРЅРѕР¶РёС‚РµР»СЏ, РЅРµ СЃС…Р»РѕРїС‹РІР°РЅРёРµ РєРІРѕС‚С‹.
        let risk_base = balance.max(self.equity_peak);
        let max_total_risk = risk_base
            * self.config.max_concurrent_risk_pct.to_f64().unwrap_or(2.0)
            / 100.0;
        let current_risk = self.global_risk.total_risk();

        if current_risk + new_risk > max_total_risk {
            tracing::warn!(
                "в›” РЎСѓРјРјР°СЂРЅС‹Р№ СЂРёСЃРє РїСЂРµРІС‹С€РµРЅ: balance={:.4} (init={:.4}+realized={:.4}), \
                 Р»РёРјРёС‚={:.4}, С‚РµРє.СЂРёСЃРє={:.4} + РЅРѕРІС‹Р№={:.4} = {:.4}",
                balance, initial, realized,
                max_total_risk, current_risk, new_risk,
                current_risk + new_risk
            );
            return false;
        }

        true
    }

    // ============================================================
    // РЈС‡С‘С‚ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РѕР№ СЃРґРµР»РєРё вЂ” Р•Р”РРќР«Р™ РјРµС‚РѕРґ (bk2)
    // ============================================================
    pub fn record_close(&mut self, net_pnl: Decimal) {
        let today = Utc::now().format("%Y-%m-%d").to_string();
        if self.stat_day != today {
            self.stat_day = today;
            self.daily_loss = 0.0;
            tracing::info!("[risk:{}] рџ“… РЅРѕРІС‹Р№ РґРµРЅСЊ вЂ” РґРЅРµРІРЅРѕР№ СѓР±С‹С‚РѕРє СЃР±СЂРѕС€РµРЅ", self.config.name);
        }
        self.realized_pnl += net_pnl;

        if net_pnl > Decimal::ZERO {
            self.total_trades += 1;
            self.win_trades += 1;
            self.gross_profit += net_pnl;
        } else if net_pnl < Decimal::ZERO {
            self.total_trades += 1;
            self.loss_trades += 1;
            self.gross_loss += net_pnl.abs();

            let loss_f64 = net_pnl.abs().to_f64().unwrap_or(0.0);
            self.daily_loss += loss_f64;
            if loss_f64 >= 0.10 {
                self.circuit_breaker.record_loss();
            }
        }

        // ============================================================
        // рџ”§ PROP-GUARD: РїРёРє СЌРєРІРёС‚Рё РјРѕРЅРѕС‚РѕРЅРЅРѕ РІРІРµСЂС…; РїСЂРѕСЃР°РґРєР° РѕС‚ РїРёРєР°
        // в‰Ґ Р»РёРјРёС‚Р° в†’ SUSPENDED.
        // ============================================================
        let initial = self.config.initial_balance.to_f64().unwrap_or(1000.0);
        let equity = initial + self.realized_pnl.to_f64().unwrap_or(0.0);
        if let Ok(mut w) = self.live_equity.write() { *w = equity; } // P16
        if equity > self.equity_peak {
            self.equity_peak = equity;
        }
        if !self.suspended && self.equity_peak > 0.0 {
            let dd = (self.equity_peak - equity) / self.equity_peak;
            if dd >= self.prop_dd_pct {
                self.suspended = true;
                self.suspend_reason = Some(format!(
                    "PROP-GUARD: РїСЂРѕСЃР°РґРєР° {:.1}% РѕС‚ РїРёРєР° СЌРєРІРёС‚Рё {:.2} (Р»РёРјРёС‚ {:.1}%)",
                    dd * 100.0, self.equity_peak, self.prop_dd_pct * 100.0
                ));
                tracing::error!("рџљЁ РђР’РўРћ-РЎРўРћРџ (PROP-GUARD): {}", self.suspend_reason.as_deref().unwrap_or(""));
            }
        }

        // ============================================================
        // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ C: СЂРµР¶РёРјС‹ РІС‹Р¶РёРІР°РЅРёСЏ вЂ” СЃРєРѕР»СЊР·СЏС‰РёР№ EV Рё РјРЅРѕР¶РёС‚РµР»СЊ СЂРёСЃРєР°.
        // РЇРґСЂРѕ: СЂРёСЃРє РќРРљРћР“Р”Рђ РЅРµ СЂР°СЃС‚С‘С‚ РїСЂРё РїР°РґР°СЋС‰РµРј EV.
        //   GROWTH   (1.0): EV20 в‰Ґ +0.02 Р DD < 8%
        //   SURVIVAL (0.5): РІСЃС‘ РѕСЃС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ, С‡С‚Рѕ РЅРµ suspended
        //   HALT     (0.0): suspended
        // ============================================================
        self.recent_ev.push_back(net_pnl.to_f64().unwrap_or(0.0));
        while self.recent_ev.len() > MODE_EV_WINDOW {
            self.recent_ev.pop_front();
        }

        let equity_now = initial + self.realized_pnl.to_f64().unwrap_or(0.0);
        let dd_now = if self.equity_peak > 0.0 {
            ((self.equity_peak - equity_now) / self.equity_peak).max(0.0)
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        let ev20 = if self.recent_ev.is_empty() {
            0.0
        } else {
            self.recent_ev.iter().sum::<f64>() / self.recent_ev.len() as f64
        };

        let old_mult = self.risk_multiplier;
        self.risk_multiplier = if self.suspended {
            0.0 // HALT
        } else if dd_now >= MODE_DD_SOFT || ev20 < 0.0 {
            0.5 // SURVIVAL
        } else if ev20 >= MODE_EV_GROWTH {
            1.0 // GROWTH
        } else {
            0.5 // РЅРµР№С‚СЂР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ Р·РѕРЅР° вЂ” SURVIVAL
        };
        if (self.risk_multiplier - old_mult).abs() > 0.01 {
            tracing::warn!(
                "[risk:{}] рџ§­ СЂРµР¶РёРј: РјРЅРѕР¶РёС‚РµР»СЊ СЂРёСЃРєР° {:.2} в†’ {:.2} | EV20={:+.4} | DD={:.1}%",
                self.config.name, old_mult, self.risk_multiplier,
                ev20, dd_now * 100.0
            );
        }

        if self.total_trades > 0 && self.total_trades % 10 == 0 {
            let wr = self.win_rate();
            let pf = self.profit_factor();
            let ev = self
                .expected_value()
                .and_then(|d| d.to_f64())
                .unwrap_or(0.0);

            tracing::info!(
                "рџ“Љ RISK STATS | trades={} | WR={:.1}% | PF={:.2} | EV={:+.4} | realized_pnl={} | peak={:.2} | mult={:.2}",
                self.total_trades,
                wr * 100.0,
                pf,
                ev,
                self.realized_pnl,
                self.equity_peak,
                self.risk_multiplier
            );
        }

        if !self.suspended && self.total_trades >= MIN_TRADES_FOR_EV_CHECK {
            if let Some(ev_dec) = self.expected_value() {
                let ev = ev_dec.to_f64().unwrap_or(0.0);
                if ev <= MIN_EV_USDT {
                    self.suspended = true;
                    self.suspend_reason = Some(format!(
                        "EV={:+.4} USDT РїРѕСЃР»Рµ {} СЃРґРµР»РѕРє вЂ” СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ СѓР±С‹С‚РѕС‡РЅР°",
                        ev, self.total_trades
                    ));
                    tracing::error!(
                        "рџљЁ РђР’РўРћ-РЎРўРћРџ: {} | WR={:.1}% | PF={:.2} | realized_pnl={}",
                        self.suspend_reason.as_deref().unwrap_or(""),
                        self.win_rate() * 100.0,
                        self.profit_factor(),
                        self.realized_pnl
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // P16: live equity accessors
    pub fn equity_handle(&self) -> Arc<RwLock<f64>> {
        self.live_equity.clone()
    }

    pub fn live_equity(&self) -> f64 {
        self.live_equity.read().map(|g| *g).unwrap_or(0.0)
    }

    pub async fn flush_stats(&mut self) {
        if let Ok(row) = sqlx::query(
            "SELECT id FROM bot_commands WHERE consumed = 0 AND command = 'resume' \
             ORDER BY id LIMIT 1",
        )
        .fetch_one(&self.pool)
        .await
        {
            use sqlx::Row;
            let id: i64 = row.try_get("id").unwrap_or(0);
            let _ = sqlx::query("UPDATE bot_commands SET consumed = 1 WHERE id = ?")
                .bind(id)
                .execute(&self.pool)
                .await;
            if self.suspended {
                self.resume();
            } else {
                tracing::info!(
                    "[risk:{}] рџ“© РєРѕРјР°РЅРґР° resume РїРѕР»СѓС‡РµРЅР° вЂ” Р±РѕС‚ РЅРµ РІ suspended (no-op)",
                    self.config.name
                );
            }
        }

        let _ = sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO risk_stats (bot_name, realized_pnl, total_trades, win_trades, loss_trades, \
             gross_profit, gross_loss, daily_loss, stat_day, suspended, suspend_reason, cb_remaining_ms, \
             equity_peak) \
             VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) \
             ON CONFLICT(bot_name) DO UPDATE SET realized_pnl=excluded.realized_pnl, \
             total_trades=excluded.total_trades, win_trades=excluded.win_trades, \
             loss_trades=excluded.loss_trades, gross_profit=excluded.gross_profit, \
             gross_loss=excluded.gross_loss, daily_loss=excluded.daily_loss, \
             stat_day=excluded.stat_day, suspended=excluded.suspended, \
             suspend_reason=excluded.suspend_reason, cb_remaining_ms=excluded.cb_remaining_ms, \
             equity_peak=excluded.equity_peak",
        )
        .bind(&self.config.name)
        .bind(self.realized_pnl.to_string())
        .bind(self.total_trades)
        .bind(self.win_trades)
        .bind(self.loss_trades)
        .bind(self.gross_profit.to_string())
        .bind(self.gross_loss.to_string())
        .bind(self.daily_loss.to_string())
        .bind(&self.stat_day)
        .bind(if self.suspended { 1i64 } else { 0i64 })
        .bind(self.suspend_reason.clone().unwrap_or_default())
        .bind(self.circuit_breaker.cooldown_remaining_ms() as i64)
        .bind(self.equity_peak)
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await;
    }

    pub fn record_trade_result(&mut self, _symbol: &str, pnl: f64) {
        let dec = Decimal::try_from(pnl).unwrap_or(Decimal::ZERO);
        self.record_close(dec);
    }

    pub fn win_rate(&self) -> f64 {
        if self.total_trades == 0 {
            return 0.0;
        }
        self.win_trades as f64 / self.total_trades as f64
    }

    pub fn profit_factor(&self) -> f64 {
        if self.gross_loss.is_zero() {
            return if self.gross_profit.is_zero() {
                0.0
            } else {
                f64::INFINITY
            };
        }
        (self.gross_profit / self.gross_loss)
            .to_f64()
            .unwrap_or(0.0)
    }

    pub fn expected_value(&self) -> Option<Decimal> {
        if self.total_trades == 0 {
            return None;
        }
        let total = Decimal::from(self.total_trades);
        let wins = Decimal::from(self.win_trades);
        let losses = Decimal::from(self.loss_trades);
        let win_rate = wins / total;

        let avg_win = if self.win_trades > 0 {
            self.gross_profit / wins
        } else {
            Decimal::ZERO
        };
        let avg_loss = if self.loss_trades > 0 {
            self.gross_loss / losses
        } else {
            Decimal::ZERO
        };

        Some(win_rate * avg_win - (Decimal::ONE - win_rate) * avg_loss)
    }

    // AUDIT-P4: СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ РјРµС‚РѕРґ Р±С‹Р» РјС‘СЂС‚РІ (РЅРёРєС‚Рѕ РЅРµ Р·РІР°Р») Рё Р·Р°РѕРґРЅРѕ РѕР±РЅСѓР»СЏР»
    // daily_loss вЂ” СЂСѓС‡РЅРѕР№ resume РѕР±С…РѕРґРёР» Р±С‹ РґРЅРµРІРЅРѕР№ СЃС‚РѕРї РѕРґРЅРёРј РєР»РёРєРѕРј.
    // РўРµРїРµСЂСЊ: СЃР±СЂР°СЃС‹РІР°РµС‚ РўРћР›Р¬РљРћ circuit breaker (panic-stop, human override).
    // Р”РЅРµРІРЅРѕР№ Р±СЋРґР¶РµС‚ Р¶РёРІС‘С‚ РґРѕ rollover РЅРѕРІРѕРіРѕ РґРЅСЏ. Р’С‹Р·С‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РёР· resume().
    pub fn reset_circuit_breaker(&mut self) {
        self.circuit_breaker.reset();
    }

    // AUDIT-P4: СЂСѓС‡РЅРѕР№ resume = human-override: СЃРЅРёРјР°РµС‚ EV-suspend Рё
    // РєСѓР»РґР°СѓРЅ circuit breaker. daily_loss РќР• С‚СЂРѕРіР°РµРј (РґРµР№СЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚ РґРѕ РїРѕР»СѓРЅРѕС‡Рё).
    pub fn resume(&mut self) {
        self.reset_circuit_breaker();
        if self.suspended {
            tracing::warn!(
                "рџџў SUSPENDED СЃР±СЂРѕС€РµРЅ РІСЂСѓС‡РЅСѓСЋ (СЂР°РЅРµРµ: {})",
                self.suspend_reason.as_deref().unwrap_or("")
            );
            self.suspended = false;
            self.suspend_reason = None;
        }
    }

    pub fn is_suspended(&self) -> bool {
        self.suspended
    }

    pub fn suspend_reason(&self) -> Option<&str> {
        self.suspend_reason.as_deref()
    }

    // ============================================================
    // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ C: РіРµС‚С‚РµСЂ РјРЅРѕР¶РёС‚РµР»СЏ СЂРёСЃРєР° (РґР»СЏ РїСЂРѕРІРѕРґР° РІ СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЋ)
    // ============================================================
    pub fn risk_multiplier(&self) -> f64 {
        self.risk_multiplier
    }

    pub fn realized_pnl(&self) -> Decimal {
        self.realized_pnl
    }
    pub fn total_trades(&self) -> u32 {
        self.total_trades
    }
    pub fn daily_loss(&self) -> f64 {
        self.daily_loss
    }
}

pub mod state_manager;
