pub use state_manager::StateManager;
=== C:\Users\BASE_PC\scalping_agentsGLM\src\state\state_manager.rs ===
use crate::config::{BotConfig, GlobalConfig};
use crate::strategies::Signal;
use anyhow::Result;
use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};
use dashmap::DashMap;
use rust_decimal::prelude::ToPrimitive;
use sqlx::sqlite::SqlitePool;
use std::sync::Arc;

const BREAKEVEN_AT_R: f64 = 1.5;
// рџ”§ РћРўРљР›Р®Р§Р•РќРћ (СЌРєСЃРїРµСЂРёРјРµРЅС‚ momentum): partial@1.5R СЃСЂРµР·Р°Р» winners
// (win СѓСЂРµР·Р°Р»СЃСЏ СЃ ~0.14 РґРѕ ~0.02 USDT). Р’РµСЂРЅСѓС‚СЊ РїСЂРё РІРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёРё partial.
// const PARTIAL_AT_R: f64 = 2.0;
// const PARTIAL_FRACTION: f64 = 0.5;
const TRAIL_ATR_MULT: f64 = 2.0;

/// вњ… A4: С‚СЂРµР№Р»РёРЅРі РґР»СЏ SpreadCapture.
const SPREAD_TRAIL_ACTIVATION_R: f64 = 0.5;
const SPREAD_TRAIL_MULT: f64 = 0.6;

/// рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B: С‡Р°СЃС‚РѕС‚Р° РїРѕРґС‚СЏР¶РєРё Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІРѕРіРѕ СЃС‚РѕРїР° вЂ” РЅРµ С‡Р°С‰Рµ, С‡РµРј СЂР°Р· РІ N СЃРµРєСѓРЅРґ
/// (Р°РЅС‚Рё-СЃРїР°Рј cancel+place РїСЂРё РєР°Р¶РґРѕРј С‚РёРєРµ).
pub const TRAIL_SYNC_MIN_INTERVAL_MS: i64 = 10_000;

fn round_to(value: f64, decimals: usize) -> f64 {
    let s = format!("{:.*}", decimals, value);
    s.parse::<f64>().unwrap_or(value)
}

fn price_precision(symbol: &str) -> usize {
    if symbol.contains("PEPE") || symbol.contains("DOGS") {
        8
    } else {
        2
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Position {
    pub symbol: String,
    pub side: PositionSide,
    pub entry_price: f64,
    pub quantity: f64,
    pub current_price: f64,
    pub extreme_price: f64,
    pub unrealized_pnl: f64,
    pub opened_at: DateTime<Utc>,
    pub stop_loss: Option<f64>,
    pub take_profit: Option<f64>,
    pub sl_order_id: Option<String>,
    pub tp_order_id: Option<String>,

    pub origin_strategy: String,
    pub funding_accrued: f64,
    pub fees_paid: f64,

    pub initial_risk: f64,
    pub atr_hint: f64,
    pub partial_taken: bool,

    /// вњ… A4: СЃРїСЂРµРґ РЅР° РјРѕРјРµРЅС‚ РІС…РѕРґР° (РґР»СЏ С‚СЂРµР№Р»РёРЅРіР° SpreadCapture).
    pub entry_spread: f64,

    /// рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B: РІСЂРµРјСЏ РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРµР№ СЃРёРЅС…СЂРѕРЅРёР·Р°С†РёРё Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІРѕРіРѕ СЃС‚РѕРїР° (Р°РЅС‚Рё-СЃРїР°Рј)
    pub last_trail_sync_ms: i64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub enum PositionSide {
    Long,
    Short,
}

pub struct StateManager {
    pub config: BotConfig,
    positions: Arc<DashMap<String, Position>>,
    pool: SqlitePool,
    fee_rate: f64,
}

impl StateManager {
    pub async fn new(config: &BotConfig, global: &GlobalConfig) -> Result<Self> {
        let db_path_str = config
            .db_path_override
            .as_ref()
            .unwrap_or(&global.db_path);

        let db_path = std::path::PathBuf::from(db_path_str);
        if let Some(parent) = db_path.parent() {
            tokio::fs::create_dir_all(parent).await.ok();
        }
        let db_url = format!("sqlite:{}?mode=rwc", db_path.display());
        let pool = SqlitePool::connect(&db_url).await?;

        let _ = sqlx::query("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL").fetch_all(&pool).await;
        let _ = sqlx::query("PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000").fetch_all(&pool).await;

        sqlx::query(
            r#"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS positions (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                bot_name TEXT NOT NULL,
                symbol TEXT NOT NULL,
                side TEXT NOT NULL,
                entry_price REAL NOT NULL,
                quantity REAL NOT NULL,
                current_price REAL,
                stop_loss REAL,
                take_profit REAL,
                opened_at TEXT NOT NULL,
                origin_strategy TEXT DEFAULT '',
                funding_accrued REAL DEFAULT 0,
                fees_paid REAL DEFAULT 0,
                initial_risk REAL DEFAULT 0,
                atr_hint REAL DEFAULT 0,
                partial_taken INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                entry_spread REAL DEFAULT 0
            )"#,
        )
        .execute(&pool)
        .await?;

        // РњРёРіСЂР°С†РёРё
        sqlx::query("ALTER TABLE positions ADD COLUMN origin_strategy TEXT DEFAULT ''")
            .execute(&pool).await.ok();
        sqlx::query("ALTER TABLE positions ADD COLUMN funding_accrued REAL DEFAULT 0")
            .execute(&pool).await.ok();
        sqlx::query("ALTER TABLE positions ADD COLUMN extreme_price REAL")
            .execute(&pool).await.ok();
        sqlx::query("ALTER TABLE positions ADD COLUMN fees_paid REAL DEFAULT 0")
            .execute(&pool).await.ok();
        sqlx::query("ALTER TABLE positions ADD COLUMN initial_risk REAL DEFAULT 0")
            .execute(&pool).await.ok();
        sqlx::query("ALTER TABLE positions ADD COLUMN atr_hint REAL DEFAULT 0")
            .execute(&pool).await.ok();
        sqlx::query("ALTER TABLE positions ADD COLUMN partial_taken INTEGER DEFAULT 0")
            .execute(&pool).await.ok();
        sqlx::query("ALTER TABLE positions ADD COLUMN entry_spread REAL DEFAULT 0")
            .execute(&pool).await.ok();
        // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B: id Р·Р°С‰РёС‚РЅС‹С… РѕСЂРґРµСЂРѕРІ РїРµСЂСЃРёСЃС‚СЏС‚СЃСЏ вЂ” СЂРµСЃС‚Р°СЂС‚ Р·РЅР°РµС‚, С‡С‚Рѕ СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕР¶РёС‚
        sqlx::query("ALTER TABLE positions ADD COLUMN sl_order_id TEXT")
            .execute(&pool).await.ok();
        sqlx::query("ALTER TABLE positions ADD COLUMN tp_order_id TEXT")
            .execute(&pool).await.ok();

        let positions = Arc::new(DashMap::new());
        let rows = sqlx::query(
            "SELECT symbol, side, entry_price, quantity, current_price, stop_loss, \
             take_profit, opened_at, origin_strategy, funding_accrued, fees_paid, \
             initial_risk, atr_hint, partial_taken, entry_spread, \
             sl_order_id, tp_order_id \
             FROM positions WHERE bot_name = ?",
        )
        .bind(&config.name)
        .fetch_all(&pool)
        .await?;

        for row in rows {
            use sqlx::Row;
            let symbol: String = row.try_get("symbol").unwrap_or_default();
            let side: String = row.try_get("side").unwrap_or_default();
            let entry_price: f64 = row.try_get("entry_price").unwrap_or(0.0);
            let quantity: f64 = row.try_get("quantity").unwrap_or(0.0);
            let current_price: f64 = row.try_get("current_price").unwrap_or(entry_price);
            let stop_loss: Option<f64> = row.try_get("stop_loss").ok();
            let take_profit: Option<f64> = row.try_get("take_profit").ok();
            let opened_at_str: String = row.try_get("opened_at").unwrap_or_default();
            let opened_at = DateTime::parse_from_rfc3339(&opened_at_str)
                .map(|d| d.with_timezone(&Utc))
                .unwrap_or_else(|_| Utc::now());

            let origin_strategy: String = row.try_get("origin_strategy").unwrap_or_default();
            let funding_accrued: f64 = row.try_get("funding_accrued").unwrap_or(0.0);
            let fees_paid: f64 = row.try_get("fees_paid").unwrap_or(0.0);
            let mut initial_risk: f64 = row.try_get("initial_risk").unwrap_or(0.0);
            let atr_hint: f64 = row.try_get("atr_hint").unwrap_or(0.0);
            let partial_taken: bool = row.try_get::<i64, _>("partial_taken").unwrap_or(0) != 0;
            let entry_spread: f64 = row.try_get("entry_spread").unwrap_or(0.0);
            let sl_order_id: Option<String> = row.try_get("sl_order_id").ok();
            let tp_order_id: Option<String> = row.try_get("tp_order_id").ok();

            if initial_risk <= 0.0 {
                if let Some(sl) = stop_loss {
                    initial_risk = (entry_price - sl).abs();
                }
            }

            let ps = if side == "BUY" {
                PositionSide::Long
            } else {
                PositionSide::Short
            };

            positions.insert(
                format!("{}_{}", config.name, symbol),
                Position {
                    symbol,
                    side: ps,
                    entry_price,
                    quantity,
                    current_price,
                    extreme_price: row.try_get("extreme_price").unwrap_or(entry_price),
                    unrealized_pnl: 0.0,
                    opened_at,
                    stop_loss,
                    take_profit,
                    sl_order_id,
                    tp_order_id,
                    origin_strategy,
                    funding_accrued,
                    fees_paid,
                    initial_risk,
                    atr_hint,
                    partial_taken,
                    entry_spread,
                    last_trail_sync_ms: 0,
                },
            );
        }

        if !positions.is_empty() {
            tracing::warn!(
                "[{}] вљ пёЏ Р’РѕСЃСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅРѕ {} РїРѕР·РёС†РёР№ РёР· Р‘Р” вЂ” РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЊС‚Рµ РЅР° Р±РёСЂР¶Рµ!",
                config.name,
                positions.len()
            );
        }

        // вњ… P22: С„РѕР»Р±СЌРє РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёРё РІС‹СЂРѕРІРЅРµРЅ СЃ ExecutionEngine (futures taker
        // 0.0005 / spot 0.00075). Р Р°РЅСЊС€Рµ Р·РґРµСЃСЊ Р±С‹Р» 0.001: РїСЂРё РѕС‚СЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІРёРё
        // fee_rate РІ РєРѕРЅС„РёРіРµ BE-С†РµРЅР° Рё net-PnL СЃС‡РёС‚Р°Р»РёСЃСЊ РїРѕ РґРІРѕР№РЅРѕР№ РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёРё.
        let fee_rate = config.fee_rate.map(|d| d.to_f64().unwrap_or(0.0005)).unwrap_or(
            if config.market_type == "futures" { 0.0005 } else { 0.00075 },
        );

        tracing::info!(
            "[{}] рџ’ё Fee rate: {:.5} ({:.3}%) РЅР° СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅСѓ вЂ” round-trip {:.3}%",
            config.name, fee_rate, fee_rate * 100.0, fee_rate * 2.0 * 100.0
        );

        Ok(Self {
            config: config.clone(),
            positions,
            pool,
            fee_rate,
        })
    }

    pub fn has_position(&self, symbol: &str) -> bool {
        let key = format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol);
        self.positions.contains_key(&key)
    }

    // ============================================================
    // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B: id Р·Р°С‰РёС‚РЅС‹С… РѕСЂРґРµСЂРѕРІ
    // ============================================================
    pub async fn set_sl_order_id(&self, symbol: &str, id: String) {
        let key = format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol);
        if let Some(mut pos) = self.positions.get_mut(&key) {
            pos.sl_order_id = Some(id.clone());
        }
        let _ = sqlx::query("UPDATE positions SET sl_order_id=? WHERE bot_name=? AND symbol=?")
            .bind(&id).bind(&self.config.name).bind(symbol)
            .execute(&self.pool).await;
    }

    pub async fn set_tp_order_id(&self, symbol: &str, id: String) {
        let key = format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol);
        if let Some(mut pos) = self.positions.get_mut(&key) {
            pos.tp_order_id = Some(id.clone());
        }
        let _ = sqlx::query("UPDATE positions SET tp_order_id=? WHERE bot_name=? AND symbol=?")
            .bind(&id).bind(&self.config.name).bind(symbol)
            .execute(&self.pool).await;
    }

    /// (sl_order_id, tp_order_id)
    pub fn get_protective_ids(&self, symbol: &str) -> (Option<String>, Option<String>) {
        let key = format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol);
        match self.positions.get(&key) {
            Some(p) => (p.sl_order_id.clone(), p.tp_order_id.clone()),
            None => (None, None),
        }
    }

    /// рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B: РјРѕР¶РЅРѕ Р»Рё СЃРµР№С‡Р°СЃ РїРѕРґС‚СЏРіРёРІР°С‚СЊ СЃС‚РѕРї (Р°РЅС‚Рё-СЃРїР°Рј)
    pub fn trail_sync_allowed(&self, symbol: &str) -> bool {
        let key = format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol);
        match self.positions.get(&key) {
            Some(p) => {
                chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis() - p.last_trail_sync_ms
                    >= TRAIL_SYNC_MIN_INTERVAL_MS
            }
            None => false,
        }
    }

    pub fn mark_trail_sync(&self, symbol: &str) {
        let key = format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol);
        if let Some(mut pos) = self.positions.get_mut(&key) {
            pos.last_trail_sync_ms = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis();
        }
    }

    // ============================================================
    // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєРё SL/TP/РјРµРЅРµРґР¶РјРµРЅС‚Р° (РґРІСѓСЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅРЅРёР№ momentum)
    // ============================================================
    pub fn check_and_update_position(&self, symbol: &str, current_price: f64) -> Option<Signal> {
        let key = format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol);
        if let Some(mut pos) = self.positions.get_mut(&key) {
            pos.current_price = current_price;
            if pos.side == PositionSide::Long && current_price > pos.extreme_price {
                pos.extreme_price = current_price;
            } else if pos.side == PositionSide::Short && current_price < pos.extreme_price {
                pos.extreme_price = current_price;
            }

            let dur = Utc::now().signed_duration_since(pos.opened_at).num_seconds() as u64;
            if dur >= self.config.max_position_duration_sec {
                return Some(self.create_close_signal(&pos, "Timeout"));
            }

            pos.unrealized_pnl = match pos.side {
                PositionSide::Long => (current_price - pos.entry_price) * pos.quantity,
                PositionSide::Short => (pos.entry_price - current_price) * pos.quantity,
            };

            let profit_per_unit = match pos.side {
                PositionSide::Long => current_price - pos.entry_price,
                PositionSide::Short => pos.entry_price - current_price,
            };
            let r_progress = if pos.initial_risk > 0.0 {
                profit_per_unit / pos.initial_risk
            } else {
                0.0
            };

            // ============================================================
            // SpreadCapture: A4-С‚СЂРµР№Р»РёРЅРі (СЃРїСЂРµРґ-СЃРїРµС†РёС„РёС‡РЅС‹Р№)
            // ============================================================
            if pos.origin_strategy == "SpreadCapture" && pos.entry_spread > 0.0 {
                let trail_dist = pos.entry_spread * SPREAD_TRAIL_MULT;

                if r_progress >= SPREAD_TRAIL_ACTIVATION_R {
                    let new_sl = match pos.side {
                        PositionSide::Long => current_price - trail_dist,
                        PositionSide::Short => current_price + trail_dist,
                    };
                    let better = match (pos.stop_loss, pos.side) {
                        (Some(cur), PositionSide::Long) => new_sl > cur,
                        (Some(cur), PositionSide::Short) => new_sl < cur,
                        (None, _) => true,
                    };
                    if better {
                        tracing::debug!(
                            "[{}] рџЋ— С‚СЂРµР№Р»РёРЅРі: SL {} в†’ {:.prec$} (R={:+.2})",
                            self.config.name, pos.symbol, new_sl, r_progress,
                            prec = price_precision(&pos.symbol)
                        );
                        pos.stop_loss = Some(new_sl);
                    }
                }

                if let Some(tp) = pos.take_profit {
                    let tp_passed = match pos.side {
                        PositionSide::Long => current_price > tp,
                        PositionSide::Short => current_price < tp,
                    };
                    if tp_passed {
                        let new_tp = match pos.side {
                            PositionSide::Long => current_price + trail_dist,
                            PositionSide::Short => current_price - trail_dist,
                        };
                        let better = match pos.side {
                            PositionSide::Long => new_tp > tp,
                            PositionSide::Short => new_tp < tp,
                        };
                        if better {
                            pos.take_profit = Some(new_tp);
                        }
                    }
                }

                if let Some(sl) = pos.stop_loss {
                    let hit = match pos.side {
                        PositionSide::Long => current_price <= sl,
                        PositionSide::Short => current_price >= sl,
                    };
                    if hit {
                        return Some(self.create_close_signal(&pos, "StopLoss"));
                    }
                }
                if let Some(tp) = pos.take_profit {
                    let hit = match pos.side {
                        PositionSide::Long => current_price >= tp,
                        PositionSide::Short => current_price <= tp,
                    };
                    if hit {
                        return Some(self.create_close_signal(&pos, "TakeProfit"));
                    }
                }
                return None;
            }

            // ============================================================
            // Momentum: РєРЅРёР¶РЅС‹Р№ РјРµРЅРµРґР¶РјРµРЅС‚ (BE/Chandelier)
            // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B: partial РћРўРљР›Р®Р§РЃРќ (СЌРєСЃРїРµСЂРёРјРµРЅС‚: partial СЃСЂРµР·Р°Р» winners)
            // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B: РїСЂРё СЃРґРІРёРіРµ SL РІРїРµСЂС‘Рґ вЂ” СЃРёРіРЅР°Р» СЃРёРЅС…СЂРѕРЅРёР·Р°С†РёРё Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІРѕРіРѕ
            // СЃС‚РѕРїР° (Exit_UpdateSL), РµСЃР»Рё Р·Р°С‰РёС‚Р° СЃС‚РѕРёС‚ РЅР° Р±РёСЂР¶Рµ.
            // ============================================================
            let mut sl_moved_to: Option<f64> = None;

            if r_progress >= BREAKEVEN_AT_R {
                let be_price = match pos.side {
                    PositionSide::Long => pos.entry_price * (1.0 + self.fee_rate * 2.0),
                    PositionSide::Short => pos.entry_price * (1.0 - self.fee_rate * 2.0),
                };
                // вњ… FIX: unwrap_or(NEG_INFINITY.max(be_price)) РґР°РІР°Р» РґР»СЏ Long
                // Р±РµР· SL cur = be_price в†’ better=false в†’ BE РЅРёРєРѕРіРґР° РЅРµ СЃС‚Р°РІРёР»СЃСЏ.
                let cur = pos.stop_loss.unwrap_or(match pos.side {
                    PositionSide::Long => f64::NEG_INFINITY,
                    PositionSide::Short => f64::INFINITY,
                });
                let better = match pos.side {
                    PositionSide::Long => be_price > cur,
                    PositionSide::Short => be_price < cur,
                };
                if better {
                    pos.stop_loss = Some(be_price);
                    sl_moved_to = Some(be_price);
                    tracing::info!(
                        "[{}] рџ›Ў BE+1R: SL {} в†’ {:.prec$} (СЃРґРµР»РєР° Р·Р°С‰РёС‰РµРЅР° РІ Р±РµР·СѓР±С‹С‚РѕРє)",
                        self.config.name, pos.symbol, be_price,
                        prec = price_precision(&pos.symbol)
                    );
                }
            }

            // [P9:trailing-mode] "atr" (default = old behavior) | "percent"
            // (config keys always) | "tightest" (min of active mechanisms).
            let trail_dist = {
                let offset = self
                    .config
                    .break_even_offset_percent
                    .to_f64()
                    .unwrap_or(0.05)
                    / 100.0;
                let activation = self
                    .config
                    .trailing_stop_activation_percent
                    .to_f64()
                    .unwrap_or(0.15)
                    / 100.0;
                let profit_pct = profit_per_unit / pos.entry_price;
                let pct_dist = if profit_pct >= activation && offset > 0.0 {
                    pos.extreme_price * offset
                } else {
                    0.0
                };
                let atr_dist = if pos.atr_hint > 0.0 {
                    TRAIL_ATR_MULT * pos.atr_hint
                } else {
                    0.0
                };
                match self.config.trailing_mode.as_str() {
                    "percent" => pct_dist,
                    "tightest" => {
                        if atr_dist > 0.0 && pct_dist > 0.0 {
                            atr_dist.min(pct_dist)
                        } else if pct_dist > 0.0 {
                            pct_dist
                        } else {
                            atr_dist
                        }
                    }
                    _ => {
                        if atr_dist > 0.0 {
                            atr_dist
                        } else {
                            pct_dist
                        }
                    }
                }
            };

                        // ============================================================
            // рџ”§ TRAILING EXIT: РІС‹С…РѕРґ РїСЂРё СЂР°СЃРїР°РґРµ РїСЂРёР±С‹Р»Рё.
            // РџРёРє R РєР°РїР°РµС‚СЃСЏ; РїР°РґРµРЅРёРµ СЃ РїРёРєР° РґРѕ +0.15R (РїРѕРєСЂС‹РІР°РµС‚ РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёРё)
            // = РЅРµРјРµРґР»РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ РІС‹С…РѕРґ. РРЅР°С‡Рµ winner С‚Р°РµС‚ РґРѕ BE-РЅСѓР»СЏ/С‚Р°Р№РјР°СѓС‚Р°
            // Рё РїСЂРµРІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ РІ в€’0.02..в€’0.03 РїРѕСЃР»Рµ taker-РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёРё.
            // ============================================================
            const TRAIL_EXIT_PEAK_R: f64 = 0.5;   // Р°РєС‚РёРІР°С†РёСЏ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ +0.5R
            const TRAIL_EXIT_FLOOR_R: f64 = 0.15; // РІС‹С…РѕРґ РїСЂРё СЂР°СЃРїР°РґРµ РґРѕ +0.15R

            let peak_r = if pos.initial_risk > 0.0 {
                let peak_profit = match pos.side {
                    PositionSide::Long => pos.extreme_price - pos.entry_price,
                    PositionSide::Short => pos.entry_price - pos.extreme_price,
                };
                peak_profit / pos.initial_risk
            } else {
                0.0
            };

            // вњ… FIX: СѓСЃР»РѕРІРёРµ r_progress >= 0 СѓР±СЂР°РЅРѕ вЂ” РµСЃР»Рё С†РµРЅР° РїСЂРѕСЃРєРѕС‡РёР»Р°
            // РЅРѕР»СЊ РјРµР¶РґСѓ С‚РёРєР°РјРё (5СЃ), РІС‹С…РѕРґ РЅРµ СЃСЂР°Р±Р°С‚С‹РІР°Р» Рё РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ РµС…Р°Р»Р°
            // РґРѕ РїРѕР»РЅРѕРіРѕ SL (-1R). РўРµРїРµСЂСЊ С„РёРєСЃРёСЂСѓРµРј РѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕРє РїРѕ СЂС‹РЅРєСѓ РїСЂРё
            // Р»СЋР±РѕРј Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёРё РЅРёР¶Рµ РїРѕР»Р° +0.15R.
            if peak_r >= TRAIL_EXIT_PEAK_R && r_progress <= TRAIL_EXIT_FLOOR_R {
                tracing::info!(
                    "[{}] рџЄ‚ TRAILING EXIT: РїРёРє R={:+.2} СѓРїР°Р» РґРѕ {:+.2} вЂ” С„РёРєСЃРёСЂСѓСЋ РѕСЃС‚Р°С‚РѕРє",
                    self.config.name, peak_r, r_progress
                );
                return Some(self.create_close_signal(&pos, "TrailExit"));
            }

                       // рџ”§ РџРћР›РЈ-РўРђР™РњРђРЈРў: 70% СЃСЂРѕРєР° РїСЂРё РїР»РѕСЃРєРѕР№ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё вЂ” РІС‹С…РѕРґ
            if dur >= self.config.max_position_duration_sec * 7 / 10
                && r_progress.abs() < 0.1
            {
                return Some(self.create_close_signal(&pos, "TimeoutFlat"));
            } 

            if trail_dist > 0.0 {
                let chandelier = match pos.side {
                    PositionSide::Long => pos.extreme_price - trail_dist,
                    PositionSide::Short => pos.extreme_price + trail_dist,
                };
                let better = match (pos.stop_loss, pos.side) {
                    (Some(cur), PositionSide::Long) => chandelier > cur,
                    (Some(cur), PositionSide::Short) => chandelier < cur,
                    (None, _) => true,
                };
                if better {
                    pos.stop_loss = Some(chandelier);
                    sl_moved_to = Some(chandelier);
                }
            }

            // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B: SL СЃРґРІРёРЅСѓР»СЃСЏ РІРїРµСЂС‘Рґ Р Р±РёСЂР¶РµРІР°СЏ Р·Р°С‰РёС‚Р° СЃС‚РѕРёС‚ в†’
            // РїСЂРѕСЃРёРј РґРІРёР¶РѕРє СЃРёРЅС…СЂРѕРЅРёР·РёСЂРѕРІР°С‚СЊ STOP_MARKET (РЅРµ С‡Р°С‰Рµ 10СЃ)
            if let Some(new_sl) = sl_moved_to {
                if pos.sl_order_id.is_some() && self.trail_sync_allowed(&pos.symbol) {
                    self.mark_trail_sync(&pos.symbol);
                    return Some(Signal {
                        symbol: pos.symbol.clone(),
                        side: if pos.side == PositionSide::Long { "SELL".into() } else { "BUY".into() },
                        price: new_sl,
                        quantity: 0.0, // 0 = РќР• Р·Р°РєСЂС‹РІР°С‚СЊ, С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ СЃРёРЅС…СЂРѕРЅРёР·Р°С†РёСЏ СЃС‚РѕРїР°
                        strategy_name: "Exit_UpdateSL".into(),
                        leverage: 1.0,
                        stop_loss: Some(new_sl),
                        take_profit: pos.take_profit,
                        atr_hint: pos.atr_hint,
                        entry_spread: pos.entry_spread,
                    });
                }
            }

            if let Some(sl) = pos.stop_loss {
                let hit = match pos.side {
                    PositionSide::Long => current_price <= sl,
                    PositionSide::Short => current_price >= sl,
                };
                if hit {
                    return Some(self.create_close_signal(&pos, "StopLoss"));
                }
            }
            if let Some(tp) = pos.take_profit {
                let hit = match pos.side {
                    PositionSide::Long => current_price >= tp,
                    PositionSide::Short => current_price <= tp,
                };
                if hit {
                    return Some(self.create_close_signal(&pos, "TakeProfit"));
                }
            }
        }
        None
    }

    fn create_close_signal(&self, pos: &Position, reason: &str) -> Signal {
        Signal {
            symbol: pos.symbol.clone(),
            side: if pos.side == PositionSide::Long {
                "SELL".into()
            } else {
                "BUY".into()
            },
            price: pos.current_price,
            quantity: pos.quantity,
            strategy_name: format!("Exit_{}", reason),
            leverage: 1.0,
            stop_loss: pos.stop_loss,
            take_profit: pos.take_profit,
            atr_hint: pos.atr_hint,
            entry_spread: pos.entry_spread,
        }
    }

    pub async fn add_position(
        &self,
        symbol: String,
        side: String,
        entry_price: f64,
        quantity: f64,
        stop_loss: Option<f64>,
        take_profit: Option<f64>,
        origin_strategy: String,
    ) {
        self.add_position_with_risk(
            symbol, side, entry_price, quantity, stop_loss, take_profit,
            origin_strategy, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0,
        )
        .await;
    }

    /// Р Р°СЃС€РёСЂРµРЅРЅРѕРµ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ. entry_spread вЂ” СЃРїСЂРµРґ РјРѕРјРµРЅС‚Р° РІС…РѕРґР°.
    pub async fn add_position_with_risk(
        &self,
        symbol: String,
        side: String,
        entry_price: f64,
        quantity: f64,
        stop_loss: Option<f64>,
        take_profit: Option<f64>,
        origin_strategy: String,
        initial_risk: f64,
        atr_hint: f64,
        entry_spread: f64,
    ) {
        let ps = if side == "BUY" {
            PositionSide::Long
        } else {
            PositionSide::Short
        };
        let opened_at = Utc::now();

        let quantity = round_to(quantity, 8);

        let initial_risk = if initial_risk > 0.0 {
            initial_risk
        } else if let Some(sl) = stop_loss {
            (entry_price - sl).abs()
        } else {
            0.0
        };

        let pos = Position {
            symbol: symbol.clone(),
            side: ps,
            entry_price,
            quantity,
            current_price: entry_price,
            extreme_price: entry_price,
            unrealized_pnl: 0.0,
            opened_at,
            stop_loss,
            take_profit,
            sl_order_id: None,
            tp_order_id: None,
            origin_strategy: origin_strategy.clone(),
            funding_accrued: 0.0,
            fees_paid: 0.0,
            initial_risk,
            atr_hint,
            partial_taken: false,
            entry_spread,
            last_trail_sync_ms: 0,
        };
        self.positions
            .insert(format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol.clone()), pos);

        if initial_risk > 0.0 && !origin_strategy.is_empty() && origin_strategy != "Reconciled" {
            tracing::info!(
                "[{}] рџ“ђ R-РјРµРЅРµРґР¶РјРµРЅС‚: {} СЂРёСЃРє/РµРґ = {:.8} ({:.3}%), ATR={:.8}, СЃРїСЂРµРґ={:.4}% | BE@+1R, trail",
                self.config.name, symbol, initial_risk,
                initial_risk / entry_price * 100.0, atr_hint, entry_spread * 100.0
            );
        }

        let _ = sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO positions \
             (bot_name, symbol, side, entry_price, quantity, current_price, \
              stop_loss, take_profit, opened_at, origin_strategy, funding_accrued, fees_paid, \
              initial_risk, atr_hint, partial_taken, entry_spread) \
             VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
        )
        .bind(&self.config.name)
        .bind(&symbol)
        .bind(&side)
        .bind(entry_price)
        .bind(quantity)
        .bind(entry_price)
        .bind(stop_loss)
        .bind(take_profit)
        .bind(opened_at.to_rfc3339())
        .bind(&origin_strategy)
        .bind(0.0_f64)
        .bind(0.0_f64)
        .bind(initial_risk)
        .bind(atr_hint)
        .bind(0i64)
        .bind(entry_spread)
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await;
    }

    pub fn origin_strategy_for(&self, symbol: &str) -> Option<String> {
        let key = format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol);
        self.positions
            .get(&key)
            .map(|p| p.origin_strategy.clone())
    }

    pub fn accrue_funding(&self, symbol: &str, amount_usdt: f64) {
        let key = format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol);
        if let Some(mut pos) = self.positions.get_mut(&key) {
            pos.funding_accrued += amount_usdt;
        }
    }

    pub fn add_fees_paid(&self, symbol: &str, amount: f64) {
        if amount <= 0.0 {
            return;
        }
        let key = format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol);
        if let Some(mut pos) = self.positions.get_mut(&key) {
            pos.fees_paid += amount;
        }
    }

    pub fn open_symbols(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        self.positions
            .iter()
            .map(|e| e.value().symbol.clone())
            .collect()
    }

    pub async fn close_position(&self, symbol: &str, exec_price: f64, exec_qty: f64) -> f64 {
        let key = format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol);

        let Some((_, pos)) = self.positions.remove(&key) else {
            tracing::warn!(
                "[{}] close_position {}: РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё РЅРµС‚ вЂ” PnL=0 (РїРѕРІС‚РѕСЂ/С„Р°РЅС‚РѕРј)",
                self.config.name, symbol
            );
            return 0.0;
        };

        let prec = price_precision(symbol);
        let entry = pos.entry_price;
        let exit = exec_price;
        let qty = round_to(exec_qty, 8);

        if qty <= 0.0 {
            self.positions.insert(key, pos);
            return 0.0;
        }

        let gross_pnl = match pos.side {
            PositionSide::Long => (exit - entry) * qty,
            PositionSide::Short => (entry - exit) * qty,
        };

        let remaining = pos.quantity - qty;
        let dust = (pos.quantity * 1e-6).max(1e-9);

        if remaining > dust {
            // Р§Р°СЃС‚РёС‡РЅРѕРµ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ (РґР»СЏ РїРѕР»РЅРѕС‚С‹; РІ momentum-СЌРєСЃРїРµСЂРёРјРµРЅС‚Рµ РѕС‚РєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРѕ)
            let share = qty / pos.quantity;
            let fees = if pos.fees_paid > 0.0 {
                pos.fees_paid * share
            } else {
                (entry + exit) * qty * self.fee_rate
            };
            let funding_share = pos.funding_accrued * share;
            let net_pnl = gross_pnl - fees + funding_share;

            let rest_qty = round_to(remaining, 8);
            let rest_funding = pos.funding_accrued - funding_share;
            let rest_fees = if pos.fees_paid > 0.0 { pos.fees_paid - fees } else { 0.0 };
            let dur = Utc::now().signed_duration_since(pos.opened_at).num_seconds();

            tracing::warn!(
                "рџ’° Р§РђРЎРўРР§РќРћР• Р—РђРљР Р«РўРР• [{}] {} | {} {:.5} РёР· {:.5} | РІС…РѕРґ={:.prec$} РІС‹С…РѕРґ={:.prec$} | {}СЃ\n   \
                 Р±СЂСѓС‚С‚Рѕ={:+.4} | РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёСЏ ({})={:.6} | funding={:+.6} | РќР•РўРўРћ={:+.4} | РћРЎРўРђРўРћРљ {:.5}",
                self.config.name, pos.symbol,
                match pos.side { PositionSide::Long => "Р›РћРќР“", PositionSide::Short => "РЁРћР Рў" },
                qty, pos.quantity, entry, exit, dur,
                gross_pnl,
                if pos.fees_paid > 0.0 { "Р¤РђРљРў" } else { "РѕС†РµРЅРєР°" },
                fees, funding_share, net_pnl, rest_qty,
                prec = prec,
            );

            let mut rest = pos;
            rest.quantity = rest_qty;
            rest.current_price = exit;
            rest.funding_accrued = rest_funding;
            rest.fees_paid = rest_fees;
            self.positions.insert(key, rest);

            let _ = sqlx::query(
                "UPDATE positions SET quantity=?, current_price=?, funding_accrued=?, fees_paid=? \
                 WHERE bot_name=? AND symbol=?",
            )
            .bind(rest_qty).bind(exit).bind(rest_funding).bind(rest_fees)
            .bind(&self.config.name).bind(symbol)
            .execute(&self.pool).await;

            return net_pnl;
        }

        // РџРѕР»РЅРѕРµ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ
        let fees = if pos.fees_paid > 0.0 {
            pos.fees_paid
        } else {
            (entry + exit) * qty * self.fee_rate
        };
        let net_pnl = gross_pnl - fees + pos.funding_accrued;
        let dur = Utc::now().signed_duration_since(pos.opened_at).num_seconds();

        tracing::info!(
            "рџ’° Р—РђРљР Р«РўРР• [{}] {} | {} {:.5} | РІС…РѕРґ={:.prec$} РІС‹С…РѕРґ={:.prec$} | {}СЃ\n   \
             Р±СЂСѓС‚С‚Рѕ=({:.prec$}в€’{:.prec$})Г—{:.5}={:+.4} | РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёСЏ ({})={:.6} | funding={:+.6}\n   \
             РќР•РўРўРћ={:+.4} USDT",
            self.config.name, pos.symbol,
            match pos.side { PositionSide::Long => "Р›РћРќР“", PositionSide::Short => "РЁРћР Рў" },
            qty, entry, exit, dur,
            if pos.side == PositionSide::Long { exit } else { entry },
            if pos.side == PositionSide::Long { entry } else { exit },
            qty, gross_pnl,
            if pos.fees_paid > 0.0 { "Р¤РђРљРў Р±РёСЂР¶Рё" } else { "РѕС†РµРЅРєР°" },
            fees, pos.funding_accrued, net_pnl,
            prec = prec,
        );

        let _ = sqlx::query("DELETE FROM positions WHERE bot_name=? AND symbol=?")
            .bind(&self.config.name).bind(symbol)
            .execute(&self.pool).await;

        net_pnl
    }

    pub async fn persist_positions(&self) {
        for entry in self.positions.iter() {
            let pos = entry.value();
            let _ = sqlx::query(
                "UPDATE positions SET stop_loss=?, extreme_price=?, funding_accrued=?, \
                 current_price=?, fees_paid=?, initial_risk=?, atr_hint=?, partial_taken=?, entry_spread=?, \
                 sl_order_id=?, tp_order_id=? \
                 WHERE bot_name=? AND symbol=?",
            )
            .bind(pos.stop_loss).bind(pos.extreme_price).bind(pos.funding_accrued)
            .bind(pos.current_price).bind(pos.fees_paid).bind(pos.initial_risk)
            .bind(pos.atr_hint)
            .bind(if pos.partial_taken { 1i64 } else { 0i64 })
            .bind(pos.entry_spread)
            .bind(&pos.sl_order_id)
            .bind(&pos.tp_order_id)
            .bind(&self.config.name).bind(&pos.symbol)
            .execute(&self.pool).await;
        }
    }

    pub async fn purge_position(&self, symbol: &str) {
        let key = format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol);
        self.positions.remove(&key);
        let _ = sqlx::query("DELETE FROM positions WHERE bot_name=? AND symbol=?")
            .bind(&self.config.name).bind(symbol)
            .execute(&self.pool).await;
        tracing::warn!("[{}] рџ§№ С„Р°РЅС‚РѕРјРЅР°СЏ РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ {} СѓРґР°Р»РµРЅР°", self.config.name, symbol);
    }

    pub async fn sync_position_qty(&self, symbol: &str, qty: f64, entry_price: f64) {
        if qty <= 0.0 {
            self.purge_position(symbol).await;
            return;
        }
        let key = format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol);
        if let Some(mut pos) = self.positions.get_mut(&key) {
            pos.quantity = qty;
            pos.entry_price = entry_price;
        }
        let _ = sqlx::query(
            "UPDATE positions SET quantity=?, entry_price=? WHERE bot_name=? AND symbol=?",
        )
        .bind(qty).bind(entry_price)
        .bind(&self.config.name).bind(symbol)
        .execute(&self.pool).await;
    }

        /// рџ†• DCA: add to existing position, recalculate avg entry
    pub async fn add_dca_order(
        &self,
        symbol: &str,
        side: &str,
        entry_price: f64,
        quantity: f64,
        stop_loss: Option<f64>,
        take_profit: Option<f64>,
        origin_strategy: String,
        initial_risk: f64,
        atr_hint: f64,
        entry_spread: f64,
    ) {
        let key = format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol);

        // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј вЂ” РµСЃС‚СЊ Р»Рё СѓР¶Рµ РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ?
        let exists = self.positions.contains_key(&key);

        if exists {
            // РџРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ РµСЃС‚СЊ вЂ” РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј avg entry
            let mut new_qty = 0.0;
            let mut new_avg = 0.0;
            if let Some(mut pos) = self.positions.get_mut(&key) {
                let old_qty = pos.quantity;
                let old_entry = pos.entry_price;
                new_qty = old_qty + quantity;
                new_avg = if new_qty > 0.0 {
                    (old_entry * old_qty + entry_price * quantity) / new_qty
                } else {
                    entry_price
                };
                pos.quantity = new_qty;
                pos.entry_price = new_avg;
                // вњ… FIX: DCA-РѕСЂРґРµСЂ РјРѕР¶РµС‚ РЅРµСЃС‚Рё СЃРІРµР¶РёРµ SL/TP вЂ” РїСЂРёРјРµРЅСЏРµРј
                if stop_loss.is_some() {
                    pos.stop_loss = stop_loss;
                }
                if take_profit.is_some() {
                    pos.take_profit = take_profit;
                }
                tracing::info!(
                    "[{}] рџ“€ DCA add: {} +{:.6} @ {:.4} в†’ avg={:.4} total={:.6}",
                    self.config.name, symbol, quantity, entry_price, new_avg, new_qty,
                );
            }
            // вњ… FIX: СѓСЃСЂРµРґРЅРµРЅРёРµ РїРµСЂСЃРёСЃС‚РёС‚СЃСЏ РІ Р‘Р” вЂ” СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ СЂРµСЃС‚Р°СЂС‚Р°
            // С‡РёС‚Р°Р»РёСЃСЊ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Рµ qty/entry (СЃСЉРµР·Р¶Р°Р»Р° Р±Р°Р·Р° PnL, reconciliation
            // РІРёРґРµР» Р»РѕР¶РЅС‹Р№ РґСЂРµР№С„ Рё В«С‡РёРЅРёР»В» РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЋ РїРѕ Р±РёСЂР¶Рµ).
            if new_qty > 0.0 {
                let _ = sqlx::query(
                    "UPDATE positions SET quantity=?, entry_price=?, \
                     stop_loss=COALESCE(?, stop_loss), take_profit=COALESCE(?, take_profit) \
                     WHERE bot_name=? AND symbol=?",
                )
                .bind(new_qty)
                .bind(new_avg)
                .bind(stop_loss)
                .bind(take_profit)
                .bind(&self.config.name)
                .bind(symbol)
                .execute(&self.pool)
                .await;
            }
        } else {
            // РќРµС‚ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё вЂ” СЃРѕР·РґР°С‘Рј РЅРѕРІСѓСЋ (РєР°Рє РѕР±С‹С‡РЅРѕ)
            self.add_position_with_risk(
                symbol.to_string(), side.to_string(), entry_price, quantity,
                stop_loss, take_profit, origin_strategy, initial_risk,
                atr_hint, entry_spread,
            ).await;
            tracing::info!(
                "[{}] рџ“€ DCA first order: {} {:.6} @ {:.4}",
                self.config.name, symbol, quantity, entry_price,
            );
        }
    }

    pub fn get_positions(&self) -> Vec<Position> {
        self.positions.iter().map(|e| e.value().clone()).collect()
    }

    /// рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B: РѕР±РЅРѕРІРёС‚СЊ sl_order_id (СЃРѕРІРјРµСЃС‚РёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃРѕ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Рј API)
    pub fn update_sl_order_id(&self, symbol: &str, id: String) {
        let key = format!("{}_{}", self.config.name, symbol);
        if let Some(mut pos) = self.positions.get_mut(&key) {
            pos.sl_order_id = Some(id);
        }
    }

    pub fn log_state(&self) {
        let positions: Vec<_> = self.positions.iter().map(|e| e.value().clone()).collect();
        if positions.is_empty() {
            return;
        }

        let mut total_upnl = 0.0;
        for p in &positions {
            total_upnl += p.unrealized_pnl;
            let prec = price_precision(&p.symbol);
            let sl_str = p.stop_loss.map(|v| format!("{:.prec$}", v, prec = prec)).unwrap_or("вЂ”".into());
            let tp_str = p.take_profit.map(|v| format!("{:.prec$}", v, prec = prec)).unwrap_or("вЂ”".into());
            let dir = match p.side { PositionSide::Long => "Р›РћРќР“", PositionSide::Short => "РЁРћР Рў" };
            let move_pct = match p.side {
                PositionSide::Long => (p.current_price - p.entry_price) / p.entry_price * 100.0,
                PositionSide::Short => (p.entry_price - p.current_price) / p.entry_price * 100.0,
            };
            let age_sec = (Utc::now() - p.opened_at).num_seconds();
            let r_now = if p.initial_risk > 0.0 {
                let profit = match p.side {
                    PositionSide::Long => p.current_price - p.entry_price,
                    PositionSide::Short => p.entry_price - p.current_price,
                };
                profit / p.initial_risk
            } else { 0.0 };
            let r_str = if p.initial_risk > 0.0 {
                format!(" | R={:+.2}{}", r_now, if p.partial_taken { " (partвњ“)" } else { "" })
            } else { String::new() };
            // рџ”§ Р­РўРђРџ B: СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃ Р·Р°С‰РёС‚ РІ СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРµ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё
            let guard = format!(
                " | рџ›Ў{}{}",
                if p.sl_order_id.is_some() { "SLвњ“" } else { "SLвњ—" },
                if p.tp_order_id.is_some() { "TPвњ“" } else { "TPвњ—" }
            );

            match prec {
                8 => tracing::info!(
                    "рџ’ј [{}] {} {} | РѕР±СЉС‘Рј={:.0} | РІС…РѕРґ={:.8} | С‚РµРє={:.8} | О”={:+.3}% | {}СЃ | SL={} | TP={} | PnL={:+.4} | funding={:+.4} | fees={:.4}{}{}",
                    self.config.name, dir, p.symbol, p.quantity, p.entry_price, p.current_price,
                    move_pct, age_sec, sl_str, tp_str, p.unrealized_pnl, p.funding_accrued,
                    p.fees_paid, r_str, guard,
                ),
                _ => tracing::info!(
                    "рџ’ј [{}] {} {} | РѕР±СЉС‘Рј={:.5} | РІС…РѕРґ={:.2} | С‚РµРє={:.2} | О”={:+.3}% | {}СЃ | SL={} | TP={} | PnL={:+.4} | funding={:+.4} | fees={:.4}{}{}",
                    self.config.name, dir, p.symbol, p.quantity, p.entry_price, p.current_price,
                    move_pct, age_sec, sl_str, tp_str, p.unrealized_pnl, p.funding_accrued,
                    p.fees_paid, r_str, guard,
                ),
            }
        }

        tracing::info!(
            "рџ’° [{}] РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚Рѕ={} | РїР»Р°РІР°СЋС‰РёР№ PnL={:+.4} USDT",
            self.config.name, positions.len(), total_upnl
        );
    }
}

use crate::strategies::Signal;
use anyhow::Result;
use chrono::Utc;
use sqlx::sqlite::SqlitePool;

pub struct TradeJournal {
    pool: SqlitePool,
    bot_name: String,
}

impl TradeJournal {
    pub async fn new(db_path: &str, bot_name: &str) -> Result<Self> {
        let url = format!("sqlite:{}?mode=rwc", db_path);
        let pool = SqlitePool::connect(&url).await?;
        sqlx::query(
            r#"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS trade_journal (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
                bot_name TEXT NOT NULL,
                timestamp TEXT NOT NULL,
                event TEXT NOT NULL,
                symbol TEXT,
                side TEXT,
                price REAL,
                quantity REAL,
                pnl REAL,
                details TEXT
            )"#,
        )
        .execute(&pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(Self { pool, bot_name: bot_name.to_string() })
    }

    pub async fn record_signal(&self, sig: &Signal) -> Result<()> {
        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO trade_journal (bot_name, timestamp, event, symbol, side, price, quantity) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
        )
        .bind(&self.bot_name)
        .bind(Utc::now().to_rfc3339())
        .bind("SIGNAL")
        .bind(&sig.symbol)
        .bind(&sig.side)
        .bind(sig.price)
        .bind(sig.quantity)
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub async fn record_execution(&self, sig: &Signal, status: &str) -> Result<()> {
        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO trade_journal (bot_name, timestamp, event, symbol, details) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
        )
        .bind(&self.bot_name)
        .bind(Utc::now().to_rfc3339())
        .bind("EXECUTION")
        .bind(&sig.symbol)
        .bind(status)
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// вњ… PATCHED: С‚РµРїРµСЂСЊ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµС‚ side, price, quantity (РґР»СЏ РјР°СЂРєРµСЂРѕРІ РЅР° РґР°С€Р±РѕСЂРґРµ)
    /// Р Р°РЅСЊС€Рµ СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏР» С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ symbol + pnl в†’ dashboard С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂРѕРІР°Р» РїРѕ !t.price в†’ 0 РјР°СЂРєРµСЂРѕРІ
    pub async fn record_close(&self, sig: &Signal, pnl: f64) -> Result<()> {
        sqlx::query(
            "INSERT INTO trade_journal (bot_name, timestamp, event, symbol, side, price, quantity, pnl) \
             VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
        )
        .bind(&self.bot_name)
        .bind(Utc::now().to_rfc3339())
        .bind("CLOSE")
        .bind(&sig.symbol)
        .bind(&sig.side)
        .bind(sig.price)
        .bind(sig.quantity)
        .bind(pnl)
        .execute(&self.pool)
        .await?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

use super::{Signal, SignalFilter, Strategy};

use ta::Next;

const TICK_SIZE: f64 = 0.00000001;
const QTY_STEP: f64 = 0.00000001;
const FRACTAL_LOOKBACK: usize = 5;
const MAX_FRACTALS: usize = 20;
/// Р‘СѓС„РµСЂ Р·Р° С„СЂР°РєС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СѓСЂРѕРІРµРЅСЊ (0.05% РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‹) вЂ” SL СЃС‚Р°РІРёС‚СЃСЏ Р—Рђ СѓСЂРѕРІРЅРµРј,
/// С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ С„РёС‚РёР»СЊ РЅРµ СЃРЅСЏР» СЃС‚РѕРї СЂРѕРІРЅРѕ РЅР° СѓСЂРѕРІРЅРµ.
const LEVEL_BUFFER_PCT: f64 = 0.0005;
/// РџРѕР» РґРёСЃС‚Р°РЅС†РёРё СЃС‚РѕРїР° (0.3% РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‹) вЂ” Р·Р°С‰РёС‚Р° РѕС‚ СЃР»РёС€РєРѕРј С‚РµСЃРЅС‹С… СЃС‚РѕРїРѕРІ.
const MIN_SL_DIST_PCT: f64 = 0.003;
/// вњ… BUG#3-С„РёРєСЃ: РјРёРЅРёРјСѓРј Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚С‹С… СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ РґРѕ РіРµРЅРµСЂР°С†РёРё СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»РѕРІ.
/// RSI(14)+С„СЂР°РєС‚Р°Р»С‹(11)+С‚СЂРµРЅРґ(13) РґРѕР»Р¶РЅС‹ РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµС‚СЊСЃСЏ. Backfill-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ
/// РѕС‚РґР°С‘С‚ 250 СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ СЃСЂР°Р·Сѓ вЂ” РїРѕСЂРѕРі РїСЂРѕС…РѕРґРёС‚СЃСЏ РјРіРЅРѕРІРµРЅРЅРѕ.
const MIN_CANDLES_WARMUP: u64 = 30;

/// ============================================================
/// вњ… BUG#3 (0 СЃРґРµР»РѕРє) вЂ” Р РђР—Р‘РћР  РџР РР§РРќР«
/// ============================================================
/// РЎС‚Р°СЂС‹Рµ РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂС‹ РґР°РІР°Р»Рё РјР°С‚РµРјР°С‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРё РќР•Р’РћР—РњРћР–РќР«Р™ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»:
///
///   net_rr = (rrВ·d в€’ 2fВ·P) / (d + 2fВ·P),   rr = 2.0, f = 0.0005
///   СѓСЃР»РѕРІРёРµ net_rr в‰Ґ min_net_rr = 1.5  вџє  d в‰Ґ 10fВ·P = 0.5% РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‹
///
/// РџСЂРё СЌС‚РѕРј РїРѕРёСЃРє РїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РєРё Р±С‹Р» РѕРіСЂР°РЅРёС‡РµРЅ 0.5% РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‹, Р° РїРѕР» СЃС‚РѕРїР° вЂ”
/// 0.3%:  d в€€ [0.3%; 0.5%)  в†’  net_rr < 1.5 Р’РЎР•Р“Р”Рђ. РќРё РѕРґРЅР° СЃРІРµС‡Р° РЅРµ
/// РјРѕРіР»Р° РїСЂРѕР№С‚Рё С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂ вЂ” 0 СЃРґРµР»РѕРє РїРѕ РїРѕСЃС‚СЂРѕРµРЅРёСЋ, Р° РЅРµ В«СЂС‹РЅРѕРє РЅРµ РґР°Р»В».
/// Р”РѕРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕ RSI 25/75 вЂ” СЌРєСЃС‚СЂРµРјР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ Р·РѕРЅР° (РµРґРёРЅРёС†С‹ СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ РІ РґРµРЅСЊ),
/// Р° С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂ С„Р»СЌС‚Р° 1.0% Р·Р° 12 СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ РєРѕРЅС„Р»РёРєС‚РѕРІР°Р» СЃ РјРѕРјРµРЅС‚РѕРј РІС…РѕРґР°.
///
/// РќРѕРІР°СЏ РєР°Р»РёР±СЂРѕРІРєР°:
///   RSI 30/70, РїРѕРёСЃРє СѓСЂРѕРІРЅСЏ РґРѕ 1.2%, min_net_rr 1.2 (РёР· РєРѕРЅС„РёРіР°),
///   SL = СѓСЂРѕРІРµРЅСЊ В± Р±СѓС„РµСЂ 0.05%, РїРѕР» 0.3% в†’ d в€€ [0.3%; 1.25%] в†’
///   net_rr в€€ [1.25; 1.78] вЂ” РїСЂРѕС…РѕРґРёС‚ РїСЂРё min_net_rr = 1.2, Р° РїСЂРё
///   min_net_rr = 1.5 (РµСЃР»Рё Р·Р°РґР°РЅ РІ РєРѕРЅС„РёРіРµ) РїСЂРѕС…РѕРґРёС‚ РїРѕР»РѕСЃР°
///   d в€€ [0.5%; 1.25%] вЂ” С‚РѕР¶Рµ РґРѕСЃС‚РёР¶РёРјРѕ.
/// ============================================================
pub struct BounceTraderStrategy {
    symbol: String,
    balance: f64,
    risk_per_trade_pct: f64,
    fee_rate: f64,
    rr_ratio: f64,
    min_net_rr: f64,
    rsi_oversold: f64,
    rsi_overbought: f64,
    trend_threshold_pct: f64,
    trend_window: usize,
    support_search_pct: f64,
    highs: VecDeque<f64>,
    lows: VecDeque<f64>,
    closes: VecDeque<f64>,
    timestamps: VecDeque<i64>,
    max_history: usize,
    rsi: RelativeStrengthIndex,
    last_rsi: f64,
    fractal_highs: VecDeque<f64>,
    fractal_lows: VecDeque<f64>,
    filter: SignalFilter,
    last_kline_ts: i64,
    n_candles: u64,
    n_backfill: u64,
    n_fractals_high: u64,
    n_fractals_low: u64,
    n_blocked_warmup: u64,
    n_blocked_trending: u64,
    n_blocked_no_fractal: u64,
    n_blocked_rsi_neutral: u64,
    n_blocked_net_rr: u64,
    n_blocked_qty: u64,
    n_signals: u64,
    // вњ… P23: slippage-РјРѕРґРµР»СЊ РїРѕ Р¶РёРІРѕРјСѓ bookTicker (Р°СѓРґРёС‚: В«СЃРїСЂРµРґ РЅРµ РјРѕРґРµР»РёСЂСѓРµС‚СЃСЏВ»)
    last_bid: f64,
    last_ask: f64,
    max_spread_pct: f64,
    funding_pct: f64, // вњ… P24: РѕС†РµРЅРєР° funding Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (Р°СѓРґРёС‚ в„–12)
    n_spread_block: u64,
}

impl BounceTraderStrategy {
    pub fn new(symbol: String, balance: f64, risk_pct: f64, fee_rate: f64) -> Self {
        Self {
            symbol,
            balance,
            risk_per_trade_pct: risk_pct,
            fee_rate,
            rr_ratio: 2.0,
            min_net_rr: 1.2,           // вњ… Р±С‹Р»Рѕ 1.5 вЂ” СЃРј. СЂР°Р·Р±РѕСЂ BUG#3 РІС‹С€Рµ
            rsi_oversold: 30.0,        // вњ… Р±С‹Р»Рѕ 25.0 вЂ” СЌРєСЃС‚СЂРµРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕ СЂРµРґРєРѕ
            rsi_overbought: 70.0,      // вњ… Р±С‹Р»Рѕ 75.0
            trend_threshold_pct: 1.5,  // вњ… Р±С‹Р»Рѕ 1.0 вЂ” РєРѕРЅС„Р»РёРєС‚РѕРІР°Р»Рѕ СЃ RSI<25
            trend_window: 12,
            support_search_pct: 0.012, // вњ… Р±С‹Р»Рѕ 0.005 вЂ” РІРјРµСЃС‚Рµ СЃ min_net_rr=1.5 РґР°РІР°Р»Рѕ 0 СЃРґРµР»РѕРє
            highs: VecDeque::with_capacity(200),
            lows: VecDeque::with_capacity(200),
            closes: VecDeque::with_capacity(200),
            timestamps: VecDeque::with_capacity(200),
            max_history: 200,
            rsi: RelativeStrengthIndex::new(14).unwrap(),
            last_rsi: 50.0,
            fractal_highs: VecDeque::with_capacity(MAX_FRACTALS),
            fractal_lows: VecDeque::with_capacity(MAX_FRACTALS),
            filter: SignalFilter::new(120, 0.15),
            last_kline_ts: 0,
            n_candles: 0,
            n_backfill: 0,
            n_fractals_high: 0,
            n_fractals_low: 0,
            n_blocked_warmup: 0,
            n_blocked_trending: 0,
            n_blocked_no_fractal: 0,
            n_blocked_rsi_neutral: 0,
            n_blocked_net_rr: 0,
            n_blocked_qty: 0,
            n_signals: 0,
            last_bid: 0.0,
            last_ask: 0.0,
            max_spread_pct: std::env::var("P23_MAX_SPREAD_PCT")
                .ok()
                .and_then(|v| v.parse::<f64>().ok())
                .map(|v| if v > 0.0 { v } else { f64::INFINITY })
                .unwrap_or(0.0010),
            // вњ… P24: funding Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (Р°СѓРґРёС‚ в„–12) вЂ” РєРѕРЅСЃРµСЂРІР°С‚РёРІРЅРѕ РѕРґРёРЅ РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» 8h,
            // РґРѕР»СЏ РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‹; РґРµС„РѕР»С‚ 0.0001 = 0.01%/8h; РјСѓСЃРѕСЂ/<=0 РІ env -> 0.0 (РІС‹РєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРѕ)
            funding_pct: std::env::var("P24_FUNDING_PCT")
                .ok()
                .and_then(|v| v.trim().parse::<f64>().ok())
                .filter(|v| *v > 0.0)
                .unwrap_or(0.0001),
            n_spread_block: 0,
        }
    }

    pub fn with_rr(mut self, rr: f64, min_net_rr: f64) -> Self {
        self.rr_ratio = rr;
        self.min_net_rr = min_net_rr;
        self
    }

    /// вњ… BUG#3-С„РёРєСЃ: РЅР°СЃС‚СЂР°РёРІР°РµРјС‹Рµ Р·РѕРЅС‹ RSI (config: rsi_oversold/rsi_overbought)
    pub fn with_rsi(mut self, oversold: f64, overbought: f64) -> Self {
        self.rsi_oversold = oversold;
        self.rsi_overbought = overbought;
        self
    }

    /// вњ… РќР°СЃС‚СЂР°РёРІР°РµРјС‹Р№ С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂ С„Р»СЌС‚Р°: |momentum Р·Р° N СЃРІРµС‡РµР№| < threshold%
    /// (config: trend_threshold_pct / trend_window). 0 РѕС‚РєР»СЋС‡Р°РµС‚ С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂ.
    pub fn with_trend_filter(mut self, threshold_pct: f64, window: usize) -> Self {
        self.trend_threshold_pct = threshold_pct;
        self.trend_window = window.max(1);
        self
    }

    /// вњ… BUG#3-С„РёРєСЃ: СЂР°РґРёСѓСЃ РїРѕРёСЃРєР° С„СЂР°РєС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ СѓСЂРѕРІРЅСЏ, РґРѕР»СЏ РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‹
    /// (config: support_search_pct РІ РџР РћР¦Р•РќРўРђРҐ, РЅР°РїСЂ. 1.2 в†’ 0.012)
    pub fn with_search_pct(mut self, fraction: f64) -> Self {
        self.support_search_pct = fraction;
        self
    }

    /// Р•РґРёРЅР°СЏ С‚РѕС‡РєР° РїРѕРїРѕР»РЅРµРЅРёСЏ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёРё (live + backfill).
    /// Р”РµРґСѓРї РїРѕ РјРѕРЅРѕС‚РѕРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё close_time, RSI Рё С„СЂР°РєС‚Р°Р»С‹ СЃС‡РёС‚Р°СЋС‚СЃСЏ Р·РґРµСЃСЊ.
    fn push_candle(&mut self, high: f64, low: f64, close: f64, ts: i64) {
        if self.timestamps.back().map(|t| *t >= ts).unwrap_or(false) {
            return; // РґСѓР±Р»СЊ/РЅРµ РїРѕ РІРѕР·СЂР°СЃС‚Р°РЅРёСЋ вЂ” РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµРј
        }
        self.highs.push_back(high);
        self.lows.push_back(low);
        self.closes.push_back(close);
        self.timestamps.push_back(ts);
        while self.highs.len() > self.max_history {
            self.highs.pop_front();
            self.lows.pop_front();
            self.closes.pop_front();
            self.timestamps.pop_front();
        }
        self.last_rsi = self.rsi.next(close);
        self.detect_fractals();
    }

    fn detect_fractals(&mut self) {
        let n = self.highs.len();
        if n < FRACTAL_LOOKBACK * 2 + 1 {
            return;
        }
        let candidate_idx = n - 1 - FRACTAL_LOOKBACK;
        if candidate_idx < FRACTAL_LOOKBACK {
            return;
        }
        let high_candidate = self.highs[candidate_idx];
        let low_candidate = self.lows[candidate_idx];
        let is_fractal_high = (candidate_idx - FRACTAL_LOOKBACK..candidate_idx + FRACTAL_LOOKBACK + 1)
            .filter(|&i| i != candidate_idx)
            .all(|i| self.highs[candidate_idx] > self.highs[i]);
        let is_fractal_low = (candidate_idx - FRACTAL_LOOKBACK..candidate_idx + FRACTAL_LOOKBACK + 1)
            .filter(|&i| i != candidate_idx)
            .all(|i| self.lows[candidate_idx] < self.lows[i]);
        if is_fractal_high {
            self.fractal_highs.push_back(high_candidate);
            while self.fractal_highs.len() > MAX_FRACTALS {
                self.fractal_highs.pop_front();
            }
            self.n_fractals_high += 1;
        }
        if is_fractal_low {
            self.fractal_lows.push_back(low_candidate);
            while self.fractal_lows.len() > MAX_FRACTALS {
                self.fractal_lows.pop_front();
            }
            self.n_fractals_low += 1;
        }
    }

    fn is_ranging(&self) -> bool {
        if self.trend_threshold_pct <= 0.0 {
            return true; // С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂ РѕС‚РєР»СЋС‡С‘РЅ РІ РєРѕРЅС„РёРіРµ
        }
        if self.closes.len() < self.trend_window + 1 {
            return false;
        }
        let recent = self.closes.len() - 1;
        let past = recent - self.trend_window;
        let momentum_pct =
            (self.closes[recent] - self.closes[past]).abs() / self.closes[past] * 100.0;
        momentum_pct < self.trend_threshold_pct
    }

    /// вњ… BUG#3-С„РёРєСЃ: Р±Р»РёР¶Р°Р№С€РёР№ С„СЂР°РєС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СѓСЂРѕРІРµРЅСЊ СЃ Р›Р®Р‘РћР™ СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅС‹ С†РµРЅС‹.
    /// РЎС‚Р°СЂР°СЏ Р»РѕРіРёРєР° Р±С‹Р»Р° РїРµСЂРµРєРѕС€РµРЅР°: РґР»СЏ BUY СЃРЅР°С‡Р°Р»Р° РёСЃРєР°Р»СЃСЏ СѓСЂРѕРІРµРЅСЊ Р’Р«РЁР•
    /// С†РµРЅС‹ (РІ 0.5%), Рё С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё РµРіРѕ РЅРµС‚ вЂ” РЅРёР¶Рµ (РІ 0.2%). РўРµРїРµСЂСЊ РїСЂРѕСЃС‚Рѕ
    /// Р±Р»РёР¶Р°Р№С€РёР№ РІ СЂР°РґРёСѓСЃРµ support_search_pct вЂ” СѓСЂРѕРІРµРЅСЊ Рё РµСЃС‚СЊ РѕСЂРёРµРЅС‚РёСЂ SL.
    fn find_nearby_level(&self, levels: &VecDeque<f64>, price: f64) -> Option<f64> {
        let mut best: Option<f64> = None;
        let mut best_dist = f64::INFINITY;
        for &lvl in levels {
            let dist = (price - lvl).abs() / price;
            if dist <= self.support_search_pct && dist < best_dist {
                best_dist = dist;
                best = Some(lvl);
            }
        }
        best
    }

    fn net_rr(&self, entry: f64, sl: f64, tp: f64, is_long: bool) -> Option<f64> {
        let fee_cost = 2.0 * self.fee_rate * entry;
        // вњ… P23: РїРѕР»РЅР°СЏ СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃРїСЂРµРґР° Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (РІС…РѕРґ РїРѕ ask, РІС‹С…РѕРґ РїРѕ bid).
        // Р”Рѕ РїРµСЂРІРѕРіРѕ bookTicker СЃРїСЂРµРґ РЅРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РµРЅ вЂ” РјРѕРґРµР»СЊ РєР°Рє СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ.
        let spread_cost = if self.last_bid > 0.0 && self.last_ask > self.last_bid {
            self.last_ask - self.last_bid
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        let (gross_profit, gross_loss) = if is_long {
            (tp - entry - spread_cost, entry - sl + spread_cost)
        } else {
            (entry - tp - spread_cost, sl - entry + spread_cost)
        };
        // вњ… P24: funding Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (Р°СѓРґРёС‚ в„–12) вЂ” РєРѕРЅСЃРµСЂРІР°С‚РёРІРЅРѕ РѕРґРёРЅ РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» 8h
        let funding_cost = entry * self.funding_pct;
        let net_profit = gross_profit - fee_cost - funding_cost;
        let net_loss = gross_loss + fee_cost + funding_cost;
        if net_loss <= 0.0 || net_profit <= 0.0 {
            return None;
        }
        Some(net_profit / net_loss)
    }

    /// вњ… P23: slippage-guard вЂ” true = СЃРїСЂРµРґ С€РёСЂРµ Р»РёРјРёС‚Р°, РІС…РѕРґ Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰С‘РЅ
    fn spread_too_wide(&mut self) -> bool {
        if self.last_bid > 0.0 && self.last_ask > self.last_bid {
            let mid = (self.last_bid + self.last_ask) * 0.5;
            if (self.last_ask - self.last_bid) / mid > self.max_spread_pct {
                self.n_spread_block += 1;
                return true;
            }
        }
        false
    }
}

impl Strategy for BounceTraderStrategy {
    fn name(&self) -> &str {
        "BounceTrader"
    }

    /// вњ… Рџ3: РµРґРёРЅСЃС‚РІРµРЅРЅР°СЏ СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ, СЏРІРЅРѕ РїРѕРїСЂРѕСЃРёРІС€Р°СЏ backfill-РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЋ
    /// РґР»СЏ РјРіРЅРѕРІРµРЅРЅРѕРіРѕ РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµРІР° RSI/С„СЂР°РєС‚Р°Р»РѕРІ/С‚СЂРµРЅРґ-С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂР°.
    /// РЎРёРіРЅР°Р»С‹ РїРѕ backfill РІСЃС‘ СЂР°РІРЅРѕ Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰РµРЅС‹ РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё process() вЂ”
    /// РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµРІ Р±РµР·РѕРїР°СЃРµРЅ.
    fn wants_backfill(&self) -> bool {
        true
    }

    fn notify_position_open(&mut self) {
        self.filter.on_position_opened();
    }

    fn notify_position_closed(&mut self, _pnl: f64) {
        self.filter.on_position_closed(self.last_kline_ts);
    }

    fn process(&mut self, msg: &WebSocketMessage) -> Result<Option<Signal>> {
        // вњ… P23: Р»РѕРІРёРј Р¶РёРІРѕР№ СЃРїСЂРµРґ РёР· bookTicker (guard РІС…РѕРґР° + СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃРїСЂРµРґР° РІ net_rr)
        if let WebSocketMessage::BookTicker(b) = msg {
            if b.symbol.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&self.symbol)
                && b.best_bid > 0.0
                && b.best_ask > b.best_bid
            {
                self.last_bid = b.best_bid;
                self.last_ask = b.best_ask;
            }
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let kline = match msg {
            WebSocketMessage::Kline(k) => k,
            _ => return Ok(None),
        };
        if !kline.symbol.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&self.symbol) {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if !kline.is_closed {
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // вњ… Рџ3: backfill-СЃРІРµС‡Рё (РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ РѕС‚ REST-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂР°, 250 С€С‚ РїСЂРё СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚Рµ) вЂ”
        // РўРћР›Р¬РљРћ РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµРІ РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂРѕРІ. РЎРёРіРЅР°Р»С‹ РїРѕ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Рј С†РµРЅР°Рј Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰РµРЅС‹:
        // РІС…РѕРґ РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРёР»СЃСЏ Р±С‹ РїРѕ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµРјСѓ СЂС‹РЅРєСѓ РїСЂРѕС‚РёРІ С†РµРЅС‹ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»Р°.
        if kline.is_backfill {
            self.n_backfill += 1;
            self.push_candle(kline.high, kline.low, kline.close, kline.timestamp);
            return Ok(None);
        }

        if kline.timestamp == self.last_kline_ts {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        self.push_candle(kline.high, kline.low, kline.close, kline.timestamp);
        self.last_kline_ts = kline.timestamp;
        self.n_candles += 1;

        let price = kline.close;

        // вњ… BUG#3-С„РёРєСЃ: РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµРІ вЂ” РЅРµ С‚РѕСЂРіРѕРІР°С‚СЊ РЅР° В«СЃС‹СЂС‹С…В» РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂР°С…
        // вњ… V3.2: РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµРІ Р·Р°СЃС‡РёС‚С‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ, РµСЃР»Рё РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ РЈР–Р• РїСЂРёС€Р»Р° РѕС‚
        // backfill-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂР° (>=100 СЃРІРµС‡РµР№: RSI/С„СЂР°РєС‚Р°Р»С‹/С‚СЂРµРЅРґ РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµС‚С‹).
        // Р Р°РЅСЊС€Рµ С‚СЂРµР±РѕРІР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ 30 Р–РР’Р«РҐ С‡Р°СЃРѕРІ РїСЂРѕСЃС‚РѕСЏ РїСЂРё РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµС‚С‹С…
        // РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂР°С… вЂ” РјС‘СЂС‚РІРѕРµ РѕР¶РёРґР°РЅРёРµ РЅР° 1h TF.
        if self.n_candles < MIN_CANDLES_WARMUP && self.n_backfill < 100 {
            self.n_blocked_warmup += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if !self.filter.can_signal(price, kline.timestamp) {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if !self.is_ranging() {
            self.n_blocked_trending += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        if self.last_rsi < self.rsi_oversold {
            // ===== LONG: РѕС‚СЃРєРѕРє РѕС‚ С„СЂР°РєС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅРѕР№ РїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РєРё =====
            let Some(support) = self.find_nearby_level(&self.fractal_lows, price) else {
                self.n_blocked_no_fractal += 1;
                return Ok(None);
            };
            // SL вЂ” Р—Рђ СѓСЂРѕРІРЅРµРј (РЅРёР¶Рµ РїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РєРё) + Р±СѓС„РµСЂ, РїРѕР» 0.3%.
            // Р•СЃР»Рё СѓСЂРѕРІРµРЅСЊ РїСЂРѕРєРѕР»РѕС‚ (support РІС‹С€Рµ С†РµРЅС‹) вЂ” СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ РїРѕР».
            let buffer = price * LEVEL_BUFFER_PCT;
            let sl_dist = ((price - support) + buffer).max(price * MIN_SL_DIST_PCT);
            let entry = round_to_tick(price, TICK_SIZE);
            let sl = round_to_tick(price - sl_dist, TICK_SIZE);
            let tp = round_to_tick(price + sl_dist * self.rr_ratio, TICK_SIZE);
            // вњ… P23: slippage-guard вЂ” СЃРїСЂРµРґ С€РёСЂРµ Р»РёРјРёС‚Р° = РІС…РѕРґ Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰С‘РЅ
            if self.spread_too_wide() {
                return Ok(None);
            }
            let Some(eff_rr) = self.net_rr(entry, sl, tp, true) else {
                return Ok(None);
            };
            if eff_rr < self.min_net_rr {
                self.n_blocked_net_rr += 1;
                return Ok(None);
            }
            let raw_qty = calc_position_size_with_fees(
                self.balance,
                entry,
                sl,
                self.risk_per_trade_pct,
                self.fee_rate * 2.0,
            );
            let quantity = floor_to_step(raw_qty, QTY_STEP);
            if quantity <= 0.0 {
                self.n_blocked_qty += 1;
                return Ok(None);
            }
            self.filter.on_signal(entry, kline.timestamp);
            self.n_signals += 1;
            tracing::info!(
                "BounceTrader BUY | RSI={:.1} support={:.4} entry={:.4} SL={:.4} TP={:.4} qty={:.6} netRR={:.2}",
                self.last_rsi, support, entry, sl, tp, quantity, eff_rr
            );
            return Ok(Some(Signal {
                symbol: kline.symbol.clone(),
                side: "BUY".to_string(),
                price: entry,
                quantity,
                strategy_name: self.name().to_string(),
                leverage: 1.0,
                stop_loss: Some(sl),
                take_profit: Some(tp),
                atr_hint: 0.0,
                entry_spread: 0.0,
            }));
        } else if self.last_rsi > self.rsi_overbought {
            // ===== SHORT: РѕС‚СЃРєРѕРє РѕС‚ С„СЂР°РєС‚Р°Р»СЊРЅРѕРіРѕ СЃРѕРїСЂРѕС‚РёРІР»РµРЅРёСЏ =====
            let Some(resistance) = self.find_nearby_level(&self.fractal_highs, price) else {
                self.n_blocked_no_fractal += 1;
                return Ok(None);
            };
            // SL вЂ” Р—Рђ СѓСЂРѕРІРЅРµРј (РІС‹С€Рµ СЃРѕРїСЂРѕС‚РёРІР»РµРЅРёСЏ) + Р±СѓС„РµСЂ, РїРѕР» 0.3%
            let buffer = price * LEVEL_BUFFER_PCT;
            let sl_dist = ((resistance - price) + buffer).max(price * MIN_SL_DIST_PCT);
            let entry = round_to_tick(price, TICK_SIZE);
            let sl = round_to_tick(price + sl_dist, TICK_SIZE);
            let tp = round_to_tick(price - sl_dist * self.rr_ratio, TICK_SIZE);
            // вњ… P23: slippage-guard вЂ” СЃРїСЂРµРґ С€РёСЂРµ Р»РёРјРёС‚Р° = РІС…РѕРґ Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰С‘РЅ
            if self.spread_too_wide() {
                return Ok(None);
            }
            let Some(eff_rr) = self.net_rr(entry, sl, tp, false) else {
                return Ok(None);
            };
            if eff_rr < self.min_net_rr {
                self.n_blocked_net_rr += 1;
                return Ok(None);
            }
            let raw_qty = calc_position_size_with_fees(
                self.balance,
                entry,
                sl,
                self.risk_per_trade_pct,
                self.fee_rate * 2.0,
            );
            let quantity = floor_to_step(raw_qty, QTY_STEP);
            if quantity <= 0.0 {
                self.n_blocked_qty += 1;
                return Ok(None);
            }
            self.filter.on_signal(entry, kline.timestamp);
            self.n_signals += 1;
            tracing::info!(
                "BounceTrader SELL | RSI={:.1} resistance={:.4} entry={:.4} SL={:.4} TP={:.4} qty={:.6} netRR={:.2}",
                self.last_rsi, resistance, entry, sl, tp, quantity, eff_rr
            );
            return Ok(Some(Signal {
                symbol: kline.symbol.clone(),
                side: "SELL".to_string(),
                price: entry,
                quantity,
                strategy_name: self.name().to_string(),
                leverage: 1.0,
                stop_loss: Some(sl),
                take_profit: Some(tp),
                atr_hint: 0.0,
                entry_spread: 0.0,
            }));
        } else {
            self.n_blocked_rsi_neutral += 1;
        }
        Ok(None)
    }

    fn diagnostics(&self) -> Option<String> {
        Some(format!(
            "BounceTrader diag | candles={} backfill={} fractals_h={} fractals_l={} signals={} | \
             warmup_block={} trending_block={} no_fractal={} rsi_neutral={} net_rr_block={} spr_block={} qty_block={} | \
             RSI={:.1} | rr={} min_net_rr={} rsi[{:.0}/{:.0}] trend<{:.1}%/{}w search={:.2}% fee={:.3}%",
            self.n_candles,
            self.n_backfill,
            self.n_fractals_high,
            self.n_fractals_low,
            self.n_signals,
            self.n_blocked_warmup,
            self.n_blocked_trending,
            self.n_blocked_no_fractal,
            self.n_blocked_rsi_neutral,
            self.n_blocked_net_rr,
            self.n_spread_block,
            self.n_blocked_qty,
            self.last_rsi,
            self.rr_ratio,
            self.min_net_rr,
            self.rsi_oversold,
            self.rsi_overbought,
            self.trend_threshold_pct,
            self.trend_window,
            self.support_search_pct * 100.0,
            self.fee_rate * 100.0
        ))
    }

    fn in_position(&self) -> bool {
        // вњ… P21: Cooldown Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ РќР• СЃС‡РёС‚Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ В«РІ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРёВ».
        // Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂ СѓС…РѕРґРёС‚ РІ Cooldown РІ РјРѕРјРµРЅС‚ Р’Р«Р”РђР§Р СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»Р° (on_signal) вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ
        // Р°РЅС‚Рё-С„Р»Р°Рї. Р Р°РЅСЊС€Рµ in_position()=true РЅР° СЃРІРµР¶РµРІС‹РґР°РЅРЅРѕРј СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»Рµ
        // РјРіРЅРѕРІРµРЅРЅРѕ СЃР°РјРѕ-Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІР°Р» РµРіРѕ РІ main.rs (В«РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ СѓР¶Рµ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚Р°В»),
        // Рё СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ РЅРµ РјРѕРіР»Р° РІРѕР№С‚Рё РЅРёРєРѕРіРґР°. Anti-flap РѕСЃС‚Р°С‘С‚СЃСЏ РІ can_signal().
        matches!(self.filter.state(), super::PositionState::InPosition)
    }
}
