//! gold_session_trader.rs вЂ” С‚РѕСЂРіРѕРІР»СЏ Р·РѕР»РѕС‚РѕРј (XAUUSDT) РїРѕ London session.
//!
//! Р›РѕРіРёРєР°:
//!   1. Time filter: С‚РѕСЂРіСѓРµРј РўРћР›Р¬РљРћ СЃРІРµС‡Рё Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚С‹Рµ РІ 10:00 Рё 14:00 UTC
//!      10:00 = London morning (РёРЅСЃС‚РёС‚СѓС†РёРѕРЅР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РїСЂРёС‚РѕРє)
//!      14:00 = pre-US open (РјР°РєСЂРѕ-РґР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РЎРЁРђ)
//!   2. Marubozu detector: body > 70% of range (С‡РёСЃС‚С‹Р№ РёРјРїСѓР»СЊСЃ, Р±РµР· С‚РµРЅРµР№)
//!   3. Volume spike: volume > 2Г— СЃСЂРµРґРЅРµРіРѕ Р·Р° 20 СЃРІРµС‡РµР№
//!   4. Direction: РІ СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅСѓ body (Р±С‹С‡СЊСЏ Marubozu в†’ LONG, РјРµРґРІРµР¶СЊСЏ в†’ SHORT)
//!   5. SL: low/high СЃРІРµС‡Рё Marubozu (РѕС‡РµРЅСЊ РєРѕСЂРѕС‚РєРёР№)
//!   6. TP: 3Г— SL distance (RR = 3.0)
//!   7. Maker entry (limit РѕСЂРґРµСЂ)
//!
//! РњР°С‚РµРјР°С‚РёРєР° (XAUUSDT 1h):
//!   ATR(14) в‰€ 0.30% per 1h
//!   Marubozu SL в‰€ 0.20-0.40% (РєРѕСЂРѕС‚РєРёР№, Р±РµР· С‚РµРЅРµР№)
//!   TP = 3Г— SL в‰€ 0.60-1.20%
//!   Round-trip fee: maker 0.02% + taker SL 0.05% = 0.07%
//!   Net RR = (0.90 - 0.07) / (0.30 + 0.07) = 0.83/0.37 = 2.24
//!   Breakeven WR = 1/(1+2.24) = 30.9%
//!   Р¦РµР»РµРІР°СЏ WR: 40-50% (РёРЅСЃС‚РёС‚СѓС†РёРѕРЅР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ momentum = РІС‹СЃРѕРєР°СЏ С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ)
//!   2 trades/day Г— 250 days = 500 trades/year
//!   EV = +0.18 USDT/trade Г— 500 = +90 USDT/РіРѕРґ РЅР° $200 = +45% РіРѕРґРѕРІС‹С…

use super::{Signal, SignalFilter, Strategy};
use crate::utils::math::{calc_position_size_with_fees, floor_to_step, round_to_tick};
use crate::websocket::WebSocketMessage;
use anyhow::Result;
use std::collections::VecDeque;

const TICK_SIZE: f64 = 0.00000001;
const QTY_STEP: f64 = 0.00000001;

/// Р§Р°СЃС‹ UTC РґР»СЏ С‚РѕСЂРіРѕРІР»Рё (London session key times)
const TRADE_HOURS_UTC: &[u32] = &[10, 14];

/// РњРёРЅРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ РѕС‚РЅРѕС€РµРЅРёРµ body Рє range РґР»СЏ Marubozu (70%)
const MARUBOZU_BODY_RATIO: f64 = 0.70;

/// РњРёРЅРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ volume spike (2Г— СЃСЂРµРґРЅРµРіРѕ)
const VOLUME_SPIKE_MULT: f64 = 2.0;

/// РћРєРЅРѕ РґР»СЏ СЃСЂРµРґРЅРµРіРѕ volume (20 СЃРІРµС‡РµР№)
const VOLUME_WINDOW: usize = 20;

/// RR ratio (TP = 3Г— SL)
const RR_RATIO: f64 = 3.0;

/// РњРёРЅРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ Net RR РїРѕСЃР»Рµ fees
const MIN_NET_RR: f64 = 1.5;

/// РњРёРЅРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ РґР»РёРЅР° body СЃРІРµС‡Рё (РІ % РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‹) РґР»СЏ РІР°Р»РёРґРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»Р°
const MIN_BODY_PCT: f64 = 0.15;

pub struct GoldSessionStrategy {
    symbol: String,
    balance: f64,
    risk_per_trade_pct: f64,
    fee_rate: f64,

    // РСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ СЃРІРµС‡РµР№
    highs: VecDeque<f64>,
    lows: VecDeque<f64>,
    opens: VecDeque<f64>,
    closes: VecDeque<f64>,
    volumes: VecDeque<f64>,
    timestamps: VecDeque<i64>,
    max_history: usize,

    // FSM
    filter: SignalFilter,
    last_kline_ts: i64,

    // РЎС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРєРё
    n_candles: u64,
    n_signals: u64,
    n_blocked_not_trade_hour: u64,
    n_blocked_no_marubozu: u64,
    n_blocked_low_volume: u64,
    n_blocked_net_rr: u64,
    n_blocked_small_body: u64,
}

impl GoldSessionStrategy {
    pub fn new(symbol: String, balance: f64, risk_pct: f64, fee_rate: f64) -> Self {
        Self {
            symbol, balance, risk_per_trade_pct: risk_pct, fee_rate,
            highs: VecDeque::with_capacity(200), lows: VecDeque::with_capacity(200),
            opens: VecDeque::with_capacity(200), closes: VecDeque::with_capacity(200),
            volumes: VecDeque::with_capacity(200), timestamps: VecDeque::with_capacity(200),
            max_history: 200,
            filter: SignalFilter::new(300, 0.10), // 5 min cooldown
            last_kline_ts: 0,
            n_candles: 0, n_signals: 0,
            n_blocked_not_trade_hour: 0, n_blocked_no_marubozu: 0,
            n_blocked_low_volume: 0, n_blocked_net_rr: 0, n_blocked_small_body: 0,
        }
    }

    /// РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР°: Р·Р°РєСЂС‹Р»Р°СЃСЊ Р»Рё СЃРІРµС‡Р° РІ С‚РѕСЂРіРѕРІС‹Р№ С‡Р°СЃ (10:00 РёР»Рё 14:00 UTC)
    fn is_trade_hour(&self, ts_ms: i64) -> bool {
        let secs_of_day = ((ts_ms / 1000) % 86_400) as u32;
        let hour = secs_of_day / 3600;
        TRADE_HOURS_UTC.contains(&hour)
    }

    /// Marubozu detector: body > 70% of range
    fn is_marubozu(&self, open: f64, high: f64, low: f64, close: f64) -> bool {
        let range = high - low;
        if range <= 0.0 { return false; }
        let body = (close - open).abs();
        let body_ratio = body / range;
        body_ratio >= MARUBOZU_BODY_RATIO
    }

    /// Volume spike: С‚РµРєСѓС‰РёР№ volume > VOLUME_SPIKE_MULT Г— СЃСЂРµРґРЅРµРіРѕ
    fn is_volume_spike(&self) -> bool {
        if self.volumes.len() < VOLUME_WINDOW + 1 { return false; }
        let current = *self.volumes.back().unwrap();
        let sum: f64 = self.volumes.iter().rev().skip(1).take(VOLUME_WINDOW).sum();
        let avg = sum / VOLUME_WINDOW as f64;
        if avg <= 0.0 { return false; }
        current >= avg * VOLUME_SPIKE_MULT
    }

    /// Р Р°СЃС‡С‘С‚ net RR РїРѕСЃР»Рµ fees
    fn net_rr(&self, entry: f64, sl: f64, tp: f64, is_long: bool) -> Option<f64> {
        let fee_cost = 2.0 * self.fee_rate * entry;
        let (gross_profit, gross_loss) = if is_long {
            (tp - entry, entry - sl)
        } else {
            (entry - tp, sl - entry)
        };
        let net_profit = gross_profit - fee_cost;
        let net_loss = gross_loss + fee_cost;
        if net_loss <= 0.0 || net_profit <= 0.0 { return None; }
        Some(net_profit / net_loss)
    }

    /// РЎСЂРµРґРЅРёР№ volume РґР»СЏ РґРёР°РіРЅРѕСЃС‚РёРєРё
    fn avg_volume(&self) -> f64 {
        if self.volumes.len() < VOLUME_WINDOW { return 0.0; }
        let sum: f64 = self.volumes.iter().rev().take(VOLUME_WINDOW).sum();
        sum / VOLUME_WINDOW as f64
    }
}

impl Strategy for GoldSessionStrategy {
    fn name(&self) -> &str { "GoldSession" }

    fn notify_position_open(&mut self) { self.filter.on_position_opened(); }
    fn notify_position_closed(&mut self, _pnl: f64) { self.filter.on_position_closed(self.last_kline_ts); }

    fn process(&mut self, msg: &WebSocketMessage) -> Result<Option<Signal>> {
        let kline = match msg { WebSocketMessage::Kline(k) => k, _ => return Ok(None) };
        if !kline.symbol.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&self.symbol) { return Ok(None); }
        if !kline.is_closed { return Ok(None); }
        if kline.timestamp == self.last_kline_ts { return Ok(None); }

        self.last_kline_ts = kline.timestamp;
        self.n_candles += 1;

        // 1. РќР°РєР°РїР»РёРІР°РµРј РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЋ
        self.highs.push_back(kline.high);
        self.lows.push_back(kline.low);
        self.opens.push_back(kline.open);
        self.closes.push_back(kline.close);
        self.volumes.push_back(kline.volume);
        self.timestamps.push_back(kline.timestamp);
        while self.highs.len() > self.max_history {
            self.highs.pop_front(); self.lows.pop_front();
            self.opens.pop_front(); self.closes.pop_front();
            self.volumes.pop_front(); self.timestamps.pop_front();
        }

        // 2. FSM: РЅРµ С‚РѕСЂРіСѓРµРј РµСЃР»Рё РІ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё РёР»Рё cooldown
        let price = kline.close;
        if !self.filter.can_signal(price, kline.timestamp) { return Ok(None); }

        // 3. Time filter: С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ 10:00 Рё 14:00 UTC
        if !self.is_trade_hour(kline.timestamp) {
            self.n_blocked_not_trade_hour += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // 4. Marubozu detector
        let open = kline.open;
        let high = kline.high;
        let low = kline.low;
        let close = kline.close;

        if !self.is_marubozu(open, high, low, close) {
            self.n_blocked_no_marubozu += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // 5. Body size check (РјРёРЅРёРјСѓРј 0.15% РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‹)
        let body_pct = (close - open).abs() / close * 100.0;
        if body_pct < MIN_BODY_PCT {
            self.n_blocked_small_body += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // 6. Volume spike
        if !self.is_volume_spike() {
            self.n_blocked_low_volume += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // 7. Direction: РІ СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅСѓ body
        let is_long = close > open;
        let (side, sl_price, tp_price) = if is_long {
            // Bullish Marubozu в†’ LONG
            let sl = low; // SL РЅР° low СЃРІРµС‡Рё
            let sl_dist = (price - sl).abs().max(price * 0.002); // min 0.2%
            let sl = price - sl_dist;
            let tp = price + sl_dist * RR_RATIO;
            ("BUY", sl, tp)
        } else {
            // Bearish Marubozu в†’ SHORT
            let sl = high; // SL РЅР° high СЃРІРµС‡Рё
            let sl_dist = (sl - price).abs().max(price * 0.002);
            let sl = price + sl_dist;
            let tp = price - sl_dist * RR_RATIO;
            ("SELL", sl, tp)
        };

        let entry = round_to_tick(price, TICK_SIZE);
        let sl = round_to_tick(sl_price, TICK_SIZE);
        let tp = round_to_tick(tp_price, TICK_SIZE);

        // 8. Net RR check
        let eff_rr = match self.net_rr(entry, sl, tp, is_long) {
            Some(rr) => rr,
            None => return Ok(None),
        };
        if eff_rr < MIN_NET_RR {
            self.n_blocked_net_rr += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // 9. Position sizing
        let raw_qty = calc_position_size_with_fees(
            self.balance, entry, sl, self.risk_per_trade_pct, self.fee_rate * 2.0,
        );
        let quantity = floor_to_step(raw_qty, QTY_STEP);
        if quantity <= 0.0 { return Ok(None); }

        // 10. Signal!
        self.filter.on_signal(entry, kline.timestamp);
        self.n_signals += 1;
        tracing::info!(
            "GOLD {} | body={:.3}% vol_spike={:.1}Г— entry={:.4} SL={:.4} TP={:.4} qty={:.6} netRR={:.2}",
            side, body_pct, kline.volume / self.avg_volume(), entry, sl, tp, quantity, eff_rr
        );

        Ok(Some(Signal {
            symbol: kline.symbol.clone(),
            side: side.to_string(),
            price: entry,
            quantity,
            strategy_name: self.name().to_string(),
            leverage: 1.0,
            stop_loss: Some(sl),
            take_profit: Some(tp),
            atr_hint: 0.0,
            entry_spread: 0.0,
        }))
    }

    fn diagnostics(&self) -> Option<String> {
        Some(format!(
            "GoldSession(candles) diag | candles={} signals={} | not_trade_hour={} no_marubozu={} small_body={} low_vol={} net_rr_block={} | avg_vol={:.0} | rr={} min_net_rr={} fee={:.4}%",
            self.n_candles, self.n_signals,
            self.n_blocked_not_trade_hour, self.n_blocked_no_marubozu,
            self.n_blocked_small_body, self.n_blocked_low_volume, self.n_blocked_net_rr,
            self.avg_volume(), RR_RATIO, MIN_NET_RR, self.fee_rate * 100.0,
        ))
    }

    fn in_position(&self) -> bool {
        // вњ… P21: Cooldown Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ РќР• СЃС‡РёС‚Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ В«РІ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРёВ».
        // Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂ СѓС…РѕРґРёС‚ РІ Cooldown РІ РјРѕРјРµРЅС‚ Р’Р«Р”РђР§Р СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»Р° (on_signal) вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ
        // Р°РЅС‚Рё-С„Р»Р°Рї. Р Р°РЅСЊС€Рµ in_position()=true РЅР° СЃРІРµР¶РµРІС‹РґР°РЅРЅРѕРј СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»Рµ
        // РјРіРЅРѕРІРµРЅРЅРѕ СЃР°РјРѕ-Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІР°Р» РµРіРѕ РІ main.rs (В«РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ СѓР¶Рµ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚Р°В»),
        // Рё СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ РЅРµ РјРѕРіР»Р° РІРѕР№С‚Рё РЅРёРєРѕРіРґР°. Anti-flap РѕСЃС‚Р°С‘С‚СЃСЏ РІ can_signal().
        matches!(self.filter.state(), super::PositionState::InPosition)
    }
}

//! keltner_dca.rs вЂ” Veles-style Keltner Pullback + DCA (5 orders) + Donchian TP/SL.
//!
//! Р›РѕРіРёРєР° (Veles Finance gold bot):
//!   1. Keltner Channel: EMA(20) В± 2Г—ATR(10)
//!   2. Entry LONG: close в‰¤ lower Keltner band Р price > EMA200 (uptrend pullback)
//!   3. DCA: 5 РѕСЂРґРµСЂРѕРІ, РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ -1% РѕС‚ РїСЂРµРґС‹РґСѓС‰РµРіРѕ
//!      Order 1: 30% planned qty @ first Keltner touch
//!      Order 2: 25% @ -1.0% from order 1
//!      Order 3: 20% @ -2.0%
//!      Order 4: 15% @ -3.5%
//!      Order 5: 10% @ -5.0%
//!   4. TP: Donchian 20-period high (РїСЂРѕР±РѕР№ РІРµСЂС…РЅРµР№ Turtle zone)
//!   5. SL: close РЅРёР¶Рµ Donchian 20-period low
//!   6. LONG-only (Р·РѕР»РѕС‚Рѕ РёРјРµРµС‚ bullish bias вЂ” central bank buying, inflation hedge)
//!
//! Veles benchmark: +10% Р·Р° 3 РјРµСЃСЏС†Р° РЅР° $300 = +40%/РіРѕРґ, Max DD -9%
//! РќР°С€ target: +15-25%/РіРѕРґ РЅР° $200 (Р±РµР· DCA alpha, СЃ DCA вЂ” РІС‹С€Рµ)
//!
//! DCA state machine:
//!   Flat в†’ Order1 в†’ Order2 в†’ Order3 в†’ Order4 в†’ Order5 в†’ InPosition в†’ Flat
//!   РљР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ DCA order вЂ” РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ signal. can_dca()=true в†’ РѕР±С…РѕРґРёС‚ has_position check.

use super::{Signal, SignalFilter, Strategy};
use crate::utils::math::{calc_position_size_with_fees, floor_to_step, round_to_tick};
use crate::websocket::WebSocketMessage;
use anyhow::Result;
use std::collections::VecDeque;

const TICK_SIZE: f64 = 0.01;
const QTY_STEP: f64 = 0.001;

/// Keltner EMA period
const KELTNER_EMA_PERIOD: usize = 20;
/// Keltner ATR multiplier
const KELTNER_ATR_MULT: f64 = 2.0;
/// ATR period for Keltner
const KELTNER_ATR_PERIOD: usize = 10;

/// Donchian channel period (Turtle zone)
const DONCHIAN_PERIOD: usize = 20;

/// DCA configuration
const DCA_MAX_ORDERS: usize = 5;
const DCA_DROP_STEPS: [f64; 5] = [0.0, -0.01, -0.02, -0.035, -0.05]; // % from order 1
const DCA_QTY_FRACTIONS: [f64; 5] = [0.30, 0.25, 0.20, 0.15, 0.10]; // 30+25+20+15+10=100%

/// EMA200 filter вЂ” only LONG above EMA200
const EMA200_PERIOD: usize = 200;
const EMA200_ALPHA: f64 = 2.0 / (EMA200_PERIOD as f64 + 1.0);

/// Min net RR after fees
const MIN_NET_RR: f64 = 1.0;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
enum DcaState {
    Flat,           // No position, waiting for Keltner touch
    Averaging,      // In DCA mode, placing orders 2-5
    FullyInvested,  // All 5 orders placed, waiting for TP/SL
}

pub struct KeltnerDcaStrategy {
    symbol: String,
    balance: f64,
    risk_per_trade_pct: f64,
    fee_rate: f64,

    // Candle history
    highs: VecDeque<f64>,
    lows: VecDeque<f64>,
    closes: VecDeque<f64>,
    volumes: VecDeque<f64>,
    timestamps: VecDeque<i64>,
    max_history: usize,

    // ATR
    atrs: VecDeque<f64>,

    // EMA for Keltner
    keltner_ema: Option<f64>,
    keltner_alpha: f64,

    // EMA200 filter
    ema200: Option<f64>,

    // DCA state
    dca_state: DcaState,
    dca_order_idx: usize,       // 0=not started, 1-5=order placed
    dca_order1_price: f64,      // price of first DCA order
    dca_total_qty: f64,        // accumulated qty across all DCA orders
    dca_avg_entry: f64,         // weighted avg entry price
    dca_sl_price: f64,          // SL price (Donchian low at time of first order)
    dca_tp_price: f64,          // TP price (Donchian high at time of first order)

    // Cooldown filter
    filter: SignalFilter,
    last_kline_ts: i64,

    // Counters
    n_candles: u64,
    diag_first_call_done: bool,
    n_signals: u64,
    n_blocked_no_keltner: u64,
    n_blocked_below_ema200: u64,
    n_blocked_rr: u64,
    n_blocked_cooldown: u64,
    n_dca_orders_placed: u64,
    n_tp_hits: u64,
    n_sl_hits: u64,
    // вњ… P23: slippage-РјРѕРґРµР»СЊ РїРѕ Р¶РёРІРѕРјСѓ bookTicker (Р°СѓРґРёС‚: В«СЃРїСЂРµРґ РЅРµ РјРѕРґРµР»РёСЂСѓРµС‚СЃСЏВ»)
    last_bid: f64,
    last_ask: f64,
    max_spread_pct: f64,
    funding_pct: f64, // вњ… P24: РѕС†РµРЅРєР° funding Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (Р°СѓРґРёС‚ в„–12)
    n_spread_block: u64,
}

impl KeltnerDcaStrategy {
    pub fn new(symbol: String, balance: f64, risk_pct: f64, fee_rate: f64) -> Self {
        Self {
            symbol, balance, risk_per_trade_pct: risk_pct, fee_rate,
            highs: VecDeque::with_capacity(300),
            lows: VecDeque::with_capacity(300),
            closes: VecDeque::with_capacity(300),
            volumes: VecDeque::with_capacity(300),
            timestamps: VecDeque::with_capacity(300),
            max_history: 300,
            atrs: VecDeque::with_capacity(50),
            keltner_ema: None,
            keltner_alpha: 2.0 / (KELTNER_EMA_PERIOD as f64 + 1.0),
            ema200: None,
            dca_state: DcaState::Flat,
            dca_order_idx: 0,
            dca_order1_price: 0.0,
            dca_total_qty: 0.0,
            dca_avg_entry: 0.0,
            dca_sl_price: 0.0,
            dca_tp_price: 0.0,
            filter: SignalFilter::new(300, 0.5),
            last_kline_ts: 0,
            n_candles: 0, n_signals: 0,
            diag_first_call_done: false,
            n_blocked_no_keltner: 0, n_blocked_below_ema200: 0,
            n_blocked_rr: 0, n_blocked_cooldown: 0,
            n_dca_orders_placed: 0, n_tp_hits: 0, n_sl_hits: 0,
            last_bid: 0.0, last_ask: 0.0,
            max_spread_pct: std::env::var("P23_MAX_SPREAD_PCT")
                .ok()
                .and_then(|v| v.parse::<f64>().ok())
                .map(|v| if v > 0.0 { v } else { f64::INFINITY })
                .unwrap_or(0.0010),
            // вњ… P24: funding Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (Р°СѓРґРёС‚ в„–12) вЂ” РєРѕРЅСЃРµСЂРІР°С‚РёРІРЅРѕ РѕРґРёРЅ РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» 8h,
            // РґРѕР»СЏ РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‹; РґРµС„РѕР»С‚ 0.0001 = 0.01%/8h; РјСѓСЃРѕСЂ/<=0 РІ env -> 0.0 (РІС‹РєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРѕ)
            funding_pct: std::env::var("P24_FUNDING_PCT")
                .ok()
                .and_then(|v| v.trim().parse::<f64>().ok())
                .filter(|v| *v > 0.0)
                .unwrap_or(0.0001),
            n_spread_block: 0,
        }
    }

    /// вњ… P23: slippage-guard вЂ” true = СЃРїСЂРµРґ С€РёСЂРµ Р»РёРјРёС‚Р°, РІС…РѕРґ Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰С‘РЅ
    fn spread_too_wide(&mut self) -> bool {
        if self.last_bid > 0.0 && self.last_ask > self.last_bid {
            let mid = (self.last_bid + self.last_ask) * 0.5;
            if (self.last_ask - self.last_bid) / mid > self.max_spread_pct {
                self.n_spread_block += 1;
                return true;
            }
        }
        false
    }

    /// Update ATR (Wilder's method)
    fn update_atr(&mut self) {
        let len = self.closes.len();
        if len < 2 { return; }
        let high = self.highs[len - 1];
        let low = self.lows[len - 1];
        let prev_close = self.closes[len - 2];
        let tr = (high - low).max((high - prev_close).abs()).max((low - prev_close).abs());
        let atr = if let Some(&prev_atr) = self.atrs.back() {
            let n = KELTNER_ATR_PERIOD as f64;
            (prev_atr * (n - 1.0) + tr) / n
        } else {
            tr
        };
        self.atrs.push_back(atr);
        if self.atrs.len() > KELTNER_ATR_PERIOD * 3 { self.atrs.pop_front(); }
    }

    fn current_atr(&self) -> f64 {
        self.atrs.back().copied().unwrap_or(0.0)
    }

    /// Update Keltner EMA(20)
    fn update_keltner_ema(&mut self, close: f64) {
        if let Some(prev) = self.keltner_ema {
            self.keltner_ema = Some(close * self.keltner_alpha + prev * (1.0 - self.keltner_alpha));
        } else if self.closes.len() >= KELTNER_EMA_PERIOD {
            let sum: f64 = self.closes.iter().take(KELTNER_EMA_PERIOD).sum();
            self.keltner_ema = Some(sum / KELTNER_EMA_PERIOD as f64);
        }
    }

    /// Update EMA200
    fn update_ema200(&mut self, close: f64) {
        if let Some(prev) = self.ema200 {
            self.ema200 = Some(close * EMA200_ALPHA + prev * (1.0 - EMA200_ALPHA));
        } else if self.closes.len() >= EMA200_PERIOD {
            let sum: f64 = self.closes.iter().take(EMA200_PERIOD).sum();
            self.ema200 = Some(sum / EMA200_PERIOD as f64);
        }
    }

    /// Keltner lower band = EMA(20) - mult Г— ATR(10)
    fn keltner_lower(&self) -> Option<f64> {
        let ema = self.keltner_ema?;
        let atr = self.current_atr();
        if atr <= 0.0 { return None; }
        Some(ema - KELTNER_ATR_MULT * atr)
    }

    /// Donchian high (N-period) вЂ” РґР»СЏ TP
    fn donchian_high(&self) -> Option<f64> {
        if self.highs.len() < DONCHIAN_PERIOD { return None; }
        let start = self.highs.len() - DONCHIAN_PERIOD;
        self.highs.iter().skip(start).fold(None, |acc, &h| {
            Some(acc.map_or(h, |m: f64| m.max(h)))
        })
    }

    /// Donchian low (N-period) вЂ” РґР»СЏ SL
    fn donchian_low(&self) -> Option<f64> {
        if self.lows.len() < DONCHIAN_PERIOD { return None; }
        let start = self.lows.len() - DONCHIAN_PERIOD;
        self.lows.iter().skip(start).fold(None, |acc, &l| {
            Some(acc.map_or(l, |m: f64| m.min(l)))
        })
    }

    /// Check if price touched/below Keltner lower band
    fn keltner_touch(&self, close: f64) -> bool {
        if let Some(lower) = self.keltner_lower() {
            return close <= lower;
        }
        false
    }

    /// Check if price is above EMA200 (uptrend filter)
    fn is_above_ema200(&self, close: f64) -> bool {
        match self.ema200 {
            Some(ema) => close > ema,
            None => false,
        }
    }

    /// Check if DCA order should be placed at current price
    fn should_place_dca_order(&self, close: f64) -> Option<usize> {
        match self.dca_state {
            DcaState::Flat => {
                // First order: Keltner touch + above EMA200
                if self.keltner_touch(close) && self.is_above_ema200(close) {
                    return Some(0); // index 0 = first order
                }
                None
            }
            DcaState::Averaging => {
                // Subsequent orders: price dropped DCA_DROP_STEPS[idx]% from order 1
                let next_idx = self.dca_order_idx; // 0-based: next order index
                if next_idx >= DCA_MAX_ORDERS { return None; }
                let drop_pct = DCA_DROP_STEPS[next_idx];
                let target_price = self.dca_order1_price * (1.0 + drop_pct);
                if close <= target_price {
                    return Some(next_idx);
                }
                None
            }
            DcaState::FullyInvested => None,
        }
    }

    /// Calculate position size for a DCA order
    fn calc_dca_qty(&self, entry: f64, sl: f64, fraction: f64) -> f64 {
        // Full position size based on risk
        let full_qty = calc_position_size_with_fees(
            self.balance, entry, sl,
            self.risk_per_trade_pct, self.fee_rate * 2.0,
        );
        // This DCA order gets 'fraction' of full size
        let dca_qty = full_qty * fraction;
        floor_to_step(dca_qty, QTY_STEP)
    }

    /// Check TP/SL for fully invested position
    fn check_exit(&self, close: f64) -> Option<&'static str> {
        if self.dca_state != DcaState::FullyInvested {
            return None;
        }
        // TP: close >= Donchian high
        if let Some(dh) = self.donchian_high() {
            if close >= dh {
                return Some("TakeProfit");
            }
        }
        // SL: close <= Donchian low
        if let Some(dl) = self.donchian_low() {
            if close <= dl {
                return Some("StopLoss");
            }
        }
        None
    }

    /// Reset DCA state after position close
    fn reset_dca(&mut self) {
        self.dca_state = DcaState::Flat;
        self.dca_order_idx = 0;
        self.dca_order1_price = 0.0;
        self.dca_total_qty = 0.0;
        self.dca_avg_entry = 0.0;
        self.dca_sl_price = 0.0;
        self.dca_tp_price = 0.0;
    }
}

impl Strategy for KeltnerDcaStrategy {
    fn name(&self) -> &str { "keltner_dca" }

    // вњ… V3.4: backfill РіСЂРµРµС‚ РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂС‹ (Keltner EMA/ATR/EMA200/Donchian),
    // РёРЅР°С‡Рµ ema200 РЅР° 4h-СЃРІРµС‡Р°С… Р¶РґР°Р» Р±С‹ 200 Р¶РёРІС‹С… Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёР№ = 33 РґРЅСЏ.
    fn wants_backfill(&self) -> bool { true }

    /// рџ†• DCA: returns true вЂ” allows multiple entries on same symbol
    fn can_dca(&self) -> bool { true }

    fn notify_position_open(&mut self) {
        // Position opened вЂ” update DCA state
        if self.dca_state == DcaState::Flat {
            self.dca_state = DcaState::Averaging;
        }
        self.dca_order_idx += 1;
        self.n_dca_orders_placed += 1;
        if self.dca_order_idx >= DCA_MAX_ORDERS {
            self.dca_state = DcaState::FullyInvested;
        }
    }

    // P16: sizing base follows live equity
    fn set_live_equity(&mut self, eq: f64) { self.balance = eq; }

    fn notify_position_closed(&mut self, _pnl: f64) {
        self.reset_dca();
        self.filter.on_position_closed(self.last_kline_ts);
    }

    fn in_position(&self) -> bool {
        // In DCA mode: NOT in_position (allows more entries via can_dca bypass)
        // Only "in position" when fully invested
        match self.dca_state {
            DcaState::Flat => false,
            DcaState::Averaging => false, // в†ђ KEY: allows DCA orders past has_position
            DcaState::FullyInvested => true,
        }
    }

    fn process(&mut self, msg: &WebSocketMessage) -> Result<Option<Signal>> {
        // вњ… P23: Р»РѕРІРёРј Р¶РёРІРѕР№ СЃРїСЂРµРґ РёР· bookTicker (guard РІС…РѕРґР° + СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃРїСЂРµРґР° РІ RR)
        if let WebSocketMessage::BookTicker(b) = msg {
            if b.symbol.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&self.symbol)
                && b.best_bid > 0.0
                && b.best_ask > b.best_bid
            {
                self.last_bid = b.best_bid;
                self.last_ask = b.best_ask;
            }
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let kline = match msg { WebSocketMessage::Kline(k) => k, _ => return Ok(None) };

        // вњ… DIAG v3.4.1: РѕРґРЅР° Р·Р°РїРёСЃСЊ Рѕ РїРµСЂРІРѕР№ СЃРІРµС‡Рµ, РґРѕС€РµРґС€РµР№ РґРѕ СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёРё
        if !self.diag_first_call_done {
            self.diag_first_call_done = true;
            tracing::info!(
                "рџ”Ќ KeltnerDCA DIAG: 1-СЏ СЃРІРµС‡Р° РґРѕС€Р»Р° РґРѕ СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёРё | self.sym={} | msg.sym={} | is_closed={} | is_backfill={} | ts={}",
                self.symbol, kline.symbol, kline.is_closed, kline.is_backfill, kline.timestamp
            );
        }

        if !kline.symbol.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&self.symbol) { return Ok(None); }
        if !kline.is_closed { return Ok(None); }
        if kline.timestamp == self.last_kline_ts { return Ok(None); }

        self.last_kline_ts = kline.timestamp;
        self.n_candles += 1;

        // Accumulate history
        self.highs.push_back(kline.high);
        self.lows.push_back(kline.low);
        self.closes.push_back(kline.close);
        self.volumes.push_back(kline.volume);
        self.timestamps.push_back(kline.timestamp);
        while self.highs.len() > self.max_history {
            self.highs.pop_front(); self.lows.pop_front();
            self.closes.pop_front(); self.volumes.pop_front();
            self.timestamps.pop_front();
        }

        // Update indicators
        self.update_atr();
        self.update_keltner_ema(kline.close);
        self.update_ema200(kline.close);

        // вњ… V3.4: backfill вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµРІ РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂРѕРІ. Р’С…РѕРґС‹/РІС‹С…РѕРґС‹
        // (EXIT CHECK, DCA-РѕСЂРґРµСЂР°) РїРѕ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёС‡РµСЃРєРёРј С†РµРЅР°Рј Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰РµРЅС‹.
        if kline.is_backfill { return Ok(None); }

        // === EXIT CHECK (if fully invested) ===
        if let Some(reason) = self.check_exit(kline.close) {
            if !self.filter.can_signal(kline.close, kline.timestamp) {
                self.n_blocked_cooldown += 1;
                return Ok(None);
            }
            self.filter.on_signal(kline.close, kline.timestamp);
            self.n_signals += 1;
            if reason == "TakeProfit" { self.n_tp_hits += 1; }
            else { self.n_sl_hits += 1; }

            let side = "SELL"; // LONG-only в†’ exit is always SELL
            let entry = round_to_tick(kline.close, TICK_SIZE);
            tracing::info!(
                "рџЋЇ KeltnerDCA EXIT {} | reason={} | avg_entry={:.4} close={:.4} qty={:.6}",
                side, reason, self.dca_avg_entry, entry, self.dca_total_qty,
            );
            return Ok(Some(Signal {
                symbol: kline.symbol.clone(),
                side: side.to_string(),
                price: entry,
                quantity: self.dca_total_qty,
                strategy_name: "Exit_KeltnerDca".to_string(),
                leverage: 1.0,
                stop_loss: None,
                take_profit: None,
                atr_hint: self.current_atr(),
                entry_spread: 0.0,
            }));
        }

        // === ENTRY / DCA CHECK ===
        let close = kline.close;

        // Check if we should place a DCA order
        let dca_idx = match self.should_place_dca_order(close) {
            Some(idx) => idx,
            None => {
                if self.dca_state == DcaState::Flat {
                    // Check why we didn't enter
                    if !self.keltner_touch(close) {
                        self.n_blocked_no_keltner += 1;
                    } else if !self.is_above_ema200(close) {
                        self.n_blocked_below_ema200 += 1;
                    }
                }
                return Ok(None);
            }
        };

        // Donchian levels for SL/TP
        let don_low = match self.donchian_low() {
            Some(dl) => dl,
            None => return Ok(None),
        };
        let don_high = match self.donchian_high() {
            Some(dh) => dh,
            None => return Ok(None),
        };

        // Entry price for this DCA order
        let entry = round_to_tick(close, TICK_SIZE);
        let sl = round_to_tick(don_low, TICK_SIZE);
        let tp = round_to_tick(don_high, TICK_SIZE);

        // вњ… P23: slippage-guard вЂ” СЃРїСЂРµРґ С€РёСЂРµ Р»РёРјРёС‚Р° = РІС…РѕРґ Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰С‘РЅ
        if self.spread_too_wide() {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        // Net RR check
        let fee_cost = 2.0 * self.fee_rate * entry;
        // вњ… P23: РїРѕР»РЅР°СЏ СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃРїСЂРµРґР° Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (РІС…РѕРґ РїРѕ ask, РІС‹С…РѕРґ РїРѕ bid).
        // Р”Рѕ РїРµСЂРІРѕРіРѕ bookTicker СЃРїСЂРµРґ РЅРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РµРЅ вЂ” РјРѕРґРµР»СЊ РєР°Рє СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ.
        let spread_cost = if self.last_bid > 0.0 && self.last_ask > self.last_bid {
            self.last_ask - self.last_bid
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        let gross_profit = tp - entry - spread_cost;
        let gross_loss = entry - sl + spread_cost;
        // вњ… P24: funding Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (Р°СѓРґРёС‚ в„–12) вЂ” РєРѕРЅСЃРµСЂРІР°С‚РёРІРЅРѕ РѕРґРёРЅ РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» 8h
        let funding_cost = entry * self.funding_pct;
        let net_profit = gross_profit - fee_cost - funding_cost;
        let net_loss = gross_loss + fee_cost + funding_cost;
        if net_loss <= 0.0 || net_profit <= 0.0 {
            self.n_blocked_rr += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let eff_rr = net_profit / net_loss;
        if eff_rr < MIN_NET_RR {
            self.n_blocked_rr += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // Position size for this DCA order
        let fraction = DCA_QTY_FRACTIONS[dca_idx];
        let qty = self.calc_dca_qty(entry, sl, fraction);
        if qty <= 0.0 { return Ok(None); }

        // Update DCA state
        if dca_idx == 0 {
            // First order вЂ” record reference price and SL/TP
            self.dca_order1_price = entry;
            self.dca_sl_price = sl;
            self.dca_tp_price = tp;
        }

        // Update accumulated position
        let new_total_qty = self.dca_total_qty + qty;
        let new_avg_entry = if self.dca_total_qty > 0.0 {
            (self.dca_avg_entry * self.dca_total_qty + entry * qty) / new_total_qty
        } else {
            entry
        };
        self.dca_total_qty = new_total_qty;
        self.dca_avg_entry = new_avg_entry;

        self.filter.on_signal(entry, kline.timestamp);
        self.n_signals += 1;

        tracing::info!(
            "рџЋЇ KeltnerDCA BUY order {}/{} | entry={:.4} qty={:.6} | avg_entry={:.4} total_qty={:.6} | SL={:.4} TP={:.4} netRR={:.2}",
            dca_idx + 1, DCA_MAX_ORDERS, entry, qty,
            self.dca_avg_entry, self.dca_total_qty,
            self.dca_sl_price, self.dca_tp_price, eff_rr,
        );

        Ok(Some(Signal {
            symbol: kline.symbol.clone(),
            side: "BUY".to_string(),
            price: entry,
            quantity: qty,
            strategy_name: self.name().to_string(),
            leverage: 1.0,
            stop_loss: Some(sl),
            take_profit: Some(tp),
            atr_hint: self.current_atr(),
            entry_spread: 0.0,
        }))
    }

    fn diagnostics(&self) -> Option<String> {
        Some(format!(
            "KeltnerDCA(candles) diag | candles={} signals={} dca_orders={} | no_keltner={} below_ema200={} rr_block={} spr_block={} cooldown={} | TP={} SL={} | state={:?} dca_idx={} avg_entry={} total_qty={} | keltner_ema={} ema200={} atr={}",
            self.n_candles, self.n_signals, self.n_dca_orders_placed,
            self.n_blocked_no_keltner, self.n_blocked_below_ema200,
            self.n_blocked_rr, self.n_spread_block, self.n_blocked_cooldown,
            self.n_tp_hits, self.n_sl_hits,
            self.dca_state, self.dca_order_idx,
            if self.dca_avg_entry > 0.0 { format!("{:.4}", self.dca_avg_entry) } else { "n/a".to_string() },
            if self.dca_total_qty > 0.0 { format!("{:.6}", self.dca_total_qty) } else { "0".to_string() },
            self.keltner_ema.map(|v| format!("{:.4}", v)).unwrap_or_else(|| "n/a".to_string()),
            self.ema200.map(|v| format!("{:.4}", v)).unwrap_or_else(|| "n/a".to_string()),
            self.current_atr(),
        ))
    }
}

//! liquidity_grab.rs вЂ” SMC (Smart Money Concepts) СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ.
//!
//! Р›РѕРіРёРєР° (СЃС‚СЂРѕРіРѕ РїСЂРёР±С‹Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ):
//!   1. Williams Fractals (5-bar swing detection)
//!   2. Liquidity Pool clustering (СЂР°РІРЅС‹Рµ fractals РІ РїСЂРµРґРµР»Р°С… 0.15%)
//!   3. Sweep detection (С†РµРЅР° РїСЂРѕР±РёРІР°РµС‚ pool, Р·Р°РєСЂС‹РІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅРѕ)
//!   4. MSS confirmation (close РїСЂРѕР±РёРІР°РµС‚ РїСЂРѕС‚РёРІРѕРїРѕР»РѕР¶РЅС‹Р№ fractal)
//!   5. Entry: LIMIT order РЅР° retest СЃР»РѕРјР°РЅРЅРѕРіРѕ pool (maker 0.02%)
//!   6. SL: Р·Р° sweep extreme (invalidation)
//!   7. TP: РЅР° СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РµРј opposite liquidity pool
//!
//! РњР°С‚РµРјР°С‚РёРєР° (PEPE 1h, real slippage 0.02% РёР· РєР°Р»РёР±СЂРѕРІРєРё 51M С‚РёРєРѕРІ):
//!   Round-trip fee: 0.02% maker entry + 0.05% taker SL + 0.02% maker TP = 0.09%
//!   TP: ~2.5% (next liquidity pool)
//!   SL: ~1.0% (sweep extreme)
//!   Net RR = (2.5 - 0.09) / (1.0 + 0.09) = 2.41/1.09 = 2.21
//!   Breakeven WR = 1/(1+2.21) = 31.2%
//!   Р¦РµР»РµРІР°СЏ WR: 40-50% (РєР°Рє Сѓ TAO momentum)
//!   РџСЂРё WR=40%: EV = 0.40Г—2.5 - 0.60Г—1.0 = +0.40 USDT/trade
//!
//! Safety:
//!   - Max 1 concurrent position (no martingale)
//!   - Cooldown 2h РјРµР¶РґСѓ С‚СЂРµР№РґР°РјРё (less noise)
//!   - Skip if pool touch_count < 2 (РЅСѓР¶РµРЅ Р·РЅР°С‡РёРјС‹Р№ СѓСЂРѕРІРµРЅСЊ)
//!   - Skip if basis > 0.5% (spot vs futures diverge)

use crate::strategies::{Signal, SignalFilter, Strategy};
use crate::utils::math::{calc_position_size_with_fees, floor_to_step, round_to_tick};
use crate::websocket::WebSocketMessage;
use anyhow::Result;
use std::collections::VecDeque;

/// Universal tick size вЂ” works for all coins (BTC, SOL, PEPE).
const TICK_SIZE: f64 = 0.00000001;
const QTY_STEP: f64 = 0.00000001;

/// Fractal lookback вЂ” СЃРІРµС‡Рё СЃР»РµРІР°/СЃРїСЂР°РІР° РґРѕР»Р¶РЅС‹ Р±С‹С‚СЊ РЅРёР¶Рµ/РІС‹С€Рµ
const FRACTAL_LOOKBACK: usize = 2;

/// РџРѕСЂРѕРі РєР»Р°СЃС‚РµСЂРёР·Р°С†РёРё fractals РІ pool (0.15% РґСЂСѓРі РѕС‚ РґСЂСѓРіР°)
const POOL_CLUSTER_THRESHOLD_PCT: f64 = 0.0015;

/// РњРёРЅРёРјСѓРј touch_count РґР»СЏ Р·РЅР°С‡РёРјРѕРіРѕ pool
const MIN_POOL_TOUCHES: usize = 2;

/// РњР°РєСЃРёРјР°Р»СЊРЅРѕРµ РІСЂРµРјСЏ РѕР¶РёРґР°РЅРёСЏ retest РїРѕСЃР»Рµ sweep (СЃРІРµС‡РµР№)
const RETEST_TIMEOUT_BARS: usize = 8;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct LiquidityPool {
    pub price: f64,
    pub is_high: bool,        // true = resistance (highs), false = support (lows)
    pub touch_count: usize,
    pub last_touch_ts: i64,
    pub swept: bool,
    pub sweep_extreme: Option<f64>, // max/min С†РµРЅР° РІ РјРѕРјРµРЅС‚ sweep
    pub sweep_ts: Option<i64>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub enum SweepDirection {
    Bullish, // sweep below support в†’ long entry (С†РµРЅР° СЃРЅСЏР»Р° СЃС‚РѕРїС‹ Р»РѕРЅРіРѕРІ, СЂР°Р·РІРѕСЂР°С‡РёРІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РІРІРµСЂС…)
    Bearish, // sweep above resistance в†’ short entry (С†РµРЅР° СЃРЅСЏР»Р° СЃС‚РѕРїС‹ С€РѕСЂС‚РѕРІ, СЂР°Р·РІРѕСЂР°С‡РёРІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РІРЅРёР·)
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct ActiveSweep {
    pub pool_price: f64,
    pub sweep_extreme: f64,
    pub direction: SweepDirection,
    pub sweep_ts: i64,
    pub bars_since_sweep: usize,
    pub mss_confirmed: bool,
}

pub struct LiquidityGrabStrategy {
    symbol: String,
    balance: f64,
    risk_per_trade_pct: f64,
    fee_rate: f64,
    rr_ratio: f64,
    min_net_rr: f64,

    // РСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ СЃРІРµС‡РµР№
    highs: VecDeque<f64>,
    lows: VecDeque<f64>,
    closes: VecDeque<f64>,
    timestamps: VecDeque<i64>,
    max_history: usize,

    // РќР°Р№РґРµРЅРЅС‹Рµ fractals (РґР»СЏ MSS detection)
    recent_fractal_highs: VecDeque<(i64, f64)>, // (ts, price)
    recent_fractal_lows: VecDeque<(i64, f64)>,

    // РљР»Р°СЃС‚РµСЂРёР·РѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ pools
    pools: Vec<LiquidityPool>,

    // РђРєС‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ sweep (РµСЃР»Рё РµСЃС‚СЊ)
    active_sweep: Option<ActiveSweep>,

    // Position state (С‡РµСЂРµР· SignalFilter)
    filter: SignalFilter,
    last_kline_ts: i64,

    // РЎС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРєРё РґР»СЏ diagnostics
    n_candles: u64,
    n_fractals: u64,
    n_pools: u64,
    n_sweeps: u64,
    n_mss_confirmed: u64,
    n_signals: u64,
    n_blocked_no_sweep: u64,
    n_blocked_no_mss: u64,
    n_blocked_low_touches: u64,
    n_blocked_retest_timeout: u64,
    n_blocked_position: u64,
    // вњ… P23: slippage-РјРѕРґРµР»СЊ РїРѕ Р¶РёРІРѕРјСѓ bookTicker (Р°СѓРґРёС‚: В«СЃРїСЂРµРґ РЅРµ РјРѕРґРµР»РёСЂСѓРµС‚СЃСЏВ»)
    last_bid: f64,
    last_ask: f64,
    max_spread_pct: f64,
    funding_pct: f64, // вњ… P24: РѕС†РµРЅРєР° funding Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (Р°СѓРґРёС‚ в„–12)
    n_spread_block: u64,
}

impl LiquidityGrabStrategy {
    pub fn new(symbol: String, balance: f64, risk_pct: f64, fee_rate: f64) -> Self {
        Self {
            symbol,
            balance,
            risk_per_trade_pct: risk_pct,
            fee_rate,
            rr_ratio: 2.5,
            min_net_rr: 1.5,
            highs: VecDeque::with_capacity(200),
            lows: VecDeque::with_capacity(200),
            closes: VecDeque::with_capacity(200),
            timestamps: VecDeque::with_capacity(200),
            max_history: 200,
            recent_fractal_highs: VecDeque::with_capacity(50),
            recent_fractal_lows: VecDeque::with_capacity(50),
            pools: Vec::new(),
            active_sweep: None,
            filter: SignalFilter::new(120, 0.20), // cooldown 2min + price move 0.20%
            last_kline_ts: 0,
            n_candles: 0,
            n_fractals: 0,
            n_pools: 0,
            n_sweeps: 0,
            n_mss_confirmed: 0,
            n_signals: 0,
            n_blocked_no_sweep: 0,
            n_blocked_no_mss: 0,
            n_blocked_low_touches: 0,
            n_blocked_retest_timeout: 0,
            n_blocked_position: 0,
            last_bid: 0.0,
            last_ask: 0.0,
            max_spread_pct: std::env::var("P23_MAX_SPREAD_PCT")
                .ok()
                .and_then(|v| v.parse::<f64>().ok())
                .map(|v| if v > 0.0 { v } else { f64::INFINITY })
                .unwrap_or(0.0010),
            // вњ… P24: funding Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (Р°СѓРґРёС‚ в„–12) вЂ” РєРѕРЅСЃРµСЂРІР°С‚РёРІРЅРѕ РѕРґРёРЅ РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» 8h,
            // РґРѕР»СЏ РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‹; РґРµС„РѕР»С‚ 0.0001 = 0.01%/8h; РјСѓСЃРѕСЂ/<=0 РІ env -> 0.0 (РІС‹РєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРѕ)
            funding_pct: std::env::var("P24_FUNDING_PCT")
                .ok()
                .and_then(|v| v.trim().parse::<f64>().ok())
                .filter(|v| *v > 0.0)
                .unwrap_or(0.0001),
            n_spread_block: 0,
        }
    }

    pub fn with_rr(mut self, rr: f64, min_net_rr: f64) -> Self {
        self.rr_ratio = rr;
        self.min_net_rr = min_net_rr;
        self
    }

    /// Р”РµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂ Williams Fractal.
    /// РЎРІРµС‡Р° i вЂ” fractal high РµСЃР»Рё highs[i] > РІСЃРµС… FRACTAL_LOOKBACK СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ СЃР»РµРІР° Рё СЃРїСЂР°РІР°.
    /// РџРѕРґС‚РІРµСЂР¶РґРµРЅРёРµ вЂ” РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ FRACTAL_LOOKBACK СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ РџРћРЎР›Р• РєР°РЅРґРёРґР°С‚Р°.
    fn detect_fractals(&mut self) {
        let n = self.highs.len();
        if n < FRACTAL_LOOKBACK * 2 + 1 {
            return;
        }

        // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј СЃРІРµС‡Сѓ (n - 1 - FRACTAL_LOOKBACK) вЂ” Сѓ РЅРµС‘ СѓР¶Рµ РµСЃС‚СЊ FRACTAL_LOOKBACK СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ
        let candidate_idx = n - 1 - FRACTAL_LOOKBACK;
        if candidate_idx < FRACTAL_LOOKBACK {
            return;
        }

        let high_candidate = self.highs[candidate_idx];
        let low_candidate = self.lows[candidate_idx];
        let ts = self.timestamps[candidate_idx];

        // Fractal HIGH
        let is_fractal_high = (candidate_idx - FRACTAL_LOOKBACK..candidate_idx + FRACTAL_LOOKBACK + 1)
            .filter(|&i| i != candidate_idx)
            .all(|i| self.highs[candidate_idx] > self.highs[i]);

        // Fractal LOW
        let is_fractal_low = (candidate_idx - FRACTAL_LOOKBACK..candidate_idx + FRACTAL_LOOKBACK + 1)
            .filter(|&i| i != candidate_idx)
            .all(|i| self.lows[candidate_idx] < self.lows[i]);

        if is_fractal_high {
            self.recent_fractal_highs.push_back((ts, high_candidate));
            if self.recent_fractal_highs.len() > 50 {
                self.recent_fractal_highs.pop_front();
            }
            self.n_fractals += 1;
            self.update_pools(high_candidate, true, ts);
        }
        if is_fractal_low {
            self.recent_fractal_lows.push_back((ts, low_candidate));
            if self.recent_fractal_lows.len() > 50 {
                self.recent_fractal_lows.pop_front();
            }
            self.n_fractals += 1;
            self.update_pools(low_candidate, false, ts);
        }
    }

    /// РљР»Р°СЃС‚РµСЂРёР·Р°С†РёСЏ fractals РІ liquidity pools.
    /// Р‘Р»РёР·РєРёРµ fractals (РІ РїСЂРµРґРµР»Р°С… POOL_CLUSTER_THRESHOLD_PCT) вЂ” РѕРґРёРЅ pool.
    fn update_pools(&mut self, price: f64, is_high: bool, ts: i64) {
        let threshold = price * POOL_CLUSTER_THRESHOLD_PCT;
        let mut found = false;

        for pool in &mut self.pools {
            if pool.is_high == is_high && !pool.swept {
                if (pool.price - price).abs() < threshold {
                    // РЈСЃСЂРµРґРЅСЏРµРј С†РµРЅСѓ pool СЃ СѓС‡С‘С‚РѕРј touch_count
                    let total_price = pool.price * pool.touch_count as f64 + price;
                    let new_count = pool.touch_count + 1;
                    pool.price = total_price / new_count as f64;
                    pool.touch_count = new_count;
                    pool.last_touch_ts = ts;
                    found = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        if !found {
            self.pools.push(LiquidityPool {
                price,
                is_high,
                touch_count: 1,
                last_touch_ts: ts,
                swept: false,
                sweep_extreme: None,
                sweep_ts: None,
            });
            self.n_pools += 1;
        }

        // РЈРґР°Р»СЏРµРј СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Рµ pools (Р±РѕР»РµРµ 100 СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ РЅР°Р·Р°Рґ)
        let cutoff_ts = self.timestamps.back().copied().unwrap_or(0) - 100 * 3_600_000;
        self.pools.retain(|p| p.last_touch_ts > cutoff_ts);

        // Cap pools count
        if self.pools.len() > 30 {
            self.pools.sort_by_key(|p| p.last_touch_ts);
            self.pools.drain(0..self.pools.len() - 30);
        }
    }

    /// РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° sweep вЂ” С†РµРЅР° РїСЂРѕР±РёР»Р° pool, РЅРѕ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹Р»Р°СЃСЊ РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅРѕ.
    /// Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ true РµСЃР»Рё СЃР»СѓС‡РёР»СЃСЏ sweep (РїРѕР»СЏ РІ pool РѕР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏСЋС‚СЃСЏ).
    fn check_sweep(&mut self, kline_high: f64, kline_low: f64, close: f64, ts: i64) -> Option<SweepDirection> {
        for pool in &mut self.pools {
            if pool.swept {
                continue;
            }
            if pool.touch_count < MIN_POOL_TOUCHES {
                continue;
            }

            if pool.is_high {
                // Resistance sweep: high РїСЂРѕР±РёР» pool price, close РІРµСЂРЅСѓР»СЃСЏ РЅРёР¶Рµ
                if kline_high > pool.price && close < pool.price {
                    pool.swept = true;
                    pool.sweep_extreme = Some(kline_high);
                    pool.sweep_ts = Some(ts);
                    self.n_sweeps += 1;
                    return Some(SweepDirection::Bearish);
                }
            } else {
                // Support sweep: low РїСЂРѕР±РёР» pool price, close РІРµСЂРЅСѓР»СЃСЏ РІС‹С€Рµ
                if kline_low < pool.price && close > pool.price {
                    pool.swept = true;
                    pool.sweep_extreme = Some(kline_low);
                    pool.sweep_ts = Some(ts);
                    self.n_sweeps += 1;
                    return Some(SweepDirection::Bullish);
                }
            }
        }
        None
    }

    /// РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° MSS вЂ” close РїСЂРѕР±РёРІР°РµС‚ РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРёР№ РїСЂРѕС‚РёРІРѕРїРѕР»РѕР¶РЅС‹Р№ fractal.
    /// Bullish MSS: close > РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРёР№ fractal_high (РїРѕСЃР»Рµ bullish sweep)
    /// Bearish MSS: close < РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРёР№ fractal_low (РїРѕСЃР»Рµ bearish sweep)
    fn check_mss(&self, close: f64, direction: SweepDirection) -> bool {
        match direction {
            SweepDirection::Bullish => {
                // РќСѓР¶РµРЅ fractal_high РџРћРЎР›Р• sweep moment (РЅРѕРІР°СЏ СЃС‚СЂСѓРєС‚СѓСЂР°)
                if let Some(&(_, fh)) = self.recent_fractal_highs.back() {
                    if close > fh {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
                false
            }
            SweepDirection::Bearish => {
                if let Some(&(_, fl)) = self.recent_fractal_lows.back() {
                    if close < fl {
                        return true;
                    }
                }
                false
            }
        }
    }

    /// РџРѕРёСЃРє TP вЂ” СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РёР№ РїСЂРѕС‚РёРІРѕРїРѕР»РѕР¶РЅС‹Р№ liquidity pool.
    fn find_target_pool(&self, direction: SweepDirection, entry: f64) -> Option<f64> {
        match direction {
            SweepDirection::Bullish => {
                // Long: РёС‰РµРј СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РёР№ resistance (unswept high pool РІС‹С€Рµ entry)
                self.pools
                    .iter()
                    .filter(|p| p.is_high && !p.swept && p.price > entry)
                    .map(|p| p.price)
                    .next()
            }
            SweepDirection::Bearish => {
                // Short: РёС‰РµРј СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РёР№ support (unswept low pool РЅРёР¶Рµ entry)
                self.pools
                    .iter()
                    .filter(|p| !p.is_high && !p.swept && p.price < entry)
                    .map(|p| p.price)
                    .next()
            }
        }
    }

    /// Р Р°СЃС‡С‘С‚ net RR РїРѕСЃР»Рµ fees.
    fn net_rr(&self, entry: f64, sl: f64, tp: f64, is_long: bool) -> Option<f64> {
        let fee_cost = 2.0 * self.fee_rate * entry;
        // вњ… P23: РїРѕР»РЅР°СЏ СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃРїСЂРµРґР° Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (РІС…РѕРґ РїРѕ ask, РІС‹С…РѕРґ РїРѕ bid).
        // Р”Рѕ РїРµСЂРІРѕРіРѕ bookTicker СЃРїСЂРµРґ РЅРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РµРЅ вЂ” РјРѕРґРµР»СЊ РєР°Рє СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ.
        let spread_cost = if self.last_bid > 0.0 && self.last_ask > self.last_bid {
            self.last_ask - self.last_bid
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        let (gross_profit, gross_loss) = if is_long {
            (tp - entry - spread_cost, entry - sl + spread_cost)
        } else {
            (entry - tp - spread_cost, sl - entry + spread_cost)
        };
        // вњ… P24: funding Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (Р°СѓРґРёС‚ в„–12) вЂ” РєРѕРЅСЃРµСЂРІР°С‚РёРІРЅРѕ РѕРґРёРЅ РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» 8h
        let funding_cost = entry * self.funding_pct;
        let net_profit = gross_profit - fee_cost - funding_cost;
        let net_loss = gross_loss + fee_cost + funding_cost;
        if net_loss <= 0.0 || net_profit <= 0.0 {
            return None;
        }
        Some(net_profit / net_loss)
    }

    /// вњ… P23: slippage-guard вЂ” true = СЃРїСЂРµРґ С€РёСЂРµ Р»РёРјРёС‚Р°, РІС…РѕРґ Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰С‘РЅ
    fn spread_too_wide(&mut self) -> bool {
        if self.last_bid > 0.0 && self.last_ask > self.last_bid {
            let mid = (self.last_bid + self.last_ask) * 0.5;
            if (self.last_ask - self.last_bid) / mid > self.max_spread_pct {
                self.n_spread_block += 1;
                return true;
            }
        }
        false
    }
}

impl Strategy for LiquidityGrabStrategy {
    fn name(&self) -> &str {
        "liquidity_grab"
    }

    // вњ… V3.4: backfill РіСЂРµРµС‚ СЃС‚СЂСѓРєС‚СѓСЂСѓ (fractals + liquidity pools),
    // С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ live-СЃРІРµС‡Рё СЃСЂР°Р·Сѓ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°Р»Рё РїРѕ РіРѕС‚РѕРІС‹Рј СѓСЂРѕРІРЅСЏРј.
    fn wants_backfill(&self) -> bool { true }

    fn process(&mut self, msg: &WebSocketMessage) -> Result<Option<Signal>> {
        // вњ… P23: Р»РѕРІРёРј Р¶РёРІРѕР№ СЃРїСЂРµРґ РёР· bookTicker (guard РІС…РѕРґР° + СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃРїСЂРµРґР° РІ net_rr)
        if let WebSocketMessage::BookTicker(b) = msg {
            if b.symbol.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&self.symbol)
                && b.best_bid > 0.0
                && b.best_ask > b.best_bid
            {
                self.last_bid = b.best_bid;
                self.last_ask = b.best_ask;
            }
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let kline = match msg {
            WebSocketMessage::Kline(k) => k,
            _ => return Ok(None),
        };
        if !kline.symbol.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&self.symbol) {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if !kline.is_closed {
            return Ok(None);
        }

        self.last_kline_ts = kline.timestamp;
        self.n_candles += 1;

        // 1. РќР°РєР°РїР»РёРІР°РµРј РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЋ
        self.highs.push_back(kline.high);
        self.lows.push_back(kline.low);
        self.closes.push_back(kline.close);
        self.timestamps.push_back(kline.timestamp);
        while self.highs.len() > self.max_history {
            self.highs.pop_front();
            self.lows.pop_front();
            self.closes.pop_front();
            self.timestamps.pop_front();
        }

        // 2. Detect fractals
        self.detect_fractals();

        // вњ… V3.4: backfill вЂ” С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµРІ СЃС‚СЂСѓРєС‚СѓСЂС‹. Sweep/MSS/retest
        // Рё РЎРР“РќРђР›Р« РїРѕ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёС‡РµСЃРєРёРј СЃРІРµС‡Р°Рј Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰РµРЅС‹: РёРЅР°С‡Рµ Р±РѕС‚ РјРѕРі Р±С‹
        // РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚СЊ РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЋ РїРѕ С†РµРЅРµ РЅРµРґРµР»СЊРЅРѕР№ РґР°РІРЅРѕСЃС‚Рё. Live-СЃРІРµС‡Рё РЅР°С‡РёРЅР°СЋС‚
        // Р»РѕРІРёС‚СЊ sweep'С‹ СЃСЂР°Р·Сѓ РЅР° РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµС‚С‹С… pools.
        if kline.is_backfill { return Ok(None); }

        // 3. Check for sweep
        if let Some(direction) = self.check_sweep(kline.high, kline.low, kline.close, kline.timestamp) {
            // РЎРѕР·РґР°С‘Рј active sweep
            let sweep_extreme = if direction == SweepDirection::Bullish {
                kline.low // support sweep, extreme = low
            } else {
                kline.high // resistance sweep, extreme = high
            };

            self.active_sweep = Some(ActiveSweep {
                pool_price: 0.0, // СѓСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІРёРј РЅРёР¶Рµ
                sweep_extreme,
                direction,
                sweep_ts: kline.timestamp,
                bars_since_sweep: 0,
                mss_confirmed: false,
            });

            // Update pool_price РІ active_sweep
            if let Some(sweep) = &mut self.active_sweep {
                // РќР°С…РѕРґРёРј pool СЃ РєРѕС‚РѕСЂРѕРіРѕ СЃР»СѓС‡РёР»СЃСЏ sweep
                for pool in &self.pools {
                    if pool.swept && pool.sweep_ts == Some(kline.timestamp) {
                        sweep.pool_price = pool.price;
                        break;
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // 4. Р•СЃР»Рё РµСЃС‚СЊ active_sweep вЂ” РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј MSS
        // РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј .take() С‡С‚РѕР±С‹ РёР·Р±РµР¶Р°С‚СЊ borrow checker РєРѕРЅС„Р»РёРєС‚Р°
        // (РЅРµР»СЊР·СЏ РѕРґРЅРѕРІСЂРµРјРµРЅРЅРѕ РґРµСЂР¶Р°С‚СЊ &mut self.active_sweep Рё Р·РІР°С‚СЊ self.check_mss)
        if let Some(mut sweep) = self.active_sweep.take() {
            sweep.bars_since_sweep += 1;

            // Timeout вЂ” retest РЅРµ СЃР»СѓС‡РёР»СЃСЏ
            if sweep.bars_since_sweep > RETEST_TIMEOUT_BARS {
                self.n_blocked_retest_timeout += 1;
                // active_sweep СѓР¶Рµ None (С‡РµСЂРµР· take) вЂ” sweep РЅРµ РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµРј
                return Ok(None);
            }

            // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° MSS
            if !sweep.mss_confirmed {
                if self.check_mss(kline.close, sweep.direction) {
                    sweep.mss_confirmed = true;
                    self.n_mss_confirmed += 1;
                } else {
                    self.n_blocked_no_mss += 1;
                    self.active_sweep = Some(sweep); // СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµРј РґР»СЏ СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РµР№ СЃРІРµС‡Рё
                    return Ok(None);
                }
            }

            // MSS confirmed вЂ” РїСЂРѕРІРµСЂСЏРµРј retest (close РІРµСЂРЅСѓР»СЃСЏ Рє pool_price)
            let retest_distance_pct =
                (kline.close - sweep.pool_price).abs() / sweep.pool_price * 100.0;
            if retest_distance_pct > 0.5 {
                // Р¦РµРЅР° РµС‰С‘ РЅРµ РІРµСЂРЅСѓР»Р°СЃСЊ Рє pool вЂ” Р¶РґС‘Рј
                self.active_sweep = Some(sweep); // СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµРј РґР»СЏ СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РµР№ СЃРІРµС‡Рё
                return Ok(None);
            }

            // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё (РЅРµ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹РІР°РµРј РµСЃР»Рё СѓР¶Рµ РІ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё)
            if !self.filter.can_signal(kline.close, kline.timestamp) {
                self.n_blocked_position += 1;
                self.active_sweep = Some(sweep); // СЃРѕС…СЂР°РЅСЏРµРј РґР»СЏ СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РµР№ СЃРІРµС‡Рё
                return Ok(None);
            }

            // Р“РѕС‚РѕРІРёРј signal
            let direction = sweep.direction;
            let entry_price = sweep.pool_price;
            let sl_price = sweep.sweep_extreme;

            // TP вЂ” СЃР»РµРґСѓСЋС‰РёР№ opposite pool, fallback РЅР° RR-based
            let tp_price = self
                .find_target_pool(direction, entry_price)
                .unwrap_or_else(|| {
                    let sl_dist = (entry_price - sl_price).abs();
                    match direction {
                        SweepDirection::Bullish => entry_price + sl_dist * self.rr_ratio,
                        SweepDirection::Bearish => entry_price - sl_dist * self.rr_ratio,
                    }
                });

            // Р”Р»СЏ long: TP РґРѕР»Р¶РЅР° Р±С‹С‚СЊ РІС‹С€Рµ entry; РґР»СЏ short вЂ” РЅРёР¶Рµ
            let (side, sl, tp) = match direction {
                SweepDirection::Bullish => {
                    let tp = tp_price.max(entry_price * 1.005); // min 0.5% TP
                    ("BUY", sl_price, tp)
                }
                SweepDirection::Bearish => {
                    let tp = tp_price.min(entry_price * 0.995);
                    ("SELL", sl_price, tp)
                }
            };

            let entry = round_to_tick(entry_price, TICK_SIZE);
            let sl = round_to_tick(sl, TICK_SIZE);
            let tp = round_to_tick(tp, TICK_SIZE);

            // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° Net RR
            let is_long = direction == SweepDirection::Bullish;
            // вњ… P23: slippage-guard вЂ” СЃРїСЂРµРґ С€РёСЂРµ Р»РёРјРёС‚Р° = РІС…РѕРґ Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰С‘РЅ
            if self.spread_too_wide() {
                return Ok(None);
            }
            let eff_rr = match self.net_rr(entry, sl, tp, is_long) {
                Some(rr) => rr,
                None => {
                    // active_sweep СѓР¶Рµ None (С‡РµСЂРµР· take)
                    return Ok(None);
                }
            };
            if eff_rr < self.min_net_rr {
                // active_sweep СѓР¶Рµ None (С‡РµСЂРµР· take)
                return Ok(None);
            }

            // Position sizing СЃ СѓС‡С‘С‚РѕРј fees
            let raw_qty = calc_position_size_with_fees(
                self.balance,
                entry,
                sl,
                self.risk_per_trade_pct,
                self.fee_rate * 2.0,
            );
            let quantity = floor_to_step(raw_qty, QTY_STEP);
            if quantity <= 0.0 {
                // active_sweep СѓР¶Рµ None (С‡РµСЂРµР· take)
                return Ok(None);
            }

            // Р РµРіРёСЃС‚СЂРёСЂСѓРµРј signal РІ filter
            self.filter.on_signal(entry, kline.timestamp);
            self.n_signals += 1;
            // active_sweep СѓР¶Рµ None (С‡РµСЂРµР· take) вЂ” sweep consumed
            return Ok(Some(Signal {
                symbol: kline.symbol.clone(),
                side: side.to_string(),
                price: entry,
                quantity,
                strategy_name: self.name().to_string(),
                leverage: 1.0,
                stop_loss: Some(sl),
                take_profit: Some(tp),
                atr_hint: 0.0,
                entry_spread: 0.0,
            }));
        } else {
            self.n_blocked_no_sweep += 1;
        }

        Ok(None)
    }

    fn diagnostics(&self) -> Option<String> {
        Some(format!(
            "LiquidityGrab(candles) diag | candles={} fractals={} pools={} sweeps={} mss={} signals={} | no_sweep={} no_mss={} low_touches={} retest_timeout={} spr_block={} position={} | rr_ratio={} min_net_rr={} fee={:.4}% | recent_highs={} recent_lows={}",
            self.n_candles,
            self.n_fractals,
            self.n_pools,
            self.n_sweeps,
            self.n_mss_confirmed,
            self.n_signals,
            self.n_blocked_no_sweep,
            self.n_blocked_no_mss,
            self.n_blocked_low_touches,
            self.n_blocked_retest_timeout,
            self.n_spread_block,
            self.n_blocked_position,
            self.rr_ratio,
            self.min_net_rr,
            self.fee_rate * 100.0,
            self.recent_fractal_highs.len(),
            self.recent_fractal_lows.len(),
        ))
    }

    fn in_position(&self) -> bool {
        // вњ… P21: Cooldown Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ РќР• СЃС‡РёС‚Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ В«РІ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРёВ».
        // Р¤РёР»СЊС‚СЂ СѓС…РѕРґРёС‚ РІ Cooldown РІ РјРѕРјРµРЅС‚ Р’Р«Р”РђР§Р СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»Р° (on_signal) вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ
        // Р°РЅС‚Рё-С„Р»Р°Рї. Р Р°РЅСЊС€Рµ in_position()=true РЅР° СЃРІРµР¶РµРІС‹РґР°РЅРЅРѕРј СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»Рµ
        // РјРіРЅРѕРІРµРЅРЅРѕ СЃР°РјРѕ-Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂРѕРІР°Р» РµРіРѕ РІ main.rs (В«РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏ СѓР¶Рµ РѕС‚РєСЂС‹С‚Р°В»),
        // Рё СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ РЅРµ РјРѕРіР»Р° РІРѕР№С‚Рё РЅРёРєРѕРіРґР°. Anti-flap РѕСЃС‚Р°С‘С‚СЃСЏ РІ can_signal().
        matches!(self.filter.state(), crate::strategies::PositionState::InPosition)
    }

    fn notify_position_open(&mut self) {
        self.filter.on_position_opened();
    }

    fn notify_position_closed(&mut self, _pnl: f64) {
        self.filter.on_position_closed(self.last_kline_ts);
    }
}

use crate::websocket::WebSocketMessage;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub enum MarketRegime {
    Trending,
    Ranging,
    Volatile,
}

/// вњ… Patched MarketRegimeDetector СЃ directional bias check.
///
/// РЎС‚Р°СЂР°СЏ Р»РѕРіРёРєР° (С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ range):
///   range > volatile_pct в†’ Volatile
///   range > trending_pct в†’ Trending
///   else в†’ Ranging
///
/// РџСЂРѕР±Р»РµРјР°: РЅРµ РѕС‚Р»РёС‡Р°РµС‚ **С‚СЂРµРЅРґ** (С†РµРЅР° 5в†’14% РІ РѕРґРЅСѓ СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅСѓ) РѕС‚ **РїРёР»С‹**
/// (5в†’10в†’5в†’10 вЂ” range 5%, РЅРѕ РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёСЏ РЅРµС‚). РћР±Р° РєР»Р°СЃСЃРёС„РёС†РёСЂРѕРІР°Р»РёСЃСЊ РєР°Рє Trending.
///
/// РќРѕРІР°СЏ Р»РѕРіРёРєР° (directional bias):
///   directional_bias = |close - first_close| / range
///   = 1.0 РµСЃР»Рё С†РµРЅР° РїСЂРѕС€Р»Р° range РІ РѕРґРЅСѓ СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅСѓ
///   = 0.0 РµСЃР»Рё С†РµРЅР° РІРµСЂРЅСѓР»Р°СЃСЊ Рє СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚Сѓ (РїРѕР»РЅР°СЏ РїРёР»Р°)
///
///   Volatile: range > volatile_pct AND directional_bias < 0.6
///   Trending: (range > trending_pct AND directional_bias >= 0.6)
///             OR (range > volatile_pct AND directional_bias >= 0.7)
///   Ranging: РёРЅР°С‡Рµ
pub struct MarketRegimeDetector {
    window: usize,
    volatile_pct: f64,
    trending_pct: f64,
    directional_threshold: f64,  // 0.6 = 60% РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРЅРѕРіРѕ РґРІРёР¶РµРЅРёСЏ
    strong_trend_threshold: f64, // 0.7 = 70% РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРЅРѕРіРѕ РґРІРёР¶РµРЅРёСЏ
    price_history: Vec<f64>,
    last_directional_bias: f64,   // РґР»СЏ diagnostics
}

impl MarketRegimeDetector {
    pub fn new(window: usize, volatile_pct: f64, trending_pct: f64) -> Self {
        Self {
            window: window.max(20),
            volatile_pct,
            trending_pct,
            directional_threshold: 0.6,
            strong_trend_threshold: 0.7,
            price_history: Vec::with_capacity(window.max(20) * 2),
            last_directional_bias: 0.0,
        }
    }

    pub fn with_atr_style(atr_period: usize, atr_threshold: f64) -> Self {
        let window = atr_period.max(10) * 20;
        let volatile = (atr_threshold / 100.0).max(0.1);
        let trending = (volatile / 6.0).max(0.02);
        Self::new(window, volatile, trending)
    }

    pub fn detect_from_msg(&mut self, msg: &WebSocketMessage) -> MarketRegime {
        match msg {
            WebSocketMessage::Trade(t) => {
                self.price_history.push(t.price);
            }
            WebSocketMessage::BookTicker(bt) => {
                let mid = (bt.best_bid + bt.best_ask) / 2.0;
                if mid > 0.0 {
                    self.price_history.push(mid);
                }
            }
            // вњ… Р“Р›РђР’РќРћР•: РѕР±СЂР°Р±РѕС‚РєР° Kline
            WebSocketMessage::Kline(k) => {
                if k.close > 0.0 {
                    self.price_history.push(k.close);
                }
            }
            _ => return MarketRegime::Ranging,
        }

        if self.price_history.len() > self.window {
            let excess = self.price_history.len() - self.window;
            self.price_history.drain(0..excess);
        }

        if self.price_history.len() < 20 {
            return MarketRegime::Ranging;
        }

        let last = match self.price_history.last() {
            Some(&p) if p > 0.0 => p,
            _ => return MarketRegime::Ranging,
        };

        let max = self
            .price_history
            .iter()
            .cloned()
            .fold(f64::NEG_INFINITY, f64::max);
        let min = self
            .price_history
            .iter()
            .cloned()
            .fold(f64::INFINITY, f64::min);
        let range_pct = (max - min) / last * 100.0;

        // вњ… Directional bias check (РЅРѕРІРѕРµ)
        let first = self.price_history[0];
        let directional_move = (last - first).abs() / last * 100.0;
        let directional_bias = if range_pct > 0.0 {
            directional_move / range_pct
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        self.last_directional_bias = directional_bias;

        // === РќРћР’РђРЇ Р›РћР“РРљРђ ===
        // Volatile: С€РёСЂРѕРєРёР№ range Р‘Р•Р— РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёСЏ (РїРёР»Р°)
        if range_pct > self.volatile_pct && directional_bias < self.strong_trend_threshold {
            return MarketRegime::Volatile;
        }

        // Trending: РµСЃС‚СЊ РґРёР°РїР°Р·РѕРЅ Рё РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ
        if range_pct > self.trending_pct && directional_bias >= self.directional_threshold {
            return MarketRegime::Trending;
        }

        // Р•СЃР»Рё range > volatile_pct РЅРѕ directional_bias >= strong_trend_threshold в†’
        // СЌС‚Рѕ СЃРёР»СЊРЅС‹Р№ С‚СЂРµРЅРґ РґР°Р¶Рµ РІ РІРѕР»Р°С‚РёР»СЊРЅРѕР№ СЃСЂРµРґРµ
        if range_pct > self.volatile_pct && directional_bias >= self.strong_trend_threshold {
            return MarketRegime::Trending;
        }

        MarketRegime::Ranging
    }

    pub fn last_range_pct(&self) -> f64 {
        if self.price_history.len() < 2 {
            return 0.0;
        }
        let last = *self.price_history.last().unwrap();
        if last <= 0.0 {
            return 0.0;
        }
        let max = self
            .price_history
            .iter()
            .cloned()
            .fold(f64::NEG_INFINITY, f64::max);
        let min = self
            .price_history
            .iter()
            .cloned()
            .fold(f64::INFINITY, f64::min);
        (max - min) / last * 100.0
    }

    /// вњ… Р”Р»СЏ diagnostics: РїРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°РµС‚ directional bias (0.0 - 1.0)
    /// 0.0 = РїРѕР»РЅР°СЏ РїРёР»Р°, 1.0 = РёРґРµР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРЅС‹Р№ С‚СЂРµРЅРґ
    pub fn last_directional_bias(&self) -> f64 {
        self.last_directional_bias
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq)]
pub enum PositionState {
    Flat,
    InPosition,
    Cooldown,
}

pub struct SignalFilter {
    state: PositionState,
    last_signal_at: Option<i64>,
    cooldown_ms: i64,
    last_signal_price: f64,
    price_delta_threshold: f64,
}

impl SignalFilter {
    pub fn new(cooldown_secs: u64, price_delta_pct: f64) -> Self {
        Self {
            state: PositionState::Flat,
            last_signal_at: None,
            cooldown_ms: cooldown_secs as i64 * 1000,
            last_signal_price: 0.0,
            price_delta_threshold: price_delta_pct / 100.0,
        }
    }

    pub fn can_signal(&mut self, current_price: f64, now_ms: i64) -> bool {
        match self.state {
            PositionState::InPosition => false,
            PositionState::Flat => true,
            PositionState::Cooldown => {
                let Some(t) = self.last_signal_at else {
                    self.state = PositionState::Flat;
                    return true;
                };
                let price_moved = if self.last_signal_price > 0.0 {
                    (current_price - self.last_signal_price).abs() / self.last_signal_price
                } else { 0.0 };
                if price_moved > self.price_delta_threshold {
                    self.state = PositionState::Flat;
                    return true;
                }
                if now_ms.saturating_sub(t) >= self.cooldown_ms {
                    self.state = PositionState::Flat;
                    return true;
                }
                false
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn on_signal(&mut self, price: f64, now_ms: i64) {
        self.state = PositionState::Cooldown;
        self.last_signal_at = Some(now_ms);
        self.last_signal_price = price;
    }

    pub fn on_position_opened(&mut self) {
        self.state = PositionState::InPosition;
    }

    pub fn on_position_closed(&mut self, now_ms: i64) {
        self.state = PositionState::Cooldown;
        self.last_signal_at = Some(now_ms);
    }

    pub fn state(&self) -> PositionState { self.state }
}

pub trait Strategy: Send + Sync {
    fn name(&self) -> &str;
    fn process(&mut self, msg: &WebSocketMessage) -> Result<Option<Signal>>;

    fn notify_position_open(&mut self) {}
    fn notify_position_closed(&mut self, _pnl: f64) {}

    fn in_position(&self) -> bool { false }

    fn diagnostics(&self) -> Option<String> { None }

    fn set_risk_multiplier(&mut self, _mult: f64) {}

    fn on_entry_failed(&mut self, _reason: &str) {}

    fn on_position_unknown(&mut self) {}

    // P16: push live equity into the strategy (sizing base)
    fn set_live_equity(&mut self, _eq: f64) {}

    fn can_dca(&self) -> bool { false }

    /// вњ… Рџ3: С…РѕС‡РµС‚ Р»Рё СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ РїРѕР»СѓС‡Р°С‚СЊ backfill-СЃРІРµС‡Рё (РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЋ РѕС‚
    /// REST-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂР°, 250 С€С‚ РїСЂРё СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚Рµ) РґР»СЏ РјРіРЅРѕРІРµРЅРЅРѕРіРѕ РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµРІР° РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂРѕРІ.
    /// РџРћ РЈРњРћР›Р§РђРќРР® false вЂ” Р·Р°С‰РёС‚Р°: СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ Р±РµР· РїРѕРґРґРµСЂР¶РєРё is_backfill
    /// РјРѕР¶РµС‚ РІС‹РґР°С‚СЊ РЎРР“РќРђР› РїРѕ СЃС‚Р°СЂРѕР№ С†РµРЅРµ (РёСЃРїРѕР»РЅРёС‚СЃСЏ РїРѕ С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµРјСѓ СЂС‹РЅРєСѓ).
    /// РџРµСЂРµРѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»СЏРµС‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ BounceTrader (СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»С‹ РїРѕ backfill Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰РµРЅС‹
    /// РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё РµРіРѕ process()).
    fn wants_backfill(&self) -> bool { false }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq)]
pub struct Signal {
    pub symbol: String,
    pub side: String,
    pub price: f64,
    pub quantity: f64,
    pub strategy_name: String,
    pub leverage: f64,
    pub stop_loss: Option<f64>,
    pub take_profit: Option<f64>,
    pub atr_hint: f64,
    pub entry_spread: f64,
}

pub struct OrderBookState {
    pub best_bid: f64,
    pub best_ask: f64,
}

pub fn create_strategy(
    config: &BotConfig,
    global_symbol: &str,
    _order_book_state: Arc<RwLock<OrderBookState>>,
    _orderbook_analyzer: OrderBookAnalyzer,
) -> Result<Box<dyn Strategy>> {
    let balance = config.initial_balance.to_f64().unwrap_or(1000.0);
    let risk_pct = config.risk_per_trade_pct.to_f64().unwrap_or(0.5);

    let is_futures = config.market_type == "futures";
    let default_fee = if is_futures { 0.0005 } else { 0.00075 };
    let fee_rate = config
        .fee_rate
        .and_then(|d| d.to_f64())
        .unwrap_or(default_fee);

    let min_net_rr = config.min_net_rr.to_f64().unwrap_or(1.2);
    let min_ev_pct = config.min_ev_pct.to_f64().unwrap_or(0.0);

    let get = |key: &str, default: f64| -> f64 {
        config
            .strategy
            .params
            .get(key)
            .and_then(|d| d.to_f64())
            .unwrap_or(default)
    };

    match config.strategy.name.as_str() {
        "momentum" => Ok(Box::new(
            momentum::MomentumStrategy::new(
                global_symbol.to_string(),
                balance,
                risk_pct,
            )
            .with_windows(
                get("lookback", 20.0) as usize,
                get("atr_period", 14.0) as usize,
                get("ofi_window", 5.0) as usize,
            )
            .with_fee(fee_rate, min_net_rr, min_ev_pct)
            .with_tp_sl(
                get("tp_atr_mult", 2.5),
                get("min_tp_pct", 0.0025),
                get("rr_ratio", 2.5),
            )
            .with_thresholds(
                get("min_atr_threshold", 0.05),
                get("ofi_threshold", 0.15),
                get("volume_anomaly_threshold", 1.5),
            )
            .with_limits(
                get("max_trades_per_day", 10.0) as u32,
                get("min_time_between_trades_ms", 300_000.0) as i64,
                get("trading_start_utc", 7.0) as u32,
                get("trading_end_utc", 22.0) as u32,
            )
            .with_regime(
                get("regime_window", 100.0) as usize,
                get("regime_volatile_pct", 0.5),
                get("regime_trending_pct", 0.1),
            ),
        )),

        "gold_session" => Ok(Box::new(
            gold_session_trader::GoldSessionStrategy::new(
                global_symbol.to_string(),
                balance,
                risk_pct,
                fee_rate,
            ),
        )),

        "liquidity_grab" => Ok(Box::new(
            liquidity_grab::LiquidityGrabStrategy::new(
                global_symbol.to_string(),
                balance,
                risk_pct,
                fee_rate,
            )
            .with_rr(2.5, 1.5),
        )),

        // вњ… BUG#3-С„РёРєСЃ: РІСЃРµ РїРѕСЂРѕРіРё BounceTrader РЅР°СЃС‚СЂР°РёРІР°СЋС‚СЃСЏ С‡РµСЂРµР·
        // [bots.<name>.strategy.params] РІ config.toml (РЅРёР¶Рµ вЂ” РґРµС„РѕР»С‚С‹):
        //   rr_ratio = 2.0, rsi_oversold = 30, rsi_overbought = 70,
        //   trend_threshold_pct = 1.5, trend_window = 12,
        //   support_search_pct = 1.2  (РІ РџР РћР¦Р•РќРўРђРҐ, СЂР°РґРёСѓСЃ РїРѕРёСЃРєР° СѓСЂРѕРІРЅСЏ)
        "bounce_trader" => Ok(Box::new(
            bounce_trader::BounceTraderStrategy::new(
                global_symbol.to_string(),
                balance,
                risk_pct,
                fee_rate,
            )
            .with_rr(get("rr_ratio", 2.0), min_net_rr)
            .with_rsi(get("rsi_oversold", 30.0), get("rsi_overbought", 70.0))
            .with_trend_filter(
                get("trend_threshold_pct", 1.5),
                get("trend_window", 12.0) as usize,
            )
            .with_search_pct(get("support_search_pct", 1.2) / 100.0),
        )),

        // PATCH P15: asian_breakout branch removed

        "keltner_dca" => Ok(Box::new(
            keltner_dca::KeltnerDcaStrategy::new(
                global_symbol.to_string(),
                balance,
                risk_pct,
                fee_rate,
            )
        )),

        other => Err(anyhow::anyhow!("Unknown strategy: {}", other)),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_filter_flat_allows_signal() {
        let mut f = SignalFilter::new(10, 0.15);
        assert!(f.can_signal(50000.0, 1_000_000));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_filter_cooldown_blocks_without_price_move() {
        let mut f = SignalFilter::new(10, 0.15);
        f.on_signal(50000.0, 1_000_000);
        assert!(!f.can_signal(50000.0, 1_000_000));
        assert!(!f.can_signal(50025.0, 1_000_000 + 5_000));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_filter_cooldown_expires_by_data_time() {
        let mut f = SignalFilter::new(10, 0.15);
        f.on_signal(50000.0, 1_000_000);
        assert!(f.can_signal(50000.0, 1_000_000 + 10_000));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_filter_bypass_on_price_delta() {
        let mut f = SignalFilter::new(10, 0.15);
        f.on_signal(50000.0, 1_000_000);
        assert!(f.can_signal(50000.0 * 1.002, 1_000_000));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_filter_in_position_blocks() {
        let mut f = SignalFilter::new(10, 0.15);
        f.on_position_opened();
        assert!(!f.can_signal(50000.0, 1_000_000));
        assert!(!f.can_signal(60000.0, 1_000_000));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_filter_after_position_closed() {
        let mut f = SignalFilter::new(10, 0.15);
        f.on_position_opened();
        f.on_position_closed(2_000_000);
        assert!(!f.can_signal(50000.0, 2_000_000));
        assert!(f.can_signal(50000.0, 2_000_000 + 10_000));
    }
}

    // рџ†• Supertrend state вЂ” Р’РђР–РќРћ: С…СЂР°РЅРёРј prev_final_upper Рё prev_final_lower РћРўР”Р•Р›Р¬РќРћ
    prev_final_upper: Option<f64>,
    prev_final_lower: Option<f64>,
    supertrend_direction: i8, // +1 = UP (long-favorable), -1 = DOWN (short-favorable)
    supertrend_value: Option<f64>,

    // вњ… P23: slippage-РјРѕРґРµР»СЊ РїРѕ Р¶РёРІРѕРјСѓ bookTicker (Р°СѓРґРёС‚: В«СЃРїСЂРµРґ РЅРµ РјРѕРґРµР»РёСЂСѓРµС‚СЃСЏВ»)
