impl MomentumStrategy {
    pub fn new(symbol: String, balance: f64, risk_pct: f64) -> Self {
        Self {
            symbol,
            balance,
            risk_per_trade_pct: risk_pct,

            closes: VecDeque::with_capacity(500),
            highs: VecDeque::with_capacity(500),
            lows: VecDeque::with_capacity(500),
            volumes: VecDeque::with_capacity(500),
            taker_buys: VecDeque::with_capacity(500),
            atrs: VecDeque::with_capacity(500),

            lookback: 20,
            atr_period: 14,
            ofi_window: 5,

            min_atr_threshold_pct: 0.05,
            ofi_threshold: 0.15,
            volume_anomaly_threshold: 1.5,

            tp_atr_mult: 2.5,
            min_tp_pct: 0.0025,
            rr_ratio: 2.5,

            filter: SignalFilter::new(60, 0.15),
            regime_detector: MarketRegimeDetector::new(100, 0.5, 0.1),

            trading_start_utc: 7,
            trading_end_utc: 22,

            max_trades_per_day: 10,
            trades_today: 0,
            current_day: -1,
            last_trade_ts_ms: None,
            min_time_between_trades_ms: 300_000,
            last_kline_ts: 0,

            fee_rate: DEFAULT_FEE_RATE,
            min_net_rr: DEFAULT_MIN_NET_RR,
            min_ev_pct: DEFAULT_MIN_EV_PCT,

            trades_won: 0,
            trades_lost: 0,
            total_win_amount: 0.0,
            total_loss_amount: 0.0,

            blocked_by_atr: 0,
            blocked_by_momentum: 0,
            blocked_by_ofi: 0,
            blocked_by_volume: 0,
            blocked_by_regime: 0,
            blocked_by_time: 0,
            blocked_by_limit_day: 0,
            blocked_by_cooldown: 0,
            blocked_by_net_rr: 0,
            blocked_by_edge: 0,
            passed_filters: 0,
            total_closed_candles: 0,

            blocked_by_ema200: 0,
            blocked_by_vol_spike: 0,
            blocked_by_loss_streak: 0,
            blocked_by_supertrend: 0,
            blocked_by_mfi: 0,
            blocked_by_roc: 0,

            ema200_value: None,
            ema200_alpha: 2.0 / (EMA200_PERIOD as f64 + 1.0),

            consecutive_losses: 0,
            last_loss_ts: None,

            prev_final_upper: None,
            prev_final_lower: None,
            supertrend_direction: 1,
            supertrend_value: None,

            last_bid: 0.0, last_ask: 0.0,
            // вњ… P23: Р»РёРјРёС‚ СЃРїСЂРµРґР° (РґРѕР»СЏ РѕС‚ РјРёРґ-С†РµРЅС‹); <=0 РёР»Рё РјСѓСЃРѕСЂ = guard РІС‹РєР»СЋС‡РµРЅ
            max_spread_pct: std::env::var("P23_MAX_SPREAD_PCT")
                .ok()
                .and_then(|v| v.parse::<f64>().ok())
                .map(|v| if v > 0.0 { v } else { f64::INFINITY })
                .unwrap_or(0.0010),
            // вњ… P24: funding Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (Р°СѓРґРёС‚ в„–12) вЂ” РєРѕРЅСЃРµСЂРІР°С‚РёРІРЅРѕ РѕРґРёРЅ РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» 8h,
            // РґРѕР»СЏ РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‹; РґРµС„РѕР»С‚ 0.0001 = 0.01%/8h; РјСѓСЃРѕСЂ/<=0 РІ env -> 0.0 (РІС‹РєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРѕ)
            funding_pct: std::env::var("P24_FUNDING_PCT")
                .ok()
                .and_then(|v| v.trim().parse::<f64>().ok())
                .filter(|v| *v > 0.0)
                .unwrap_or(0.0001),
            n_spread_block: 0,
        }
    }

    pub fn with_windows(mut self, lookback: usize, atr_period: usize, ofi_window: usize) -> Self {
        self.lookback = lookback.max(2);
        self.atr_period = atr_period.max(2);
        self.ofi_window = ofi_window.max(1);
        self
    }

    pub fn with_fee(mut self, fee_rate: f64, min_net_rr: f64, min_ev_pct: f64) -> Self {
        self.fee_rate = fee_rate;
        self.min_net_rr = min_net_rr;
        self.min_ev_pct = min_ev_pct;
        self
    }

    pub fn with_tp_sl(mut self, tp_atr_mult: f64, min_tp_pct: f64, rr_ratio: f64) -> Self {
        self.tp_atr_mult = tp_atr_mult.max(0.5);
        self.min_tp_pct = min_tp_pct.max(0.0005);
        self.rr_ratio = rr_ratio.max(1.0);
        self
    }

    pub fn with_thresholds(
        mut self,
        min_atr_threshold_pct: f64,
        ofi_threshold: f64,
        volume_anomaly_threshold: f64,
    ) -> Self {
        self.min_atr_threshold_pct = min_atr_threshold_pct.max(0.0);
        self.ofi_threshold = ofi_threshold.clamp(0.02, 0.95);
        self.volume_anomaly_threshold = volume_anomaly_threshold.max(1.0);
        self
    }

    pub fn with_limits(
        mut self,
        max_trades_per_day: u32,
        min_time_between_trades_ms: i64,
        trading_start_utc: u32,
        trading_end_utc: u32,
    ) -> Self {
        self.max_trades_per_day = max_trades_per_day.max(1);
        self.min_time_between_trades_ms = min_time_between_trades_ms.max(0);
        self.trading_start_utc = trading_start_utc;
        self.trading_end_utc = trading_end_utc;
        self
    }

    pub fn with_regime(
        mut self,
        window: usize,
        volatile_pct: f64,
        trending_pct: f64,
    ) -> Self {
        self.regime_detector = MarketRegimeDetector::new(window, volatile_pct, trending_pct);
        self
    }

    fn min_tp_pct(&self) -> f64 {
        let fee_rt = 2.0 * self.fee_rate;
        let denom = 1.0 - (1.0 / self.rr_ratio) * self.min_net_rr;
        if denom <= 0.0 {
            return f64::INFINITY;
        }
        (fee_rt * (1.0 + self.min_net_rr) / denom).max(0.001)
    }

    fn update_atr(&mut self) {
        let len = self.closes.len();
        if len < 2 {
            return;
        }
        let high = self.highs[len - 1];
        let low = self.lows[len - 1];
        let prev_close = self.closes[len - 2];

        let tr = (high - low)
            .max((high - prev_close).abs())
            .max((low - prev_close).abs());

        let atr = if let Some(&prev_atr) = self.atrs.back() {
            let n = self.atr_period as f64;
            (prev_atr * (n - 1.0) + tr) / n
        } else {
            tr
        };
        self.atrs.push_back(atr);
        let max_hist = self.atr_period * 3 + 10;
        while self.atrs.len() > max_hist {
            self.atrs.pop_front();
        }
    }

    fn current_atr(&self) -> f64 {
        self.atrs.back().copied().unwrap_or(0.0)
    }

    fn calculate_momentum(&self) -> f64 {
        if self.closes.len() < self.lookback {
            return 0.0;
        }
        let now = *self.closes.back().unwrap();
        let prev = self.closes[self.closes.len() - self.lookback];
        if prev <= 0.0 {
            0.0
        } else {
            (now - prev) / prev
        }
    }

    fn calculate_ofi(&self) -> f64 {
        let n = self.ofi_window.min(self.taker_buys.len());
        if n == 0 {
            return 0.0;
        }
        let start = self.taker_buys.len() - n;
        let mut buy = 0.0;
        let mut sell = 0.0;
        for i in start..self.taker_buys.len() {
            buy += self.taker_buys[i];
            sell += (self.volumes[i] - self.taker_buys[i]).max(0.0);
        }
        let total = buy + sell;
        if total <= 0.0 {
            0.0
        } else {
            (buy - sell) / total
        }
    }

    fn volume_anomaly(&self) -> f64 {
        let len = self.volumes.len();
        if len < 10 {
            return 1.0;
        }
        let window = 100.min(len - 1);
        let start = len - 1 - window;
        let mut sum = 0.0;
        for i in start..(len - 1) {
            sum += self.volumes[i];
        }
        let avg = sum / window as f64;
        if avg <= 0.0 {
            1.0
        } else {
            self.volumes[len - 1] / avg
        }
    }

    // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 2: Volume spike rejection
    fn is_volume_spike(&self) -> bool {
        let len = self.volumes.len();
        if len < VOL_SPIKE_WINDOW + 1 {
            return false;
        }
        let current = self.volumes[len - 1];
        let mut sum = 0.0;
        for i in (len - 1 - VOL_SPIKE_WINDOW)..(len - 1) {
            sum += self.volumes[i];
        }
        let avg = sum / VOL_SPIKE_WINDOW as f64;
        if avg <= 0.0 {
            return false;
        }
        current >= avg * VOL_SPIKE_REJECTION_MULT
    }

    // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 1: EMA200
    fn update_ema200(&mut self, close: f64) {
        if let Some(prev) = self.ema200_value {
            let new_ema = close * self.ema200_alpha + prev * (1.0 - self.ema200_alpha);
            self.ema200_value = Some(new_ema);
        } else if self.closes.len() >= EMA200_PERIOD {
            let sum: f64 = self.closes.iter().take(EMA200_PERIOD).sum();
            self.ema200_value = Some(sum / EMA200_PERIOD as f64);
        }
    }

    fn is_above_ema200(&self, close: f64) -> bool {
        match self.ema200_value {
            Some(ema) => close > ema * (1.0 + EMA200_DEAD_ZONE_PCT),
            None => false,
        }
    }

    fn is_below_ema200(&self, close: f64) -> bool {
        match self.ema200_value {
            Some(ema) => close < ema * (1.0 - EMA200_DEAD_ZONE_PCT),
            None => false,
        }
    }

    // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 4: Supertrend вЂ” РџР РђР’РР›Р¬РќРђРЇ СЂРµР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ СЃ carry-forward
    // РђР»РіРѕСЂРёС‚Рј:
    //   basic_upper = HL2 + mult Г— ATR(N)
    //   basic_lower = HL2 - mult Г— ATR(N)
    //   final_upper = if basic_upper < prev_final_upper OR prev_close > prev_final_upper
    //                 then basic_upper else prev_final_upper
    //   final_lower = if basic_lower > prev_final_lower OR prev_close < prev_final_lower
    //                 then basic_lower else prev_final_lower
    //   direction flips:
    //     UPв†’DOWN РµСЃР»Рё close < final_lower (supertrend = final_upper)
    //     DOWNв†’UP РµСЃР»Рё close > final_upper (supertrend = final_lower)
    fn update_supertrend(&mut self) {
        let len = self.closes.len();
        if len < SUPERTREND_PERIOD + 1 {
            return;
        }

        let high = self.highs[len - 1];
        let low = self.lows[len - 1];
        let hl2 = (high + low) / 2.0;
        let close = self.closes[len - 1];

        let atr_window = SUPERTREND_PERIOD.min(self.atrs.len());
        if atr_window == 0 {
            return;
        }
        let atr_sum: f64 = self.atrs.iter().rev().take(atr_window).sum();
        let atr = atr_sum / atr_window as f64;

        let basic_upper = hl2 + SUPERTREND_MULT * atr;
        let basic_lower = hl2 - SUPERTREND_MULT * atr;

        let prev_close = self.closes[len - 2];

        let final_upper = match self.prev_final_upper {
            Some(prev_up) => {
                if basic_upper < prev_up || prev_close > prev_up {
                    basic_upper
                } else {
                    prev_up
                }
            }
            None => basic_upper,
        };

        let final_lower = match self.prev_final_lower {
            Some(prev_lo) => {
                if basic_lower > prev_lo || prev_close < prev_lo {
                    basic_lower
                } else {
                    prev_lo
                }
            }
            None => basic_lower,
        };

        let new_dir = if self.supertrend_direction == 1 {
            if close < final_lower { -1 } else { 1 }
        } else {
            if close > final_upper { 1 } else { -1 }
        };

        self.prev_final_upper = Some(final_upper);
        self.prev_final_lower = Some(final_lower);
        self.supertrend_direction = new_dir;
        self.supertrend_value = Some(if new_dir == 1 { final_lower } else { final_upper });
    }

    fn supertrend_is_up(&self) -> bool {
        self.supertrend_direction == 1
    }

    // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 5: MFI
    fn calculate_mfi(&self) -> Option<f64> {
        let len = self.closes.len();
        if len < MFI_PERIOD + 1 {
            return None;
        }
        let mut pos_flow = 0.0;
        let mut neg_flow = 0.0;
        let start = len - MFI_PERIOD - 1;
        for i in start..(len - 1) {
            let typical_price = (self.highs[i + 1] + self.lows[i + 1] + self.closes[i + 1]) / 3.0;
            let prev_typical = (self.highs[i] + self.lows[i] + self.closes[i]) / 3.0;
            let money_flow = typical_price * self.volumes[i + 1];
            if typical_price > prev_typical {
                pos_flow += money_flow;
            } else if typical_price < prev_typical {
                neg_flow += money_flow;
            }
        }
        if neg_flow <= 0.0 {
            return Some(100.0);
        }
        let ratio = pos_flow / neg_flow;
        Some(100.0 - 100.0 / (1.0 + ratio))
    }

    // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 6: ROC
    fn calculate_roc(&self) -> Option<f64> {
        let len = self.closes.len();
        if len < ROC_PERIOD + 1 {
            return None;
        }
        let current = *self.closes.back().unwrap();
        let past = self.closes[len - 1 - ROC_PERIOD];
        if past <= 0.0 {
            return None;
        }
        Some((current - past) / past)
    }

    fn is_trading_hour(&self, ts_ms: i64) -> bool {
        let secs_of_day = ((ts_ms / 1000) % 86_400) as u32;
        let hour = secs_of_day / 3600;
        if self.trading_start_utc <= self.trading_end_utc {
            hour >= self.trading_start_utc && hour < self.trading_end_utc
        } else {
            hour >= self.trading_start_utc || hour < self.trading_end_utc
        }
    }

    fn net_rr(&self, entry: f64, sl: f64, tp: f64, is_long: bool) -> Option<f64> {
        let fee_cost = 2.0 * self.fee_rate * entry;
        // вњ… P23: РїРѕР»РЅР°СЏ СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃРїСЂРµРґР° Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (РІС…РѕРґ РїРѕ ask, РІС‹С…РѕРґ РїРѕ bid).
        // Р”Рѕ РїРµСЂРІРѕРіРѕ bookTicker СЃРїСЂРµРґ РЅРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РµРЅ вЂ” РјРѕРґРµР»СЊ РєР°Рє СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ.
        let spread_cost = if self.last_bid > 0.0 && self.last_ask > self.last_bid {
            self.last_ask - self.last_bid
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        let (gross_profit, gross_loss) = if is_long {
            (tp - entry - spread_cost, entry - sl + spread_cost)
        } else {
            (entry - tp - spread_cost, sl - entry + spread_cost)
        };
        // вњ… P24: funding Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (Р°СѓРґРёС‚ в„–12) вЂ” РєРѕРЅСЃРµСЂРІР°С‚РёРІРЅРѕ РѕРґРёРЅ РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» 8h
        let funding_cost = entry * self.funding_pct;
        let net_profit = gross_profit - fee_cost - funding_cost;
        let net_loss = gross_loss + fee_cost + funding_cost;
        if net_loss <= 0.0 || net_profit <= 0.0 {
            return None;
        }
        Some(net_profit / net_loss)
    }

    // вњ… P23: slippage-guard вЂ” true = СЃРїСЂРµРґ С€РёСЂРµ Р»РёРјРёС‚Р°, РІС…РѕРґ Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰С‘РЅ
    fn spread_too_wide(&mut self) -> bool {
        if self.last_bid > 0.0 && self.last_ask > self.last_bid {
            let mid = (self.last_bid + self.last_ask) * 0.5;
            if (self.last_ask - self.last_bid) / mid > self.max_spread_pct {
                self.n_spread_block += 1;
                return true;
            }
        }
        false
    }

    fn expected_value(&self, wr: f64, avg_win: f64, avg_loss: f64) -> f64 {
        wr * avg_win - (1.0 - wr) * avg_loss
    }

    pub fn record_trade_result(&mut self, pnl: f64) {
        if pnl > 0.0 {
            self.trades_won += 1;
            self.total_win_amount += pnl;
            self.consecutive_losses = 0;
        } else {
            self.trades_lost += 1;
            self.total_loss_amount += pnl.abs();
            self.consecutive_losses = self.consecutive_losses.saturating_add(1);
            self.last_loss_ts = Some(chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis());
        }
    }
}

impl Strategy for MomentumStrategy {
    fn name(&self) -> &str { "momentum" }

    // вњ… V3.4: backfill-РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµРІ. РРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂС‹ (EMA200/Supertrend/ATR/regime)
    // РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµРІР°СЋС‚СЃСЏ РЅР° 250 СЃРІРµС‡Р°С… РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёРё СЃСЂР°Р·Сѓ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚Р°, Р° РЅРµ С‡РµСЂРµР·
    // 8+ РґРЅРµР№ Р¶РёРІС‹С… 1h-СЃРІРµС‡РµР№. РЎРёРіРЅР°Р»С‹ РїРѕ backfill Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰РµРЅС‹ guard'РѕРј
    // РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё process() (if kline.is_backfill { return Ok(None); }).
    fn wants_backfill(&self) -> bool { true }

    fn notify_position_open(&mut self) {
        self.filter.on_position_opened();
    }

    // P16: sizing base follows live equity
    fn set_live_equity(&mut self, eq: f64) { self.balance = eq; }

    fn notify_position_closed(&mut self, pnl: f64) {
        self.filter.on_position_closed(self.last_kline_ts);
        self.record_trade_result(pnl);
    }

    fn in_position(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self.filter.state(), PositionState::InPosition)
    }

    fn diagnostics(&self) -> Option<String> {
        let total = self.blocked_by_atr
            + self.blocked_by_momentum
            + self.blocked_by_ofi
            + self.blocked_by_volume
            + self.blocked_by_regime
            + self.blocked_by_time
            + self.blocked_by_limit_day
            + self.blocked_by_cooldown
            + self.blocked_by_net_rr
            + self.blocked_by_edge
            + self.blocked_by_ema200
            + self.blocked_by_vol_spike
            + self.blocked_by_loss_streak
            + self.blocked_by_supertrend
            + self.blocked_by_mfi
            + self.blocked_by_roc
            + self.n_spread_block
            + self.passed_filters;

        Some(format!(
            "Momentum(candles) diag v3full | candles={} total={} | vol={} atr={} mom={} ofi={} regime={} time={} lim={} cd={} rr={} spr={} edge={} ema={} vspk={} lstreak={} st={} mfi={} roc={} passed={} | min_tp={:.4}% fee_rt={:.4}% lb={} atr_p={} ofi_win={} regime_range={:.3}% last_atr={:.4} rr_edgeв‰Ґ{:.2} trades={}/{} | ema200={} st_dir={} cons_loss={}",
            self.total_closed_candles, total,
            self.blocked_by_volume, self.blocked_by_atr, self.blocked_by_momentum, self.blocked_by_ofi,
            self.blocked_by_regime, self.blocked_by_time, self.blocked_by_limit_day,
            self.blocked_by_cooldown, self.blocked_by_net_rr, self.n_spread_block, self.blocked_by_edge,
            self.blocked_by_ema200, self.blocked_by_vol_spike, self.blocked_by_loss_streak,
            self.blocked_by_supertrend, self.blocked_by_mfi, self.blocked_by_roc,
            self.passed_filters,
            self.min_tp_pct() * 100.0, 2.0 * self.fee_rate * 100.0,
            self.lookback, self.atr_period, self.ofi_window,
            self.regime_detector.last_range_pct(), self.current_atr(),
            self.min_net_rr * EDGE_QUALITY_MULT,
            self.trades_today, self.max_trades_per_day,
            self.ema200_value.map(|v| format!("{:.4}", v)).unwrap_or_else(|| "n/a".to_string()),
            if self.supertrend_direction == 1 { "UP" } else { "DOWN" },
            self.consecutive_losses,
        ))
    }

    fn process(&mut self, msg: &WebSocketMessage) -> Result<Option<Signal>> {
        // вњ… P23: Р»РѕРІРёРј Р¶РёРІРѕР№ СЃРїСЂРµРґ РёР· bookTicker (guard РІС…РѕРґР° + СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃРїСЂРµРґР° РІ net_rr)
        if let WebSocketMessage::BookTicker(b) = msg {
            if b.symbol.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&self.symbol)
                && b.best_bid > 0.0
                && b.best_ask > b.best_bid
            {
                self.last_bid = b.best_bid;
                self.last_ask = b.best_ask;
            }
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let kline = match msg {
            WebSocketMessage::Kline(k) => k,
            _ => return Ok(None),
        };
        if !kline.symbol.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&self.symbol) { return Ok(None); }
        if !kline.is_closed { return Ok(None); }

        self.last_kline_ts = kline.timestamp;
        self.total_closed_candles += 1;

        let regime = self.regime_detector.detect_from_msg(msg);

        let day = kline.timestamp / 86_400_000;
        if day != self.current_day {
            self.current_day = day;
            self.trades_today = 0;
        }

        self.closes.push_back(kline.close);
        self.highs.push_back(kline.high);
        self.lows.push_back(kline.low);
        self.volumes.push_back(kline.volume);
        self.taker_buys.push_back(kline.taker_buy_volume);

        let max_hist = self.lookback.max(self.atr_period * 2).max(self.ofi_window).max(EMA200_PERIOD).max(120) + 10;
        while self.closes.len() > max_hist {
            self.closes.pop_front(); self.highs.pop_front(); self.lows.pop_front();
            self.volumes.pop_front(); self.taker_buys.pop_front();
        }

        self.update_atr();
        self.update_ema200(kline.close);
        self.update_supertrend();

        if kline.is_backfill { return Ok(None); }
        if !self.filter.can_signal(kline.close, kline.timestamp) { return Ok(None); }
        if self.closes.len() < self.lookback.max(self.atr_period) { return Ok(None); }

        // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 3: Loss-streak cooldown
        if self.consecutive_losses >= LOSS_STREAK_THRESHOLD {
            if let Some(last_ts) = self.last_loss_ts {
                let now = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis();
                if now - last_ts < LOSS_COOLDOWN_MS {
                    self.blocked_by_loss_streak += 1;
                    return Ok(None);
                }
            }
        }

        // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 2: Volume spike rejection
        if self.is_volume_spike() {
            self.blocked_by_vol_spike += 1;
            tracing::debug!("рџЋЇ [{}] vol-spike rejection", self.symbol);
            return Ok(None);
        }

        let vol_anom = self.volume_anomaly();
        if vol_anom < self.volume_anomaly_threshold {
            self.blocked_by_volume += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        let atr = self.current_atr();
        let atr_pct = atr / kline.close;
        let min_atr_pct = self.min_atr_threshold_pct / 100.0;
        if atr_pct < min_atr_pct {
            self.blocked_by_atr += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        let ofi = self.calculate_ofi();
        if ofi.abs() < self.ofi_threshold {
            self.blocked_by_ofi += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        let momentum = self.calculate_momentum();
        if momentum.abs() < 0.0005 {
            self.blocked_by_momentum += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if (ofi > 0.0) != (momentum > 0.0) {
            self.blocked_by_momentum += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        let is_long = momentum > 0.0;

        // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 6: ROC вЂ” block SHORT РЅР° РїР°РЅРёС‡РµСЃРєРѕРј РґР°РјРїРµ (Р»РѕРІР»СЏ РЅРѕР¶РµР№)
        let roc = self.calculate_roc();
        let is_dump = roc.map(|r| r < ROC_DUMP_THRESHOLD).unwrap_or(false);
        if is_dump && !is_long {
            self.blocked_by_roc += 1;
            tracing::debug!(
                "рџЋЇ [{}] ROC block SHORT: ROC={:.2}% вЂ” Р»РѕРІР»СЏ РЅРѕР¶РµР№",
                self.symbol, roc.unwrap_or(0.0) * 100.0
            );
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 5: MFI вЂ” С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂ Р±РѕРєРѕРІРёРєР°
        if regime != MarketRegime::Trending {
            if let Some(mfi) = self.calculate_mfi() {
                if mfi > MFI_OVERBOUGHT || mfi < MFI_OVERSOLD {
                    self.blocked_by_mfi += 1;
                    return Ok(None);
                }
            }
        }

        // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 1+4: EMA200 + Supertrend вЂ” BINARY trend filter (РѕР±Р° С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂР°)
        let above_ema = self.is_above_ema200(kline.close);
        let below_ema = self.is_below_ema200(kline.close);
        let st_up = self.supertrend_is_up();

        // LONG: close > EMA200 Р Supertrend UP
        if is_long && (!above_ema || !st_up) {
            self.blocked_by_ema200 += 1;
            tracing::debug!(
                "рџЋЇ [{}] trend block LONG: above_ema={} st_up={} вЂ” РєРѕРЅС‚СЂ-С‚СЂРµРЅРґ",
                self.symbol, above_ema, st_up
            );
            return Ok(None);
        }
        // SHORT: close < EMA200 Р Supertrend DOWN (С‚.Рµ. !st_up)
        if !is_long && (!below_ema || st_up) {
            self.blocked_by_ema200 += 1;
            tracing::debug!(
                "рџЋЇ [{}] trend block SHORT: below_ema={} st_up={} вЂ” РєРѕРЅС‚СЂ-С‚СЂРµРЅРґ",
                self.symbol, below_ema, st_up
            );
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // --- Regime ---
        if regime != MarketRegime::Trending {
            self.blocked_by_regime += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        if !self.is_trading_hour(kline.timestamp) {
            self.blocked_by_time += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        if self.trades_today >= self.max_trades_per_day {
            self.blocked_by_limit_day += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if let Some(last_ts) = self.last_trade_ts_ms {
            if kline.timestamp - last_ts < self.min_time_between_trades_ms {
                self.blocked_by_cooldown += 1;
                return Ok(None);
            }
        }

        let min_tp = self.min_tp_pct();
        let tp_pct = (atr_pct * self.tp_atr_mult).max(min_tp).max(self.min_tp_pct).min(0.02);
        let sl_pct = tp_pct / self.rr_ratio;

        let (side, sl, tp) = if is_long {
            ("BUY", kline.close * (1.0 - sl_pct), kline.close * (1.0 + tp_pct))
        } else {
            ("SELL", kline.close * (1.0 + sl_pct), kline.close * (1.0 - tp_pct))
        };

        let entry = round_to_tick(kline.close, BTC_TICK_SIZE);
        let sl = round_to_tick(sl, BTC_TICK_SIZE);
        let tp = round_to_tick(tp, BTC_TICK_SIZE);

        let eff_rr = match self.net_rr(entry, sl, tp, is_long) {
            Some(rr) => rr,
            None => {
                self.blocked_by_net_rr += 1;
                return Ok(None);
            }
        };

        if eff_rr + 1e-4 < self.min_net_rr {
            self.blocked_by_net_rr += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if eff_rr < self.min_net_rr * EDGE_QUALITY_MULT {
            self.blocked_by_edge += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // вњ… P23: slippage-guard вЂ” СЃРїСЂРµРґ С€РёСЂРµ Р»РёРјРёС‚Р° = РІС…РѕРґ Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰С‘РЅ
        if self.spread_too_wide() {
            return Ok(None);
        }

        let total_trades = self.trades_won + self.trades_lost;
        if total_trades >= 20 {
            let wr = self.trades_won as f64 / total_trades as f64;
            let avg_win = if self.trades_won > 0 { self.total_win_amount / self.trades_won as f64 } else { 0.0 };
            let avg_loss = if self.trades_lost > 0 { self.total_loss_amount / self.trades_lost as f64 } else { 0.0 };
            if avg_loss > 0.0 {
                let ev = self.expected_value(wr, avg_win, avg_loss);
                let ev_pct = ev / avg_loss * 100.0;
                if ev_pct < self.min_ev_pct {
                    return Ok(None);
                }
            }
        }

        let raw_qty = calc_position_size_with_fees(
            self.balance, entry, sl,
            self.risk_per_trade_pct, self.fee_rate * 2.0,
        );
        let quantity = floor_to_step(raw_qty, BTC_QTY_STEP);
        if quantity <= 0.0 { return Ok(None); }

        self.passed_filters += 1;
        self.trades_today += 1;
        self.last_trade_ts_ms = Some(kline.timestamp);
        self.filter.on_signal(entry, kline.timestamp);

        tracing::info!(
            "рџЋЇ [{}] v3full signal: {} qty={:.5} @ {:.4} SL={:.4} TP={:.4} | EMA200={} ST={} ROC={:.2}% MFI={} vol_anom={:.2}x rr={:.2}",
            self.symbol, side, quantity, entry, sl, tp,
            self.ema200_value.map(|v| format!("{:.4}", v)).unwrap_or_else(|| "n/a".to_string()),
            if st_up { "UP" } else { "DOWN" },
            roc.map(|r| r * 100.0).unwrap_or(0.0),
            self.calculate_mfi().map(|m| format!("{:.0}", m)).unwrap_or_else(|| "n/a".to_string()),
            vol_anom, eff_rr,
        );

        Ok(Some(Signal {
            symbol: kline.symbol.clone(),
            side: side.to_string(),
            price: entry,
            quantity,
            strategy_name: self.name().to_string(),
            leverage: 1.0,
            stop_loss: Some(sl),
            take_profit: Some(tp),
            atr_hint: atr,
            entry_spread: 0.0,
        }))
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod p24_tests {
    use super::*;

    // вњ… P24: funding СЃС‚СЂРѕРіРѕ СѓС…СѓРґС€Р°РµС‚ net_rr; =0 -> С„РѕСЂРјСѓР»Р° РєР°Рє РґРѕ P24
    #[test]
    fn test_net_rr_funding_p24() {
        let mut s = MomentumStrategy::new("TAOUSDT".to_string(), 1000.0, 0.5);
        s.last_bid = 0.0; s.last_ask = 0.0; // Р±РµР· СЃРїСЂРµРґР° вЂ” С‡РёСЃС‚С‹Р№ СЌС„С„РµРєС‚ funding
        s.funding_pct = 0.0001;
        let with_f = s.net_rr(100.0, 99.0, 102.0, true).unwrap();
        s.funding_pct = 0.0;
        let no_f = s.net_rr(100.0, 99.0, 102.0, true).unwrap();
        let fee = 2.0 * DEFAULT_FEE_RATE * 100.0;
        let exp_with = (2.0 - fee - 0.01) / (1.0 + fee + 0.01);
        let exp_no = (2.0 - fee) / (1.0 + fee);
        assert!((with_f - exp_with).abs() < 1e-9, "with_f={with_f} exp={exp_with}");
        assert!((no_f - exp_no).abs() < 1e-9, "no_f={no_f} exp={exp_no}");
        assert!(with_f < no_f);
        // С€РѕСЂС‚ вЂ” СЃРёРјРјРµС‚СЂРёС‡РЅРѕ
        s.funding_pct = 0.0001;
        let sh = s.net_rr(100.0, 101.0, 98.0, false).unwrap();
        assert!(sh > 0.0 && sh < no_f);
    }

    // вњ… P24: Р±РѕР»СЊС€РѕР№ funding РіР°СЃРёС‚ СЃРґРµР»РєСѓ -> None (СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ РјРѕР»С‡РёС‚, РЅРµ С‚РѕСЂРіСѓРµС‚ РІ РјРёРЅСѓСЃ)
    #[test]
    fn test_net_rr_funding_none() {
        let mut s = MomentumStrategy::new("TAOUSDT".to_string(), 1000.0, 0.5);
        s.last_bid = 0.0; s.last_ask = 0.0;
        s.funding_pct = 0.5; // 50% Р·Р° РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» вЂ” РІС‹СЂРѕР¶РґРµРЅРЅС‹Р№ СЃР»СѓС‡Р°Р№
        assert_eq!(s.net_rr(100.0, 99.0, 100.05, true), None);
    }
}

//! momentum.rs v3 вЂ” СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёСЏ РјРѕРјРµРЅС‚СѓРјР° СЃ СЂР°СЃС€РёСЂРµРЅРЅС‹РјРё С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂР°РјРё.
//!
//! РџРђРўР§Р Р¤РђР—Р« A (8 СѓР»СѓС‡С€РµРЅРёР№):
//!   1. Supertrend(M15-equivalent on 1h) вЂ” trend filter
//!   2. EMA200 вЂ” РіР»РѕР±Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ binary trend filter
//!   3. MFI(14) вЂ” С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂ Р±РѕРєРѕРІРёРєР° (РїРµСЂРµРєСѓРїР»РµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ/РїРµСЂРµРїСЂРѕРґР°РЅРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ)
//!   4. ROC(12) вЂ” РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂ СЂРµР·РєРёС… РїР°РґРµРЅРёР№ (РґР»СЏ reversal long)
//!   5. Volume spike REJECTION вЂ” РЅРµ РІС…РѕРґРёС‚СЊ РїСЂРё vol > NГ— avg (РёРјРїСѓР»СЊСЃ, РЅРµ СЂР°Р·РІРѕСЂРѕС‚)
//!   6. Cooldown 4С‡ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ 2 РїРѕРґСЂСЏРґ Р»РѕСЃСЃРѕРІ (СЃРЅРёР¶Р°РµС‚ РґСѓР±Р»СЊ-РѕС€РёР±РєРё)
//!   7. SL СЂР°СЃС€РёСЂРµРЅ (rr_ratio 2.5 в†’ 1.5) вЂ” Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ breathing room РґР»СЏ BE
//!   8. Breakeven РЅР° +1.5R (РЅРµ +1R) вЂ” РїСѓСЃС‚СЊ С‚СЂРµРЅРґ СЂР°Р·РѕРІСЊС‘С‚СЃСЏ
//!
//! Р’СЃРµ РїР°С‚С‡Рё вЂ” additions, РїСѓР±Р»РёС‡РЅС‹Р№ API РЅРµ С‚СЂРѕРЅСѓС‚.

// ROC(12) = (close - close_12_periods_ago) / close_12_periods_ago

    enable_loss_cooldown: bool,

impl MomentumStrategy {
    pub fn new(symbol: String, balance: f64, risk_pct: f64) -> Self {
        Self {
            symbol,
            balance,
            risk_per_trade_pct: risk_pct,

            closes: VecDeque::with_capacity(500),
            highs: VecDeque::with_capacity(500),
            lows: VecDeque::with_capacity(500),
            volumes: VecDeque::with_capacity(500),
            taker_buys: VecDeque::with_capacity(500),
            atrs: VecDeque::with_capacity(500),

            lookback: 20,
            atr_period: 14,
            ofi_window: 5,

            min_atr_threshold_pct: 0.05,
            ofi_threshold: 0.15,
            volume_anomaly_threshold: 1.5,

            tp_atr_mult: 2.5,
            min_tp_pct: 0.0025,
            rr_ratio: 2.5,

            filter: SignalFilter::new(60, 0.15),
            regime_detector: MarketRegimeDetector::new(100, 0.5, 0.1),

            trading_start_utc: 7,
            trading_end_utc: 22,

            max_trades_per_day: 10,
            trades_today: 0,
            current_day: -1,
            last_trade_ts_ms: None,
            min_time_between_trades_ms: 300_000,
            last_kline_ts: 0,

            fee_rate: DEFAULT_FEE_RATE,
            min_net_rr: DEFAULT_MIN_NET_RR,
            min_ev_pct: DEFAULT_MIN_EV_PCT,

            trades_won: 0,
            trades_lost: 0,
            total_win_amount: 0.0,
            total_loss_amount: 0.0,

            blocked_by_atr: 0,
            blocked_by_momentum: 0,
            blocked_by_ofi: 0,
            blocked_by_volume: 0,
            blocked_by_regime: 0,
            blocked_by_time: 0,
            blocked_by_limit_day: 0,
            blocked_by_cooldown: 0,
            blocked_by_net_rr: 0,
            blocked_by_edge: 0,
            n_spread_block: 0,
            last_bid: 0.0,
            last_ask: 0.0,
            max_spread_pct: std::env::var("P23_MAX_SPREAD_PCT")
                .ok()
                .and_then(|v| v.parse::<f64>().ok())
                .map(|v| if v > 0.0 { v } else { f64::INFINITY })
                .unwrap_or(0.0010),
            // вњ… P24: funding Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (Р°СѓРґРёС‚ в„–12) вЂ” РєРѕРЅСЃРµСЂРІР°С‚РёРІРЅРѕ РѕРґРёРЅ РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» 8h,
            // РґРѕР»СЏ РѕС‚ С†РµРЅС‹; РґРµС„РѕР»С‚ 0.0001 = 0.01%/8h; РјСѓСЃРѕСЂ/<=0 РІ env -> 0.0 (РІС‹РєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРѕ)
            funding_pct: std::env::var("P24_FUNDING_PCT")
                .ok()
                .and_then(|v| v.trim().parse::<f64>().ok())
                .filter(|v| *v > 0.0)
                .unwrap_or(0.0001),
            passed_filters: 0,
            total_closed_candles: 0,

            // рџ†• РРЅРёС†РёР°Р»РёР·Р°С†РёСЏ РЅРѕРІС‹С… СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРєРѕРІ
            blocked_by_supertrend: 0,
            blocked_by_ema200: 0,
            blocked_by_mfi: 0,
            blocked_by_vol_spike: 0,
            blocked_by_loss_streak: 0,

            consecutive_losses: 0,
            last_loss_ts: None,

            supertrend_value: None,
            supertrend_direction: 1,

            ema200_value: None,
            ema200_alpha: 2.0 / (EMA200_PERIOD as f64 + 1.0),

            enable_supertrend: true,
            enable_ema200: true,
            enable_mfi: true,
            enable_vol_spike_rejection: true,
            enable_loss_cooldown: true,
        }
    }

    pub fn with_windows(mut self, lookback: usize, atr_period: usize, ofi_window: usize) -> Self {
        self.lookback = lookback.max(2);
        self.atr_period = atr_period.max(2);
        self.ofi_window = ofi_window.max(1);
        self
    }

    pub fn with_fee(mut self, fee_rate: f64, min_net_rr: f64, min_ev_pct: f64) -> Self {
        self.fee_rate = fee_rate;
        self.min_net_rr = min_net_rr;
        self.min_ev_pct = min_ev_pct;
        self
    }

    pub fn with_tp_sl(mut self, tp_atr_mult: f64, min_tp_pct: f64, rr_ratio: f64) -> Self {
        self.tp_atr_mult = tp_atr_mult.max(0.5);
        self.min_tp_pct = min_tp_pct.max(0.0005);
        self.rr_ratio = rr_ratio.max(1.0);
        self
    }

    pub fn with_thresholds(
        mut self,
        min_atr_threshold_pct: f64,
        ofi_threshold: f64,
        volume_anomaly_threshold: f64,
    ) -> Self {
        self.min_atr_threshold_pct = min_atr_threshold_pct.max(0.0);
        self.ofi_threshold = ofi_threshold.clamp(0.02, 0.95);
        self.volume_anomaly_threshold = volume_anomaly_threshold.max(1.0);
        self
    }

    pub fn with_limits(
        mut self,
        max_trades_per_day: u32,
        min_time_between_trades_ms: i64,
        trading_start_utc: u32,
        trading_end_utc: u32,
    ) -> Self {
        self.max_trades_per_day = max_trades_per_day.max(1);
        self.min_time_between_trades_ms = min_time_between_trades_ms.max(0);
        self.trading_start_utc = trading_start_utc;
        self.trading_end_utc = trading_end_utc;
        self
    }

    pub fn with_regime(
        mut self,
        window: usize,
        volatile_pct: f64,
        trending_pct: f64,
    ) -> Self {
        self.regime_detector = MarketRegimeDetector::new(window, volatile_pct, trending_pct);
        self
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // рџ†• РџРђРўР§: Р’РєР»/РІС‹РєР» РЅРѕРІС‹С… С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂРѕРІ (РґР»СЏ A/B С‚РµСЃС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёСЏ)
    // ========================================================================
    pub fn with_v3_filters(
        mut self,
        enable_supertrend: bool,
        enable_ema200: bool,
        enable_mfi: bool,
        enable_vol_spike: bool,
        enable_loss_cooldown: bool,
    ) -> Self {
        self.enable_supertrend = enable_supertrend;
        self.enable_ema200 = enable_ema200;
        self.enable_mfi = enable_mfi;
        self.enable_vol_spike_rejection = enable_vol_spike;
        self.enable_loss_cooldown = enable_loss_cooldown;
        self
    }

    fn min_tp_pct(&self) -> f64 {
        let fee_rt = 2.0 * self.fee_rate;
        let denom = 1.0 - (1.0 / self.rr_ratio) * self.min_net_rr;
        if denom <= 0.0 {
            return f64::INFINITY;
        }
        (fee_rt * (1.0 + self.min_net_rr) / denom).max(0.001)
    }

    fn update_atr(&mut self) {
        let len = self.closes.len();
        if len < 2 {
            return;
        }
        let high = self.highs[len - 1];
        let low = self.lows[len - 1];
        let prev_close = self.closes[len - 2];

        let tr = (high - low)
            .max((high - prev_close).abs())
            .max((low - prev_close).abs());

        let atr = if let Some(&prev_atr) = self.atrs.back() {
            let n = self.atr_period as f64;
            (prev_atr * (n - 1.0) + tr) / n
        } else {
            tr
        };
        self.atrs.push_back(atr);
        let max_hist = self.atr_period * 3 + 10;
        while self.atrs.len() > max_hist {
            self.atrs.pop_front();
        }
    }

    fn current_atr(&self) -> f64 {
        self.atrs.back().copied().unwrap_or(0.0)
    }

    fn calculate_momentum(&self) -> f64 {
        if self.closes.len() < self.lookback {
            return 0.0;
        }
        let now = *self.closes.back().unwrap();
        let prev = self.closes[self.closes.len() - self.lookback];
        if prev <= 0.0 {
            0.0
        } else {
            (now - prev) / prev
        }
    }

    fn calculate_ofi(&self) -> f64 {
        let n = self.ofi_window.min(self.taker_buys.len());
        if n == 0 {
            return 0.0;
        }
        let start = self.taker_buys.len() - n;
        let mut buy = 0.0;
        let mut sell = 0.0;
        for i in start..self.taker_buys.len() {
            buy += self.taker_buys[i];
            sell += (self.volumes[i] - self.taker_buys[i]).max(0.0);
        }
        let total = buy + sell;
        if total <= 0.0 {
            0.0
        } else {
            (buy - sell) / total
        }
    }

    fn volume_anomaly(&self) -> f64 {
        let len = self.volumes.len();
        if len < 10 {
            return 1.0;
        }
        let window = 100.min(len - 1);
        let start = len - 1 - window;
        let mut sum = 0.0;
        for i in start..(len - 1) {
            sum += self.volumes[i];
        }
        let avg = sum / window as f64;
        if avg <= 0.0 {
            1.0
        } else {
            self.volumes[len - 1] / avg
        }
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 5: Volume spike rejection вЂ” С‚РµРєСѓС‰Р°СЏ СЃРІРµС‡Р° > 3Г— avg
    // Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ true РµСЃР»Рё С‚РµРєСѓС‰Р°СЏ СЃРІРµС‡Р° РёРјРµРµС‚ Р°РЅРѕРјР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РѕР±СЉС‘Рј (РїР°РЅРёРєР°/РёРјРїСѓР»СЊСЃ).
    // РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ РґР»СЏ REJECTION вЂ” РќР• РІС…РѕРґРёС‚СЊ, С‚.Рє. СЌС‚Рѕ РїСЂРѕРґРѕР»Р¶РµРЅРёРµ РёРјРїСѓР»СЊСЃР°,
    // Р° РЅРµ СЂР°Р·РІРѕСЂРѕС‚.
    // ========================================================================
    fn is_volume_spike(&self) -> bool {
        let len = self.volumes.len();
        if len < 21 {
            return false;
        }
        let current = self.volumes[len - 1];
        let window = 20;
        let start = len - 1 - window;
        let mut sum = 0.0;
        for i in start..(len - 1) {
            sum += self.volumes[i];
        }
        let avg = sum / window as f64;
        if avg <= 0.0 {
            return false;
        }
        current >= avg * VOL_SPIKE_REJECTION_MULT
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 1+2: Supertrend + EMA200 вЂ” РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂС‹ С‚СЂРµРЅРґР°
    // ========================================================================

    /// Update EMA200 incrementally. Called on each new kline.
    fn update_ema200(&mut self, close: f64) {
        if let Some(prev) = self.ema200_value {
            let new_ema = close * self.ema200_alpha + prev * (1.0 - self.ema200_alpha);
            self.ema200_value = Some(new_ema);
        } else if self.closes.len() >= EMA200_PERIOD {
            // Seed: first EMA = SMA of first N closes
            let sum: f64 = self.closes.iter().take(EMA200_PERIOD).sum();
            self.ema200_value = Some(sum / EMA200_PERIOD as f64);
        }
        // Else: not enough data yet, ema200_value stays None
    }

    /// Calculate Supertrend value for the current bar.
    /// Supertrend = HL2 В± multiplier Г— ATR(10).
    /// Direction flips when price crosses the band.
    fn update_supertrend(&mut self) {
        let len = self.closes.len();
        if len < SUPERTREND_PERIOD + 1 {
            return;
        }
        let high = self.highs[len - 1];
        let low = self.lows[len - 1];
        let hl2 = (high + low) / 2.0;

        // ATR over SUPERTREND_PERIOD
        let atr_window = SUPERTREND_PERIOD.min(self.atrs.len());
        if atr_window == 0 {
            return;
        }
        let atr_sum: f64 = self.atrs.iter().rev().take(atr_window).sum();
        let atr = atr_sum / atr_window as f64;

        let close = self.closes[len - 1];

        let upper_band = hl2 + SUPERTREND_MULT * atr;
        let lower_band = hl2 - SUPERTREND_MULT * atr;

        // Track previous band to handle flips
        let (prev_lower, prev_upper) = if let Some(prev_st) = self.supertrend_value {
            // We store last "anchor" вЂ” need separate tracking for upper/lower
            // Simplified: recompute both as current bands
            (lower_band, upper_band)
        } else {
            (lower_band, upper_band)
        };

        // Final bands with carry-forward (Supertrend semantics)
        let final_upper = if upper_band < prev_upper || close > prev_upper {
            upper_band
        } else {
            prev_upper
        };
        let final_lower = if lower_band > prev_lower || close < prev_lower {
            lower_band
        } else {
            prev_lower
        };

        // Direction logic
        let new_dir = if self.supertrend_direction == 1 {
            if close < final_lower { -1 } else { 1 }
        } else {
            if close > final_upper { 1 } else { -1 }
        };

        self.supertrend_direction = new_dir;
        self.supertrend_value = Some(if new_dir == 1 { final_lower } else { final_upper });
    }

    /// True if Supertrend is UP (allow LONG, block SHORT).
    fn supertrend_is_up(&self) -> bool {
        self.supertrend_direction == 1
    }

    /// True if close > EMA200 (allow LONG, block SHORT).
    fn above_ema200(&self) -> bool {
        match self.ema200_value {
            Some(ema) => self.closes.back().map(|c| *c > ema).unwrap_or(false),
            None => false, // not enough data
        }
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 3: MFI (Money Flow Index) РґР»СЏ Р±РѕРєРѕРІРёРєР°
    // MFI = 100 - 100/(1 + MoneyFlowRatio)
    // MoneyFlowRatio = PositiveMoneyFlow / NegativeMoneyFlow Р·Р° N РїРµСЂРёРѕРґРѕРІ
    // ========================================================================
    fn calculate_mfi(&self) -> Option<f64> {
        let len = self.closes.len();
        if len < MFI_PERIOD + 1 {
            return None;
        }
        let mut pos_flow = 0.0;
        let mut neg_flow = 0.0;
        let start = len - MFI_PERIOD - 1;
        for i in start..(len - 1) {
            let typical_price = (self.highs[i + 1] + self.lows[i + 1] + self.closes[i + 1]) / 3.0;
            let prev_typical = (self.highs[i] + self.lows[i] + self.closes[i]) / 3.0;
            let money_flow = typical_price * self.volumes[i + 1];
            if typical_price > prev_typical {
                pos_flow += money_flow;
            } else if typical_price < prev_typical {
                neg_flow += money_flow;
            }
        }
        if neg_flow <= 0.0 {
            return Some(100.0);
        }
        let ratio = pos_flow / neg_flow;
        Some(100.0 - 100.0 / (1.0 + ratio))
    }

    // ========================================================================
    // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 4: ROC (Rate of Change) вЂ” РґРµС‚РµРєС‚РѕСЂ СЂРµР·РєРёС… РїР°РґРµРЅРёР№
    // ROC = (close - close_N_ago) / close_N_ago
    // ========================================================================
    fn calculate_roc(&self) -> Option<f64> {
        let len = self.closes.len();
        if len < ROC_PERIOD + 1 {
            return None;
        }
        let current = *self.closes.back().unwrap();
        let past = self.closes[len - 1 - ROC_PERIOD];
        if past <= 0.0 {
            return None;
        }
        Some((current - past) / past)
    }

    fn is_trading_hour(&self, ts_ms: i64) -> bool {
        let secs_of_day = ((ts_ms / 1000) % 86_400) as u32;
        let hour = secs_of_day / 3600;
        if self.trading_start_utc <= self.trading_end_utc {
            hour >= self.trading_start_utc && hour < self.trading_end_utc
        } else {
            hour >= self.trading_start_utc || hour < self.trading_end_utc
        }
    }

    fn net_rr(&self, entry: f64, sl: f64, tp: f64, is_long: bool) -> Option<f64> {
        let fee_cost = 2.0 * self.fee_rate * entry;
        // вњ… P23: РїРѕР»РЅР°СЏ СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃРїСЂРµРґР° Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (РІС…РѕРґ РїРѕ ask, РІС‹С…РѕРґ РїРѕ bid).
        // Р”Рѕ РїРµСЂРІРѕРіРѕ bookTicker СЃРїСЂРµРґ РЅРµРёР·РІРµСЃС‚РµРЅ вЂ” РјРѕРґРµР»СЊ РєР°Рє СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ.
        let spread_cost = if self.last_bid > 0.0 && self.last_ask > self.last_bid {
            self.last_ask - self.last_bid
        } else {
            0.0
        };
        let (gross_profit, gross_loss) = if is_long {
            (tp - entry - spread_cost, entry - sl + spread_cost)
        } else {
            (entry - tp - spread_cost, sl - entry + spread_cost)
        };
        // вњ… P24: funding Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (Р°СѓРґРёС‚ в„–12) вЂ” РєРѕРЅСЃРµСЂРІР°С‚РёРІРЅРѕ РѕРґРёРЅ РёРЅС‚РµСЂРІР°Р» 8h
        let funding_cost = entry * self.funding_pct;
        let net_profit = gross_profit - fee_cost - funding_cost;
        let net_loss = gross_loss + fee_cost + funding_cost;
        if net_loss <= 0.0 || net_profit <= 0.0 {
            return None;
        }
        Some(net_profit / net_loss)
    }

    /// вњ… P23: slippage-guard вЂ” true = СЃРїСЂРµРґ С€РёСЂРµ Р»РёРјРёС‚Р°, РІС…РѕРґ Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰С‘РЅ
    fn spread_too_wide(&mut self) -> bool {
        if self.last_bid > 0.0 && self.last_ask > self.last_bid {
            let mid = (self.last_bid + self.last_ask) * 0.5;
            if (self.last_ask - self.last_bid) / mid > self.max_spread_pct {
                self.n_spread_block += 1;
                return true;
            }
        }
        false
    }

    fn expected_value(&self, wr: f64, avg_win: f64, avg_loss: f64) -> f64 {
        wr * avg_win - (1.0 - wr) * avg_loss
    }

    pub fn record_trade_result(&mut self, pnl: f64) {
        if pnl > 0.0 {
            self.trades_won += 1;
            self.total_win_amount += pnl;
            // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 6: СЃР±СЂРѕСЃ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРєР° РїРѕРґСЂСЏРґ Р»РѕСЃСЃРѕРІ РїСЂРё win
            self.consecutive_losses = 0;
        } else {
            self.trades_lost += 1;
            self.total_loss_amount += pnl.abs();
            // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 6: РёРЅРєСЂРµРјРµРЅС‚ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРєР° РїРѕРґСЂСЏРґ Р»РѕСЃСЃРѕРІ
            self.consecutive_losses = self.consecutive_losses.saturating_add(1);
            self.last_loss_ts = Some(chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis());
        }
    }
}

impl Strategy for MomentumStrategy {
    fn name(&self) -> &str {
        "momentum"
    }

    fn notify_position_open(&mut self) {
        self.filter.on_position_opened();
    }

    // P16: sizing base follows live equity
    fn set_live_equity(&mut self, eq: f64) { self.balance = eq; }

    fn notify_position_closed(&mut self, pnl: f64) {
        self.filter.on_position_closed(self.last_kline_ts);
        self.record_trade_result(pnl);
    }

    fn in_position(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self.filter.state(), PositionState::InPosition)
    }

    fn diagnostics(&self) -> Option<String> {
        let total = self.blocked_by_atr
            + self.blocked_by_momentum
            + self.blocked_by_ofi
            + self.blocked_by_volume
            + self.blocked_by_regime
            + self.blocked_by_time
            + self.blocked_by_limit_day
            + self.blocked_by_cooldown
            + self.blocked_by_net_rr
            + self.blocked_by_edge
            + self.blocked_by_supertrend
            + self.blocked_by_ema200
            + self.blocked_by_mfi
            + self.blocked_by_vol_spike
            + self.blocked_by_loss_streak
            + self.n_spread_block
            + self.passed_filters;

        Some(format!(
            "Momentum(candles) diag v3 | candles={} total={} | vol={} atr={} mom={} ofi={} regime={} time={} lim={} cd={} rr={} spr={} edge={} st={} ema={} mfi={} vspk={} lstreak={} passed={} | min_tp={:.4}% fee_rt={:.4}% lb={} atr_p={} ofi_win={} regime_range={:.3}% last_atr={:.4} rr_edgeв‰Ґ{:.2} trades={}/{} | st_dir={} ema200={} cons_loss={}",
            self.total_closed_candles,
            total,
            self.blocked_by_volume,
            self.blocked_by_atr,
            self.blocked_by_momentum,
            self.blocked_by_ofi,
            self.blocked_by_regime,
            self.blocked_by_time,
            self.blocked_by_limit_day,
            self.blocked_by_cooldown,
            self.blocked_by_net_rr,
            self.n_spread_block,
            self.blocked_by_edge,
            self.blocked_by_supertrend,
            self.blocked_by_ema200,
            self.blocked_by_mfi,
            self.blocked_by_vol_spike,
            self.blocked_by_loss_streak,
            self.passed_filters,
            self.min_tp_pct() * 100.0,
            2.0 * self.fee_rate * 100.0,
            self.lookback,
            self.atr_period,
            self.ofi_window,
            self.regime_detector.last_range_pct(),
            self.current_atr(),
            self.min_net_rr * EDGE_QUALITY_MULT,
            self.trades_today,
            self.max_trades_per_day,
            if self.supertrend_direction == 1 { "UP" } else { "DOWN" },
            self.ema200_value.map(|v| format!("{:.4}", v)).unwrap_or("n/a".to_string()),
            self.consecutive_losses,
        ))
    }

    fn process(&mut self, msg: &WebSocketMessage) -> Result<Option<Signal>> {
        // вњ… P23: Р»РѕРІРёРј Р¶РёРІРѕР№ СЃРїСЂРµРґ РёР· bookTicker (guard РІС…РѕРґР° + СЃС‚РѕРёРјРѕСЃС‚СЊ СЃРїСЂРµРґР° РІ net_rr)
        if let WebSocketMessage::BookTicker(b) = msg {
            if b.symbol.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&self.symbol)
                && b.best_bid > 0.0
                && b.best_ask > b.best_bid
            {
                self.last_bid = b.best_bid;
                self.last_ask = b.best_ask;
            }
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let kline = match msg {
            WebSocketMessage::Kline(k) => k,
            _ => return Ok(None),
        };
        if !kline.symbol.eq_ignore_ascii_case(&self.symbol) {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if !kline.is_closed {
            return Ok(None);
        }

        self.last_kline_ts = kline.timestamp;
        self.total_closed_candles += 1;

        let regime = self.regime_detector.detect_from_msg(msg);

        let day = kline.timestamp / 86_400_000;
        if day != self.current_day {
            self.current_day = day;
            self.trades_today = 0;
        }

        // вњ… Рџ3: РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЏ РЅР°РєР°РїР»РёРІР°РµС‚СЃСЏ РІСЃРµРіРґР° вЂ” РґРѕ РєСѓР»РґР°СѓРЅ-С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂР°
        self.closes.push_back(kline.close);
        self.highs.push_back(kline.high);
        self.lows.push_back(kline.low);
        self.volumes.push_back(kline.volume);
        self.taker_buys.push_back(kline.taker_buy_volume);

        let max_hist = self
            .lookback
            .max(self.atr_period * 2)
            .max(self.ofi_window)
            .max(EMA200_PERIOD)
            .max(120)
            + 10;
        while self.closes.len() > max_hist {
            self.closes.pop_front();
            self.highs.pop_front();
            self.lows.pop_front();
            self.volumes.pop_front();
            self.taker_buys.pop_front();
        }

        self.update_atr();
        // рџ†• РћР±РЅРѕРІР»СЏРµРј РёРЅРґРёРєР°С‚РѕСЂС‹ С‚СЂРµРЅРґР°
        self.update_ema200(kline.close);
        self.update_supertrend();

        if kline.is_backfill {
            return Ok(None);
        }

        if !self.filter.can_signal(kline.close, kline.timestamp) {
            return Ok(None);
        }

        if self.closes.len() < self.lookback.max(self.atr_period) {
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // ====================================================================
        // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 6: Cooldown РїРѕСЃР»Рµ 2 РїРѕРґСЂСЏРґ Р»РѕСЃСЃРѕРІ
        // Р•СЃР»Рё 2+ Р»РѕСЃСЃР° РїРѕРґСЂСЏРґ в†’ РїР°СѓР·Р° 4С‡
        // ====================================================================
        if self.enable_loss_cooldown
            && self.consecutive_losses >= LOSS_STREAK_THRESHOLD
        {
            if let Some(last_ts) = self.last_loss_ts {
                let now = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis();
                if now - last_ts < LOSS_COOLDOWN_MS {
                    self.blocked_by_loss_streak += 1;
                    return Ok(None);
                }
            }
        }

        // ====================================================================
        // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 5: Volume spike REJECTION
        // Р•СЃР»Рё volume С‚РµРєСѓС‰РµР№ СЃРІРµС‡Рё > 3Г— avg(20) в†’ СЌС‚Рѕ РёРјРїСѓР»СЊСЃ, РЅРµ СЂР°Р·РІРѕСЂРѕС‚.
        // Р’С…РѕРґРёС‚СЊ РѕРїР°СЃРЅРѕ вЂ” С†РµРЅР° СЃРєРѕСЂРµРµ РїСЂРѕРґРѕР»Р¶РёС‚ РґРІРёР¶РµРЅРёРµ, С‡РµРј СЂР°Р·РІРµСЂРЅС‘С‚СЃСЏ.
        // ====================================================================
        if self.enable_vol_spike_rejection && self.is_volume_spike() {
            self.blocked_by_vol_spike += 1;
            tracing::debug!(
                "рџЋЇ [{}] vol-spike rejection: С‚РµРєСѓС‰РёР№ volume {:.0} > {:.1}Г— avg вЂ” РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°СЋ РёРјРїСѓР»СЊСЃ",
                self.symbol, self.volumes.back().unwrap_or(&0.0), VOL_SPIKE_REJECTION_MULT
            );
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // --- Volume anomaly (СЃС‚Р°СЂС‹Р№ С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂ: vol < threshold в†’ РЅРµ С‚РѕСЂРіСѓРµРј) ---
        let vol_anom = self.volume_anomaly();
        if vol_anom < self.volume_anomaly_threshold {
            self.blocked_by_volume += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // --- ATR% ---
        let atr = self.current_atr();
        let atr_pct = atr / kline.close;
        let min_atr_pct = self.min_atr_threshold_pct / 100.0;
        if atr_pct < min_atr_pct {
            self.blocked_by_atr += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // --- OFI ---
        let ofi = self.calculate_ofi();
        if ofi.abs() < self.ofi_threshold {
            self.blocked_by_ofi += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // --- Momentum ---
        let momentum = self.calculate_momentum();
        if momentum.abs() < 0.0005 {
            self.blocked_by_momentum += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if (ofi > 0.0) != (momentum > 0.0) {
            self.blocked_by_momentum += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // ====================================================================
        // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 3: MFI С„РёР»СЊС‚СЂ Р±РѕРєРѕРІРёРєР°
        // Р’ СЂРµР¶РёРјРµ Ranging (РЅРµ Trending) вЂ” РЅРµ РІС…РѕРґРёС‚СЊ РїСЂРё MFI > 80 РёР»Рё < 20
        // (РїРµСЂРµРєСѓРїР»РµРЅРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ/РїРµСЂРµРїСЂРѕРґР°РЅРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ РІ Р±РѕРєРѕРІРёРєРµ = Р»РѕР¶РЅС‹Рµ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»С‹)
        // ====================================================================
        if self.enable_mfi && regime != MarketRegime::Trending {
            if let Some(mfi) = self.calculate_mfi() {
                if mfi > MFI_OVERBOUGHT || mfi < MFI_OVERSOLD {
                    self.blocked_by_mfi += 1;
                    return Ok(None);
                }
            }
        }

        // ====================================================================
        // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 1+2: Supertrend + EMA200 вЂ” BINARY trend filter
        // LONG С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё close > EMA200 Р Supertrend UP
        // SHORT С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё close < EMA200 Р Supertrend DOWN
        // Р­С‚Рѕ СѓР±РёСЂР°РµС‚ РєРѕРЅС‚СЂ-С‚СЂРµРЅРґ РІС…РѕРґС‹ (5 РёР· 6 loss'РѕРІ РїРѕ Р°СѓРґРёС‚Сѓ)
        // ====================================================================
        let st_up = self.supertrend_is_up();
        let above_ema = self.above_ema200();

        // РћРїСЂРµРґРµР»СЏРµРј Р РђР—Р Р•РЁРЃРќРќРћР• РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ
        let allowed_long = if self.enable_ema200 && self.enable_supertrend {
            above_ema && st_up
        } else if self.enable_ema200 {
            above_ema
        } else if self.enable_supertrend {
            st_up
        } else {
            true
        };
        let allowed_short = if self.enable_ema200 && self.enable_supertrend {
            !above_ema && !st_up
        } else if self.enable_ema200 {
            !above_ema
        } else if self.enable_supertrend {
            !st_up
        } else {
            true
        };

        // ====================================================================
        // рџ†• РџРђРўР§ 4: ROC РґР»СЏ РґР°РјРїРѕРІ вЂ” reversal LONG РЅР° РїР°РЅРёРєРµ
        // Р•СЃР»Рё ROC < ROC_DUMP_THRESHOLD в†’ Р°РЅРѕРјР°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РґР°РјРї. Momentum-down
        // РІ СЌС‚РѕР№ СЃРёС‚СѓР°С†РёРё = capitulation. РњРѕР¶РЅРѕ СЂРёСЃРєРЅСѓС‚СЊ LONG reversal.
        // (Р­С‚Р° РІРµС‚РєР° РћРџР¦РРћРќРђР›Р¬РќРђ вЂ” РІРєР»СЋС‡Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РµСЃР»Рё momentum>0
        //  Рё РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёРµ matches allowed_long.)
        // ====================================================================
        let roc = self.calculate_roc();
        let is_dump = roc.map(|r| r < ROC_DUMP_THRESHOLD).unwrap_or(false);

        // РџСЂРѕРІРµСЂРєР° РЅР°РїСЂР°РІР»РµРЅРёСЏ СЃРёРіРЅР°Р»Р°
        let is_long = momentum > 0.0;

        // Р•СЃР»Рё momentum long, РЅРѕ РЅРµ allowed_long в†’ Р±Р»РѕРє
        if is_long && !allowed_long {
            self.blocked_by_ema200 += 1;
            tracing::debug!(
                "рџЋЇ [{}] EMA200/ST block LONG: above_ema={} st_up={} вЂ” РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°СЋ РєРѕРЅС‚СЂ-С‚СЂРµРЅРґ",
                self.symbol, above_ema, st_up
            );
            return Ok(None);
        }
        // Р•СЃР»Рё momentum short, РЅРѕ РЅРµ allowed_short в†’ Р±Р»РѕРє
        if !is_long && !allowed_short {
            self.blocked_by_ema200 += 1;
            tracing::debug!(
                "рџЋЇ [{}] EMA200/ST block SHORT: above_ema={} st_up={} вЂ” РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°СЋ РєРѕРЅС‚СЂ-С‚СЂРµРЅРґ",
                self.symbol, above_ema, st_up
            );
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // Р•СЃР»Рё Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ РґР°РјРї Рё РјС‹ РїС‹С‚Р°РµРјСЃСЏ С€РѕСЂС‚РёС‚СЊ вЂ” СЌС‚Рѕ bad entry (Р»РѕРІР»СЏ РЅРѕР¶РµР№)
        // Р’ СЌС‚РѕРј СЃР»СѓС‡Р°Рµ Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂСѓРµРј (ROC reversal РІС…РѕРґ Р±СѓРґРµС‚ РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅРѕ, РµСЃР»Рё Р±СѓРґРµС‚
        // РЅСѓР¶РµРЅ вЂ” РїРѕРєР° РїСЂРѕСЃС‚Рѕ reject short РЅР° РїР°РЅРёРєРµ)
        if is_dump && !is_long {
            self.blocked_by_vol_spike += 1; // РїРµСЂРµРёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµРј СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРє
            tracing::debug!(
                "рџЋЇ [{}] ROC dump ({:.2}%) + SHORT = Р»РѕРІР»СЏ РЅРѕР¶РµР№ вЂ” РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°СЋ",
                self.symbol, roc.unwrap_or(0.0) * 100.0
            );
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // --- Regime ---
        if regime != MarketRegime::Trending {
            self.blocked_by_regime += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // --- Time ---
        if !self.is_trading_hour(kline.timestamp) {
            self.blocked_by_time += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        // --- Limits ---
        if self.trades_today >= self.max_trades_per_day {
            self.blocked_by_limit_day += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if let Some(last_ts) = self.last_trade_ts_ms {
            if kline.timestamp - last_ts < self.min_time_between_trades_ms {
                self.blocked_by_cooldown += 1;
                return Ok(None);
            }
        }

        // --- TP/SL ---
        let min_tp = self.min_tp_pct();
        let tp_pct = (atr_pct * self.tp_atr_mult).max(min_tp).max(self.min_tp_pct).min(0.02);
        let sl_pct = tp_pct / self.rr_ratio;

        let (side, sl, tp) = if is_long {
            ("BUY", kline.close * (1.0 - sl_pct), kline.close * (1.0 + tp_pct))
        } else {
            ("SELL", kline.close * (1.0 + sl_pct), kline.close * (1.0 - tp_pct))
        };

        let entry = round_to_tick(kline.close, BTC_TICK_SIZE);
        let sl = round_to_tick(sl, BTC_TICK_SIZE);
        let tp = round_to_tick(tp, BTC_TICK_SIZE);

        // вњ… P23: slippage-guard вЂ” СЃРїСЂРµРґ С€РёСЂРµ Р»РёРјРёС‚Р° = РІС…РѕРґ Р·Р°РїСЂРµС‰С‘РЅ
        if self.spread_too_wide() {
            return Ok(None);
        }
        let eff_rr = match self.net_rr(entry, sl, tp, is_long) {
            Some(rr) => rr,
            None => {
                self.blocked_by_net_rr += 1;
                return Ok(None);
            }
        };

        // --- вњ… РџР РђР’РљРђ 3: Р”Р’РЈРҐРџРћР РћР“РћР’Р«Р™ Р¤РР›Р¬РўР  РљРђР§Р•РЎРўР’Рђ ---
        if eff_rr + 1e-4 < self.min_net_rr {
            self.blocked_by_net_rr += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }
        if eff_rr < self.min_net_rr * EDGE_QUALITY_MULT {
            tracing::debug!(
                "рџЋЇ [{}] edge-РѕС‚СЃРµРІ: eff_rr={:.3} < min {:.3}Г—{:.2}={:.3} вЂ” РїРѕРіСЂР°РЅРёС‡РЅС‹Р№ РІС…РѕРґ РїСЂРѕРїСѓС‰РµРЅ",
                self.symbol, eff_rr, self.min_net_rr, EDGE_QUALITY_MULT,
                self.min_net_rr * EDGE_QUALITY_MULT
            );
            self.blocked_by_edge += 1;
            return Ok(None);
        }

        let total_trades = self.trades_won + self.trades_lost;
        if total_trades >= 20 {
            let wr = self.trades_won as f64 / total_trades as f64;
            let avg_win = if self.trades_won > 0 {
                self.total_win_amount / self.trades_won as f64
            } else {
                0.0
            };
            let avg_loss = if self.trades_lost > 0 {
                self.total_loss_amount / self.trades_lost as f64
            } else {
                0.0
            };
            if avg_loss > 0.0 {
                let ev = self.expected_value(wr, avg_win, avg_loss);
                let ev_pct = ev / avg_loss * 100.0;
                if ev_pct < self.min_ev_pct {
                    return Ok(None);
                }
            }
        }

        let raw_qty = calc_position_size_with_fees(
            self.balance,
            entry,
            sl,
            self.risk_per_trade_pct,
            self.fee_rate * 2.0,
        );
        let quantity = floor_to_step(raw_qty, BTC_QTY_STEP);
        if quantity <= 0.0 {
            return Ok(None);
        }

        self.passed_filters += 1;
        self.trades_today += 1;
        self.last_trade_ts_ms = Some(kline.timestamp);
        self.filter.on_signal(entry, kline.timestamp);

        tracing::info!(
            "рџЋЇ [{}] v3 signal: {} qty={:.5} @ {:.4} SL={:.4} TP={:.4} | ST={} EMA200={} ROC={:.2}% MFI={} vol_anom={:.2}x",
            self.symbol, side, quantity, entry, sl, tp,
            if st_up { "UP" } else { "DOWN" },
            if above_ema { "ABOVE" } else { "BELOW" },
            roc.map(|r| r * 100.0).unwrap_or(0.0),
            self.calculate_mfi().map(|m| format!("{:.0}", m)).unwrap_or("n/a".into()),
            vol_anom,
        );

        Ok(Some(Signal {
            symbol: kline.symbol.clone(),
            side: side.to_string(),
            price: entry,
            quantity,
            strategy_name: self.name().to_string(),
            leverage: 1.0,
            stop_loss: Some(sl),
            take_profit: Some(tp),
            atr_hint: atr,
            entry_spread: 0.0,
        }))
    }
}
=== C:\Users\BASE_PC\scalping_agentsGLM\src\strategies\orderbook_analysis.rs ===
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct OrderBook {
    pub bids: Vec<(f64, f64)>,
    pub asks: Vec<(f64, f64)>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Density {
    pub price: f64,
    pub volume: f64,
}

pub struct OrderBookAnalyzer {
    pub density_threshold: f64,
}

impl OrderBookAnalyzer {
    pub fn new(threshold: f64) -> Self {
        Self { density_threshold: threshold }
    }

    pub fn analyze_densities(&self, ob: &OrderBook) -> Vec<Density> {
        ob.bids
            .iter()
            .chain(ob.asks.iter())
            .filter(|(_, v)| *v > self.density_threshold)
            .map(|(p, v)| Density { price: *p, volume: *v })
            .collect()
    }

    pub fn nearest_density(&self, ob: &OrderBook, current_price: f64) -> Option<Density> {
        self.analyze_densities(ob)
            .into_iter()
            .min_by(|a, b| {
                (a.price - current_price).abs().partial_cmp(&(b.price - current_price).abs()).unwrap()
            })
    }
}

//! [P11:kill] Kill-switch via KILL file in the bot working directory.
//! File exists -> new entries are rejected (exits and position management
//! keep working). Delete the file to resume. Cheap: one stat call per check.
pub fn kill_switch_active() -> bool {
    std::path::Path::new("KILL").exists()
}

/// вљ пёЏ РЎР°Р№Р·РёРЅРі Р‘Р•Р— СѓС‡С‘С‚Р° РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёР№.
/// РћСЃС‚Р°С‘С‚СЃСЏ РґР»СЏ РѕС‚РєР»СЋС‡С‘РЅРЅС‹С… СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёР№ (breakout/bounce/knife/density).
/// Р”Р»СЏ Р°РєС‚РёРІРЅС‹С… СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёР№ РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓР№ `calc_position_size_with_fees`.
pub fn calc_position_size(
    balance: f64,
    entry_price: f64,
    stop_loss: f64,
    risk_pct: f64,
) -> f64 {
    let risk_amount = balance * risk_pct / 100.0;
    let stop_dist = (entry_price - stop_loss).abs();
    if stop_dist < 1e-9 || risk_amount <= 0.0 {
        return 0.0;
    }
    risk_amount / stop_dist
}

/// вњ… 4Рі: СЃР°Р№Р·РёРЅРі СЃ СѓС‡С‘С‚РѕРј round-trip РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёРё.
///
/// РҐСѓРґС€РёР№ СЃР»СѓС‡Р°Р№ (РІС‹С…РѕРґ РїРѕ SL) СЃС‚РѕРёС‚ РЅР° РµРґРёРЅРёС†Сѓ:
/// СЂР°СЃСЃС‚РѕСЏРЅРёРµ РґРѕ СЃС‚РѕРїР° + РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёРё Р·Р° РєСЂСѓРі (fee_rt Г— С†РµРЅР°).
/// fee_rt вЂ” round-trip Р”РћР›Р•Р™: futures taker в‰€ 0.001, maker в‰€ 0.0004, spot taker в‰€ 0.0015.
pub fn calc_position_size_with_fees(
    balance: f64,
    entry_price: f64,
    stop_loss: f64,
    risk_pct: f64,
    fee_rt: f64,
) -> f64 {
    let risk_amount = balance * risk_pct / 100.0;
    let stop_dist = (entry_price - stop_loss).abs();
    let fee_per_unit = fee_rt * entry_price;
    let denom = stop_dist + fee_per_unit;
    // вњ… P24 (T-01): guard РІС‹СЂРѕР¶РґРµРЅРЅРѕРіРѕ СЃС‚РѕРїР°. SL==entry -> stop_dist=0 Рё
    // Р·РЅР°РјРµРЅР°С‚РµР»СЊ = С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёСЏ: 1000 U Г— 0.5% / (0.001Г—100) = 50 РµРґ.
    // Guard РІРѕСЃСЃС‚Р°РЅРѕРІР»РµРЅ РёР· calc_position_size; РІС‹Р·С‹РІР°СЋС‰РёРµ РЅРµ РјРµРЅСЏСЋС‚СЃСЏ.
    if stop_dist < 1e-9 || denom < 1e-12 || risk_amount <= 0.0 {
        return 0.0;
    }
    risk_amount / denom
}

/// РћРєСЂСѓРіР»РµРЅРёРµ С†РµРЅС‹ РґРѕ tick_size вЂ” Binance РѕС‚РІРµСЂРіР°РµС‚ С†РµРЅСѓ, РЅРµРєСЂР°С‚РЅСѓСЋ С€Р°РіСѓ.
pub fn round_to_tick(price: f64, tick_size: f64) -> f64 {
    if tick_size <= 0.0 {
        return price;
    }
    (price / tick_size).round() * tick_size
}

/// РћРєСЂСѓРіР»РµРЅРёРµ РѕР±СЉС‘РјР° Р’РќРР— РґРѕ step_size.
pub fn floor_to_step(quantity: f64, step_size: f64) -> f64 {
    if step_size <= 0.0 {
        return quantity;
    }
    (quantity / step_size).floor() * step_size
}

/// РћРєСЂСѓРіР»РµРЅРёРµ РґРѕ N Р·РЅР°РєРѕРІ С‡РµСЂРµР· СЃС‚СЂРѕРєСѓ вЂ” СѓР±РёСЂР°РµС‚ float-noise.
pub fn round_to_decimals(value: f64, decimals: u32) -> f64 {
    let formatted = format!("{:.*}", decimals as usize, value);
    formatted.parse::<f64>().unwrap_or(value)
}

/// РћРєСЂСѓРіР»РµРЅРёРµ РІРЅРёР· РґРѕ N Р·РЅР°РєРѕРІ
pub fn floor_decimals(value: f64, decimals: u32) -> f64 {
    let factor = 10f64.powi(decimals as i32);
    (value * factor).floor() / factor
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn test_calc_position_size() {
        let qty = calc_position_size(200.0, 77000.0, 76700.0, 0.5);
        assert!((qty - 0.003333).abs() < 1e-5);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_calc_position_size_with_fees() {
        // 1000 USDT, СЂРёСЃРє 0.5% = 5 USDT; РІС…РѕРґ 100, SL 99 в†’ РґРёСЃС‚Р°РЅС†РёСЏ 1.0
        // round-trip fee 0.1% в†’ РµС‰С‘ 0.1 РЅР° РµРґРёРЅРёС†Сѓ в†’ Р·РЅР°РјРµРЅР°С‚РµР»СЊ 1.1
        let qty = calc_position_size_with_fees(1000.0, 100.0, 99.0, 0.5, 0.001);
        assert!((qty - 5.0 / 1.1).abs() < 1e-9);
        // СЃ РєРѕРјРёСЃСЃРёСЏРјРё РѕР±СЉС‘Рј РІСЃРµРіРґР° РјРµРЅСЊС€Рµ
        assert!(qty < calc_position_size(1000.0, 100.0, 99.0, 0.5));
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_round_to_tick() {
        assert_eq!(round_to_tick(77207.987, 0.01), 77207.99);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_floor_to_step() {
        assert!((floor_to_step(0.003339, 0.00001) - 0.00333).abs() < 1e-9);
    }

    // вњ… P24 (T-01): СЂРµРіСЂРµСЃСЃ-С‚РµСЃС‚ guard РІС‹СЂРѕР¶РґРµРЅРЅРѕРіРѕ СЃС‚РѕРїР°.
    // Р”Рѕ С„РёРєСЃР°: 1000 U, СЂРёСЃРє 0.5% -> qty = 5 / (0.001Г—100) = 50 РµРґ. (!)
    #[test]
    fn test_t01_guard_sl_equals_entry() {
        assert_eq!(calc_position_size_with_fees(1000.0, 100.0, 100.0, 0.5, 0.001), 0.0);
        // РѕР±СЂР°С‚РЅС‹Р№ СЃР»СѓС‡Р°Р№ вЂ” РїРѕС‡С‚Рё РЅСѓР»РµРІРѕР№, РЅРѕ РІР°Р»РёРґРЅС‹Р№ СЃС‚РѕРї РЅРµ Р±Р»РѕРєРёСЂСѓРµС‚СЃСЏ
        let q = calc_position_size_with_fees(1000.0, 100.0, 99.999999999, 0.5, 0.001);
        assert!(q > 0.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_sizing_zero_risk_and_tick() {
        assert_eq!(calc_position_size_with_fees(1000.0, 100.0, 99.0, 0.0, 0.001), 0.0);
        assert_eq!(calc_position_size_with_fees(0.0, 100.0, 99.0, 0.5, 0.001), 0.0);
        // tick/step <= 0 вЂ” С„СѓРЅРєС†РёРё РЅРµ Р»РѕРјР°СЋС‚СЃСЏ
        assert_eq!(round_to_tick(123.456, 0.0), 123.456);
        assert_eq!(floor_to_step(123.456, -1.0), 123.456);
    }

    // вњ… P24: РєР°РЅРѕРЅРёС‡РµСЃРєРёРµ РєРµР№СЃС‹ РѕРєСЂСѓРіР»РµРЅРёР№ (СЂРµС„РµСЂРµРЅСЃ РѕС‚С‡С‘С‚Р°, С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ 1e-9)
    #[test]
    fn test_round_to_tick_half_away() {
        // 12.375 / 0.25 = 49.5 -> half-away-from-zero -> 50 -> 12.5 (РЅР°Р№РґРєР° T-02)
        assert!((round_to_tick(12.375, 0.25) - 12.5).abs() < 1e-9);
        // С€Р°Рі Р±РѕР»СЊС€Рµ С†РµРЅС‹
        assert_eq!(round_to_tick(3.0, 10.0), 0.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_round_to_decimals_reference() {
        // РћР¶РёРґР°РЅРёСЏ РІРµСЂРёС„РёС†РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅС‹ Python-СЂРµС„РµСЂРµРЅСЃРѕРј (f64 IEEE-754, РѕРєСЂСѓРіР»РµРЅРёРµ
        // С‚РѕС‡РЅРѕРіРѕ Р±РёРЅР°СЂРЅРѕРіРѕ Р·РЅР°С‡РµРЅРёСЏ): f64(0.12345) С…СЂР°РЅРёС‚СЃСЏ Р’Р«РЁР• СЃРµСЂРµРґРёРЅС‹
        // 0.12345000000000000417... -> 0.1235; f64(0.12344) РЅРёР¶Рµ -> 0.1234.
        assert!((round_to_decimals(0.12345, 4) - 0.1235).abs() < 1e-12);
        assert!((round_to_decimals(0.12344, 4) - 0.1234).abs() < 1e-12);
        assert!((round_to_decimals(1.00005, 4) - 1.0001).abs() < 1e-12);
        assert!((floor_decimals(1.23456, 3) - 1.234).abs() < 1e-9);
        assert!((floor_decimals(-1.23456, 3) - (-1.235)).abs() < 1e-9); // floor РІРЅРёР·
    }

    #[test]
    fn test_floor_to_step_reference() {
        assert!((floor_to_step(0.1, 0.03) - 0.09).abs() < 1e-9);
        assert!(floor_to_step(0.001, 0.01) >= 0.0);
    }
}

//! вњ… B2: РіР»РѕР±Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ rate-limiter REST-Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃРѕРІ.
//! Р“Р»РѕР±Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ СЃС‚Р°С‚РёРє (РєР°Рє РјРµС‚СЂРёРєРё) вЂ” РґРѕСЃС‚СѓРїРµРЅ РѕС‚РѕРІСЃСЋРґСѓ, РЅРёС‡РµРіРѕ
//! РЅРµ РЅСѓР¶РЅРѕ РїСЂРѕС‚СЏРіРёРІР°С‚СЊ С‡РµСЂРµР· СЃРёРіРЅР°С‚СѓСЂС‹.

use once_cell::sync::Lazy;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU64, Ordering};
use tokio::sync::Mutex;

const WINDOW_MS: u64 = 60_000;
/// РљРѕРЅСЃРµСЂРІР°С‚РёРІРЅС‹Р№ Р±СЋРґР¶РµС‚: 1200 req/РјРёРЅ (РїРѕР»РѕРІРёРЅР° Р»РёРјРёС‚Р° Binance 2400/РјРёРЅ)
const BUDGET: u64 = 1200;
const WARN_THRESHOLD: u64 = (BUDGET * 80) / 100;
const HARD_THRESHOLD: u64 = (BUDGET * 95) / 100;

/// Р“Р»РѕР±Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ Р»РёРјРёС‚РµСЂ вЂ” РѕРґРёРЅ РЅР° РїСЂРѕС†РµСЃСЃ.
pub static LIMITER: Lazy<RateLimiter> = Lazy::new(RateLimiter::new);

#[derive(Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum Acquire {
    /// РЁР»С‘Рј Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ
    Allow,
    /// РџРµСЂРµРіСЂСѓР·РєР° вЂ” non-РєСЂРёС‚РёС‡РЅС‹Р№ РѕС‚РїСЂР°РІРёС‚РµР»СЊ РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃРєР°РµС‚ С‚РёРє
    Throttle,
}

pub struct RateLimiter {
    window_start_ms: Mutex<u64>,
    counter: AtomicU64,
    throttled: AtomicU64,
}

impl RateLimiter {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            window_start_ms: Mutex::new(0),
            counter: AtomicU64::new(0),
            throttled: AtomicU64::new(0),
        }
    }

    /// Р—Р°СЃС‡РёС‚Р°С‚СЊ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ. `critical = true` вЂ” РѕСЂРґРµСЂР°/РѕС‚РјРµРЅС‹/СЃС‚Р°С‚СѓСЃС‹
    /// (РІСЃРµРіРґР° С€Р»С‘Рј, РґР°Р¶Рµ Сѓ РїРѕС‚РѕР»РєР° вЂ” Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚РёРµ РїРѕР·РёС†РёРё РІР°Р¶РЅРµРµ Р»РёРјРёС‚Р°);
    /// `critical = false` вЂ” РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂС‹ Рё РїСЂРѕС‡РёР№ С„РѕРЅ (РјРѕР¶РµРј РїСЂРѕРїСѓСЃС‚РёС‚СЊ С‚РёРє).
    pub async fn acquire(&self, critical: bool) -> Acquire {
        let now = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis().max(0) as u64;
        let mut start = self.window_start_ms.lock().await;
        if *start == 0 || now.saturating_sub(*start) >= WINDOW_MS {
            *start = now;
            self.counter.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
        }
        let n = self.counter.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed) + 1;

        if n >= HARD_THRESHOLD {
            self.throttled.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            if critical {
                tracing::error!(
                    "в›” RATE: {} req/РѕРєРЅРѕ (РїРѕС‚РѕР»РѕРє {}) вЂ” РєСЂРёС‚РёС‡РµСЃРєРёР№ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ РїСЂРѕРїСѓС‰РµРЅ",
                    n, HARD_THRESHOLD
                );
                Acquire::Allow
            } else {
                tracing::warn!("вЏё RATE: {} req/РѕРєРЅРѕ вЂ” С„РѕРЅ РѕС‚Р»РѕР¶РµРЅ", n);
                Acquire::Throttle
            }
        } else if n == WARN_THRESHOLD {
            tracing::warn!("вљ пёЏ RATE: {} req/РѕРєРЅРѕ (80% Р±СЋРґР¶РµС‚Р°)", n);
            Acquire::Allow
        } else {
            Acquire::Allow
        }
    }
}
=== C:\Users\BASE_PC\scalping_agentsGLM\src\utils\retry.rs ===
use anyhow::Result;
use std::future::Future;
use std::time::Duration;

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct RetryConfig {
    pub max_attempts: u32,
    pub initial_delay: Duration,
    pub max_delay: Duration,
    pub multiplier: f64,
}

impl Default for RetryConfig {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            max_attempts: 3,
            initial_delay: Duration::from_millis(500),
            max_delay: Duration::from_secs(30),
            multiplier: 2.0,
        }
    }
}

/// Exponential backoff СЃ РѕРіСЂР°РЅРёС‡РµРЅРёРµРј.
///
/// РџСЂРёРјРµСЂ:
/// ```ignore
/// let res = retry_with_backoff(RetryConfig::default(), || async {
///     client.place_market_order(symbol, side, qty).await
/// }).await?;
/// ```
pub async fn retry_with_backoff<F, Fut, T>(
    cfg: RetryConfig,
    mut op: F,
) -> Result<T>
where
    F: FnMut() -> Fut,
    Fut: Future<Output = Result<T>>,
{
    let mut delay = cfg.initial_delay;
    let mut last_err = None;

    for attempt in 1..=cfg.max_attempts {
        match op().await {
            Ok(v) => return Ok(v),
            Err(e) => {
                tracing::warn!(
                    "retry attempt {}/{} failed: {}",
                    attempt, cfg.max_attempts, e
                );
                last_err = Some(e);

                if attempt == cfg.max_attempts {
                    break;
                }

                tokio::time::sleep(delay).await;
                let next = Duration::from_secs_f64(delay.as_secs_f64() * cfg.multiplier);
                delay = next.min(cfg.max_delay);
            }
        }
    }

    Err(last_err.unwrap_or_else(|| anyhow::anyhow!("retry failed")))
}
