//! [P11:tg] Telegram alerts: closed trades (trade_journal) + suspension
//! transitions (risk_stats). Enabled via env TG_BOT_TOKEN + TG_CHAT_ID;
//! if either is missing the module is a silent no-op (one info line).
use serde_json::json;
use sqlx::sqlite::SqlitePool;
use sqlx::Row;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::time::Duration;

fn creds() -> Option<(String, String)> {
    let t = std::env::var("TG_BOT_TOKEN").unwrap_or_default();
    let c = std::env::var("TG_CHAT_ID").unwrap_or_default();
    if t.is_empty() || c.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    Some((t, c))
}

/// Fire-and-forget text message (never panics, never blocks the caller).
pub async fn notify(event: &str, detail: &str) {
    let (token, chat) = match creds() {
        Some(x) => x,
        None => return,
    };
    let url = format!("https://api.telegram.org/bot{}/sendMessage", token);
    let client = reqwest::Client::builder()
        .timeout(Duration::from_secs(8))
        .pool_idle_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(20)).tcp_keepalive(std::time::Duration::from_secs(15))/* P19-POOL */.build()
        .unwrap_or_default();
    let body = json!({
        "chat_id": chat,
        "text": format!("[scalping_agents] {}\n{}", event, detail),
    });
    let _ = client.post(&url).json(&body).send().await;
}

/// [P11:tg] Background poller: CLOSE events from journal db (30s cycle),
/// suspension transitions from per-bot risk_stats. History is silenced:
/// last_close_id is initialized from the latest id at startup.
pub fn spawn_poller(bots: Vec<(String, String)>, journal_db: String) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        if creds().is_none() {
            tracing::info!("Telegram: TG_BOT_TOKEN/TG_CHAT_ID not set - alerts disabled");
            return;
        }
        tracing::info!("Telegram: alerts ON (closes + suspensions, 30s poll)");
        let mut pools: HashMap<String, SqlitePool> = HashMap::new();
        let mut last_close_id: i64 = -1;
        let mut last_susp: HashMap<String, bool> = HashMap::new();
        loop {
            // 1) journal CLOSE events
            if let Some(pool) = pool_for(&mut pools, &journal_db, true).await {
                if let Ok(rows) = sqlx::query(
                    "SELECT id, bot_name, symbol, side, price, pnl FROM trade_journal \
                     WHERE event = 'CLOSE' ORDER BY id ASC",
                )
                .fetch_all(&pool)
                .await
                {
                    for r in rows {
                        let id: i64 = r.try_get("id").unwrap_or(0);
                        if last_close_id < 0 {
                            last_close_id = id; // skip history on startup
                            continue;
                        }
                        if id <= last_close_id {
                            continue;
                        }
                        last_close_id = id;
                        let bot: String = r.try_get("bot_name").unwrap_or_default();
                        let sym: String = r.try_get("symbol").unwrap_or_default();
                        let side: String = r.try_get("side").unwrap_or_default();
                        let price: f64 = r.try_get("price").unwrap_or(0.0);
                        let pnl: Option<f64> = r.try_get("pnl").ok().flatten();
                        let pnl_s = match pnl {
                            Some(p) => format!("{:+.2} USDT", p),
                            None => "n/a".to_string(),
                        };
                        notify(
                            "Trade closed",
                            &format!("{} {} {} @ {:.6}\nPnL: {}", bot, side, sym, price, pnl_s),
                        )
                        .await;
                    }
                }
            }
            // 2) suspension transitions per bot
            for (name, db) in &bots {
                let Some(pool) = pool_for(&mut pools, db, false).await else {
                    continue;
                };
                if let Ok(Some(row)) = sqlx::query(
                    "SELECT suspended, suspend_reason FROM risk_stats WHERE bot_name = ?",
                )
                .bind(name)
                .fetch_optional(&pool)
                .await
                {
                    let susp: i64 = row.try_get("suspended").unwrap_or(0);
                    let now = susp != 0;
                    let prev = last_susp.get(name).copied().unwrap_or(false);
                    if now && !prev {
                        let reason: String = row.try_get("suspend_reason").unwrap_or_default();
                        let why = if reason.is_empty() {
                            "reason n/a"
                        } else {
                            reason.as_str()
                        };
                        notify("Bot SUSPENDED", &format!("{}: {}", name, why)).await;
                    }
                    last_susp.insert(name.clone(), now);
                }
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_secs(30)).await;
        }
    });
}

async fn pool_for(
    pools: &mut HashMap<String, SqlitePool>,
    db: &str,
    create: bool,
) -> Option<SqlitePool> {
    if let Some(p) = pools.get(db) {
        return Some(p.clone());
    }
    let mode = if create { "rwc" } else { "rw" };
    let url = format!("sqlite:{}?mode={}", db, mode);
    match SqlitePool::connect(&url).await {
        Ok(p) => {
            pools.insert(db.to_string(), p.clone());
            Some(p)
        }
        Err(_) => None,
    }
}

// вњ… P27: РіР»РѕР±Р°Р»СЊРЅС‹Р№ РїСѓР»СЊСЃ bookTicker РїРѕ Р’РЎР•Рњ С…Р°Р±Р°Рј (WsHealth::book_tickers вЂ”
// per-hub; СЌС‚РѕС‚ СЃСѓРјРјР°СЂРЅС‹Р№ вЂ” РґР»СЏ heartbeat-СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё РІ main.rs Рё РІРЅРµС€РЅРµРіРѕ РјРѕРЅРёС‚РѕСЂРёРЅРіР°).
pub static BT_TOTAL: AtomicU64 = AtomicU64::new(0);

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct WsHealth {
    pub received: AtomicU64,
    pub parsed: AtomicU64,
    pub trades: AtomicU64,
    pub book_tickers: AtomicU64,
    pub klines: AtomicU64,
    /// рџ”§ FIX: СЃРІРµС‡Рё, РѕРїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ REST-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂРѕРј С‡РµСЂРµР· hub.publish()
    /// (РґР»СЏ futures вЂ” РћРЎРќРћР’РќРћР™ РїРѕС‚РѕРє СЃРІРµС‡РµР№, WS РёС… РЅРµ РѕС‚РґР°С‘С‚).
    pub rest_klines: AtomicU64,
    /// вњ… DIAG v3.4.1: РїСѓР±Р»РёРєР°С†РёРё РїСЂРё РЅСѓР»РµРІС‹С… РїСЂРёС‘РјРЅРёРєР°С… (РїРѕС‚РµСЂСЏ backfill)
    pub publish_fails: AtomicU64,
    pub order_books: AtomicU64,
    pub reconnects: AtomicU64,
    pub total_silence_seconds: AtomicU64,
    pub latency_sum_ms: AtomicU64,
    pub latency_count: AtomicU64,
}

impl WsHealth {
    pub fn new() -> Arc<Self> {
        Arc::new(Self::default())
    }

    pub fn parse_rate(&self) -> f64 {
        let r = self.received.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
        if r == 0 {
            return 0.0;
        }
        self.parsed.load(Ordering::Relaxed) as f64 / r as f64
    }

    pub fn avg_latency_ms(&self) -> f64 {
        let c = self.latency_count.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
        if c == 0 {
            return 0.0;
        }
        self.latency_sum_ms.load(Ordering::Relaxed) as f64 / c as f64
    }
}

pub struct WebSocketHub {
    tx: broadcast::Sender<WebSocketMessage>,
    health: Arc<WsHealth>,
    /// вњ… v3.5: РєСЌС€ РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРёС… REST-СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ (СЃС‚СЂР°С…РѕРІРєР° РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµРІР°).
    /// Broadcast С‚РµСЂСЏРµС‚ СЃРѕРѕР±С‰РµРЅРёСЏ, РѕРїСѓР±Р»РёРєРѕРІР°РЅРЅС‹Рµ РїСЂРё РЅСѓР»РµРІС‹С… РїСЂРёС‘РјРЅРёРєР°С…
    /// (СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚ Р±РѕС‚Р° Р·Р°РґРµСЂР¶Р°РЅ reconciliation-СЂРµС‚СЂР°СЏРјРё / СЂРµСЃС‚Р°СЂС‚ СЃСѓРїРµСЂРІРёР·РѕСЂР°).
    /// РљСЌС€ СЃС‚СЂРѕРіРѕ РІРѕР·СЂР°СЃС‚Р°РµС‚ РїРѕ timestamp вЂ” РґСѓР±Р»Рё РЅРµ РїСЂРѕС…РѕРґСЏС‚.
    kline_cache: Arc<std::sync::Mutex<std::collections::VecDeque<KlineMessage>>>,
    _handle: JoinHandle<()>,
}

/// вњ… v3.5: РіР»СѓР±РёРЅР° РєСЌС€Р° СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ С…Р°Р±Р° (250 backfill Г— 2 + Р·Р°РїР°СЃ Р¶РёРІС‹С… СЃРІРµС‡РµР№)
const KLINE_CACHE_CAP: usize = 600;

impl WebSocketHub {
    pub async fn new(ws_base_url: &str, symbol: &str) -> Result<Arc<Self>> {
        let sym = symbol.to_lowercase();

        let is_futures = ws_base_url.contains("fstream")
            || ws_base_url.contains("futures")
            || ws_base_url.contains("binancefuture");

        // вњ… 3Рі: fstream РёР· РЅР°С€РµРіРѕ СЂРµРіРёРѕРЅР° РѕС‚РґР°С‘С‚ С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ bookTicker (РїСЂРѕР±РЅРёРє, 2 СЃРµС‚Рё).
        // РЎРІРµС‡Рё вЂ” С‡РµСЂРµР· REST-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂ.
        let streams = if is_futures {
            format!("{s}@bookTicker", s = sym)
        } else {
            format!(
                "{s}@aggTrade/{s}@bookTicker/{s}@depth20@100ms/{s}@kline_1m",
                s = sym
            )
        };

        tracing::info!(
            "рџ“Ў WebSocket Hub | {} | {}",
            if is_futures { "FUTURES" } else { "SPOT" },
            streams
        );

        // v3.7b FIX: Bybit v5 WS does NOT support Binance combined-stream URLs.
        // ws_base_url already holds the correct path (/v5/public/linear);
        // subscription is sent as JSON-op right after connect (see connect_and_process).
        
        // вњ… P25: С‚РёРїРёР·РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅР°СЏ Р±РёСЂР¶Р° РІРјРµСЃС‚Рѕ to_lowercase().contains(...)
        let ex = Exchange::from_url(ws_base_url);
        let is_bybit = ex == Exchange::Bybit;
        let is_okx = ex == Exchange::Okx;
        let mut endpoints: Vec<String> = if is_bybit || is_okx {
            vec![ws_base_url.trim_end_matches('/').to_string()]
        } else {
            vec![format!(
                "{}/stream?streams={}",
                ws_base_url.trim_end_matches('/'),
                streams
            )]
        };

        // рџ”§ РџРђРўР§-FALLBACK (23.10, Р¶РёРІРѕР№ Р»РѕРі): Binance-fallback'Рё РёРјРµСЋС‚ СЃРјС‹СЃР»
        // РўРћР›Р¬РљРћ РґР»СЏ Binance-РїРµСЂРІРёС‡РЅС‹С… С…Р°Р±РѕРІ. Р”Р»СЏ Bybit-СЃРёРјРІРѕР»РѕРІ (XAUUSDT Рё
        // С‚.Рї.) С‚Р°РєРѕРіРѕ СЃРёРјРІРѕР»Р° РЅР° Binance РќР•Рў вЂ” В«backupВ» РїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЃСЏ (HTTP 101,
        // PONG С…РѕРґРёС‚), РЅРѕ РґР°РЅРЅС‹С… РЅРµ РѕС‚РґР°СЃС‚: WS РІС‹РіР»СЏРґРёС‚ Р¶РёРІС‹Рј, СЂС‹РЅРѕРє РјРѕР»С‡РёС‚ вЂ”
        // РјР°СЃРєРёСЂРѕРІРєР° СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅРѕР№ РїСЂРѕР±Р»РµРјС‹ РІРјРµСЃС‚Рѕ С‡РµСЃС‚РЅРѕРіРѕ РїРµСЂРµРїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёСЏ.
        if !is_futures && ex != Exchange::Bybit { // вњ… P25: СЃРµРјР°РЅС‚РёРєР° РїСЂРµР¶РЅСЏСЏ
            for &ep in WS_ENDPOINTS {
                let full = format!("{}/stream?streams={}", ep.trim_end_matches('/'), streams);
                if !endpoints.contains(&full) {
                    endpoints.push(full);
                }
            }
        }

        let (tx, _rx) = broadcast::channel::<WebSocketMessage>(5000);
        let tx_clone = tx.clone();

        // v3.7b: Bybit JSON-op subscription, sent after EVERY (re)connect.
        // kline/orderbook intentionally NOT subscribed: 1m candles in the shared
        // stream could confuse strategies expecting 4h candles from the REST
        // poller; orderbook needs local snapshot+delta merge (v3.8). Live price
        // for DCA comes from tickers (best bid/ask).
        let subscribe_msg: Option<String> = if is_bybit {
            Some(format!(
                "{{\"op\":\"subscribe\",\"args\":[\"tickers.{s}\",\"publicTrade.{s}\"]}}",
                s = symbol.to_uppercase()
            ))
        } else if is_okx {
            let inst = okx_inst_id(&symbol);
            Some(format!(
                "{{\"op\":\"subscribe\",\"args\":[{{\"channel\":\"tickers\",\"instId\":\"{i}\"}},{{\"channel\":\"trades\",\"instId\":\"{i}\"}}]}}",
                i = inst
            ))
        } else {
            None
        };
        let health = WsHealth::new();
        let health_for_task = health.clone();
        let kline_cache = Arc::new(std::sync::Mutex::new(
            std::collections::VecDeque::with_capacity(KLINE_CACHE_CAP + 40),
        ));

        for (i, ep) in endpoints.iter().enumerate() {
            tracing::info!("   [{}] {}", i, ep);
        }

        spawn_daily_report(health.clone());

        let handle = tokio::spawn(async move {
            let mut endpoint_idx: usize = 0;
            let mut backoff_sec = 1u64;
            let mut consecutive_failures: u32 = 0;
            let mut last_switch = Instant::now();

            loop {
                let url = &endpoints[endpoint_idx];

                // ============================================================
                // рџ”§ РџРђРўР§-BACKOFF: connect_and_process РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ РІСЂРµРјСЏ Р¶РёР·РЅРё
                // СЃРµСЃСЃРёРё. Р”РѕР»РіР°СЏ (в‰Ґ WS_SESSION_OK_SECS) вЂ” СѓСЃРїРµС…: backoff РІ 1СЃ,
                // СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРє СЃР±РѕРµРІ РІ РЅРѕР»СЊ в†’ РїРѕСЃР»Рµ RST РїРµСЂРµРїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёРµ РјРіРЅРѕРІРµРЅРЅРѕРµ.
                // РљРѕСЂРѕС‚РєР°СЏ вЂ” СЃС‡РёС‚Р°РµС‚СЃСЏ СЃР±РѕРµРј (С‚РѕС‚ Р¶Рµ РјРµС…Р°РЅРёР·Рј, С‡С‚Рѕ Рё Err,
                // РІРєР»СЋС‡Р°СЏ РїРµСЂРµРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёРµ endpoint'РѕРІ РґР»СЏ СЃРїРѕС‚Р°).
                // ============================================================
                match connect_and_process(
                    url,
                    ex, // вњ… P25: Р±РёСЂР¶Р° РѕРїСЂРµРґРµР»РµРЅР° РѕРґРёРЅ СЂР°Р· РІ WebSocketHub::new
                    subscribe_msg.as_deref(),
                    tx_clone.clone(),
                    health_for_task.clone(),
                )
                .await
                {
                    Ok(lifetime_secs) => {
                        if lifetime_secs >= WS_SESSION_OK_SECS {
                            backoff_sec = 1;
                            consecutive_failures = 0;
                            last_switch = Instant::now();
                            tracing::info!(
                                "рџ”Њ WS-СЃРµСЃСЃРёСЏ Р·Р°РІРµСЂС€РµРЅР° С‡РёСЃС‚Рѕ (РїСЂРѕР¶РёР»Р° {}СЃ) вЂ” backoff СЃР±СЂРѕС€РµРЅ",
                                lifetime_secs
                            );
                        } else {
                            health_for_task.reconnects.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                            metrics::WS_RECONNECTS.with_label_values(&["global"]).inc();
                            consecutive_failures += 1;

                            // рџ”‡ V3.2 С€СѓРј-РіРµР№С‚: РїРµСЂРІС‹Рµ 3 СЃР±РѕСЏ Р»РѕРіРёСЂСѓРµРј РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№,
                            // РґР°Р»СЊС€Рµ РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ 10-Р№ (С‚РµСЃС‚РЅРµС‚ РјРѕР¶РµС‚ СЂРІР°С‚СЊ СЃРµСЃСЃРёРё
                            // РєР°Р¶РґС‹Рµ ~60СЃ вЂ” РёРЅР°С‡Рµ Р»РѕРі СЂР°Р·Р±СѓС…Р°РµС‚).
                            if consecutive_failures <= 3 || consecutive_failures % 10 == 0 {
                                tracing::warn!(
                                    "рџ”Њ WS-СЃРµСЃСЃРёСЏ РєРѕСЂРѕС‚РєР°: {}СЃ (СЃР±РѕР№ {}/{}) | РїРµСЂРµРїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёРµ С‡РµСЂРµР· {} СЃРµРє",
                                    lifetime_secs,
                                    consecutive_failures,
                                    RECONNECT_THRESHOLD_FOR_SWITCH,
                                    backoff_sec
                                );
                            }

                            let should_switch =
                                consecutive_failures >= RECONNECT_THRESHOLD_FOR_SWITCH
                                    && endpoints.len() > 1
                                    && last_switch.elapsed() > Duration::from_secs(60);

                            if should_switch {
                                endpoint_idx = (endpoint_idx + 1) % endpoints.len();
                                consecutive_failures = 0;
                                last_switch = Instant::now();
                                backoff_sec = 1;
                                tracing::warn!(
                                    "рџ”Ђ РџРµСЂРµРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёРµ РЅР° backup endpoint [{}]: {}",
                                    endpoint_idx,
                                    endpoints[endpoint_idx]
                                );
                            } else {
                                sleep(Duration::from_secs(backoff_sec)).await;
                                backoff_sec = (backoff_sec * 2).min(30);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Err(e) => {
                        health_for_task.reconnects.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                        metrics::WS_RECONNECTS.with_label_values(&["global"]).inc();
                        consecutive_failures += 1;

                        // рџ”‡ V3.2 С€СѓРј-РіРµР№С‚: РїРµСЂРІС‹Рµ 3 С„РµР№Р»Р° Р»РѕРіРёСЂСѓРµРј РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№,
                        // РґР°Р»СЊС€Рµ РєР°Р¶РґС‹Р№ 10-Р№. Bybit testnet РјРѕР¶РµС‚ РѕС‚РґР°РІР°С‚СЊ
                        // 504 С‡Р°СЃР°РјРё вЂ” РёРЅР°С‡Рµ 2 СЃС‚СЂРѕРєРё РјСѓСЃРѕСЂР° РІ РјРёРЅСѓС‚Сѓ.
                        if consecutive_failures <= 3 || consecutive_failures % 10 == 0 {
                            tracing::error!(
                                "рџ”Њ РћС€РёР±РєР° WS #{} (endpoint {}): {} | РїРµСЂРµРїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёРµ С‡РµСЂРµР· {} СЃРµРє",
                                consecutive_failures,
                                endpoint_idx,
                                e,
                                backoff_sec
                            );
                        }

                        let should_switch = consecutive_failures >= RECONNECT_THRESHOLD_FOR_SWITCH
                            && endpoints.len() > 1
                            && last_switch.elapsed() > Duration::from_secs(60);

                        if should_switch {
                            endpoint_idx = (endpoint_idx + 1) % endpoints.len();
                            consecutive_failures = 0;
                            last_switch = Instant::now();
                            backoff_sec = 1;
                            tracing::warn!(
                                "рџ”Ђ РџРµСЂРµРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёРµ РЅР° backup endpoint [{}]: {}",
                                endpoint_idx,
                                endpoints[endpoint_idx]
                            );
                        } else {
                            sleep(Duration::from_secs(backoff_sec)).await;
                            backoff_sec = (backoff_sec * 2).min(30);
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        });

        Ok(Arc::new(Self {
            tx,
            health,
            kline_cache,
            _handle: handle,
        }))
    }

    pub fn subscribe(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<WebSocketMessage> {
        self.tx.subscribe()
    }

    /// вњ… v3.5: РїРѕСЃР»РµРґРЅРёРµ REST-СЃРІРµС‡Рё РёР· РєСЌС€Р° С…Р°Р±Р° вЂ” РґР»СЏ РіР°СЂР°РЅС‚РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРЅРѕРіРѕ
    /// РїСЂРѕРіСЂРµРІР° СЃС‚СЂР°С‚РµРіРёРё РјРёРЅСѓСЏ broadcast (СЃРј. main.rs, Р±Р»РѕРє v3.5).
    pub fn recent_klines(&self) -> Vec<KlineMessage> {
        self.kline_cache
            .lock()
            .unwrap()
            .iter()
            .cloned()
            .collect()
    }

    pub fn health(&self) -> Arc<WsHealth> {
        self.health.clone()
    }

    /// вњ… 3Рі: РёРЅСЉРµРєС†РёСЏ СЃРѕРѕР±С‰РµРЅРёСЏ РІ РєР°РЅР°Р» С…Р°Р±Р° (РґР»СЏ REST-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂР° СЃРІРµС‡РµР№)
    pub fn publish(&self, msg: WebSocketMessage) {
        // рџ”§ FIX: REST-СЃРІРµС‡Рё СЃС‡РёС‚Р°РµРј РѕС‚РґРµР»СЊРЅРѕ РѕС‚ WS-klines вЂ” РёРЅР°С‡Рµ heartbeat
        // РґР»СЏ futures РІРµС‡РЅРѕ РїРѕРєР°Р·С‹РІР°РµС‚ В«СЃРІРµС‡Рё=0В» (fstream kline РЅРµ РѕС‚РґР°С‘С‚).
        if let WebSocketMessage::Kline(k) = &msg {
            self.health.rest_klines.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            // вњ… v3.5: РїР°СЂР°Р»Р»РµР»СЊРЅРѕ РєР»Р°РґС‘Рј СЃРІРµС‡Сѓ РІ РєСЌС€ С…Р°Р±Р°. Р•СЃР»Рё РїСЂРёС‘РјРЅРёРєРѕРІ
            // РІ РјРѕРјРµРЅС‚ РїСѓР±Р»РёРєР°С†РёРё РЅРµС‚, send() РЅРёР¶Рµ РїРѕС‚РµСЂСЏРµС‚ СЃРѕРѕР±С‰РµРЅРёРµ вЂ”
            // РєСЌС€ РїРѕР·РІРѕР»РёС‚ РґРѕР±СЂР°С‚СЊ РёСЃС‚РѕСЂРёСЋ РїСЂРё СЃС‚Р°СЂС‚Рµ С†РёРєР»Р° Р±РѕС‚Р°.
            if let Ok(mut cache) = self.kline_cache.lock() {
                if cache.back().map_or(true, |last| k.timestamp > last.timestamp) {
                    cache.push_back(k.clone());
                    while cache.len() > KLINE_CACHE_CAP {
                        cache.pop_front();
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        // вњ… DIAG v3.4.1: СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ РѕС€РёР±РєР° send() РіР»РѕС‚Р°Р»Р°СЃСЊ вЂ” С‚РµРїРµСЂСЊ РІРёРґРЅРѕ,
        // РµСЃР»Рё СЃРІРµС‡Рё РїСѓР±Р»РёРєСѓСЋС‚СЃСЏ РїСЂРё РЅСѓР»РµРІС‹С… РїСЂРёС‘РјРЅРёРєР°С… (РїРѕС‚РµСЂСЏ backfill).
        if let Err(e) = self.tx.send(msg) {
            let n = self.health.publish_fails.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
            if n < 3 || n % 100 == 0 {
                tracing::warn!(
                    "hub.publish: РќР•Рў РїСЂРёС‘РјРЅРёРєРѕРІ вЂ” СЃРѕРѕР±С‰РµРЅРёРµ РїРѕС‚РµСЂСЏРЅРѕ (РїРѕС‚РµСЂСЏ #{})",
                    n + 1
                );
                let _ = e; // RecvError Р±РµР· РїРѕР»РµР·РЅРѕР№ РЅР°РіСЂСѓР·РєРё
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Р’РѕР·РІСЂР°С‰Р°РµС‚ Ok(СЃРµРєСѓРЅРґ Р¶РёР·РЅРё СЃРµСЃСЃРёРё). РџСЂРёС‡РёРЅР° СЂР°Р·СЂС‹РІР° СѓР¶Рµ Р·Р°Р»РѕРіРёСЂРѕРІР°РЅР°
/// РІРЅСѓС‚СЂРё (RST / Close / PONG-dead) вЂ” СЃСѓРїРµСЂРІРёР·РѕСЂСѓ РІР°Р¶РЅР° С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»РёС‚РµР»СЊРЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ.
async fn connect_and_process(
    url: &str,
    ex: Exchange, // вњ… P25
    subscribe_msg: Option<&str>,
    tx: broadcast::Sender<WebSocketMessage>,
    health: Arc<WsHealth>,
) -> Result<u64> {
    tracing::info!("РџРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡Р°СЋСЃСЊ Рє {}", url);

    let connect_result = timeout(
        Duration::from_secs(HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_SECS),
        connect_async(url),
    )
    .await;
    let ws = match connect_result {
        Ok(Ok((ws, response))) => {
            tracing::info!("вњ… WS РџРћР”РљР›Р®Р§РЃРќ | HTTP {}", response.status());
            ws
        }
        Ok(Err(e)) => return Err(anyhow!("WS handshake failed: {}", e)),
        Err(_) => {
            return Err(anyhow!(
                "WS handshake timeout ({}s)",
                HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_SECS
            ))
        }
    };

    // рџ”§ РџРђРўР§-BACKOFF: РІ scope Р¤РЈРќРљР¦РР (РЅРµ match-arm) вЂ” РЅСѓР¶РµРЅ РІ С‚РѕС‡РєРµ РІРѕР·РІСЂР°С‚Р°
    let started = Instant::now();

    let (write, mut read) = ws.split();
    let write = Arc::new(Mutex::new(write));

    // вњ… P25: Р±РёСЂР¶Р° РїСЂРёС…РѕРґРёС‚ РїР°СЂР°РјРµС‚СЂРѕРј (СЂР°РЅСЊС€Рµ вЂ” СЌРІСЂРёСЃС‚РёРєР° РїРѕ СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёРјРѕРјСѓ subscribe_msg).
    // РџР°СЂРёС‚РµС‚: subscribe_msg Р±С‹Р» Some С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ РґР»СЏ bybit/okx; okx-СЃРѕРѕР±С‰РµРЅРёРµ СЃРѕРґРµСЂР¶РёС‚ instId.
    let is_okx = ex == Exchange::Okx;
    let is_bybit = ex == Exchange::Bybit;
    // v3.7b: cache of last bid/ask for Bybit tickers. Bybit deltas carry only
    // changed fields - without this cache BookTicker would be choppy.
    let mut bt_cache = BybitTickerCache::default();

    if let Some(sub) = subscribe_msg {
        let mut guard = write.lock().await;
        if let Err(e) = guard.send(Message::Text(sub.to_string())).await {
            return Err(anyhow!("Bybit subscribe send failed: {}", e));
        }
        tracing::info!("[bybit-ws] subscription sent: {}", sub);
    }

    let mut ping_interval = tokio::time::interval(Duration::from_secs(20));
    ping_interval.tick().await;

    let mut heartbeat = tokio::time::interval(Duration::from_secs(60));
    heartbeat.tick().await;

    let mut last_msg_at = Instant::now();
    let mut last_pong_at = Instant::now();

    loop {
        tokio::select! {
            biased;

            msg = read.next() => {
                match msg {
                    Some(Ok(Message::Ping(payload))) => {
                        let mut guard = write.lock().await;
                        if let Err(e) = guard.send(Message::Pong(payload)).await {
                            tracing::error!("Pong send: {}", e);
                            break;
                        }
                    }
                    Some(Ok(Message::Pong(_))) => {
                        last_pong_at = Instant::now();
                    }
                    Some(Ok(Message::Text(text))) => {
                        health.received.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                        metrics::WS_MESSAGES_RECEIVED.with_label_values(&["global"]).inc();
                        last_msg_at = Instant::now();

                        // v3.7b: Bybit application pong counts as liveness.
                        if is_bybit && (text.contains("\"op\":\"pong\"") || text.contains("\"op\": \"pong\"")) {
                            last_pong_at = Instant::now();
                            continue;
                        }

                        let parsed_msg = if is_okx {
                            if text.trim().eq_ignore_ascii_case("pong") {
                                last_pong_at = Instant::now();
                                continue;
                            }
                            parse_okx_message(&text)
                        } else if is_bybit {
                            parse_bybit_message(&text, &mut bt_cache)
                        } else {
                            parse_message(&text)
                        };
                        if let Some(m) = parsed_msg {
                            health.parsed.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                            metrics::WS_MESSAGES_PARSED.with_label_values(&["global"]).inc();

                            match &m {
                                WebSocketMessage::Trade(_) => { health.trades.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed); }
                                WebSocketMessage::BookTicker(_) => { health.book_tickers.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed); BT_TOTAL.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed); }
                                WebSocketMessage::Kline(_) => { health.klines.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed); }
                                WebSocketMessage::OrderBook(_) => { health.order_books.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed); }
                            }

                            if let WebSocketMessage::Trade(ref t) = m {
                                let local_ts_ms = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis();
                                let raw_latency = (local_ts_ms - t.event_time).max(0) as u64;
                                if raw_latency < CLOCK_SKEW_THRESHOLD_MS {
                                    health.latency_sum_ms.fetch_add(raw_latency, Ordering::Relaxed);
                                    health.latency_count.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                                    metrics::WS_LATENCY_MS
                                        .with_label_values(&[ex.as_str()]) // вњ… P25: source = СЂРµР°Р»СЊРЅР°СЏ Р±РёСЂР¶Р° СЃРµСЃСЃРёРё
                                        .observe(raw_latency as f64);
                                }
                            }

                            let _ = tx.send(m);
                        }
                    }
                    Some(Ok(Message::Close(frame))) => {
                        tracing::warn!("РЎРµСЂРІРµСЂ Р·Р°РєСЂС‹Р»: {:?}", frame);
                        break;
                    }
                    Some(Err(e)) => {
                        tracing::error!("WS read: {}", e);
                        break;
                    }
                    None => {
                        tracing::warn!("WS РїРѕС‚РѕРє Р·Р°РєРѕРЅС‡РёР»СЃСЏ");
                        break;
                    }
                    _ => {}
                }
            }

            _ = ping_interval.tick() => {
                let mut guard = write.lock().await;
                // v3.7b: Bybit requires APPLICATION-level JSON ping (op:ping)
                // every 20s; protocol RFC-ping stays for Binance.
                let ping_frame = if is_okx {
                    Message::Text("ping".to_string())
                } else if is_bybit {
                    Message::Text("{\"op\":\"ping\"}".to_string())
                } else {
                    Message::Ping(vec![])
                };
                if let Err(e) = guard.send(ping_frame).await {
                    tracing::error!("Ping send: {}", e);
                    break;
                }
            }

            _ = heartbeat.tick() => {
                let rate = health.parse_rate();
                let silence_sec = last_msg_at.elapsed().as_secs();
                let pong_age_sec = last_pong_at.elapsed().as_secs();

                metrics::WS_PARSE_RATE.with_label_values(&["global"]).set(rate);
                metrics::WS_SILENCE_SECONDS.with_label_values(&["global"]).set(silence_sec as f64);

                if pong_age_sec > PONG_DEAD_THRESHOLD_SEC {
                    health.total_silence_seconds.fetch_add(pong_age_sec, Ordering::Relaxed);
                    metrics::WS_TOTAL_SILENCE_SECONDS
                        .with_label_values(&["global"])
                        .inc_by(pong_age_sec as f64);
                    tracing::error!(
                        "рџ’“ PONG РЅРµ РїСЂРёС…РѕРґРёР» {} СЃРµРє вЂ” СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёРµ РјРµСЂС‚РІРѕ, РїРµСЂРµРїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёРµ",
                        pong_age_sec
                    );
                    break;
                }

                if silence_sec > MARKET_DATA_SILENCE_LOG_SEC {
                    tracing::info!(
                        "рџ’“ РўРёС€РёРЅР° РІ market data {} СЃРµРє, РЅРѕ PONG Р±С‹Р» {} СЃРµРє РЅР°Р·Р°Рґ вЂ” СЃРѕРµРґРёРЅРµРЅРёРµ Р¶РёРІРѕ",
                        silence_sec,
                        pong_age_sec
                    );
                }

                // рџ”§ FIX: РґРѕР±Р°РІР»РµРЅ СЃС‡С‘С‚С‡РёРє СЃРІРµС‡Рё_REST вЂ” РїСѓР±Р»РёРєР°С†РёРё REST-РїРѕР»Р»РµСЂР°.
                // Р”Р»СЏ futures СЌС‚Рѕ РћРЎРќРћР’РќРћР™ РїРѕС‚РѕРє СЃРІРµС‡РµР№; В«СЃРІРµС‡Рё=0В» РїСЂРё СЂР°СЃС‚СѓС‰РµРј
                // СЃРІРµС‡Рё_REST вЂ” РЅРѕСЂРјР° (WS kline РЅРµ РѕС‚РґР°С‘С‚СЃСЏ), Р° РЅРµ РїРѕР»РѕРјРєР°.
                tracing::info!(
                    "рџ’“ heartbeat: РїРѕР»СѓС‡РµРЅРѕ={} СЂР°Р·РѕР±СЂР°РЅРѕ={} ({:.1}%) | С‚СЂРµР№РґС‹={} РєРЅРёРіРё={} СЃРІРµС‡Рё={} СЃРІРµС‡Рё_REST={} | С‚РёС€РёРЅР°={}СЃ | PONG_age={}СЃ | latencyв‰€{:.0}РјСЃ",
                    health.received.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
                    health.parsed.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
                    rate * 100.0,
                    health.trades.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
                    health.book_tickers.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
                    health.klines.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
                    health.rest_klines.load(Ordering::Relaxed),
                    silence_sec,
                    pong_age_sec,
                    health.avg_latency_ms()
                );

                if health.received.load(Ordering::Relaxed) > 100 && rate < 0.95 {
                    tracing::warn!("вљ пёЏ РќРёР·РєРёР№ parse rate: {:.1}%", rate * 100.0);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    Ok(started.elapsed().as_secs())
}

fn spawn_daily_report(health: Arc<WsHealth>) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut interval = tokio::time::interval(Duration::from_secs(24 * 3600));
        interval.tick().await;
        loop {
            interval.tick().await;

            let received = health.received.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
            let parsed = health.parsed.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
            let rate = if received > 0 { parsed as f64 / received as f64 * 100.0 } else { 0.0 };
            let reconnects = health.reconnects.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
            let silence = health.total_silence_seconds.load(Ordering::Relaxed);
            let latency = health.avg_latency_ms();
            let rest_klines = health.rest_klines.load(Ordering::Relaxed);

            tracing::info!("в•”в•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•—");
            tracing::info!("в•‘   рџ“Љ РЎРЈРўРћР§РќР«Р™ РћРўР§РЃРў WebSocket                 в•‘");
            tracing::info!("в• в•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•Ј");
            tracing::info!("в•‘  РџРѕР»СѓС‡РµРЅРѕ СЃРѕРѕР±С‰РµРЅРёР№: {:>15}          в•‘", received);
            tracing::info!("в•‘  Р Р°Р·РѕР±СЂР°РЅРѕ:          {:>15} ({:.2}%) в•‘", parsed, rate);
            tracing::info!("в•‘  РЎСЂРµРґРЅСЏСЏ Р»Р°С‚РµРЅС‚РЅРѕСЃС‚СЊ:{:>13.0} РјСЃ       в•‘", latency);
            tracing::info!("в•‘  РџРµСЂРµРїРѕРґРєР»СЋС‡РµРЅРёР№:    {:>15}          в•‘", reconnects);
            tracing::info!("в•‘  РћР±С‰РµРµ РІСЂРµРјСЏ С‚РёС€РёРЅС‹: {:>13} СЃ          в•‘", silence);
            tracing::info!("в•‘  РЎРІРµС‡РµР№ РѕС‚ REST:     {:>15}          в•‘", rest_klines);
            tracing::info!("в•љв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ђв•ќ");

            health.received.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
            health.parsed.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
            health.reconnects.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
            health.total_silence_seconds.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
            health.latency_sum_ms.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
            health.latency_count.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
            health.rest_klines.store(0, Ordering::Relaxed);
        }
    });
}

// v3.7b: cache of last best bid/ask for converting Bybit tickers
// (snapshot + delta) into full BookTickerMessage values.
#[derive(Default, Clone, Copy)]
struct BybitTickerCache {
    bid: f64,
    ask: f64,
    bid_qty: f64,
    ask_qty: f64,
}

// v3.7b: Bybit v5 envelope parser {"topic":...,"type":...,"ts":...,"data":...}.
// Handles exactly what we subscribe to: tickers.XAUUSDT (best bid/ask) and
// publicTrade.XAUUSDT (trade tape). Everything else is silently ignored.
fn parse_bybit_message(
    text: &str,
    bt_cache: &mut BybitTickerCache,
) -> Option<WebSocketMessage> {
    let root: Value = serde_json::from_str(text).ok()?;
    let topic = root.get("topic")?.as_str()?.to_string();
    let root_ts = root.get("ts").and_then(|v| v.as_i64()).unwrap_or(0);
    let data = root.get("data")?.clone();

    if topic.starts_with("tickers.") {
        // data is an object; delta carries only the changed fields.
        let get_f = |key: &str| -> Option<f64> {
            data.get(key)
                .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                .filter(|s| !s.is_empty())
                .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
        };
        if let Some(v) = get_f("bid1Price") {
            bt_cache.bid = v;
        }
        if let Some(v) = get_f("ask1Price") {
            bt_cache.ask = v;
        }
        if let Some(v) = get_f("bid1Size") {
            bt_cache.bid_qty = v;
        }
        if let Some(v) = get_f("ask1Size") {
            bt_cache.ask_qty = v;
        }
        if bt_cache.bid <= 0.0 || bt_cache.ask <= 0.0 {
            return None; // one side still unknown (wait for full snapshot)
        }
        let mut timestamp = data.get("timestamp").and_then(|v| v.as_i64()).unwrap_or(0);
        if timestamp <= 0 {
            timestamp = root_ts;
        }
        if timestamp <= 0 {
            timestamp = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis();
        }
        return Some(WebSocketMessage::BookTicker(BookTickerMessage {
            symbol: data
                .get("symbol")
                .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
                .unwrap_or("UNKNOWN")
                .to_string(),
            best_bid: bt_cache.bid,
            best_ask: bt_cache.ask,
            bid_qty: bt_cache.bid_qty,
            ask_qty: bt_cache.ask_qty,
            timestamp,
        }));
    }

    if topic.starts_with("publicTrade.") {
        // data is an array: [{"T":ms,"s":"XAUUSDT","S":"Buy|Sell","v":..,"p":..}]
        let arr = data.as_array()?;
        let t = arr.last()?; // most recent trade in the batch
        let price = t.get("p")?.as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
        let volume = t.get("v")?.as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
        let symbol = t.get("s")?.as_str()?.to_string();
        let mut timestamp = t.get("T").and_then(|v| v.as_i64()).unwrap_or(0);
        if timestamp <= 0 {
            timestamp = root_ts;
        }
        let event_time = if root_ts > 0 { root_ts } else { timestamp };
        // Bybit S="Buy" means aggressive buyer (taker buy) -> buyer is NOT maker.
        let is_buyer_maker = t.get("S").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) == Some("Sell");
        return Some(WebSocketMessage::Trade(TradeMessage {
            symbol,
            price,
            volume,
            timestamp,
            event_time,
            is_buyer_maker,
        }));
    }

    None
}

fn parse_message(text: &str) -> Option<WebSocketMessage> {
    let root: Value = serde_json::from_str(text).ok()?;

    let (stream, data) = if let Some(s) = root.get("stream").and_then(|s| s.as_str()) {
        (s.to_string(), root.get("data")?.clone())
    } else {
        (String::new(), root.clone())
    };

    if stream.contains("@aggTrade") {
        let symbol = data.get("s")?.as_str()?.to_string();
        let price = data.get("p")?.as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
        let volume = data.get("q")?.as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
        let timestamp = data.get("T")?.as_i64().unwrap_or(0);
        let event_time = data.get("E").and_then(|v| v.as_i64()).unwrap_or(timestamp);
        let is_buyer_maker = data.get("m").and_then(|m| m.as_bool()).unwrap_or(false);
        return Some(WebSocketMessage::Trade(TradeMessage {
            symbol, price, volume, timestamp, event_time, is_buyer_maker,
        }));
    }

    if stream.contains("@bookTicker") {
        let symbol = data.get("s")?.as_str()?.to_string();
        let best_bid = data.get("b")?.as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
        let best_ask = data.get("a")?.as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
        let bid_qty = data.get("B").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
        let ask_qty = data.get("A").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
        let timestamp = data
            .get("E")
            .and_then(|v| v.as_i64())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis());
        return Some(WebSocketMessage::BookTicker(BookTickerMessage {
            symbol, best_bid, best_ask, bid_qty, ask_qty, timestamp,
        }));
    }

    if stream.contains("@depth") {
        let symbol = data.get("s").and_then(|s| s.as_str()).unwrap_or("").to_string();
        let bids = parse_levels(data.get("bids")?)?;
        let asks = parse_levels(data.get("asks")?)?;
        return Some(WebSocketMessage::OrderBook(OrderBookMessage { symbol, bids, asks }));
    }

    if stream.contains("@kline") {
        let k = data.get("k")?;
        let symbol = k.get("s")?.as_str()?.to_string();
        let open = k.get("o")?.as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
        let high = k.get("h")?.as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
        let low = k.get("l")?.as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
        let close = k.get("c")?.as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
        let volume = k.get("v")?.as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
        // вњ… taker buy base asset volume вЂ” k.V
        let taker_buy_volume = k
            .get("V")
            .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
            .and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())
            .unwrap_or(0.0);
        // вњ… close_time вЂ” k.T
        let timestamp = k.get("T").and_then(|v| v.as_i64()).unwrap_or(0);
        let is_closed = k.get("x").and_then(|v| v.as_bool()).unwrap_or(false);
        return Some(WebSocketMessage::Kline(KlineMessage {
            symbol, open, high, low, close, volume, taker_buy_volume, timestamp, is_closed,
            is_backfill: false,
        }));
    }

    None
}

fn parse_levels(v: &Value) -> Option<Vec<(f64, f64)>> {
    Some(
        v.as_array()?
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|level| {
                let arr = level.as_array()?;
                let price = arr.first()?.as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
                let qty = arr.get(1)?.as_str()?.parse::<f64>().ok()?;
                Some((price, qty))
            })
            .collect(),
    )
}

pub struct WebSocketConnection;
pub async fn create_connection(
    _ws_base_url: &str,
    _symbol: &str,
    _bot_name: &str,
) -> Result<(tokio::sync::mpsc::Receiver<WebSocketMessage>, JoinHandle<()>)> {
    Err(anyhow!("create_connection СѓСЃС‚Р°СЂРµР» вЂ” РёСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓР№С‚Рµ WebSocketHub"))
}

// v4.0: OKX v5 envelope {"arg":{"channel":...,"instId":...},"data":[...]}.
// tickers = full snapshot (bidPx/askPx/bidSz/askSz); trades = tape.
fn parse_okx_message(text: &str) -> Option<WebSocketMessage> {
    let root: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(text).ok()?;
    let channel = root.get("arg")?.get("channel")?.as_str()?.to_string();
    let data = root.get("data")?.clone();

    if channel == "tickers" {
        let t = data.as_array()?.first()?;
        let bid = t.get("bidPx").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok());
        let ask = t.get("askPx").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok());
        let (bid, ask) = match (bid, ask) {
            (Some(b), Some(a)) if b > 0.0 && a > 0.0 => (b, a),
            _ => return None, // one side absent - wait for next snapshot
        };
        let bid_qty = t.get("bidSz").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
        let ask_qty = t.get("askSz").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0.0);
        let timestamp = t
            .get("ts")
            .and_then(|v| v.as_str())
            .and_then(|s| s.parse::<i64>().ok())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis());
        let symbol = okx_symbol_from_inst_id(t.get("instId").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or(""));
        return Some(WebSocketMessage::BookTicker(BookTickerMessage {
            symbol,
            best_bid: bid,
            best_ask: ask,
            bid_qty,
            ask_qty,
            timestamp,
        }));
    }

    if channel == "trades" {
        let t = data.as_array()?.last()?;
        let price = t.get("px").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())?;
        let volume = t.get("sz").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).and_then(|s| s.parse::<f64>().ok())?;
        let ts = t.get("ts").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).and_then(|s| s.parse::<i64>().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
        let symbol = okx_symbol_from_inst_id(t.get("instId").and_then(|v| v.as_str()).unwrap_or(""));
        let is_buyer_maker = t.get("side").and_then(|v| v.as_str()) == Some("sell");
        return Some(WebSocketMessage::Trade(TradeMessage {
            symbol,
            price,
            volume,
            timestamp: ts,
            event_time: ts,
            is_buyer_maker,
        }));
    }

    None
}

// v4.0: "XAU-USDT-SWAP" -> "XAUUSDT" (engine canonical symbol)
fn okx_symbol_from_inst_id(inst_id: &str) -> String {
    let s = inst_id.trim().to_uppercase();
    if let Some(base) = s.strip_suffix("-USDT-SWAP") {
        format!("{}USDT", base)
    } else if let Some(base) = s.strip_suffix("-SWAP") {
        base.to_string()
    } else {
        s
    }
}

// v4.0: "XAUUSDT" -> "XAU-USDT-SWAP"
fn okx_inst_id(symbol: &str) -> String {
    let s = symbol.trim().to_uppercase();
    if s.contains('-') {
        if s.ends_with("-SWAP") { s } else { format!("{}-SWAP", s) }
    } else if let Some(base) = s.strip_suffix("USDT") {
        format!("{}-USDT-SWAP", base)
    } else {
        s
    }
}

//! вњ… WebUI: РґР°С€Р±РѕСЂРґ + JSON API + Prometheus РЅР° РѕРґРЅРѕРј РїРѕСЂС‚Сѓ (9090).
//! v2: /api/klines (РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ prod/testnet), /api/resume (РєРѕРјР°РЅРґР° Р±РѕС‚Сѓ),
//! Chart.js Р·Р°С€РёС‚ РІ Р±РёРЅР°СЂРЅРёРє вЂ” РґР°С€Р±РѕСЂРґ СЂР°Р±РѕС‚Р°РµС‚ Р±РµР· РёРЅС‚РµСЂРЅРµС‚Р°.
//! v3: closes РґР»СЏ РіСЂР°С„РёРєРѕРІ, fees РІ РїРѕР·РёС†РёСЏС….
//! v4: вњ… Р‘Р°Р»Р°РЅСЃ вЂ” availableBalance USDT СЃ Р±РёСЂР¶Рё, РєРµС€ 30СЃ (РѕРґРёРЅ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ
//!     РЅР° Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚, РЅРµ РЅР° Р±РѕС‚Р° вЂ” РєР»СЋС‡Рё Сѓ С‚СЂС‘С… Р±РѕС‚РѕРІ РѕРґРЅРѕРіРѕ Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚Р°).
//! v5: вњ… /api/tvmarkers вЂ” Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚С‹Рµ СЃРґРµР»РєРё РґР»СЏ TradingView-РјР°СЂРєРµСЂРѕРІ.

use crate::metrics::REGISTRY;
use prometheus::Encoder;
use serde_json::json;
use sqlx::sqlite::SqlitePool;
use sqlx::Row;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use tokio::io::{AsyncReadExt, AsyncWriteExt};
use tokio::net::TcpListener;
use tokio::task::JoinHandle;

pub struct BotInfo {
    pub name: String,
    pub symbol: String,
    pub strategy: String,
    pub market: String,
    pub simulate: bool,
    pub interval: String,
    pub db_path: String,
    pub rest_url: String,
    // вњ… v4: РґР»СЏ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃР° Р±Р°Р»Р°РЅСЃР° (С‚РѕС‚ Р¶Рµ РїСЂРѕС†РµСЃСЃ, РєР»СЋС‡Рё СѓР¶Рµ РІ РєРѕРЅС„РёРіРµ вЂ”
    // СЂРёСЃРє РЅСѓР»РµРІРѕР№; РЅР°СЂСѓР¶Сѓ С‡РµСЂРµР· API РЅРµ РѕС‚РґР°СЋС‚СЃСЏ)
    pub api_key: String,
    pub api_secret: String,
}

struct Shared {
    bots: Vec<BotInfo>,
    journal_db: String,
    pools: Mutex<HashMap<String, SqlitePool>>,
    /// "market|SYMBOL|interval" в†’ (РјРѕРјРµРЅС‚ РєРµС€РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёСЏ, С‚РµР»Рѕ РѕС‚РІРµС‚Р°)
    klines_cache: Mutex<HashMap<String, (std::time::Instant, String)>>,
    /// вњ… v4: api_key в†’ (РјРѕРјРµРЅС‚, balance). 30СЃ вЂ” РѕРґРёРЅ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ РЅР° Р°РєРєР°СѓРЅС‚.
    balance_cache: Mutex<HashMap<String, (std::time::Instant, Option<f64>)>>,
}

// ============================================================
// AUDIT-P2: С‚РѕРєРµРЅ-РіРµР№С‚ СѓРїСЂР°РІР»СЏСЋС‰РёС… POST-РєРѕРјР°РЅРґ (/api/resume).
// WEBUI_TOKEN Р·Р°РґР°РЅ -> РєРѕРјР°РЅРґР° С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ СЃ РІРµСЂРЅС‹Рј ?token=...
// WEBUI_TOKEN РЅРµ Р·Р°РґР°РЅ -> СЂР°Р·СЂРµС€Р°РµРј, РЅРѕ РћР”РРќ СЂР°Р· РїСЂРµРґСѓРїСЂРµР¶РґР°РµРј РІ Р»РѕРі
// (РєРЅРѕРїРєР° Resume РІ РґР°С€Р±РѕСЂРґРµ С€Р»С‘С‚ Р·Р°РїСЂРѕСЃ Р±РµР· С‚РѕРєРµРЅР° вЂ” РЅРµ Р»РѕРјР°РµРј UX).
// Р’РєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ Р·Р°С‰РёС‚Сѓ: setx WEBUI_TOKEN <СЃРµРєСЂРµС‚>  (Р»Р°С‚РёРЅРёС†Р°/С†РёС„СЂС‹,
// Р±РµР· СЃРїРµС†СЃРёРјРІРѕР»РѕРІ вЂ” С‚РѕРєРµРЅ РёРґС‘С‚ РІ URL query), РїРѕС‚РѕРј РїРµСЂРµР·Р°РїСѓСЃРє Р±РѕС‚Р°.
// ============================================================
static RESUME_UNPROTECTED_WARNED: std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool =
    std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool::new(false);

fn webui_token_ok(query: &str) -> bool {
    use std::sync::atomic::Ordering;
    let expected = match std::env::var("WEBUI_TOKEN") {
        Ok(t) if !t.trim().is_empty() => t.trim().to_string(),
        _ => {
            if !RESUME_UNPROTECTED_WARNED.swap(true, Ordering::Relaxed) {
                tracing::warn!("WebUI: WEBUI_TOKEN РЅРµ Р·Р°РґР°РЅ вЂ” /api/resume Р‘Р•Р— Р·Р°С‰РёС‚С‹ (drive-by CSRF РІРѕР·РјРѕР¶РµРЅ). Р’РєР»СЋС‡РёС‚СЊ: setx WEBUI_TOKEN <СЃРµРєСЂРµС‚>");
            }
            return true;
        }
    };
    let given = query.split("&").find_map(|kv| {
        kv.split_once("=")
            .filter(|(k, _)| *k == "token")
            .map(|(_, v)| v.to_string())
    });
    match given {
        Some(g) if g == expected => true,
        _ => {
            tracing::warn!("WebUI: POST РѕС‚РєР»РѕРЅС‘РЅ вЂ” token РѕС‚СЃСѓС‚СЃС‚РІСѓРµС‚/РЅРµРІРµСЂРµРЅ (РѕР¶РёРґР°Р»СЃСЏ WEBUI_TOKEN)");
            false
        }
    }
}

pub fn spawn(bots: Vec<BotInfo>, journal_db: String) -> JoinHandle<()> {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let shared = Arc::new(Shared {
            bots,
            journal_db,
            pools: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()),
            klines_cache: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()),
            balance_cache: Mutex::new(HashMap::new()),
        });

        let listener = match TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:9090").await { // AUDIT-P2: С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ localhost
            Ok(l) => l,
            Err(e) => {
                tracing::error!("WebUI: РЅРµ СѓРґР°Р»РѕСЃСЊ Р·Р°РЅСЏС‚СЊ РїРѕСЂС‚ 9090: {}", e);
                return;
            }
        };
        tracing::info!("WebUI server on 127.0.0.1:9090 (AUDIT-P2: РґРѕСЃС‚СѓРї С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ СЃ СЌС‚РѕР№ РјР°С€РёРЅС‹)");

        loop {
            let (mut socket, _) = match listener.accept().await {
                Ok(x) => x,
                Err(e) => { tracing::error!("WebUI accept: {}", e); continue; }
            };
            let shared = shared.clone();
            tokio::spawn(async move {
                let mut buf = [0u8; 4096];
                let n = socket.read(&mut buf).await.unwrap_or(0);
                let req = String::from_utf8_lossy(&buf[..n]).to_string();

                let first_line = req.lines().next().unwrap_or("");
                let mut parts = first_line.split(' ');
                let method = parts.next().unwrap_or("GET").to_string();
                let raw_path = parts.next().unwrap_or("/").to_string();
                let (path, query) = match raw_path.split_once('?') {
                    Some((p, q)) => (p.to_string(), q.to_string()),
                    None => (raw_path, String::new()),
                };

                let (body, ctype) = if method == "POST" {
                    // AUDIT-P2: С‚РѕРєРµРЅ-РіРµР№С‚ СѓРїСЂР°РІР»СЏСЋС‰РёС… POST-РєРѕРјР°РЅРґ (drive-by CSRF:
                    // СЃС‚РѕСЂРѕРЅРЅСЏСЏ СЃС‚СЂР°РЅРёС†Р° РІ Р±СЂР°СѓР·РµСЂРµ РјРѕР¶РµС‚ POST-РёС‚СЊ РЅР° localhost:9090).
                    if !webui_token_ok(&query) {
                        (r#"{"ok":false,"error":"unauthorized: WEBUI_TOKEN"}"#.into(), "application/json")
                    } else {
                        match path.as_str() {
                            "/api/resume" => (resume_command(&shared, &query).await, "application/json"),
                            _ => (r#"{"ok":false,"error":"not found"}"#.into(), "application/json"),
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    match path.as_str() {
                        "/metrics" => {
                            let mut out = Vec::new();
                            let _ = REGISTRY.register(Box::new(prometheus::IntGaugeVec::clone(&crate::metrics::BOTS_ACTIVE)));
                            let encoder = prometheus::TextEncoder::new();
                            encoder.encode(&REGISTRY.gather(), &mut out).unwrap_or_default();
                            (String::from_utf8_lossy(&out).to_string(), "text/plain; version=0.0.4")
                        }
                        "/api/dashboard" => (dashboard_json(&shared).await, "application/json"),
                        "/api/tvmarkers" => (tv_markers_json(&shared).await, "application/json"),
                        "/api/klines" => (klines_json(&shared, &query).await, "application/json"),
                        "/prism" => (include_str!("prism.html").to_string(), "text/html; charset=utf-8"),
                        "/chart.umd.min.js" => {
                            (include_str!("chart.umd.min.js").to_string(), "application/javascript; charset=utf-8")
                        }
                        _ => (include_str!("dashboard.html").to_string(), "text/html; charset=utf-8"),
                    }
                };

                let head = format!(
                    "HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Type: {}\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nCache-Control: no-store\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n",
                    ctype,
                    body.len()
                );
                let _ = socket.write_all(head.as_bytes()).await;
                let _ = socket.write_all(body.as_bytes()).await;
                let _ = socket.shutdown().await;
            });
        }
    })
}

/// вњ… Р¤3: РєРѕРјР°РЅРґР° resume РґР»СЏ Р±РѕС‚Р°.
async fn resume_command(shared: &Shared, query: &str) -> String {
    let mut bot_name = String::new();
    for kv in query.split('&') {
        if let Some((k, v)) = kv.split_once('=') {
            if k == "bot" { bot_name = v.to_string(); }
        }
    }
    if bot_name.is_empty() {
        return r#"{"ok":false,"error":"bot required"}"#.into();
    }
    let Some(b) = shared.bots.iter().find(|b| b.name == bot_name) else {
        return r#"{"ok":false,"error":"unknown bot"}"#.into();
    };
    let url = format!("sqlite:{}?mode=rwc", b.db_path);
    let pool = match SqlitePool::connect(&url).await {
        Ok(p) => p,
        Err(e) => return format!(r#"{{"ok":false,"error":"db: {}"}}"#, e),
    };
    let _ = sqlx::query(
        "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS bot_commands (\
         id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, \
         command TEXT NOT NULL, \
         created_at TEXT NOT NULL, \
         consumed INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0)",
    )
    .execute(&pool)
    .await;
    match sqlx::query("INSERT INTO bot_commands (command, created_at) VALUES ('resume', ?)")
        .bind(chrono::Utc::now().to_rfc3339())
        .execute(&pool)
        .await
    {
        Ok(_) => r#"{"ok":true}"#.into(),
        Err(e) => format!(r#"{{"ok":false,"error":"{}"}}"#, e),
    }
}

// ============================================================
// вњ… v4: Р‘Р°Р»Р°РЅСЃ вЂ” availableBalance USDT СЃ С„СЊСЋС‡РµСЂСЃРЅРѕРіРѕ REST.
// РўР° Р¶Рµ РїРѕРґРїРёСЃСЊ HMAC-SHA256, С‡С‚Рѕ РІ PowerShell-СЃРІРµСЂРєР°С… Рё РєР»РёРµРЅС‚Р°С….
// None РїСЂРё Р»СЋР±РѕР№ РѕС€РёР±РєРµ в†’ РґР°С€Р±РѕСЂРґ РїРѕРєР°Р¶РµС‚ В«вЂ”В».
// ============================================================
async fn binance_futures_balance(api_key: &str, api_secret: &str, rest_url: &str) -> Option<f64> {
    use hmac::{Hmac, Mac};
    use sha2::Sha256;
    let ts = chrono::Utc::now().timestamp_millis();
    let query = format!("timestamp={}&recvWindow=5000", ts);
    let mut mac = Hmac::<Sha256>::new_from_slice(api_secret.as_bytes()).ok()?;
    mac.update(query.as_bytes());
    let sig: String = mac.finalize().into_bytes().iter().map(|b| format!("{:02x}", b)).collect();
    let url = format!("{}/fapi/v2/balance?{}&signature={}",
        rest_url.trim_end_matches('/'), query, sig);
    let client = reqwest::Client::builder()
        .timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(8)).pool_idle_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(20)).tcp_keepalive(std::time::Duration::from_secs(15))/* P19-POOL */.build().ok()?;
    let resp: serde_json::Value = client.get(&url)
        .header("X-MBX-APIKEY", api_key).send().await.ok()?
        .json().await.ok()?;
    resp.as_array()?
        .iter().find(|b| b["asset"].as_str() == Some("USDT"))?
        ["availableBalance"].as_str()?.parse().ok()
}

/// вњ… Р¤1: РїСЂРѕРєСЃРё СЃРІРµС‡РµР№ Binance Futures.
// ============================================================
// [OKX-MIG] OKX support: config probe, balance, klines proxy.
// Reads config.toml directly: exchange = "okx" + [okx] section
// (api_key / api_secret / passphrase). One OKX account for all
// bots - same idea as the shared Binance balance cache. When
// config.toml says binance (or [okx] absent) everything falls
// back to the original Binance behavior.
// ============================================================
fn read_okx_cfg() -> (bool, Option<(String, String, String)>) {
    let txt = std::fs::read_to_string("config.toml").unwrap_or_default();
    let mut exchange = String::new();
    let mut k = String::new();
    let mut s = String::new();
    let mut p = String::new();
    let mut in_okx = false;
    for line in txt.lines() {
        let t = line.trim();
        if t.is_empty() || t.starts_with('#') { continue; }
        if t.starts_with('[') { in_okx = t.to_lowercase().starts_with("[okx"); continue; }
        if let Some((key, raw)) = t.split_once('=') {
            let key = key.trim().to_lowercase();
            let raw = raw.trim();
            let val = if raw.starts_with('"') {
                raw[1..].split('"').next().unwrap_or("").to_string()
            } else {
                raw.split('#').next().unwrap_or("").trim().to_string()
            };
            if in_okx {
                match key.as_str() {
                    "api_key" | "key" => k = val,
                    "api_secret" | "secret" => s = val,
                    "passphrase" | "pass" => p = val,
                    _ => {}
                }
            } else if key == "exchange" && exchange.is_empty() {
                exchange = val;
            }
        }
    }
    let creds = if k.is_empty() || s.is_empty() || p.is_empty() { None } else { Some((k, s, p)) };
    (exchange == "okx", creds)
}

/// [OKX-MIG] minimal base64 encoder (keeps Cargo.toml dependency-free).
fn okx_b64(data: &[u8]) -> String {
    const T: &[u8; 64] = b"ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
    let mut out = String::new();
    for chunk in data.chunks(3) {
        let b0 = chunk[0] as u32;
        let b1 = *chunk.get(1).unwrap_or(&0) as u32;
        let b2 = *chunk.get(2).unwrap_or(&0) as u32;
        let n = (b0 << 16) | (b1 << 8) | b2;
        out.push(T[(n >> 18) as usize & 63] as char);
        out.push(T[(n >> 12) as usize & 63] as char);
        out.push(if chunk.len() > 1 { T[(n >> 6) as usize & 63] as char } else { '=' });
        out.push(if chunk.len() > 2 { T[n as usize & 63] as char } else { '=' });
    }
    out
}

/// [OKX-MIG] availableBalance USDT from OKX futures account.
/// Sign = base64(hmac_sha256(timestamp + "GET" + requestPath, secret)).
async fn okx_balance(api_key: &str, api_secret: &str, passphrase: &str) -> Option<f64> {
    use hmac::{Hmac, Mac};
    use sha2::Sha256;
    const BASE: &str = "https://www.okx.com";
    let ts = chrono::Utc::now().format("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%S%.3fZ").to_string();
    let path = "/api/v5/account/balance?ccy=USDT";
    let prehash = format!("{}GET{}", ts, path);
    let mut mac = Hmac::<Sha256>::new_from_slice(api_secret.as_bytes()).ok()?;
    mac.update(prehash.as_bytes());
    let sig = okx_b64(&mac.finalize().into_bytes());
    let client = reqwest::Client::builder()
        .timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(8)).pool_idle_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(20)).tcp_keepalive(std::time::Duration::from_secs(15))/* P19-POOL */.build().ok()?;
    let resp: serde_json::Value = client.get(&format!("{}{}", BASE, path))
        .header("OK-ACCESS-KEY", api_key)
        .header("OK-ACCESS-SIGN", sig)
        .header("OK-ACCESS-TIMESTAMP", ts)
        .header("OK-ACCESS-PASSPHRASE", passphrase)
        .header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        .send().await.ok()?.json().await.ok()?;
    if resp["code"].as_str() != Some("0") { return None; }
    let details = resp["data"][0]["details"].as_array()?;
    for d in details {
        if d["ccy"].as_str() == Some("USDT") {
            let v = d["availEq"].as_str().or_else(|| d["availBal"].as_str())?;
            return v.parse::<f64>().ok();
        }
    }
    None
}

/// [OKX-MIG] OKX candles converted to Binance kline format,
/// so dashboard.html needs no changes. Cache: same klines_cache.
async fn okx_klines(symbol: &str, interval: &str, shared: &Shared, cache_key: &str) -> String {
    use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
    let inst = match symbol.strip_suffix("USDT") {
        Some(s) => format!("{}-USDT-SWAP", s),
        None => return format!("{{\"error\":\"bad okx symbol: {}\"}}", symbol),
    };
    let bar = match interval {
        "1h" => "1H", "2h" => "2H", "4h" => "4H", "6h" => "6H",
        "12h" => "12H", "1d" => "1D", other => other,
    };
    let ms: i64 = match interval {
        "1m" => 60_000, "3m" => 180_000, "5m" => 300_000, "15m" => 900_000,
        "30m" => 1_800_000, "1h" => 3_600_000, "2h" => 7_200_000,
        "4h" => 14_400_000, "6h" => 21_600_000, "12h" => 43_200_000,
        "1d" => 86_400_000, _ => 900_000,
    };
    let url = format!("https://www.okx.com/api/v5/market/candles?instId={}&bar={}&limit=200", inst, bar);
    let client = reqwest::Client::builder()
        .timeout(Duration::from_secs(10)).pool_idle_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(20)).tcp_keepalive(std::time::Duration::from_secs(15))/* P19-POOL */.build().unwrap_or_default();
    match client.get(&url).send().await {
        Ok(resp) if resp.status().is_success() => match resp.json::<serde_json::Value>().await {
            Ok(v) if v["code"].as_str() == Some("0") => {
                let mut out: Vec<serde_json::Value> = Vec::new();
                if let Some(rows) = v["data"].as_array() {
                    for r in rows {
                        if let Some(a) = r.as_array() {
                            if a.len() < 8 { continue; }
                            let g = |i: usize| a[i].as_str().unwrap_or("0").to_string();
                            let ts0: i64 = a[0].as_str().unwrap_or("0").parse().unwrap_or(0);
                            // OKX row: [ts,o,h,l,c,vol(contracts),volCcy(base),volCcyQuote,confirm]
                            // Binance: [openTime,o,h,l,c,vol(base),closeTime,quoteVol,...]
                            out.push(json!([
                                ts0, g(1), g(2), g(3), g(4), g(6),
                                ts0 + ms - 1, g(7), 0, "0", "0", "0"
                            ]));
                        }
                    }
                }
                out.reverse();
                let body = serde_json::to_string(&out).unwrap_or_else(|_| "[]".to_string());
                shared.klines_cache.lock().unwrap()
                    .insert(cache_key.to_string(), (Instant::now(), body.clone()));
                body
            }
            Ok(v) => format!("{{\"error\":\"okx code: {}\"}}", v["code"].as_str().unwrap_or("?")),
            Err(_) => "{\"error\":\"okx body read failed\"}".to_string(),
        },
        _ => "{\"error\":\"okx upstream unavailable\"}".to_string(),
    }
}

async fn klines_json(shared: &Shared, query: &str) -> String {
    use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

    let mut symbol = String::new();
    let mut interval = "15m".to_string();
    let mut market = "prod".to_string();
    for kv in query.split('&') {
        if let Some((k, v)) = kv.split_once('=') {
            match k {
                "symbol" => symbol = v.to_uppercase(),
                "interval" => interval = v.to_string(),
                "market" => market = v.to_lowercase(),
                _ => {}
            }
        }
    }

    if symbol.is_empty() {
        return r#"{"error":"symbol required"}"#.into();
    }
    if !["1m","3m","5m","15m","30m","1h","2h","4h","6h","12h","1d"].contains(&interval.as_str()) {
        return r#"{"error":"bad interval"}"#.into();
    }

    let cache_key = format!("{}|{}|{}", market, symbol, interval);
    {
        let cache = shared.klines_cache.lock().unwrap();
        if let Some((t, body)) = cache.get(&cache_key) {
            if t.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(30) {
                return body.clone();
            }
        }
    }

    // [OKX-MIG] route to OKX candles (converted to Binance format) when config.toml says okx
    let (okx_on, _) = read_okx_cfg();
    if okx_on {
        return okx_klines(&symbol, &interval, shared, &cache_key).await;
    }

    let base = match market.as_str() {
        "testnet" => shared.bots.iter()
            .find(|b| b.market == "futures")
            .map(|b| b.rest_url.trim_end_matches('/').to_string())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "https://testnet.binancefuture.com".to_string()),
        _ => "https://fapi.binance.com".to_string(),
    };

    let url = format!("{}/fapi/v1/klines?symbol={}&interval={}&limit=200", base, symbol, interval);
    let client = reqwest::Client::builder()
        .timeout(Duration::from_secs(10))
        .pool_idle_timeout(std::time::Duration::from_secs(20)).tcp_keepalive(std::time::Duration::from_secs(15))/* P19-POOL */.build()
        .unwrap_or_default();

    match client.get(&url).send().await {
        Ok(resp) if resp.status().is_success() => match resp.text().await {
            Ok(body) => {
                shared.klines_cache.lock().unwrap().insert(cache_key, (Instant::now(), body.clone()));
                body
            }
            Err(_) => r#"{"error":"body read failed"}"#.into(),
        },
        _ => r#"{"error":"upstream unavailable"}"#.into(),
    }
}

/// рџ”§ v5: TradingView-РјР°СЂРєРµСЂС‹ вЂ” Р·Р°РєСЂС‹С‚С‹Рµ СЃРґРµР»РєРё РёР· Р¶СѓСЂРЅР°Р»Р°
/// РІ С„РѕСЂРјР°С‚Рµ [{bot, time, symbol, side, price, pnl}].
/// РСЃРїРѕР»СЊР·СѓРµС‚СЃСЏ РІРєР»Р°РґРєРѕР№ В«Р“СЂР°С„РёРєВ» (TV-СЂРµР¶РёРј) РґР»СЏ С‚РѕС‡РµРє РїРѕРІРµСЂС… РіСЂР°С„РёРєР°.
async fn tv_markers_json(shared: &Shared) -> String {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    if let Some(pool) = pool_for(shared, &shared.journal_db).await {
        if let Ok(rows) = sqlx::query(
            "SELECT bot_name, timestamp, symbol, side, price, pnl \
             FROM trade_journal WHERE event = 'CLOSE' AND price > 0 \
             ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 200",
        )
        .fetch_all(&pool)
        .await
        {
            for r in rows {
                out.push(json!({
                    "bot": r.try_get::<String, _>("bot_name").unwrap_or_default(),
                    "time": r.try_get::<String, _>("timestamp").unwrap_or_default(),
                    "symbol": r.try_get::<String, _>("symbol").unwrap_or_default(),
                    "side": r.try_get::<String, _>("side").unwrap_or_default(),
                    "price": r.try_get::<f64, _>("price").unwrap_or(0.0),
                    "pnl": r.try_get::<Option<f64>, _>("pnl").ok().flatten().unwrap_or(0.0),
                }));
            }
        }
    }
    json!(out).to_string()
}

/// РџСѓР» Рє Р‘Р” СЃ РєРµС€РёСЂРѕРІР°РЅРёРµРј.
async fn pool_for(shared: &Shared, db: &str) -> Option<SqlitePool> {
    {
        let pools = shared.pools.lock().unwrap();
        if let Some(p) = pools.get(db) {
            return Some(p.clone());
        }
    }
    let url = format!("sqlite:{}?mode=rw", db);
    match SqlitePool::connect(&url).await {
        Ok(p) => {
            shared.pools.lock().unwrap().insert(db.to_string(), p.clone());
            Some(p)
        }
        Err(_) => None,
    }
}

async fn dashboard_json(shared: &Shared) -> String {
    let mut bots = Vec::new();
    let (okx_on, okx_creds) = read_okx_cfg();

    for b in &shared.bots {
        let mut positions = Vec::new();
        if let Some(pool) = pool_for(shared, &b.db_path).await {
            if let Ok(rows) = sqlx::query(
                "SELECT symbol, side, entry_price, quantity, current_price, stop_loss, \
                 take_profit, opened_at, origin_strategy, funding_accrued, fees_paid \
                 FROM positions WHERE bot_name = ?",
            )
            .bind(&b.name)
            .fetch_all(&pool)
            .await
            {
                for r in rows {
                    let side: String = r.try_get("side").unwrap_or_default();
                    let entry: f64 = r.try_get("entry_price").unwrap_or(0.0);
                    let cur: f64 = r.try_get("current_price").unwrap_or(entry);
                    let qty: f64 = r.try_get("quantity").unwrap_or(0.0);
                    let upnl = if side == "BUY" { (cur - entry) * qty } else { (entry - cur) * qty };
                    positions.push(json!({
                        "symbol": r.try_get::<String, _>("symbol").unwrap_or_default(),
                        "side": side,
                        "entry": entry,
                        "qty": qty,
                        "current": cur,
                        "sl": r.try_get::<Option<f64>, _>("stop_loss").ok().flatten(),
                        "tp": r.try_get::<Option<f64>, _>("take_profit").ok().flatten(),
                        "opened_at": r.try_get::<String, _>("opened_at").unwrap_or_default(),
                        "origin": r.try_get::<String, _>("origin_strategy").unwrap_or_default(),
                        "funding": r.try_get::<f64, _>("funding_accrued").unwrap_or(0.0),
                        "fees": r.try_get::<f64, _>("fees_paid").unwrap_or(0.0),
                        "upnl": upnl,
                    }));
                }
            }
        }

        let mut risk = json!({});
        if let Some(pool) = pool_for(shared, &b.db_path).await {
            if let Ok(row) = sqlx::query(
                "SELECT realized_pnl, total_trades, win_trades, loss_trades, daily_loss, \
                 suspended, suspend_reason FROM risk_stats WHERE bot_name = ?",
            )
            .bind(&b.name)
            .fetch_one(&pool)
            .await
            {
                let pnl: String = row.try_get("realized_pnl").unwrap_or_else(|_| "0".into());
                let dl: String = row.try_get("daily_loss").unwrap_or_else(|_| "0".into());
                let total: i64 = row.try_get("total_trades").unwrap_or(0);
                let wins: i64 = row.try_get("win_trades").unwrap_or(0);
                let wr = if total > 0 { wins as f64 / total as f64 * 100.0 } else { 0.0 };
                risk = json!({
                    "pnl": pnl.parse::<f64>().unwrap_or(0.0),
                    "trades": total,
                    "wins": wins,
                    "losses": total - wins,
                    "win_rate": wr,
                    "daily_loss": dl.parse::<f64>().unwrap_or(0.0),
                    "suspended": row.try_get::<i64, _>("suspended").unwrap_or(0) != 0,
                    "suspend_reason": row.try_get::<String, _>("suspend_reason").unwrap_or_default(),
                });
            }
        }

        // Р‘Р°Р»Р°РЅСЃ вЂ” РєРµС€ 30СЃ РџРћ api_key
        let balance = {
            let cached = {
                let c = shared.balance_cache.lock().unwrap();
                c.get(&b.api_key)
                    .filter(|(t, _)| t.elapsed() < std::time::Duration::from_secs(30))
                    .map(|(_, v)| *v)
            };
            match cached {
                Some(v) => v,
                None => {
                    let v = if b.market == "futures" && !b.simulate {
                        if okx_on {
                            match &okx_creds {
                                Some((k, s, p)) => okx_balance(k, s, p).await,
                                None => None,
                            }
                        } else {
                            binance_futures_balance(&b.api_key, &b.api_secret, &b.rest_url).await
                        }
                    } else { None };
                    shared.balance_cache.lock().unwrap()
                        .insert(b.api_key.clone(), (std::time::Instant::now(), v));
                    v
                }
            }
        };

        bots.push(json!({
            "name": b.name,
            "symbol": b.symbol,
            "strategy": b.strategy,
            "market": b.market,
            "simulate": b.simulate,
            "interval": b.interval,
            "balance": balance,
            "positions": positions,
            "risk": risk,
        }));
    }

    // Р¶СѓСЂРЅР°Р»: trades (150, РІСЃРµ СЃРѕР±С‹С‚РёСЏ) + closes (500, С‚РѕР»СЊРєРѕ CLOSE)
    let mut trades = Vec::new();
    let mut closes = Vec::new();
    if let Some(pool) = pool_for(shared, &shared.journal_db).await {
        if let Ok(rows) = sqlx::query(
            "SELECT bot_name, timestamp, event, symbol, side, price, quantity, pnl \
             FROM trade_journal ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 150",
        )
        .fetch_all(&pool)
        .await
        {
            for r in rows {
                trades.push(json!({
                    "bot": r.try_get::<String, _>("bot_name").unwrap_or_default(),
                    "time": r.try_get::<String, _>("timestamp").unwrap_or_default(),
                    "event": r.try_get::<String, _>("event").unwrap_or_default(),
                    "symbol": r.try_get::<String, _>("symbol").unwrap_or_default(),
                    "side": r.try_get::<String, _>("side").unwrap_or_default(),
                    "price": r.try_get::<f64, _>("price").unwrap_or(0.0),
                    "qty": r.try_get::<f64, _>("quantity").unwrap_or(0.0),
                    "pnl": r.try_get::<Option<f64>, _>("pnl").ok().flatten(),
                }));
            }
        }
        if let Ok(rows) = sqlx::query(
            "SELECT bot_name, timestamp, symbol, side, price, quantity, pnl \
             FROM trade_journal WHERE event = 'CLOSE' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 500",
        )
        .fetch_all(&pool)
        .await
        {
            for r in rows {
                closes.push(json!({
                    "bot": r.try_get::<String, _>("bot_name").unwrap_or_default(),
                    "time": r.try_get::<String, _>("timestamp").unwrap_or_default(),
                    "event": "CLOSE",
                    "symbol": r.try_get::<String, _>("symbol").unwrap_or_default(),
                    "side": r.try_get::<String, _>("side").unwrap_or_default(),
                    "price": r.try_get::<f64, _>("price").unwrap_or(0.0),
                    "qty": r.try_get::<f64, _>("quantity").unwrap_or(0.0),
                    "pnl": r.try_get::<Option<f64>, _>("pnl").ok().flatten(),
                }));
            }
        }
    }

    json!({
        "bots": bots,
        "trades": trades,
        "closes": closes,
        "generated_at": chrono::Utc::now().to_rfc3339(),
    })
    .to_string()
}
